Catalog number : M00221
Uniform title: tripurārahasya māhatmyakaṇḍham
Main title: tripurārahasyam māhatmyakaṇḍham
Description:
Part 2 of gurumaṇḍalagranthamālāyā vol. 28 part 2 from chapter 51 to end.
Notes:
Transcribed by the staff of Muktabodha under the supervision of Mark S.G. Dyczkowski.

Revision 0: Sept. 18, 2010.
Publication country : India
########################################################################### 

p.514)

ekapañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ 

lalitāmāhātmye yāgana tuṣṭāyāḥ śrīdevyāḥsamāvirbhāvapuraḥ saraṃ 
tadadbhutarūpavarṇanam 

śṛṇu kumbhaja ! tenaivaṃ nirastāḥ sarvato'marāḥ |
duḥkhitāḥ kānaneṣvadridarīṣvantarguhāsu ca || 1 ||

nadīkuñjeṣu daityeśatrastā daityairalakṣitāḥ |
ūṣurdīrghatamaḥ kālaṃ kvaciddaityairvilakṣitāḥ || 2 ||

ākṛṣṭāstāḍitāḥ sevāṃ kuryurbhṛtyā yathā tathā |
evaṃ varṣasahasrāṇāmatītaṃ dvisahasrakam || 3 ||

tadā kvacittu devendro dadarśā'ṅgirasaṃ gurum |
praṇamya taṃ prāha śakraḥ baddhā'ñjalipuṭastadā || 4 ||

guro! māṃ paśya śocantaṃ deveśaṃ tvatsamāśrayam |
cirāya paridṛṣṭo'si śiṣye mayi dayāṃ kuru || 5 ||

nā'haṃ soḍhumitaḥ śaktaḥ kaṣṭamevaṃvidhaṃ vibho |
purā vidhiḥ prārthito'pi mayā bhūyaḥ svayaṃ jagau || 6 ||

nā'dhunā'sya pratīkāre kālo devapate śṛṇu |
pratīkṣa kālaṃ kālena vinā no sidhyatīhitam || 7 ||

p.515)

tasyā'dya vatsarā vṛttā asaṃkhyeyā yugātmakāḥ |
na taṃ kālaṃ prapaśyāmi leśato'pi sukhāvaham || 8 ||

paśya māṃ malinaṃ dīnaṃ kṣāmā'ṅgaṃ sarvato bhayam |
eṣa kuñjaḥ kaṇṭakitaḥ svargo nandanameva ca || 9 ||

sudharmā cā'tra maśakā gandharvāḥ svaramaṇḍalāḥ |
vandinaḥ karaṭā ete śālūrāḥ stutivācanāḥ || 10 ||

rambhādyāḥ khadyotagaṇā haṃsatūlaṃ śilātalam |
upadhānaṃ gulmapādo mantrā'hvānaṃ śivādhvaniḥ || 11 ||

pariṣadbakakaṅkādyāḥ paṭavāso mahīrajaḥ |
cāmarāḥ kaṇṭakitaruśākhā vātyāpravepitāḥ || 12 ||

evaṃ samācārayutaṃ paśyaṃstena drutaṃ manaḥ |
nūnaṃ bhāgyaṃ mamaivaitadityuktvā mūrcchito'bhavat || 13 ||

taṃ duḥkhamūrcchitaṃ dṛṣṭvā guruḥ kāruṇyamāgataḥ |
samāśvāsyā'marapatiḥ provācā'ṅgiraso vacaḥ || 14 ||

śṛṇu devarṣabha! budhā nā'nuśocanti kutracit |
āpatsu harṣakalitāḥ sampatsu samatāṃ gatāḥ || 15 ||

p.516)

sañjanā vikṛtiṃ naiva gacchanti viśadā'śayāḥ |
śokaṃ jitvā vimarśena sandhairyeṇa mahājanāḥ || 16 ||

prabhavanti śubhāyaiva kālamātrapratīkṣakāḥ |
śṛṇu te'trā'bhidhāsyāmi vyavasāyaṃ phalāvaham || 17 ||

tapasā toṣya bhūteśaṃ prāptavān sarvato'bhayam |
na yonijairmānasairvā mṛtyustasya śiveritaḥ || 18 ||

dvaividhyamantarā loke nā'sti kaścana kutracit |
devādyaiḥ puruṣaiścā'pi nā'sya mṛtyurudīritaḥ || 19 ||

ato'maragaṇairyuktastripurāṃ parameśvarīm |
prayāhi śaraṇaṃ nā'sti rakṣako'nyaḥ kathañcana || 20 ||

ityuktvā mārutaṃ devaṃ dadhyau devagurustadā |
dhyātamātro jagatprāṇaḥ praṇamya samupasthitaḥ || 21 ||

vācaspatistamāhā'tha śṛṇu māruta! me vacaḥ |
tvaṃ sarvadāgatiḥ sarvaprāṇaścā'pi mahābalaḥ || 22 ||

naya śakraṃ himagireḥ śikharaṃ vyomamārgataḥ |
śīghramanyān surān sarvān pāvakapramukhānapi || 23 ||

tatastato daityagaṇairānayā'lakṣito bhṛśam |
tatra pauruṣayogena sādhyaṃ sarvasamīhitam || 24 ||

p.517)

omityuktvā nimeṣeṇa devān sarvān samānayat |
sametā himavacchṛṅge śakrādyāḥ sarvato'marāḥ || 25 ||

atha vāyuṃ preṣayitvā cā'nināya guṇeśvarān |
niścitya bhaṇḍadaityasya vijayāya samīhitam || 26 ||

tripurāṃ parameśānīṃ vinā neha gatiḥ kvacit |
evaṃ vidhimukhā devāstripurāṃ parameśvarīm || 27 ||

dadhyuḥ sarvajagaddhātrīṃ nigṛhītā'kṣamārutāḥ |
evaṃ teṣāmagāddhyānaniṣṭhānāṃ vatsaraṃ śatam || 28 ||

atha devā mantrayitvā tripurāprītihetave |
mahāyāgena tripurāmayajan tantramārgataḥ || 29 ||

samāpte'tha mahāyāge mantradravyasusambhṛte |
yatra brahmā'bhavadbrahmā ācāryo gurureva ca || 30 ||

ṛtvijaḥ śivaviṣṇvādyā yāge tasmiṃstadā'bhavan |
prāpte'ntyadivase tasmiṃścidbhāvapravibhāvite || 31 ||

yāgavanhau cidākāre mahākuṇḍasamedhite |
ayajat pūrṇayā'hutyā gururdhyāyan parāmbikām || 32 ||

tadā bhaktyā saṃstuvatsu deveṣu tripurāmbikā |
prādurāsit kuṇḍamadhyāccidagnerjvalato'ntarāt || 33 ||

p.518) 

pūrṇā'hutyāṃ hutāyāṃ vai kuṇḍe jajvāla pāvakaḥ |
yojanaikasamucchrāyā jvālā'tyantaṃ vyavardhata || 34 ||

tatrā'bhavanmahāśabdaḥ parvatasyeva bhidyataḥ |
śatā'śaninipātātmā bhindaṃcchrotrāṇi nākinām || 35 ||

tato'marāṇāṃ cakṣūṃṣiṃ muṣṇan prauḍhena tejasā |
sakṛt sudāminīkoṭisaṅghaṭṭasadṛśacchaviḥ || 36 ||

mahāprakāśa udabhūdvahnijvālā'ntare tadā |
mahatā dhvaninā tadvattejasā ca budhāstadā || 37 ||

badhirā'ndhīkṛtāḥ sarve mūrcchitā āpatan bhuvi |
vinā guruṃ trimūrtibhyaḥ patitāḥ sarva eva te || 38 ||

tattejomadhyataḥ prādurāsīcchrītripurā'mbikā |
taruṇā'ruṇapuñjā'ṅgasampradāyatanucchaviḥ || 39 ||

vidyullateva vidyotattanūdyattanuvallarī |
vidyullatāvikasitāpūrṇacandrā'mbujā'nanā || 40 ||

kavarītimiraughodyatsindūrā'ruṇasuprabhā |
tārakājālavadvyomakavarīratnabhūṣaṇā || 41 ||

p.519)

dīrghaveṇībhogivaktraniryatsīmantajihvikā |
sindūrā'ruṇalohityā'kṣiptakeśanabho'ṅgaṇā || 42 ||

viparyastā'rdhacandrā'ṅkaphāle rājadviśeṣikā |
kuntalā'ligaṇā'kīrṇavikasadvadanā'mbujā || 43 ||

mukhasaundaryasarasīnīlā'mbujanibhekṣaṇā |
kandarpakoṭijananamantrā'kūtasamīkṣaṇā || 44 ||

kṣīrā'bdhikoṭilaharīsambhavā'pāṅgasundarā |
cāmpeyakalikāsampatprapoṣakasunāsikā || 45 ||

kuruvindā'darśaśobhāprollasadgaṇḍamaṇḍalā |
apāṅgasaridunmajjattāṭaṅkamaṇinīrajā || 46 ||

tāṭaṅkā'mbujasaṃsthānamuktā'valimarālikā |
karṇabhūṣākānticāpakaṭākṣaśarasantatiḥ || 47 ||

pakvadāḍimabījātmadantalauhityadacchadā |
vikasadraktakumudā'ntarakiñjalkadacchaviḥ || 48 ||

pravāladacchadodañcaddantakundasukorakā |
mandahaṃsakṣīranidhipravālaradanacchadā || 49 ||

mukhamāṇikyakamalavṛntā'bhacivukojjvalā |
maṇigraiveyakākalpalasadgīvā'bjasundarā || 50 ||

pāṇiratnamṛṇālodyatpadmakośastanadvayī |
māṇikyadehalatikāstanastavakaśobhinī || 51 ||

p.520)

mukhacandraprabhābhītinilīnakucakokikā |
mandasmitasaritpūrakhelatkucamarālikā || 52 ||

tanuvallīśākhikodyatpāṇipallavalohitā |
karapravālā'grajapākorakā'ṅgulimaṇḍitā || 53 ||

sṛṇipāśadhanurbāṇairlasadbāhucatuṣṭayā |
valayā'ṅgadaratnormikūrpāsavilasadbhujā || 54 ||

mukhapadmamṛṇālā'bhamuktāhāralatojjvalā |
romā'litanunālodyatkucadvandvā'bjakuḍmalā || 55 ||

romā'livallarīmūlanābhinimnā'lavālinī |
nābhisaronnītaromalatāśaivālavallikā || 56 ||

kausumbharatnavasanasamācchannanitambinī |
ūrdhvā'ṅgadhāraṇāmātranimittaprāptamadhyamā || 57 ||

sūkṣmā'bhrā'bhāṃ'śukā'ntasthadṛśyatārakabhūṣaṇā |
māṇikyakadalīkāṇḍapāribhāvyūrumaṇḍalā || 58 ||

padmarāganiṣaṅgā'bhajaṅghāyugasumaṇḍitā |
kamaṭhīpṛṣṭhavibhavasūcanaprapadā'nvitā || 59 ||

maṇinūpurasaṃrājatkiṅkiṇīcārusiñjitā |
vikasatpadmasaubhāgyavadānyapadapaṅkajā || 60 ||

p.521)

pādāmbujapravilasanmaṇihaṃsakamaṇḍitā |
nakhacandrasudhāsyandahṛtasannatataptatā || 61 ||

marālīmandagamanakulā'cāryapadadvayī |
bhaktavāñchāvitaraṇapadamandārapallavā || 62 ||

evamāvirbhavantīṃ tāṃ dṛṣṭvā brahmādayastadā |
daṇḍavat praṇatā bhūtvā jaya devīti tāṃ jaguḥ || 63 ||

tatra vācaspatirnatvā bhūyaḥ protthāya tāṃ parām |
paśyaṃstaddarśanodbhūtaharṣavāridhisamplutaḥ || 64 ||

uttaṃsitakaradvandvasampuṭodyatsaroruhaḥ |
netrā'mbujasravaddharṣavāripūrapariplutaḥ || 65 ||

astauṣīdatisupremabharagadgadamantharaḥ |
racanācāturīmodairmodayaṃstripurāmbikām || 66 ||

p.522)

tava vibhavavilāsaṃ varṇituṃ me na śaktiryadidamapi mayoktaṃ tāvakī naiva 
śaktiḥ |
na hi bhavati tato me vākpatitvasya hāniryadi bhavati tadā syāttvatprasādasya 
hāniḥ || 67 ||

suragururahamevaṃ vacmi yattattvameva tvamahamidamitīyatsarvamamba tvameva |
tata iha na hi me sto hānilābhau taraṅge madhurakaṭurasau vā 
kṣīrasindhorivā'nyau || 68 ||

yadiha vividhabhedaṃ prekṣate 
mūḍhadṛṣṭirmukuratalavirājaccitravadbhāsamānam |
yadidamiha purastāddarśitaṃ divyarūpaṃ tadapi ca kṛpayā naḥ 
pūrvavanmāyayaiva || 69 ||

vibudhasamudayānāṃ svaṃ varaṃ manyamāno janani tava vilāsairmohito 
mūḍhabudhiḥ |
taba niravadhiśaktiṃ varṇituṃ dṛśyabhedaṃ vihatatarkaistairthikastvāmajānan || 
70 ||

vividhavacanajālairasti nāstīti pakṣairvivadanaparamāṇāṃ yāti kālo vṛthaiva |

p.523) 

kṣaṇamapi jagadamba tvatkalāṃ cinmayīṃ ye nijahṛdi vimṛśantaḥ saṃsthitāste 
hi dhanyāḥ || 71 ||

vigataviṣayatṛṣṇāvāsane svā'ntaraṅge vimalamukuratulye niścale svātmanaiva |
tava janani kalāṃ tāṃ yoginaḥ prekṣamāṇāḥ paramasukhapadasthāste hi 
dhanyā jayanti || 72 ||

iti ciratarakālaṃ tvatsvarūpaṃ vimṛśyā'ntarabhuvi dṛḍhabhāvāḥ 
syurnisargasvabhāvāḥ |
tadanu bahiraśeṣaṃ tvatsvarūpaṃ mṛśantaḥ tvayi paramavilāsāste hi 
yogīndrapūjyāḥ || 73 ||

ahamapi lalite tvatpādapadmasya kañcit paricayamabhigamyā'ntaḥ sadā sarvato'pi 
|
vacanaviracanānāṃ cintanānāmapi tvāmaṇu kimapi vinā nā'vaimi tattvāṃ 
nato'smi || 74 ||

iti stutvā'maraguruḥ praṇato daṇḍavadbhuvi |
guṇabhūtā vidhimukhā api tuṣṭuvurambikām || 75 ||

jaya jaya janani jagallayapālanasarjanavibhave samācitirūpe |
sarvā'bhāsanatanurapi vitatā saṃvidanuttaramātraśarīrā || 76 ||

p.524)

naipuṇyametaddrpaṇasadṛśaṃ bhāhanirope'pyaticitraṃ te |
vijayatyetattava durghaṭanāghaṭanāśaktirmahatī sattā || 77 ||

svaṃ rūpaṃ tadvitatamapīśvari durghaṭaśaktyā parimitarūpam |
kṛtvā darśanadṛśyavibhedān vidhidhān sarvān paribhāsayasi || 78 ||

evaṃ svīyaṃ rūpamanekaṃ parimitarūpā paśyantī tvam |
bandhkaṃ citparimṛṣyā'ntaryatnādbhūyo bhāsi yathāvat || 79 ||

svātmā'darśe pravitatalīlāṃ bhāvayasītthaṃ svātantryāttvam |
dṛṣṭvā kalpitametatsvīyaṃ nandasyaniśaṃ devi ! namaste || 80 ||

santatalīlāmiti yaḥ kaścana śaktyā bhinnastava jānīyāt |
sa ca lokānāṃ tvamiva maheśvari ! līlādraṣṭā nandati devaḥ || 81 ||

gūḍhaṃ rūpaṃ tava savilāsaṃ draṣṭuṃ śaktā na hi ye dīnāḥ |
teṣāmetatparamaṃ rūpaṃ prakaṭitamakṣṇoḥ phalasaṃjananam || 82 ||

p.525)

kuṅkumaśoṇaṃ gurukucanamraṃ candrakalāḍhyaṃ sulilatarūpam |
sṛṇiśaracāpān pāśaṃ bibhradvayamiha bhūyaḥ praṇamāmastat || 83 ||

stutvaivamapi brahmādyāḥ praṇemuḥ pādasannidhau |
atha tān prāha paramā vatsā uttiṣṭhata drutam || 84 ||

eṣā'haṃ kṛtasannāhā prāptā śatruvadhāya vaḥ |
kiṃ vo'bhilaṣitaṃ bhūyaḥ pratīcchadhvaṃ guṇeśvarāḥ || 85 ||

tvamapyāṅgirasa brūhi vāñchitaṃ kiṃ dadāmi te |
niśamyaivaṃ vaco devyāḥ procurbrahmādayastata. || 86 ||

iti śrīmaditihāsottame tripurārahasye māhātmyakhaṇḍe lalitāmāhātmye 
bhagavatīlalitā'virbhāvavarṇanaṃ nāmaikapañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 4233 ||



p.526)

dvipañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ

devebhyaḥ samāśvastebhyo vṛhaspatyanurodhena devyā svarūpadarśanārthaṃ 
śrīsuktajapavidhānavarṇanapuraḥsaraṃ mantribhiḥ saha 
mantraṇamanubhaṇḍadvārā devagaṇatrāsāya sasajjībhavanaṃ 
tripurāvirbhāvavarṇanam 


śṛṇu kumbhodbhava ! mune ! brahmādināṃ vacastataḥ |
prāhurbrahmamukhā devīṃ bhaktinamrasumūrtayaḥ || 1 ||

bhūya evaṃ mahāpatsu jagatāṃ rakṣaṇodyatā |
vidheyamamarā'rīṇāṃ śatrūṇāṃ pravihiṃsanam || 2 ||

mātaḥ ! śṛṇu kathaṃ bhaṇḍadaityo bhūyastvayā hataḥ |
nūnaṃ trilocanenā'sau sarvato'maratāṃ gataḥ || 3 ||

kathaṃ vā'marakāryaṃ syāt kathaṃ cā'suraghātanam |
śrutvaivaṃ dhātṛmukhyānāṃ vacaḥ sā prāha śaṅkarī || 4 ||

mā kṛthāḥ padmayone tvaṃ śaṅkāmatrā'surā'hatau |
mā'haṃ yonisamudbhūtā manasā vā'pi kasyacit || 5 ||

agnikuṇḍasamudbhūtā strīsvarūpā'smyalaukikī |
aprasiddhena cā'streṇa nihanmyenaṃ mahāsuram || 6 ||

śutvaivaṃ lalitāvākyaṃ jahuścintāṃ guṇeśvarāḥ |
athovācā'maraguruḥ parāśaktiṃ kṛtāñjaliḥ || 7 ||

devi ! tvāṃ sarvajananīmevaṃrūpāṃ maheśvarīm |
paśyantu śakrapramukhā bhūyāsustena pāvitāḥ || 8 ||

p.527)

niśāmyā'ṅgirasavacaḥ prāha sā tripureśvarī |
śṛṇu jīvaśakramukhā naivaṃ śaktimayīṃ tanum || 9 ||

mamaite draṣṭumapyarhā nā'nyairdṛṣṭamidaṃ vapuḥ |
na hi sādhāraṇaḥ kaścidevaṃrūpāṃ prapaśyati || 10 ||

yūyaṃ me'syāstanorbhaktāścaitaddhyānaparāḥ sadā |
ato vo darśitaṃ caitannānyairdṛṣṭaṃ kadācana || 11 ||

rūpāntaraṃ parimitaṃ tat prapaśyatu vajrabhṛt |
atha bhūyo'pi bahudhā prārthayāmāsa tāṃ guruḥ || 12 ||

pradarśayaitacchakrāya ceti sā gurumabravīt |
yadi māmīdṛśīṃ śakraḥ paśyettacchṛṇu vacmi te || 13 ||

māṃ samārādhayatu sa śrīsūktavidhinā tataḥ |
darśayiṣyāmi tasmai svamidaṃ rūpaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 14 ||

ityuktvā mūrcchitān devān sudhādṛṣṭyā prabodhya sā |
antardhānaṃ yayau tatra brahmādīnāṃ prapaśyatām || 15 ||

athotthitāḥ śakramukhā devāḥ sarve bṛhaspateḥ |
śrutvā śrītripurādevyā āvirbhāvaṃ tato vṛṣā || 16 ||

devaiḥ sameto guruṇā vijñātopāsanakramaḥ |
lakṣmīsūktavidhānena bhaktiśradhā'tinirbharaḥ || 17 ||

ārādhayāmāsa parāṃ tripurāṃ parameśvarīm |
evaṃ tasyā'rādhayato māsāḥ ṣaṭ pañca cā'tyayuḥ || 18 ||

p.528)

tadantare daityagururjñātvā'maraviceṣṭitam |
bhaṇḍāsurāya tadvṛttaṃ jñāpayāmāsa sarvaśaḥ || 19 ||

śṛṇu daityapate śatruḥ sureśo himavadgirau |
sāmprataṃ paramāṃ śaktimupatiṣṭhati bhaktitaḥ || 20 ||

sā prasannā kṣaṇenaiva tvāṃ saputraṃ sabāndhāvam |
dhvaṃsayiṣyati dāvāgnistṛṇadārucayaṃ yathā || 21 ||

yatasva tāvadeva tvaṃ yāvannāśaṃ na yāsyasi |
nā'tropāsyo dṛśyate'nyaḥ sāmno balavadāśrite || 22 ||

divaṃ bhuvaṃ samarpyā'śu sadhanaṃ saparicchadam |
indrāya pātālamātrarājyastāṃ jagadīśvarīm || 23 ||

prayāhi śaraṇaṃ devīmanyathā nāśameṣyasi |
ityuktvā pūjitastena jagāmā'bhimatāṃ gatim || 24 ||

atha bhaṇḍāsuro daityo mantribhirmantraśikṣaṇaiḥ |
mantrayāmāsa daityo'sau sevitaḥ śūnyake pure || 25 ||

rājñā'jñaptā mantriṇaste procuḥ svasvamatā'nugam |
kaścit sāma bhedamanyaḥ parodānamuvācaha || 26 ||

daṇḍamāhustathā kecidevaṃ vyākulitā sabhā |
tadantare viśukrasya putraḥ śukrasamo mataḥ || 27 ||

p.529)

śrutavarmeti vikhyātaḥ prāha natvā sureśvaram |
śṛṇu rājannarthaśāstramaryādāsahitaṃ vacaḥ || 28 ||

bāhyadṛṣṭirdoṣavatī nisargeṇaiva cañcalā |
dṛṣṭyā buddhyākhyayā sattvarūpayā vimṛśet kṛtim || 29 ||

avimṛśyakaro vahnau pataṅga iva naśyati |
suvimṛśyakaro yastu taṃ sampat pravṛṇotyalam || 30 ||

mantrī vṛṭhā'bhiniveśaḥ sphuṭadṛṣṭirdratagrahaḥ |
rājā'himatavaktā ca satataṃ svārthatatparaḥ || 31 ||

nāśayedrājyasahitaṃ rājānaṃ nā'tra saṃśayaḥ |
yathā naurāsanacchidrā sapānthaṃ nāvikaṃ jale || 32 ||

tasmāddurmantriṇastyājyā rājñā rājyaṃ bubhūṣatā |
śṛṇvahaṃ te'bhidhāsyāmi kartavyā'nantarakriyām || 33 ||

mahārāja ! tvayā devāttapasā toṣitāt purā |
aprāptamabhayaṃ strībhyo matvā'lpamiti te mudhā || 34 ||

tadatra na hi visrambhaḥ purā mahiṣadānavaḥ |
rājyaṃ tribhuvane prāpya striyā vinihato balī || 35 ||

purā śumbhaniśumbhau ca balināvativikramau |
hatau yuddhe caṇḍikayā sabalāviti naḥ śrutam || 36 ||

p.530)

jaye liṅgaṃ na hetuḥ syānnimittaṃbuddhivikramau |
anyacca te'bhidhāsyāmi cirametaddhi saṃsthitam || 37 ||

daityadānavarakṣaḥsu yo yaḥ śubhataro hyabhūt |
taṃ tamuddharate viṣṇurmārgasthamiva kaṇṭakam || 38 ||

taṃ naḥ śrutaṃ tena daityaripuṇā hariṇā khalu |
toṣitā paramāśaktirāvirbhūtā vadhāya te || 39 ||

homā'gnikuṇḍādyadyonimanobhyāṃ na ca sā'bhavat |
amānasā yonijebhyo nā'maratvaṃ tvayā vṛtam || 40 ||

ācāryo bhagavān śukraḥ sarvabudhimatāṃ varaḥ |
sa yadāha na cā'lpaṃ tadvidhi daityagaṇeśvara ! || 41 ||

tasmādgurūktameveha yuktaṃ me pratibhāsate |
manīṣiṇā sarvayatnairnirasyaṃ kālajaṃ bhayam || 42 ||

abhaye vikramo varyaḥ śāntireva bhayā'game |
sarvathā mūlanāśo hi śaktyā'pohyaḥ prajānatā || 43 ||

kṛtte mūle phalāśā kā mūlagope phalodayaḥ |
atra me'bhimataṃ rājan śṛṇu buddhyā vicāritam || 44 ||

p.531)

gurūktarītyā saṃśāntiṃ sampādya prakṛte punaḥ |
tapasā pūrvavaddevān śeṣaṃ bhayamapohya ca || 45 ||

tato bhūyastrijagatīṃ vaśe kuru suvikramaḥ |
iti te'bhihito mantro nā'taḥ kṣemaṃ bhavet kvacit || 46 ||

vimṛśya yadabhīṣṭaṃ te tadācara na te ciram |
śrutavarmavacaḥ śrutvā bhaṇḍo daityapatirvacaḥ || 47 ||

prāha taṃ dūṣayanneva pakṣaṃ daityasabhāgataḥ |
martukāmasyā'gadavanna tattasya hyarocata || 48 ||

tadantare madonmatto daityaḥ provāca bhūpatim |
daityeśvara mayā proktaṃ śṛṇvatrā'tisukhāvaham || 49 ||

na praveśyā mantragoṣṭhyāṃ bālāḥ kātaratāṃ gatāḥ |
eṣa bālastava bhujabalaṃ no vetti kiñcana || 50 ||

bhīrorviprasyośanaso vākyādatitarāmayam |
rājñāṃ yuddhaprasaṅgeṣu kā viprasya bhavenmatiḥ || 51 ||

biprā vaitānadhiṣaṇā vedavidyāvicakṣaṇāḥ |
nimantraṇavidhānajñāsteṣāṃ yuddheṣu kā gatiḥ || 52 ||

svadhītestīkṣṇadhārājñāḥ śūrāḥ kuśasamitstṛtau |
na khaḍgadhārāṃ jānanti na śūrāḥ śarasaṃstṛtau || 53 ||

p.532)

hanta viprairapi yadi yuddhaṃ mantreṇa jīyate |
grāmasiṃhabibhīṣitaistadebhā mūtramutsṛjuḥ || 54 ||

yadi viprā yuddhakṛtyamapi jānīyuruddhatam |
tarhi mattebhakumbhasya vipāṭo jambukaiḥ kṛtaḥ || 55 ||

uttiṣṭhaiṣa kṣaṇo rājanna viśrāntiṃ samarhati |
yoṣidvā puruṣo vāpi nopekṣyaḥ śatrutāṃ gataḥ || 56 ||

hantā'dya yoṣiddaityendra merūnmūlanavikramam |
vijeṣyati pratāpā'rkasantāpitajagattrayam || 57 ||

tadā nīlotpaladalairnirbhidyeta mahīdharaḥ |
kuśāgrairapi viddhaḥ syāllohaśailo nirantaraḥ || 58 ||

atha vā tvamihaivā'sva kāmabhogaparaḥ sukham |
māmā'jñāpaya te bhṛtyaṃ gatvā'haṃ tānnimeṣataḥ || 59 ||

brahmādīṃstāṃ ca baddhvā vā hatvā vopaharāmi te |
śrutvaivaṃ daityavacanaṃ prahṛṣṭo bhaṇḍadānavaḥ || 60 ||

prahasya śrutavarmāṇaṃ prāha kālavaśaṃ gataḥ |
vatsa ! kiṃ yuddhavārtābhireva bhīto'si vai mudhā || 61 ||

p.533)

naivamasmatkule kaścijjāto vā bhavitā'pi vā |
yaḥ striyo yuddhavārtābhireva bhīto napuṃsakaḥ || 62 ||

bālo'si gaccha kāntābhiścāṭubhāṣaṇamarhasi |
dhiṅmāṃ yastvādṛśaṃ cakre mantriṇaṃ mantranāśakam || 63 ||

dhīrāṇāmapi dhairyā'drivajravākkauśalaṃ khalam |
ityuktvā protthitaḥ khaḍgahasto devān vihiṃsitum || 64 ||

koṭikoṭyasurairyuktaḥ śūraiścitrā'yudhairyayau |
yatra śakramukhā devāstripurāṃ samupasthitāḥ || 65 ||

āgataṃ bhaṇḍadaityeśaṃ śrutvā śakramukhāḥ surāḥ |
atyantabhītā deveśīmuccaḥ pāhīti saṃjaguḥ || 66 ||

bhītān viditvā tripurā prāha nityāṃ caturdaśīm |
gaccha jvālāmālini tvaṃ rakṣa bhītān surān drutam || 67 ||

ājñaptaivaṃ yato daityā āyāntyamarahiṃsakāḥ |
jvālayā tān rurodhoccairjvālāmālinikā śivā || 68 ||

p.534)

dṛṣṭvā jvālāvṛtaṃ deśaṃ matvā dāvāgnisamplutān |
hatān devān praharṣeṇa bhaṇḍadaityaḥ punaryayau || 69 ||

devāśca daityasenānāṃ śrutvā kolāhalaṃ bhṛśam |
vitrastā martukāmāste chittvā'ṅgāni samantataḥ || 70 ||

agnikuṇḍe devatāyāḥ prītaye juhavuḥ pṛthak |
aśeṣā'ṅgāni hutvaivaṃ pūrṇāhutitayā tanum || 71 ||

viśiṣṭāṃ hotukāmāste peṭhurmantrānupāṃśutaḥ |
tadantare sā tripurā bhaktarakṣāparāyaṇā || 72 ||

kuṇḍamadhyādāvirāsīttaḍitkoṭisamaprabhā |
jātaścaṭacaṭāśabdaḥ kuṇḍamadhyānmahonnataḥ || 73 ||

jvālā vyavardhatā'tīva vahneste dadṛśuḥ surāḥ |
jvālāmadhye mahādevīṃ sarvasaundaryasantatim || 74 ||

hṛṣṭā jaya jayetyuccairavocurdaṇḍavannatāḥ |
athotthāya śakramukhāstuṣṭuvustāṃ maheśvarīm || 75 ||

ājagmurbrahmaviṣṇvādyā munayaścarṣayastathā |
puṣpavṛṣṭirabhūdvyomno nedurdundubhayo'pi ca || 76 ||

vavurvātāḥ sukhasparśāḥ sugandhā mṛdusañcarāḥ |
brahmādyāḥ sarvatastasyāḥ pūjāṃ cakruryathāvidhi || 77 ||

p.535)

mahopacāramālyādyairupahārairviśeṣataḥ |
vividhairbhakṣyabhojyādyairāsavaiḥ piśitairapi || 78 ||

evaṃ sampūjya deveśīṃ toṣayāmāsuruccakaiḥ |
gāyanairnṛtyavādyārdyairutsavairapi bhūriśaḥ || 79 ||


iti śrīmaditihāsottame tripurārahasye māhātmyakhaṇḍe lalitopākhyāne 
devānāmārttaprārthanayā tripurāvirbhāvavarṇanaṃ nāma 
dvipañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 4312 ||




p.536)

tripañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ

lalitāmāhātmye yāgena tuṣṭāyāḥ śrīdevyāḥsarvadevadarśanārthaṃ 
śrīmeruparvate svādhiṣṭhānasthāpanavarṇanam  

atha śakramukhā devā dṛṣṭvā tāṃ parameśvarīm |
vidhātṛmukhyānūcuste devyāḥ saṃsthānahetave || 1 ||

śrutvā'maravaco brahmamukhāstāṃ jagadīśvarīm |
praṇamya prārthayāmāsuryattacchṛṇu ghaṭodbhava ! || 2 ||

parameśvari ! śakrādyaistava saṃsthānamīhitam |
asyāṃ jagatyāṃ yannityaṃ paśyāmastvāṃ maheśvarīm || 3 ||

vayaṃ guṇeśvarā nityaṃ tvatkṛpā'ttamahitvataḥ |
prāpya tvadīyaṃ te lokaṃ paśyāmastvatpadāmbujam || 4 ||

ete śakrādayo devā niyatyā niyatāstava |
brahmāṇḍabhedaṃ nā'rhanti tattvāṃ draṣṭumabhīpsavaḥ || 5 ||

tadatra lokaṃ nirmāya nivāsaṃ kartumarhasi |
prārthitetthaṃ brahmamukhaistripurāmbā maheśvarī || 6 ||

meruśṛṅge viśvakarmanirmite nagare śubhe |
avasat saparīvārā yathā loke svake tathā || 7 ||

p.537) 

athā'rthitā nijā'rdhāṃśaśavibhāgaparikalpitam |
kāmeśvaraṃ patiṃ cakre samūḍhā'bhavadambikā || 8 ||

itthaṃ hayā'syagaditaṃ niśamya ghaṭasambhavaḥ |
papraccha vibhavaśrutyāṃ parāyāṃ jātakautukaḥ || 9 ||

hayagrīva ! dayāsindho ! kutra saṃsthaṃ hi tatpuram | 
kīdṛśaṃ kiṃ pramāṇañca yasmin vasati sā parā || 10 ||

indrādīnāmapraveśyaṃ viśvaśilpiḥ kathaṃ nu tam |
jñātavānnāma kiṃ tasya saṃsthānañcā'pi kīdṛśam || 11 ||

athā'pi śakrapramukhaiḥ śrīsūktavidhinā nanu |
ārādhitā sā tripurā prasannā'bhavadambikā || 12 ||

vidhānaṃ kīdṛśaṃ tasya sūktañcā'pi kathaṃvidham |
kiṃ māhātmyaṃ bhavet sūktametanme vaktumarhasi || 13 ||

bhaktasya tava śiṣyasya kṛpāṃ kuru hayānana ! |
itthaṃ paryanuyuktaḥ sa hayāsyo munisattamaḥ || 14 ||

matvā yogyaṃ hi tachrutvā nijagāda sa harṣitaḥ |
śṛṇu kumbhaja vakṣyāmi bhaktiśradhāyuto hyasi || 15 ||

naitadaśraddadhānāya cā'bhaktāya śaṭhāya ca |
vaktumarhaṃ sarvathaiva dhanyo'si tvaṃ mahītale || 16 ||

p.538)

yatkathāśravaṇotsāha īdṛśastava dṛśyate |
etasya kāraṇaṃ vedmi naitadalpaphalaṃ mune ! || 17 ||

guṇamūrtiṣu sarveṣāṃ bhaktiḥ sādhāraṇī bhavet |
tripurāpādabhaktistu durlabhā sarvato bhavet || 18 ||

yatra syāt tripurābhaktistañjanma caramaṃ bhavet |
śivaviṣṇvādibhaktistu dvāaramatra prakīrtitam || 19 ||

krameṇā'rādhya viṣṇvādīn prāpya śrīpādasevanam |
mucyate sarvathā janturnānyathā kalpakoṭibhiḥ || 20 ||

atropapattiṃ vakṣyāmi saṃśayaste bhavenna hi |
loke sāmrājyayuktasya ye'nugāstāratamyataḥ || 21 ||

mukhyā madhyāstathā hīnāste tuṣṭāḥ svapadaṃ prati |
anirodhaṃ svoparisthaprāptau mārgaṃ diśanti ca || 22 ||

yathā'dau dvārikastuṣṭo dvāreṣvapratibandhanam |
prāṅgaṇeśaprāptimāpa diśatyeva ghaṭodbhava ! || 23 ||

brahmādyā guṇasambhūtāstatsamārādhakā iti |
na sandehastava bhavet suprasiddhatayaiva ca || 24 ||

p.539)

vastvadvaite'pi laukikyā dṛṣṭyā tanniyateḥ sphuṭam |
mukhyagauṇaprabhedaśca yathā deheṣu vai śiraḥ || 25 ||

nā'trā'śvāso duṣkṛtināmandhānāñca prapaśyatām |
ye ca pādabhavā devāḥ sadā sevanatatparāḥ || 26 ||

tvaṃ mahābhāgyavān brahman prāpto yacchrīparāratim |
etat saṅgasya māhātmyamaho bhāgyamaho tapaḥ || 27 ||

śṛṇu brahmaṃstava parāpādabhaktiprayojakam |
yatte priyā satī lopāmudrā'khyā rājakanyakā || 28 ||

purā sā pitṛgehasthā prāpa bhaktiṃ parāpade |
taddhetuṃ te pravakṣyāmi na tajjānāti kaścana || 29 ||

tripurāmukhyaśaktistu bhagamālinikā'bhidhā |
tatsevanaparo rājā sarvadā sarvabhāvataḥ || 30 ||

bālyādiyaṃ śuddhacittā pitṛsevāparāyaṇā |
piturdṛṣṭvopāsanāyāḥ kramaṃ devyā yathākramam || 31 ||

rājyakarma kare tasmiṃstāṃ samārādhayatyasau |
evaṃ cirā'rādhanena bhaktyā bhāvanayā'pi ca || 32 ||

tutoṣa sā bhagavatī vareṇa samacchandayat |
vavre cā'sau sarvajagatpūjyāyāḥ pādasevanam || 33 ||

p.540)

prasannā sā'pi sadvidyāṃ traipurīṃ samalakṣayat |
lakṣitā cā'pi tāṃ vidyāṃ vāksamudrapariplutām || 34 ||

samuddharadratnamiva tatastasya prasādanāt |
vidyā ṛṣitvaṃ samprāptā tannāmnā sā sphuṭaṅgatā || 35 ||

tasyāḥ saṅgaprabhāveṇa tava bhaktisamudgamaḥ |
iti śrutvā kumbhabhavaḥ patnyā vibhavavistaram || 36 ||

prasannaḥ pūjayāmāsa patnīṃ śrīpādatatparām |
athā'vadaddhayāsyo'pi prasannaḥ kumbhajanmane || 37 ||

śṛṇu te sampravakṣyāmi kalaśīsuta ! saṃyataḥ |
śrīsūktasya vidhānaṃ yatpṛṣṭaṃ paramapāvanam || 38 ||

na vaktavyamabhaktāya nā'nyadevaparāya ca |
vidyevaitadgopitavyaṃ sarvatrā'tisugopitam || 39 ||

śrīsūktaṃ ṣoḍaśarcaṃ tadādi vedasamudbhavam |
purā śriyā sudṛṣṭaṃ tadvedā'bdheḥ samyaguddhṛtam || 40 ||

tenā'rādhya parāṃ śaktiṃ tripurāṃ suvidhānataḥ |
samprāptā lokamātṛtvaṃ sarvapūjyatvamāgatā || 41 ||

ārādhitā vatsarāṇāmarbudānyekaviṃśatiḥ |
prasannā chandayāmāsa vareṇa tripurā parā || 42 ||

p.541)

tayā vṛtañca sāyujyaṃ tataḥ prāha parāmbikā |
vatse ! tvayā vinā viṣṇuraprabhuḥ paripālane || 43 ||

apālitaṃ tena caitadviśīryeta nimeṣataḥ |
tato me niyaterbhaṅga eva nā'rhasi samprati || 44 ||

tat sāmprataṃ diśāmyevaṃ tvadākhyāṃ sarvadā'pyaham |
dhārayāmi svarūpañca tatte vakṣyāmi śrūyatām || 45 ||

śrīvidyetyahamākhyātā śrīpuraṃ me puraṃ bhavet |
śrīcakraṃ me bhaveccakraṃ śrīkramaḥ syānmama kramaḥ || 46 ||

śrīsūktametadbhūyānme vidyā śrīṣoḍaśī bhavet |
mahālakṣmītyahaṃ khyātā tvattādātmyena saṃsthitā || 47 ||

tvaṃ caturdhā matsamīpe pūjāṃ prāpsyasi saṃsthitā |
yadyattava priyaṃ loke tanmamā'pi priyaṃ bhavet || 48 ||

pūjyase bhārgave vāre bhaktaistvaṃ suvidhānataḥ |
tatrā'hamapi pūjyā syāṃ bhaktairapi viśeṣataḥ || 49 ||

tatra sūktabhavairmantraiḥ pūjitā hyupacārakaiḥ |
hutā'pi vividhairdravyaiḥ prasannā vāñchitā'rthadā || 50 ||

abhiṣiktā ca salilairdugdhādyaiḥ phalajairasaiḥ |
sarvakāmapradā yantre mūrtyādike'pi vā || 51 ||

p.542)

nitye naimittike kāmye pūjane'nyatra cā'pi me |
lakṣmīsūktaṃ ṣoḍaśarcaṃ devyāḥ snānavidhau paṭhet || 52 ||

sakṛdāvartanāllakṣmīḥ sadā tasya gṛhe vaset |
pūjā'śaktau kevalaṃ vā sūktante yaḥ paṭhet sadā || 53 ||

tasyā'khilaṃ kṛtaṃ pūrṇaṃ bhavettuṣṭā bhavāmyaham |
vidyā'pi tvatsamāyogādbhaviṣyati mama priyā || 54 ||

ahaṃ vidyātmikā yattadbījaṃ te sarvaśobhanam |
pūrṇā tena samādiṣṭā mahāśrīṣoḍaśākṣarī || 55 ||

upacāreṣu pūjāyā mantrān sūktabhavān paṭhet |
sā pūjā syānmahāpūjā prasannā'haṃ tato drutam || 56 ||

sūkte'rtharūpā guptā'haṃ madbījañcā'pi gopitam |
pūrṇarūpā prārthitā'haṃ ṛṣibhiḥ pāvakāt pituḥ || 57 ||

nā'nyat priyataraṃ loke tvatsūktādbhavati kvacit |
tvamahaṃ devyahaṃ tvañca nāvayorantaraṃ bhavet || 58 ||

īkṣate yo'ntaraṃ tasya bhaveyuḥ paramāpadaḥ |
iti dattvā varaṃ tasyai ramāyai tripurā parā || 59 ||

jagāma vyomatāṃ sadyaḥ proktametadghaṭodbhava |
śrīsūktasya mahitvaṃ tenaitadbrūyāḥ kathañcana ||60 ||


abhakteṣu śaṭheṣvantaḥśraddhāvirahiteṣu ca |
atha vakṣye śrīpurasya vṛttaṃ lokottaraṃ śṛṇu || 61 ||

yasya śravaṇamātreṇa sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate |
yadā sā paramā devī prārthitā vidhimukhyakaiḥ || 62 ||

śakrādīnāṃ darśanārthaṃ sthitiṃ kurviti kumbhaja ! |
aṅgīcakāra saṃsthānaṃ līlāvigrahadhāriṇī || 63 ||

tatastvaṣṭāramāhūya meruśṛṅge mahonnate |
ājñāpayadvidhirdevyāḥ śrīpurasya vinirmitau || 64 ||

viśvakarmā tathā'jñataḥ praṇamya prapitāmaham |
bhagavanna mayā jñātaṃ tatpuraṃ kutra saṃsthitam || 65 ||

kiṃ pramāṇaṃ kimākāraṃ kimmāṃ kiṃvidhaṃ sthitam |
etanme śaṃsa bhagavan vidhāsye'haṃ niśamya tat || 66 ||

atha prāha jagatstraṣṭā śṛṇu viśvakṛte bruve |
śrīpurasya pramāṇañca saṃsthānaṃ prakṛtiṃ tathā || 67 ||

rūpañcā'pi sphuṭataraṃ sāvadhānamanāḥ śṛṇu |
yadcchrutvā tripurāpādabhaktimāsādayecchubhām || 68 ||

kanakādriḥ sumervākhyo jagaccitrakalevaraḥ |
tasya madhye mahāśṛṅgo yaścatu'śatayojanaḥ || 69 ||

p.544)

tatra śrītripurādevyāḥ puraṃ kuru jagatkṛte |
śrutvā mayā yathāproktaṃ kartvyaṃ suvidhānataḥ || 70 ||

manoharaṃ cārutaraṃ vaprabhedasuśobhitam |
yathā jagatpratiṛtistanme nigadataḥ śṛṇu || 71 ||

iti śrīmaditihāsottame tripurārahasye māhātmyakhaṇḍe 
hayagrīvāgastyasamvāde śrīsūktavidhānapuraḥsaraṃ 
śrīcakrasaṃsthānavidhivaraṇanaṃ nāma tripañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 4383 ||

p.545)

catuḥpañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ  
śrīpuravarṇanam 

anekakoṭisaṅkhyānāṃ bahiḥsthitaḥ |
ūrdhvato'nantasaṅkhyākaḥ sudhāsindhurjagatkute || 1 ||

sumerusadṛśonnabhranṛtyadvīcitaraṅgakaḥ |
tasya madhye manidvīpaḥ prabhākṣiptatamastatiḥ || 2 ||

yojanānāṃ koṭiśatairvistṛto'timanoharaḥ |
kalpadrumavanaiścitraiścitritā paritaḥ sthalī || 3 ||

tatra sthitaṃ puraṃ tasyāḥ sarvaratnasamujjvalam |
yojanānāṃ caturlakṣairdīrghāyamasuvistṛtam || 4 ||

tāvanmitastasya sālo lohasāramayasthitaḥ |
caturasraḥ sarvabhadraḥ samujjvalitadiktaṭaḥ || 5 ||

catuḥsahasramunnamro yojanānāṃ savaprakaḥ |
caturdhāryuto bāhye parikheṇa virājitaḥ || 6 ||

tadantaḥ saptasāhasrayojanā'ntaritaḥ sthitaḥ |
prākāraḥ kāṃsyaracitaḥ proktamāno'tibhāsuraḥ || 7 ||

tadantare kalpavṛkṣavāṭīgaṇaśatā'kule |
daśayojanavistīrṇavāpīśatavirājite || 8 ||

śatayojanavistīrṇapraphullakamalā'karaiḥ |
dikṣvaṣṭasu yutesvarvāyasmayārdhabahirbhuvi (???) || 9 ||

kāṃsyakḷptā'ntarabhuvi nānāpakṣimṛgairyute |
kamalā'karā'ntarbhuvi kḷptakāṃsyagṛhāṣṭake || 10 ||

anekacitraracite pañcā'varaṇarājite |
"śatayojanavistārasamunnamre suśobhite || 11 ||

mahāsane mahākālaḥ kālīsaṃśliṣṭavigrahaḥ |
tripurāmbāpādasevāprāptasaubhāgyanirbharaḥ || 12 ||

ātmānamaṣṭadhā kṛtvā sthito gehāṣṭake pṛthak |
asaṃkhyaparivāraighaiḥ sevitaḥ satataṃsthitaḥ || 13 ||

antaḥkāṃsyamayādvaprāt proktayojanadūrataḥ |
prākārastāmraracitastaruṇā'ruṇasamprabhaḥ || 14 ||

proktamānadvāravāpāpadminīgṛhamadhyabhūḥ |
bahirantaḥ kāṃsyatāmramayasthalayutā'ntaraḥ || 15 ||

tadantaraṃ proktavattu kalpavṛkṣavanairyutam |
gṛheṣu rājate teṣu vasantartuḥ supeśalaḥ || 16 ||

madhumādhavaśaktibhyāṃ sevamānaḥ parāmbikām |
saṃyutaḥ svaparīvāraiḥ samedhate nijaśriyā || 17 ||

p. 547)

sālamānaṃ dvāramapi vāpikāḥ padminībhuvaḥ |
proktarītyaiva vijñeyā viśeṣaḥ procyate kramāt || 18 ||

atha sīsamayaḥ sālaḥ santānavanamaṇḍitaḥ |
grīṣmartuḥ saṃsthitaḥ śukraśuciśaktiparīvṛtaḥ || 19 ||

ārakūṭamayo vapro haricandanavāṭikaḥ |
nabhonabhasyaśaktibhyāṃ varṣartustatra śobhate || 20 ||

pañcalohamayaḥ sālo mandāravanamaṇḍitaḥ |
śaradṛturiṣorjā (?) bhyāṃ śaktibhyāṃ tatra rājate || 21 ||

tato rājatasālo vai pārijātavanā'vṛtaḥ |
sahassahasyaśaktibhyāṃ himantastatra rājate || 22 ||

tapanīyamayassālo bālasūryaśataprabhaḥ |
kadambavipinaṃ tatra hālā'modasumeduram || 23 ||

bhṛṅgasaṅgītamadhuraṃ lāsyagītarddhisaṅkulam |
kīramaṇḍalacāṭūktimadhuradhvanisundaram || 24 ||

tatra sā mantriṇī devī saṃsthitā lokamohanī |
saṅgītamāṭṛkā devī mātaṅgītyabhiviśrutā || 25 ||

śaukādyāśyāmalā cānya śārikādyā hasantikā |
vīṇāveṇumukhā tadvacchyāmalā laghupūrvikā || 26 ||

śyāmalā rājapūrvā syāt svayaṃ yā mantriṇī matā |
śrīmātṛkā tatpriyā'nyā (i?) cetyevamaṣṭadiksthitāḥ || 27 ||

mātaṅgakanyākoṭīnāṃ koṭibhiḥ parivāritā |
atha vapraḥ puṣparāgamayaḥ pūrvoktavat sthitaḥ || 28 ||

siddheśaḥ siddhasaṃvītastatra dhāyati tām parām |
padmarāgamayo vaprastadanto'gniśikhā'ruṇaḥ || 29 ||

caraṇastripurābhaktā vasanti yuvatīyutāḥ |
sālo gomedamaṇijastatra bhairavanāyakaiḥ || 30 ||

kālasaṅkarṣiṇī devī vaṭukaughasamāvṛtā |
karālā kālameghābhā karavālakṛtā'yudhā || 31 ||

aṣṭakoṭibhairavāṇāṃ nāthairaṣṭasubhairavaiḥ |
dvātriśatkoṭibaṭukayutaḥ saṃsevitā sadā || 32 ||

mahārājñīpādapadmaṃ dhyāyantī suvirājate |
tato vajramayaḥ sālastatra vajrā nadī sthitā || 33 ||

parikhāvadveṣṭayitvā sudhāsindhupraveśinī |
taṭābhyāṃ gaomedavajramayābhyāṃ pravibhāsinī || 34 ||

tatra vajraśvarī devī vajrabhūṣā samujjvalā |
vajrapradānasantuṣṭā vajripramukhapūjitā || 35 ||

p.549)

vaidūryasālastasyāntastatra nāgā diteḥ sutāḥ |
nirantara. dhyāyamānāḥ parāmbāpādapaṅkajam || 36 ||

athendranīlasālaḥsyāt tatra martyāḥ samāsthitāḥ |
muktāvaprastadantasthā dikpālā dikṣu vai kramāt || 37 ||

atha sālo mārakataḥ svarṇatālavanairyutaḥ |
tāladrusyandadatyantahālāsaurabhasambhūtaḥ || 38 ||

tatra śrīdaṇḍinī devī svapne syādebhirāvṛtā 
aparādhijanān daṇḍeyojayantī susaṃsthitā || 39 ||

madhusevonmiṣatsvāntarā'nandābdhipariplutā |
tato vidrumasālaḥ syādvaprāttatrā'ntare sthitaḥ || 40 ||

anekasiddhamunibhiryogibhiśca samāvṛtaḥ |
navaratnakṛto vaprastato'tiruciraḥ śubhaḥ || 41 ||

tatra viṣṇuḥ pārṣadādisevitā'ṅghrirvirājate |
tato'pyanekaratnaughamayo vapro'tibhāsuraḥ || 42 ||

śivaḥ pramathasaṃsevyastatrā'dhyāste trilocanaḥ |
tato manomayaḥ sālo'ruṇoruṇasamacchaviḥ || 43 ||

p.550)

tanmadhye'mṛtavāpī syāt paritaḥ parikhā'kṛtiḥ |
praphullapadmasaṅkīrṇā tryaṃśemadhyabhuvaḥ sthitā || 44 ||

haṃsaiḥ kāraṇḍavaiḥ kokaiḥ sārasaiḥ suvirājitā |
tanmadhye navaratnāḍhyanaukāyāṃ suvirājite || 45 ||

padmā'sane samāsīnā tārā bhaktaughatāriṇī |
asaṃkhyaratnapotasthaśaktibhiḥ parivāritā || 46 ||

daṇḍinyājñāṃ vinā kañcinna santārayati kvacit |
atha budhimayaḥ sālo viśadaścandravimbavat || 47 ||

ānandavāpikā tatra proktavatsarvaśobhanā |
tatra'mṛteśī taraṇisaṃsthānā śaktibhirvṭā || 48 ||

surānandamayi tasyāmāmodabharitā'ntarā |
ājñāṃvinā'mṛteśānyā nainat pibati kaścana || 49 ||

na santaredvāpikāñcā'mṛtaiśvaryābhirakṣitām | 
ahaṅkāramayastasmādvaraṇaḥ śyāmalā'kṛtiḥ || 50 ||

nīlameghapratīkāśastadantarvāpikā'pi ca |
mimarśā'khyā jñānarasavahaniścalaśitalā || 51 ||

tatra naukāsthitā devī kurukullā maheśvarī 
tadājñāmantarā tatra naiṣat prāpnotī tadrasam || 52 ||

pītvā talleśakamapi samstā'jñānasambhavam |
tyajanti tāpaṃ pa"yanti jagattattvamanāvṛtam || 53 ||

atha sūryātmakaḥ sālaḥ prakāśanikaraḥ sthitaḥ |
tatrā'ntare samāsīnaḥ sūryo jalajaviṣṭare || 54 ||

p.551)

mārtaṇḍabhairavādyaiśca ravibhardvādaśātmabhiḥ |
sametaḥ sūryasadṛśadehairbhaktairabhiṣṭutaḥ | 55 ||

tataḥ śaśāṅkā'varaṇaḥ koṭicandrasamaprabhaḥ |
tatra somaḥ sthito rājā kiraṃstatra sudhāṃ'śubhiḥ || 56 ||

tadantare tu śṛṅgārasālo'tyantavicitritaḥ |
kaustubhā'khyamaṇibrātanirmito rucirā'kṛtiḥ || 57 ||

tanmadhye parighā svacchaśṛṅgārarasasambhḍrtā|
tatra kaustubhasaṅkḷptapote śṛṅgāranāmake || 58 ||

kandarpaḥ saṃsthito ratyā prītyā sammilitaḥ svayam |
anekakoṭikandarapiratiprītigaṇairapi || 59 ||

sevyamāno modate śrītripurābhaktaśekharaḥ |
tatpāre tu mahāpadmavanamāmodameduram || 60 ||

sthalapadmavanaṃ teṣām kāṇḍāḥ patrāṇi kesarāḥ |
karṇikāścāpi cā'tyantadīrghāstatte bravīmyaham || 61 ||

sahastrayojanā'yāmāḥ kāṇḍā gavyūtipīvarāḥ | 
viṃśatiryojanānāntu patrā'yāmaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 62 ||

triṃśadyojanadīrghāṇi patrāṇi kalaśīsuta ! |
yojanānāṃ pañcadaśa kesarāṇāñca dīrghatā || 63 ||

karṇikā yojanadaśavistārāḥ samprakīrtitāḥ |
tatra bhṛṅgāmaṇinibhāḥ śailaśṛṅgamitāḥ sthitāḥ || 64 ||

p.552)

gṛhaṃ śrītripurādevyāstadantaratibhasuram |
cintāmaṇipravekā'tiracitaṃ citracitritam || 65 ||

caturdvārayautaṃ tatra divyā'dhārasusambhṛtam |
vahnimūrtimayā'dhāro vartulastatkalāyutaḥ || 66 ||

yojanānāṃ pañcaśataṃ vistāre'rdhaṃ samunnatau |
tatra sūryātmakaṃ pātraṃ kalādvādaśasamyutam || 67 ||

yojanānāṃ sahasrantu vistāre sārdhamunnate |
tatrā'mṛtā'savaḥ pūrṇaḥ svādyo mañjulasaurabhaḥ || 68 ||

sudhāṃśurūpaḥ saṃyuktaḥ kalābhiḥ parito vidhuḥ |
tatra potasthitaḥ khelaṃstaraṅgeṣvāsavā'mṛte || 69 ||

śrīcakrasthāḥ śaktyo yāstāsāṃ pānāya kalpitam |
mahāpadmā'ṭavīsaṃsthāścā'pi yāḥ śaktyo'malāḥ || 70 ||

mukhyaśaktiprasādantu prāpya modanti santatam |
tatrā'gneyaṃ yaṃ mahākuṇḍaṃ cidagnijvālamālitam || 71 ||

sahasrayojana'yāmaṃ tathā nimnaṃ trimekhalam |
śatayojanamunnamrā vistṛtā mekhalāḥ pṛthak || 72 ||

tatra śrītripurādevyā janakaścinmayā'nalaḥ |
dvisahasrayojanonnatajvālāmālayā'vṛtaḥ || 73 ||

p.553)

gṛhasya paścimadiśi sthitaścakātmako rathaḥ |
nānāmaṇigaṇā'kīrṇaḥ sahasrayojanā'yataḥ || 74 ||

caturguṇonnato bhūminavakenā'tisundaraḥ |
daśendriyahayopeto manaḥ sārathisaṃyutaḥ || 75 ||

āmnāyacakraśca'ṅgāro yogaraśmirmarudhvajaḥ |
nabhao vitānaḥ sumahān sarvalokamayaḥ sthitaḥ || 76 ||

cintāmaṇigṛhe tasminmadhye śrīcakranāyakaḥ |
navā'varaṇadevībhiryutaḥ paramaśobhanaḥ || 77 ||

tanmadhye tripurā devī pañcabrahmātmamañcke |
kāmeśvarā'ṅksaṃsthānā jyotīrūpā virājate || 78 ||

iti śrīmaditihāsottame tripurārahasye māhātmyakhaṇḍe śrītripurāpākhyāne 
śrīpuravarṇanaṃ nāma catuḥpañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 4461 ||

p.554)

śrutvaivamadbhutaṃ vṛttaṃ viśvakarmā'tivismitaḥ |
papracchā'nantaraṃ vākyaṃ sarvalokapitāmaham || 1 ||

pitāmahā'tra sandeho bhagavan bahudhā mama |
hṛdi tat paripṛcchāmi vaktuṃ tanme tvamarhasi || 2 ||

kenedaṃ nirmitaṃ sthānaṃ kadā kena ca hetunā |
na gatistatra cā'nyeṣāṃ guṇajebhya iti śrutam || 3 ||

punastatrā'marā martyā nāgādyāścā'pi varṇitāḥ |
kathametat sthitaṃ deva kṛpāṃ kuru mayīśvara || 4 ||

āpṛṣṭa evaṃ lokeśaḥ prāha taṃ viśvaślpinam |
tvaṣṭaḥ ! śṛṇu samākhyāsye caitadvṛttaṃ sugopitam || 5 ||

tadā vayaṃ sṛṣṭimukhakriyākṛtiparāyaṇāḥ |
sadā samākulasvāntaistoṣitā'smābhirambikā || 7 ||

p.555)

tripurā paramāśaktiḥ kevalā citirūpī | 
niṣkalā'kāśarupeṇa kevalaṃ vaṅmayī śivā || 8 ||

āhā'śarīriṇī vatsāḥ kiṃ vaḥ samabhivāṅchitam |
saṃśrutvaivaṃ vācaṃ natvā'smābhiḥ samīritam || 9 ||

stutvā tāṃ vividhaistaotrairbaddhā'ñjalibhirādarāt |
mahādevi vayañcā'smin jagatkṛtye nirantaram || 10 ||

bhavatyā viniyuktā vai naiṣadvindāmaheratim |
tvatsvarūpasthitiṃ tyaktvā na kiñcidabhirocate || 11 ||

sudhārasā'svadanānāṃ yathā kṣārodasevane |
tatkṛpāṃ kuru deveśi ! na ramāmo'tra karmasu || 12 ||

evamuktā brahmamukhaistripurā prāha naḥ punaḥ |
vidhimukhyāḥ sāmprataṃ no bhavedetat kathañcana || 13 ||

niyatirme'tidurvārā saṅkḷptā prāṅmayaiva sā |
cintituṃ nā'rhathaivaṃ vo dāsye'nyadabhivāñchitam || 14 ||

tato'smābhiḥ prārthitā sā tripurā lokabhāvanī |
yadyevamamba tattvañca guṇamūrtirmaheśvarī || 15 ||

p.556)

rājarājeśvarī bhūtvā'khilā'ṇḍānāṃ praśāsane |
kuru tatra vayaṃ nityaṃ tvanmūrtterabhiṣevaṇāt || 16 ||

prāpsyāmo'tra ratiṃ loke tvadājñāpālanādapi |
ityasmadīritaṃ śrutvā prāha sā tripurā parā || 17 ||

astvevaṃ vo hitārthāya dṛśyamūrtirbhavāmyaham |
nā'ṭrā'ṇḍeṣu sthitiṃ prāpsye kintvaśeṣā'ṇḍavartinām || 18 ||

sādhāraṇaṃ sudhā'mbhodhau ratnadvīpaṃ sṛjāmyaham |
tatra māṃ bhaktibhāvena yajantu jagadīśvarāḥ || 19 ||

tatra kuṇḍe samidhyā'gniṃ cinmayaṃ jvalite śucau |
yathepsitaṃ dhyāyata māṃ strīpumprakṛtitaḥ śubhām || 20 ||

dhyānā'nurūpā vo bhūtvā praśāsiṣye'ṇḍasantatim |
tatrā'khilā'ṇḍasaṃsthānāṃ brahmādīnāṃ nirantaram || 21 ||

diśāmi darśanaṃ strīyaṃ sthūlabhāvasya saṃsthitā |
ityuktvā'smān vihdhimukhānantardhānaṃ jagāma sā || 22 ||

atha tasyā vacanato brahmāṇḍānnirgatā vayam |
pīyūṣā'bdhiṃ samāsādya ratnadvīpaṃ samantataḥ || 23 ||

p.557)

vicinvataścireṇā'tha maṇidvīpo'nusāditaḥ |
navaratnamayaḥ |svīyacchavivytāptadigantaraḥ || 24 ||

tatra gatvā vinirmāya kuṇḍaṃ paramaśobhanam |
proktamānaṃ tatra vahniyojanaṃ syātkathantvati || 25 ||

cintāṃ prāptā vayaṃ tatra nā'gnirasti nacendhanam |
atha devaḥ śivaḥ kuṇḍe svākṣṇo'gnimanukalpayat || 26 ||

anindhanantu taṃ dṛṣṭvā praśamā'bhimukhaṃ śucim | 
viṣṇu svayaṃ svacerto'śairedhāṃsi samkalpayat || 27 ||

atha prajvalite vahnau vidhisvaṃ samakalpayam |
manaḥpaśuṃ buddhimajyamahaṅkāraṃ havistathā || 28 ||

karaṇāni ca pātrāṇi addhotuṃ devatāṃ parām |
abhidhyāyamahaṃ svā'ntarvahnau strīmūrtimīdṛśīm || 29 ||

dhyātvā havirhṛtaṃ yāvattāvat sā parameśvarī |
kuṇḍavahneḥ samudabhūt dhyānarūpā'nusāriṇī || 30 ||

parāśaktiriyaṃ tatra tato'smābhirabhiṣṭutā |
niryayau pāvakāttasmādbahistrailokyasundarī || 31 ||

athā'vadanmāṃ paramā kvā'sanaṃ me vinirdiśa |
ādiṣṭa evaṃ paramamāsanaṃ kalpitaṃ mayā || 32 ||

p.558)

sparśamātrādviśīrṇaṃ tadāsanaṃ bhūya eva tat |
kalpitaṃ vividhaṃ sāraṃ sparśācchīrṇa babhau kṣaṇāt || 33 ||

sarvasāmarthyavihitānyāsanānyapi tāni me |
paraḥśataṃ viśīrṇāni tadā'haṃ lajjito'bhavam || 34 ||

athā'virāsīnnimiṣāt sarvadevaḥ sadāśivaḥ |
tadicchayaiva turyaśca tau tāṃ devīṃ praṇematuḥ || 35 ||

tāvabravīt parā devī sā kalpayatamāsanam |
dadhyau sadāśivaḥ śrutvā vākyaṃ tasyāḥ kṣṇādatha || 36 ||

anekakoṭisaṃkhyātā āyayurguṇamūrtayaḥ |
sadāśivā īśvarāśca tebhyaḥ sarvebhyaḥ eva ca || 37 ||

sāraṃ samākṛṣya pañcamūrtīścakre'tisundarīḥ |
sadāśivāṃ'śena tatra sindūranicayā'ruṇam || 38 ||

phalakaṃ kalpayattasya mūrtyā saṅghaṭitaṃ śubham |
īśarudraharividhiśaktisaṅghacatuṣṭayaiḥ || 39 ||

pādāṃstanmūrtighaṭitānnirmame sa sadāśivaḥ |
aśeṣapṛthivīsārasambhṛtā'staraṇaṃ tataḥ || 40 ||

haṃsatūlasaṃhativat komalaṃ tasya mūrdhani |
āstṛtaṃ meruśikharisamudāyasusādhitam || 41 ||

upadhānaṃ viracayaddevadevaḥ sadāśivaḥ |
praṇamya prāha tāṃ devīṃ siddhamamba! samāruha || 42 ||

p.559)

tanmadhye taṃ guṇeśānāṃ namaskṛtya sadāśivam |
procurdeva naupayikaṃ pīṭhametaddhi mandiram || 43 ||

ākāśasaṃśrayaṃ tasmānmandiraṃ kuru sundaram |
sadāśivo'pi yuktaṃ tanmatvā cakre gṛhaṃ śubham || 44 ||

cintāmaṇigaṇaiḥ kīrṇa nānāratnopabhūṣitam |
yojanānāṃ dve sahasre vistṛtau gṛharājakaḥ || 45 ||

caturdvāraścaturvedamaya āmnāyamaṇḍitaḥ |
nabhomayavitānena mahatā'tivirājitaḥ || 46 ||

matvā gṛhaṃ tadā'kāśena manoharamuttamam |
sasarja tasya parito mahapadmavanaṃ śubham || 47 ||

samproktalakṣaṇaṃ tena mandiraṃ tadvyarājata |
evaṃ tanmandire ratnasiṃhāsanamadhīśvarī || 48 ||

samāruhat sā tripurā bhāsayantī samantataḥ |
ratnasiṃhāsane tasmin saṃsthitā sā maheśvarī || 49 ||

asaṃkhyairvidhiviṣṇvīśaturyamūrtisadāśivaiḥ |
saṃstutā bahudhā prāha sadāśivamadhīśvarī || 50 ||

sadāśiva mamā'khyānamanākhyāyā'dbhutaṃ kuru |
ākhyātā'haṃ yena loke bhavāmi viditā'kṛtiḥ || 51 ||

evaṃ tayā niyukto'tha devadevaḥ sadāśiva |
yāvattasyāḥ samākhyānaṃ cintayāmāsa mānase || 52 ||

p.560)

tāvadbrahmaviṣṇurudramukhaiḥ samprārthito'bhavat |
deva naupayikaṃ hyetadbhāsate na parāmbikā || 53 ||

citirdrṣṭṛmayī nityā sadasadrūpakāraṇam |
dhyānā'nurodhavapuṣā dayayā dṛśyatāṃ gatā || 54 ||

yoṣidrūpadharā devī vinā puruṣasaṃśrayam |
nā'tyartha śobhate lokavigānāditi manmahe || 55 ||

na dvacillokavitatau nārī narasamāśrayam |
ṛte sambhavate vallī taruhīnā yathā bhuvi || 56 ||

tadetasyā jagaddhātryā anurūpaṃ patiṃ śubham |
paraṃ kalpaya yannaḥ syāt sarveṣāmabhivāṇchitam || 57 ||

śrutvaiyaṃ guṇamurtīnāmuktaṃ devaḥ sadāśiva |
cintayitvā ciraṃ vākyaṃ prāha vyavasitā'ntaraḥ || 58 ||

śṛṇvantu guṇajātā me vākyametat samāhitāḥ |
iyaṃ parātparā devī saścinmātrasvarūpī || 59 ||

ekā'sīt sarvapurataḥ svātatryā'tmavidhāyinī | 
na sannā'sadabhinnaṃ vā vibhinnaṃ kimapi hyabhūt || 60 ||

sā svasvabhāvavibhavabhareṇaivā'vabhāsayat |
etajagaścakracitraṃ svātmanyādarśalīlayā || 61 ||

p.561)

naitasyāḥ sadṛśaḥ ko'pi kṛta evā'dhiko bhavet |
sadasyāḥ sarvalokaikamāturbhartā'tra ko bhavet || 62 ||

na hi me'tra bhavecchaktiḥ kartuṃ syāt khalu kiñcat | 
vadatyevaṃ sadā pūrve śiva'smākaṃ prapaśyatām || 63 ||

viditvā'smatvamīhāṃ sā tripurā parameśvarī |
īṣat smitaṃ kṛtavatī koṭipūrṇendubhāsanam || 64 ||

athā'bhavanmahāśabdo brahmāṇḍaughavibhedanaḥ |
vidalan śrotrajālāni badhirīkṛtadiktaṭaḥ || 65 ||

prajajvāla mahātejaḥ sarvadiṅmaṇḍalā'danam |
taḍitkoṭyu dayā'bhāsaṃ sarvacakṣuḥ pramoṣaṇam || 66 ||

tena śabdena mahatā tejasā ca samantataḥ | 
badhirā'ndhīkṛtāḥ kiṃ tadityāścaryaṃ samāgatāḥ || 67 ||

dṛṣṭā'smābhiḥ kṣaṇenaiva tataḥ sā parameśvarī |
samānasundarā'ṅgasya paryaṅkamaṇiśobhinī || 68 ||

mithunībhūtasaṃsthānā pratibimbaṃ śritā yathā |
dṛṣṭvā samānapuruṣavāmā'ṅkasuvirājitām || 69 ||

praharṣamatūlaṃ prāptāḥ sveṣṭasiddherjagadvidhe | 
svā'ṅgasaundaryalālityabharaṇenaiva parasparam || 70 ||

p.562)

kṣipanniva tanmithunaṃ parīrambhasukholvaṇam |
athā'.bravīnnaḥ paramā devā vaḥ siddhamīpsitam || 71 ||

ahaṃ dvidhā hi sañjātā bhavadīpsitahetave |
vadantvāvayornāmani (?) ryāvaḥ khyātiṃ yatastviha || 72 ||

śrutvā sadāśivo devaḥ prekṣā'smānnāma karmaṇi |
vimūḍhānāha natvā tāṃ devyasmatkāmapūraṇāt || 73 ||

kāmeśvarī tvaṃ devaśca bhavet kāmeśvarastathā |
rājarājātmanāṃ nastvamīśanāścā'pi sāmpratam || 74 ||

rājarājeśvarī tvaṃ vai rājarājeśvarastvayam |
tvaṃ vai tripurasundarī caiṣa tripurasundaraḥ || 75 ||

atha dhātumukhāḥ stutvā vayaṃ tāṃ parameśvarīm |
aprārthayanta bhūyastāṃ matvā tatraikalāṃ sthitām || 76 ||

mātastvaṃ parivāreṇa sthātumatrār'hasīśvarī |
dṛṣṭvaikalāṃ tvāṃ na vayaṃ hṛṣyāmo'titarāṃ śive || 77 ||

evaṃ sā prārthitā'smābhiḥ svadehāt kṣaṇamātrataḥ |
prāṃ svasadṛśīṃśaktimasṛjat pratibimbavat || 78 ||

ityāhvayata tāṃ śaktiṃ mahātripurasundarīm |
iti sṛja śakticakraṃ mamā'nuguṇamuttamam || 79 ||

sarvalokepsitakaraṃ parivāraṃ madaṃśataḥ |
omityuktvā ca sā devī śakticakraṃ mahā'dbhutam || 80 ||

p.563)

sasarja śrīmahādevyāḥ śaktyā'varaṇamadbhutam |
tāvanmadhye mahādevī svayaṃ ṣo'śadhā'bhavat || 81 ||

svayaṃ tato'tiriktā'bhūddevīi saptadaśī tadā |
etasminnantare sā'pi mahatripurasundarrī || 82 ||

nav aśaktīḥ sasarjā'ṅgāt sṛṣṭvā sā prāha tāḥ prati |
pṛthaksṛjethāḥ svasvā'nurūpāḥ śaktīrmanoharāḥ || 83 ||

mahāsundaryeti coktāḥ sasṛjustāḥ pṛthak pṛthak |
evaṃ sṛṣṭparīvārā mahāśrītripurā'mbikā || 84 ||

sthānaṃ tāsāṃ jagaścakramayaṃnirmāya satvaram |
tattatsthānasthitābhiḥ sā śaktibhiḥ sevitā'bhavat || 85 ||

iti śrīmaditihāsottame tripurārahasye māhātmyakhaṇḍe lalitāmāhātmye 
brahmaviśvakarmmasamvāde śrīpure kāmeśvarīkāmeśvarayutaṃ 
śakticakrasaṃsthānavarṇanaṃ nāma pañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 4546 ||

p.564)

ṣaṭpañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ
brahmaviśvakarmmasavādeśrīcakradevatānirūpaṇavarṇanam


śurvetthamadbhutaṃ vṛttaṃ viśvakarmā jagadvidhiḥ |
papracchaḥ bhūyaḥ praṇatastacchru tāvatikautukī || 1 ||

bhagavan śaktyo devyā sṛṣṭā yāstripurāmbayā |
nṛpasaṃkyā nāma tāsāṃ rūpaṃ sthānañca me vada || 2 ||

mahātripurasundaryā sṛṣṭā yāstāḥ samīraya |
kiṃrūpāḥ kati saṃkhyākāstatsṛṣṭāścā'pi varṇaya || 3 ||

sthānañcā'pi jagaccakrapratirūpaṃ kimātmakam |
sṛṣṭaṃ śrītripurāśaktyā kutra kāḥ śaktyaḥ sthitā || 4 ||

etanme sarvamākyāhi śrotuṃ me prasamīhitam |
iti pṛṣṭo viśvakṛtā prītaḥ prāha caturmukhaḥ || 5 ||

śṛṇu tvaṣṭarguhyatamaṃ sāvadhānena cetasā |
evamādau visṛṣṭā sā mhātripurasundarī || 6 ||

navaśaktīrasṛjata śaktyaughasya visarjane |
samājñaptāstayā tāstu prāhustripurasundarīm || 7 ||

p.565)

kathaṃ vayaṃ sṛjāmo'mba śaktīste samudīritāḥ |
śrutveti prāha sā bhūyo mahātripurasundarī || 8 ||

ātmayogaṃ samāśritya drutaṃ sṛjatha mā ciram |
ityuktvā'dyā samasṛjaddśaśaktīḥ sulohitāḥ || 9 ||

tāścā'ṇimādisiddhīnāṃ pradā loke'bhavannatha |
pāśā'bjā'ṅkuśapāṇayaḥ śaśikhaṇḍaśikhaṇḍakāḥ || 10 ||

aṇimā laghimā caiva mahimeśitvikā tataḥ |
vaśitvā prākāmyakā ca bhuktīcchāprāptayaḥ kramāt || 11 ||

sarakāmā ca siddhyantā daśa devyaḥ samudgatāḥ |
atha tā navaśakyastu prāhurdevīṃ svamātaram || 12 ||

nāma naḥ pradiśā'mbā'śu tataḥ śaktiḥ sṛjāmahe |
śrutvā'ha tā mahāśaktīrmahātripurasundarī || 13 ||

yogena sarjanādyūyaṃ yoginyastu bhaviṣyatha |
athā'dyā prārthayāmāsa tripureśīṃ praṇamya tu || 14 ||

mātaḥ sthānaṃ kalpayā'śu śaktīnāṃ samavasthiteḥ |
prārthitaivaṃ parāśaktiḥ sasarja sthānamadbhutam || 15 ||

caturasrā vedikaikā yojanadvitayonnatā |
dviśataṃ yojanānāntu vistāreṇa vinirmitā || 16 ||

tasyopari caturdikṣu yojanānāṃ catuṣṭayam |
bahirvihāya tasyāntaścaturddhāraṃ suśobhanam || 17 ||

p.566)

yojanānāntu viṃśatyā vistṛtaṃ sumanoharam |
ṣaṭyojanasudīrghañca tatsambaddha tadantataḥ || 18 ||

yojanadvayamunnamrā caturasrasubhūmikā |
dviyojanā tato'ntastu yojanoccā tathā punaḥ || 19 ||

evaṃ bhūmitrayaṃ tasya cā'ntaḥ ṣoṣaśayojane |
bhūmikā yojanonnamnā ṣoḍaśacchadasannibhā || 20 ||

tadantaryojanonnamrā vasupatrasamākṛtiḥ |
yojanānāntu viṃśatyā bhūmikā'timanoharā || 21 ||

ādityayojanā'yāmā tadantaryojanonnatā |
bhūmikā śakrakoṇābhā tadantaścā'pi tāvatī || 22 ||

bhūmikā daśakoṇābhā tasyā'ntaḥ śivayojanā |
yojanoccā pūrvavattu daśakoṇasamaprabhā || 23 ||

antare daśasaṃkhyātayojanā'yāmataḥ sthitā |
vasukoṇātmikā bhūmiryojanocchrāyaśobhinī || 24 ||

tasyā'ntastryasrabhūmistu rasayojanasammitā |
yojanoścā vadūrdhvasthā tāvaducchrāyaśobhinī || 25 ||

yojanā'yatavistārā bindubhūmiḥ suvartulā |
prakalpya sthānamevaṃ sā vṛttabhūmau svayaṃ sthitā || 26 ||

pañcabrahmāsanā'sīnakāmeśotsaṅgaśobhinī |

p.567)

svāṅgodbhūtāḥ śaktayo yāḥ svasamā nṛpasaṃkhyakāḥ || 27 ||

tāḥ svabhūmeradhastryasrabhūmau saṃveśitāḥ kramāt |
indoḥ kalāmayaḥ pakṣadinā'dhiṣṭhātṛdevatāḥ || 28 ||

nityā'khyāḥ kāraṇenā'sāṃ nāmāni ca vadāmi te |
ādyā kāmeśvarī nityā dvitīyā bhagamālinī || 29 ||

nityaklinnā ca bheruṇḍā tataḥ syādvahnivāsinī |
mahāvidyeśvarī paścācchivadūtī tataḥ param || 30 ||

tvaritā kulasundarī nityā nīlapatākikā |
vijayā sarvamaṅgalā jvālāmālā tataḥ param || 31 ||

citreti nāma kramato vāme pṛṣṭhe ca dakṣiṇe |
pañcakakramayogena tryasrabhūmau niveśitāḥ || 32 ||

svayaṃ saptadaśī nityā ṣoḍaśī yā puroditā |
sā tapovīryasaṃyogāt sāyujyaṃ samupāgatā || 33 ||

s.ṣṭādau tatra yā devī mahātripurasundarī |
tāṃ bindubhūmyadhiṣṭhātrīṃ kṛtvā svāgre niveśayat || 34 ||

yoginyo'pi tayā sṛṣṭā navasaṃkhyāḥ puroditāḥ |
tābhiścā'pi pṛthakkṛtyairnām prāptaṃ yathākramam || 35 ||

p568)

prakaṭā ca tato guptā tathā guptatarā parā |
sampradāyā'kulottīrṇā nirbhagā ca rahasyakā || 36 ||

tathaivā'tirahasyākhyā parā'pararahasyakā | 
niveśitāḥ krameṇaitā devyā cakreṣu tacchṇṇu || 37 ||

caturasre nṛpadale nāgapatre tataḥ param | 
manudigdiṅnāgavahnikoṇeṣūrdhve pṛthak pṛthak || 38 ||

tataḥ sā prakaṭā'khyānā yoginī svavinirmitāḥ | 
daśaśaktīradho bhūmyāṃ krameṇa viniveśayat || 39 ||

punaḥ sṛṣṭā'ṣṭaśaktīstu brāhmyākīrmātṛkā'tmikāḥ |
vargaśaktīrdvitīyasyāṃ bhūmikāyāṃ niveśayat || 40 ||

tataḥ sasarja śaktināṃ daśakaṃ prakaṭā'bhidhā |
tṛtīyabhūnikāyāṃ tāḥ krameṇā'bhiniveśayat || 41 ||

atha guptā'bhidhānā'pi dṛṣtvā nṛpaniveśanam |
asṛjattāvatīḥ śaktīratha guptatarā'bhidhā || 42 ||

sampradāyā'kulottīrṇā nirbhagāca pṛthak pṛthak |
svassaṃsthānasammityā'sṛjacchaktīrvicitritāḥ || 43 ||

śaktīrvibhāvayāmāsa rahasyāḥ saptasaṅkhykāḥ | 
rahasyataranāmnī ca śaktimekāṃ sasarja ha || 44 ||

p.569)

aṇimādyā api pṛthak sasṛjuḥ śatarśo'śataḥ |
śaktīḥ svasvasamākārā'yudhavastravibhūṣaṇāḥ || 45 ||

binducakreśvarī yā sā mahātripurasundarī |
viniyuktā mūladevyā śaktīnāmabhidhā'kṛtau || 46 ||

cakāra tattatkarmāṇanurūpaṃ nāma pṛthak pṛthak |
tṛtīyabhūmikādevyo yāḥ sṛṣṭā daśasaṅkhyakāḥ || 47 ||

mudrātattvavidhānajñāḥ sarvamudrātmamūrtayaḥ |
āharanti mudaṃ devyastasmānmudrāsamā'hvyāḥ || 48 ||

mudmutpādya yā lokān kṣobhayantyatimātrakam |
drāvayatyākaraṣayati vaśayenmādayatyapi || 49 ||

bījayatyaṅkurayati cārayatyapi khe tathā |
trikhaṇḍayati yonyātmabhāvamānayatīśvarī || 50 ||

iti mudrā vīryavatyaḥ khyātāḥ saṅkṣobhiṇīti ca |
vidrāviṇyākarṣaṇī ca tataḥ paścādvaśaṅkarī || 51 ||

unmādinī mahāṅkuśā trikhaṇḍā khecarī tathā |
bījā yonih kramādetā dikṣu koṇeṣvadhordhvayoḥ || 52 ||

bhūmikātritaye'ṣyevaṃ brāhmī māheśvarī tathā |
kaumārī vaiṣṇavī vaiṣṇavī vārāhī māhendrī tataḥ param || 53 ||

cāmuṇḍā ca mahālakṣmīḥ (:) evamādye sthitā imāḥ |
brhmādīnāṃ samānāṅgā'yudhā'bharaṇavāsasaḥ || 54 ||

p.570)

mudrāstu lohitā'bhāsāḥ pāśamudrā'ṅkuśairyutāḥ | 
lohitā'ṅkuśadhāriṇyaḥ padmarāgasubhūṣaṇāḥ || 55 ||

atha guptā'khyayā sṛṣṭā bhakteṣṭākarṣaṇāttathā |
kāmākarṣiṇikā'dyā syād buddhyākarṣiṇikā parā || 56 ||

ahaṅkārā'karṣiṇī ca śabdā'karṣiṇikā tathā |
sparśā'karṣiṇikā tadvadrūpā'karṣiṇikā tataḥ || 57 ||

rasā'karṣiṇikā gandhā'karṣiṇī cittapūrvikā |
ākarṣiṇī dairyapūrvā karṣiṇī smṛtipūrvikā || 58 ||

nāmapūrvā'karṣiṇī ca bījā'karṣiṇikā parā |
ātmā'karṣiṇikā tadvadamṛtā'karṣiṇī tataḥ || 59 ||

śarīrā'karṣiṇītyevaṃ ṣoḍaśacchadaśaktyaḥ |
bhāvitā guptayoginyā'grapatrādvāmataḥ sthitāḥ || 60 ||

śūbhrāḥśubhrāṃ'śukakalpāḥ pūrṇatāruṇyaśobhitāḥ |
pāśadānasudhākumbhā'ṅkuśā'yudhapariṣkṛtāḥ || 61 ||

anaṅgakusumā'naṅgamekhalā'naṅgapūrvikā |
madanā'naṅgamadanā'turā'naṅgā'dyarekhikā || 62 ||

anṅgaveśinyanaṅgā'ṅkuśā'naṅgā'dyamālinī |
etā guptatarā'khyotthā nāgacchadaniveśanāḥ || 63 ||

p.571)

raktavarṇā'śukakalpā dikkoṇakramataḥ sthitāḥ |
pāśaṃ nīlotpalaṃ pātramaṅkuśaṃ pāṇiṣusthitam || 64 ||

saṃkṣobhiṇī vidrāviṇī cā'karṣiṇī cā'lhādinī |
sammohinī stambhinī ca jṛmbhiṇī ca vaśaṅkarī || 65 ||

rañjinī conmādinī ca tataścaivār'thasādhinī |
sampattipūraṇī mantramayī dvandvakṣayaṅkarī || 66 ||

sampradāyodbhāvitatā manukoṇeṣu saṃsthitāḥ |
agrādvāmakrameṇaiva raktābhāṃ'śukabhūṣaṇāḥ || 67 ||

pāśā'mṛtaghaṭā'darśā'ṅkuśaśaobhicaturbhujāḥ |
siddhīpradā sampatpradā tataḥ paścātpriyaṅkarī || 68 ||

maṅgalā'jñākāriṇī ca tataḥ kāmapradā smṛtā |
duḥkhādvimocanī tdvanmṛtyupraśamanī tataḥ || 69 ||

vignānnivāriṇī cā'ṅgasundarī tadanantaram |
saubhāgyadāyinī paṅktikoṇe prāgvat samāsthitāḥ || 70 ||

pāśaṃ bhūṣaṇapeṭīñcā'ṅkuśaṃ pāṇicatuṣṭayaiḥ |
dadhānā viśadā'kārāṃ'śukabhūṣaṇabhūṣitāḥ || 71 ||

kulottīrṇā samuditāḥ śaktyo yauvanā'nvitāḥ |
jñānaśaktiraiśvaryapradā tatojñānamayī smṛtā || 72 ||

tato vyādhivināśinī cā'dhārādyāsvarupikā |
tataḥ pāpaharā'nandamayī rakṣāsvarūpī || 73 ||

p.572)

īpsitādyāpradā ceti daśāre pūrvavat sthitāḥ |
pāśadānajñānaṭaṅkakarā raktāṅgabhūṣaṇāḥ || 74 ||

nigarbhākhyā bhāvitāstu taruṇyaḥ śaktyo'malāḥ |
vaśinī modinī tadvadvimalā cā'ruṇā tataḥ || 75 ||

jayinī sarveśvarī ca kaulinī ceti śaktyaḥ |
rahasyayoginījātāḥ padmarāgasamaprabhāḥ || 76 ||

cāpadānapustakeṣukarā lohitabhūṣaṇāḥ |
nāgakoṇeṣu pūrvoktakrameṇa suvirājitāḥ || 77 ||

śaktirekā cā'tirahasyā'khyotthodakṣakoṇake |
evaṃ tāḥ śaktayaścakre sṛṣṭāḥ prakṛtiśaktibhiḥ || 78 ||

iti śrīmaditihāsottame tripurārahasye māhātmyakhaṇḍe śrīlalitāmāhatmye 
śrīcakradevatānirūpaṇaṃ nāma ṣaṭpañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 4624 ||

p.573)

saptañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ 
śrīcakradevatānirūpaṇavarṇanam 

śrutvātvaṣṭeritaṃ dhātuḥ punaḥ papraccha sādaram |
pitāmaha śrutañcaitacchrīcakraṃ śaktimaṇḍalam || 1 ||

śaktīnāṃ nāma sampattihetuṃ me pṛcchate vada |
tathā'ṣṭā're trikoṇe ca kutaḥ sthānaṃ viśaktikam || 2 ||

ṣoḍaśī sa mahānityā kena sāyujyamāgamat |
evaṃ tvaṣṭā'nuyukto'tha prītaḥ prāha caturmukhaḥ || 3 ||

viśvakartaḥ śṛṇu mahāguhyametannigūhitam |
mahāśrītripureśānyā sṛṣṭāstri (ni?) tyāstu ṣoḍaśa || 4 ||

mahātripurasundaryā sṛṣṭāstvanyāstu śaktayaḥ |
(?) nāma tāsāṃ pṛthaksvīyakriyayaiva nirupitam || 5 ||

yā sā śaktirmahādevī tripurā'khyā mahattarā |
tasyāḥ sadā vilasitaṃ jagadetaścarā'caram || 6 ||

brhmādimūrtiṣvṃśena saṃsthitā sā maheśvarī |
jagaścakrakriyākāṇḍaṃ nirvāhayati cinmayī || 7 ||

p.574)

sā'khaṇḍaikarasāpyambā citimātraśarīriṇī | 
anekadhā'pi bhavati svasvātantryā'vabhāsinī || 8 ||

yathā sahasraraśmīnāṃ piṇḍo haṃso nabhomaṇiḥ |
eko'pyenakadhāpyāste yathā vā vārnidhiḥ sthitaḥ || 9 ||

taraṅgormiphenamaya eko'nekātmanā tathā |
darpaṇaḥ pratibimbānāṃ sahasreṇa yathā sthitaḥ | 10 ||

tathaiṣā paramā cā'pi bhedā'bhedamayī sthitā |
śaktryo'śātmikāstasyā aṇimādyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 11 ||

tasyāḥ svātantryavaśataḥ sthūlā'kāraparigrahaḥ |
yā śaktiryatkriyāheturnāma tasyāstathāsthitam || 12 ||

evaṃ sṛṣṭāḥ śaktyastā mahāmāyāvimohitāḥ |
spardhāñcakruḥ saṃsthitiṣu sthānanācoccabhāvataḥ || 13 ||

dṛṣṭvā tacchakticakreṣu kaśmalaṃ smupasthitam |
mahādvyājñayā prāha mahātripurasundarī || 14 ||

śṛṇudhvaṃ śaktyaḥ sarvā mama vākyaṃ hi sāmpratam |
spardhākrodhātmanāmatra sthānaṃ devīsamīpataḥ || 15 ||

durlabhaṃ tattapoyogāt sthānaṃ vindatha nā'nyathā |
ityuktvā'dau svayañcakre tapaḥ sumahadadbhutam || 16 ||

p.575)

dṛṣṭvā tapasyabhiratāṃ mahātripurasundarīm |
anyāḥ sarvā api tapaścakrurniyatamānasāḥ || 17 ||

evaṃ śaktigaṇe tatra tapasyabhirate sati |
agāt kālo dīrghatamastadā tuṣṭā'vadat parā || 18 ||

vatsāstuṣṭā'smi tapasā sthānaṃ yogyaṃ diśāmivaḥ | 
ityuktvā sthānamadiśanmahātripurasundarī || 19 ||

ya tasyai sarvacakrāṇāmīśvarītvaṃ dadau parā |
atha nityā'tmaśaktīnāṃ tryasrabhūmau niveśanam || 20 ||

pratipārśva pañcakasya cā'tha nityā tu ṣaḍaśī |
pradiṣṭasammukhasthānā'pyamanyata na tadbahu || 21 ||

punarātiṣṭhadatyantaṃ tapaḥ paramaduṣkaram |
evaṃ kāmeśvarī tadvadbhagamālinikā'pi ca || 22 ||

na tatsthānamalaṃ matvā tapaścakraturuttamam |
evaṃ tapasyabhirate śaktimaṇḍalake śivā || 23 ||

ekākinīṃ svāmapaśyanna tat samyagamanyata |
tāvadvijñāyā'bhimataṃ mahātripurasundarī || 24 ||

tripurā'ṅgāyudhebhyaśca daśaśaktīrvisarjayat |
tā visṛṣṭāḥ sthite sthānaṃ mahātripurasundarīm || 25 ||

papracchuḥ prāha tā devī tapasā sthānamīkṣtha |
ityuktvā tapa evaoccaiścakruḥ prāk śaktibhiḥ saha || 26 ||

p.576)

punardevī svaparito matvaikāmavalokayat |
avalokanato jātāḥ śaktyo vedasaṅkhyakāḥ || 27 ||

kāñcanā'bhāḥ padmadharāḥ śubhravastravibhūṣaṇāḥ |
dṛṣṭvā śaktigaṇaṃ sarvaṃ tapasyabhirataṃ sadā || 28 ||

mano dadhustapasyeva tvaṣṭastā api saṃyayuḥ |
bhūyo dadarśa parito jātaṃ śakticatuṣṭayam || 29 ||

pāśā'bhayavarasṛṇidharā raktāṅgabhūṣaṇāḥ |
triṇetrāśca tapasyeva ratā dṛṣṭvā ca pūrvajāḥ || 30 ||

punaḥ sṛṣṭāstathā'nyāśca pāśāpustakamālikāḥ |
dadhānā aṅkuśañcāpi raktavarṇavibhūṣaṇāḥ || 31 ||

punaḥ sṛṣṭāśśktyaśca tāvatyaḥ sphaṭikaprabhāḥ |
pāśābhayavarasṛṇidharaśca tapa āsthitāḥ || 32 ||

sṛṣṭāḥ punaśca tāvatyoraktā'kāravibhūṣaṇāḥ |
tapasyeva ratāstāśca dṛṣṭvā śaktīḥ puraḥ sthitāḥ || 33 ||

atha dṛṣṭvā tapaḥ prauḍhaṃ prāha tāṃ tripureśvarī | 
nityāṃ ṣoḍaśikāṃ vatse varaṃ brūhyabhivāñchitam || 34 ||

sāyujyameva sā vabre sarvāṃ matvā'lpikāṃ sthitim |
prāptā ca parayā tatra sāyujyaṃ ṣoḍaśī parā || 35 ||

p.577)

yā jātāḥ svāṅgato devyāścatasraḥ pañcadhā ca tāḥ |
svayantu pañcamī bhūtvā tāsāṃ tuṣṭā samāsthitā || 36 ||

pañca pañcakatāṃ tena prāpustā nāmapañcakam |
ratneśvarītvamādyānāṃ kāmadhuktvaṃ tataḥ param || 37 ||

tathā kalplateśitvaṃ tataḥ kośeśvarīpadam |
lakṣmīśvarītvamantyānāṃ śaktivṛndā'sane sthitim || 38 ||

pratyekañca dadau śaktisahasreśvaratāṃ śivā |
ratnāḥ kāmadaghāḥ kalpalatākośā'bhidhāyutāḥ || 39 ||

lakṣmīsamākyāstāḥ śaktīstvaṣṭarjānīhi tāḥ pṛthak |
pañcaviṃśatisāhasrasaṃkhyāstāstu pṛthakpṛthak || 40 ||

sevamānāḥ svasvanāthaṃ pratyekantusahasrakam |
pañcasāhasra sasaṃkhyākā mūladevyāstu śaktyaḥ || 41 ||

sidhālakṣmīstatra cā'dyā rājamātaṅginī parā |
tṛtīyā bhuvaneśānī turyā vārāhikā matā || 42 ||

ratneśvarya emāḥ proktāstataḥ kāmadughāḥ śṛṇu |
sudhāpīṭheśarī cā'dyā sudhāsūśca tataḥ parā || 43 ||

p.578)

tṛtīyā cā'mṛteśānī turyā cā'nnaprapūraṇī |
atha kalpalatāstatra pañcakāmā tataḥ parā || 44 ||

pārijāteśvarī cā'pi kumārī ca tataḥ parā |
pañcabāṇeśvarī cā'tha paraṃjyotistataḥ parā || 45 ||

parāniṣkalaśāmbhavī cā'japā mārṭkā parā |
iti kośeśvarīḥ praoktāḥ śṛṇu lakṣmīśvarīstataḥ || 46 ||

lakṣmīścā'tha mahālakṣmīstriśaktyādyā tataḥ parā |
sāmrājyalakṣmīśca ramā ceti pañcakadevatāḥ || 47 ||

evaṃ pañcakadevīnāṃ tapasā paritoṣitā |
dadau samīpasaṃsthānaṃ binducakropari kramāt || 48 ||

āgneyādiṣu koṇeṣu tapasastu prakarṣataḥ |
mātaṅginyāśca vārāhyā dadāvatulasampadam || 49 ||

mantriṇītvaṃ sarvalokakāryamantaṇahetutām |
vārāhyā daṇḍinītvañcaca duṣṭadaṇḍanehatutām || 50 ||

tataḥ kālāntare hemaharinmaṇisuvaprayoḥ |
madhyaṃ sthānaṃ dadau devī prasannā parisevanāt || 51 ||

evaṃ tapoviśeṣeṇa tuṣṭā sthānaṃ punardadau |
kāmeśvarī ca vajreśī bhagamālinikā ca yā || 52 ||

p.579)

tābhyaḥ samayadevītvaṃ dattvā svā'gre ca pṛṣṭhataḥ |
vāme ca dakṣiṇe pārśve sthānaṃ devyabhikalpayat || 53 ||

udbhāvitā tu yā devī rahasyataranāma yā |
sā prāpa tapasotkarṣādvajrasālā'ntare bhūvi || 54 ||

ādhipatyaṃ vajranadītīre mandirasaṃsthitim |
vajratneśvarītvañca tena vajreśvarī matā || 55 ||

punastrikoṇakoṇeṣu sthānaṃ kāmeśvarīmukhāḥ |
prāpustapasa ādhikyāttathā kāmeśvarī punaḥ || 56 ||

aṣṭā'reṣu dvitīyañca sthānaṃ prātā tapobalāt |
tathā'nyā api śaktistu mahātripurasundarī || 57 ||

devyā tayā ca sthāneṣu krameṇa viniveśyat |
evaṃ navasu cakreṣu vibhatya vinive ya ca || 58 ||

svayaṃ tattadadhiṣṭhātrī navadhā samajāyata |
tripurā tripureśī ca tathā tripurasundarī || 59 ||

tatastripuravāsinī tripurā śrīstataḥ parā |
tripuramālinī tripurā siddhā tripurā'mbikā || 60 ||

navamī ca svayaṃ tantra mahātripurasundarī | 
tattadāvaraṇā'dyā'gre sthitā tatsadṛśā'kṛtiḥ || 61 ||

mūladevyā'bhimukhyena saṃsthitāḥ sarvaśaktyaḥ |
tathā brahmādibhirdṛṣṭvā sarvaṃ śaktigaṇā'vṛtam || 62 ||

matvā na tat samyagiti kāmeśaḥ prārthito'bhavat |
kāmeśa bhagavan deva tvamapi sṛja svāṃ'śataḥ || 63 ||

p.580)

parivāraṃ śivākāraṃ naitadaupayikaṃ yataḥ |
manyāmahe śaktimahe śaktigaṇaṃ kevalaṃ viṣamantviti || 64 ||

atha kāmeśvaro devaḥ svāṃ'śācchaktyaṃśato'pi ca |
ubhayāṃ'śāttadanyasmādasṛjadvedasaṅkhyakān || 65 ||

mitrīśaṣaṣṭhīśauḍḍīśacaryānāthā'bhidhāstataḥ | 
pustakā'bhītiaraciddadhānān sphaṭikaprabhān || 66 ||

śvetavastrā'kalpayutāṃstriṇetrān raktaśaktikān |
padmā'sanasamāsīnān prasannavadanekṣaṇān || 67 ||

tān gurun śaktimantrāṇāmakarocchāntavigrahān |
pṛthagvidyāgurun yūyaṃ sṛjadhvamiti cā'bravīt || 68 ||

athā'dyāstatra divyākhyān siddhākhyānaparastathā |
tṛtīyo mānavā'khyāṃstu gaṇānasṛjata kramāt || 69 ||

munivedanāgasaṃkhyān sarvavidyāgurunatha |
nāthānnavā'sṛjatturyaḥ kālamūrtīnmahāprabhān || 70 ||

p.581)

gurumaṇḍalamevantu sṛṣṭamugrataraṃ tapaḥ |
taptvā prāpa sthānamapi tvaritā pṛṣṭhabhāgake || 71 ||

tatra tripaṅktirupeṇa mandirāṇi krameṇa tu |
divyasiddhamānavaughaguruṇāṃ saṃsthitāni hi || 72 ||

tatra sthānaṃ prāpnuvanti tripurāṃ parameśvarīm |
pūrṇopāsanayogenopāsya śāstroktamārgataḥ || 73 ||

gurubhirbodhitāḥ paścāt prayānti paramaṃ padam |
iti te pṛṣṭamākhyātaṃ rahasyaṃ paramaṃ mahat || 74 ||

iti śrīmaditihāsottame tripurārahasye māhātmyakhaṇḍe lalilāmāhātmye 
śrīcakre divyaughādidevatānirūpaṇavarṇanaṃ nāma 
saptapañcāśattmo'dhyāḥ || 4689 ||

p.582)

atha bhūyaścaturvaktraṃ tvaṣṭā papraccha sādaram |
jagadvidhe! tvāṃ pṛcchāmi guravastvaugharupiṇaḥ || 1 ||

kutaḥ samānasaṅkhyākā nā'bhavaṃstanmameraya | 
nāma teṣāñca sarveṣāmathā'gneḥ kuṇḍamīritam || 2 ||

tatrā'gniścinmayaḥ kena jātastadapi varṇaya |
tatra kuṇḍe bhavadbhiśca strīrūpā kena hetunā || 3 ||

vicintitā parāśaktirvadaitat kāraṇaṃ vibho |
na tṛpyāmyadbhutakathāṃ saṃśṛṇvannapi bhūriśaḥ || 4 ||

ko nu tṛpyetparāśakteḥ svātmamūrteḥ kathā'mṛtam |
pibannapi ciraṃ daivahataṃ sthāṇumṛte kvacit || 5 ||

tanme prabruhi bhagavan śrotavyaṃ yadi me bhavet |
śrutvā viśvakrtoktaṃ tat prasannaḥ prapitāmahaḥ || 6 ||

samāhitaḥ śṛṇu kathāṃ tvaṣṭaścitrāṃ purātanīm |
pārvatīṃ sarvato guptāṃ prasannaḥ prabruve tava || 7 ||

p.583)

mitrīśādyāstu ye sṛṣṭā rājarājeśvareṇa te |
vāgvidyāguravaḥ sarve svacchasaṃvittirupiṇaḥ || 8 ||

paśyantī madhyamā ceti vaikharī ca parā tathā |
caturvidhā śabdamayī parāśaktirvijṛmbhate || 9 ||

tat kramaṃ te pravakṣyāmi sarvatraiva sugopitam |
tripurākhyā hi yā śaktiḥ svacchasaṃvedanāmayī || 10 ||

na tatra vāgindriyāṇi mano vā'pyasti sarvathā |
sarvāstisārabhūtā'tmarūpī parameśvarī || 11 ||

na dharmo dharmavān vā'pi sarvadharmavivarjanāt |
sarvā'śrayacitiḥ svacchā kevalā vāgagocarā || 12 ||

tasya caitanyamātmeti na tato vidyate'dhikam |
tadeva sarvasvātantryamato māyākhyamīritam || 13 ||

atarkyā'paryanuyojyamāhātmyamakhilā'śrayam |
svayaṃ svātmānamamalaṃ vikalpayati bhūriśaḥ || 14 ||

līlayaiva sarvajagajjālameṣa samīrataḥ |
vikalpa eva śabdātmā sthūlamadhyavibhedataḥ || 15 ||

sūkṣmakāraṇabhedābhyāṃ caturvidhamiti sthitam |
tatra sthūlantu yadrūpaṃ vaikharīti prakīrtitam || 16 ||

p.584)

tatra dhvanyātmako'vyakto varṇātmā vyakta īritaḥ |
tallaukika'laukikatvādvidhā sarvatra saṃsthitaḥ || 17 ||

tatrā'vyaktaḥ saptavidho nādabrahmāṅkurātmakaḥ |
ṣaḍjarṣabhau ca gāndhāramadhyapañcamadhaivatāḥ || 18 ||

niṣāda iti sptaite tantrīkaṇṭhādijāḥ svarāḥ |
rāgā'bhdibhūtā'gnidasranāgasaṃkhyāvibhedinaḥ || 19 ||

evaṃ tu bhūyo bhedena na saṅkhyeyā bhavanti te |
tadetaddevasaṃsthānaṃ guravaḥ saptasaṅkhyakā || 20 ||

divyaughāstena samproktā mitrīśena prabhāvitāḥ | 
vyaktastvalaukiko vidyāmayaḥ kūṭacatuṣṭam || 21 ||

tadāśrayāḥ siddhagaṇāstenaite vedasaṅkhyakāḥ |
siddhaughaguravaḥ proktāḥ ṣaṣṭhīśapratibhāvitāḥ || 22 ||

laukikomātṛkā'tmā syādaṣṭakuṭeśvarīmayaḥ | 
tatsaṃśrayā mānavāḥ syuratastā vasusaṃkhyakāḥ || 23 ||

ekonaviṃśatimitā evamoghatrapasthitāḥ |
auḍḍīśaprabhavā ete vasusaṃkhyāstu mānavāḥ || 24 ||

p.585)

śabdaḥ kālamayaḥ sarvo mātrātrayaparā'vadhiḥ |
dhvanirāśiḥ samāyukto varaṇarāśyaṣṭakena ca || 25 ||

tena syurnavanāthā vai caryānāthasamudbhavāḥ | 
kālasyā'vachedakāste turīyapadasaṃśrayāḥ || 26 ||

prakāśo'tha vimarśakhyo'thā'nando jñānasaṃjñakaḥ |
satyaḥ pūrṇaḥ svabhāvā'khyaḥ pratibhaḥ subhagā'bhidhaḥ || 27 ||

iti me navanāthāstu oghānāmadhināyakāḥ |
oghaścaturvidho vidyāvibhedādyugabhedataḥ || 28 ||

miśrītatvācchaktimiśrā guravaḥ saṃvyavasthitāḥ |
oghastu pañcamo'pi syādguruvidyāpravartakaḥ || 29 ||

dvādaśaikādaśarasamitāste cakravāhyagāḥ |
vayaṃ divyabhāvāḥ siddhāḥ kumārādyāśca mānavāḥ || 30 ||

nṛsiṃhādyā iti proktā guravastvoghasambhavāḥ |
athā'gneścinmayatvaṃ te varṇayāmi kramācchṛṇu || 31 ||

guṇamūrtau tṛtīyo yo mahādevo maheśvaraḥ |
tamaḥ kriyaḥ sattvamūrtirvikasaścinmayā'ntaraḥ || 32 ||

dehināṃ dehamadhyasthaṃ mārgatrayamanāmayam |
dakṣavāmamadhyasaṃsthaṃ paiṅgaleḍāsuṣumṇakam || 33 ||

p.586)

icchākriyājñānamayaṃ tatra bhinnaṃ dvayaṃ sthitam |
baddhaṃ madhyaṃ jñānamārgaṃ vidvāṃsaḥ rāhurāryakāḥ || 34 ||

citspandarūpī ruddhāpārśvābhyāmūrdhvanirgatā |
kriyāmicchāṃ cetayati martyāstena hi cetanāḥ || 35 ||

kryecchāmātrasaṃyuktā jñānamārgasya rodhataḥ |
āśrayībhūtacinmātrasyordhvavāhaśca cetanāḥ || 36 ||

tiraścāmūrdhvamārgāṇāṃ saṃrodhāttiryagā sthiteḥ |
na cetayanti te bhūtaṃ bhavyañca kvacidātmani || 37 ||

tenā'cetanatulyāste tiryañcaḥ sarva eva hi |
evaṃ sthite mahādevo yastrinetrastato'dhikaḥ || 38 ||

asmākamantaḥsambhinnā madhyanāḍī cidāspadā |
tenā'ntarasmadvijñānaṃ pūrṇaṃ samupalakṣyate || 39 ||

mahādevasyā'ntare tu jñānodrekāttadūrdhvataḥ | 
phāladeśe vinirbhinna madhyanāḍī cidātmikā || 40 ||

antastamodrekabhāvāścidevā'gnitayā sthitā |
tasmāttannetrasambhūtaścidagniriti viśrutaḥ || 41 ||

atha tatte'bhidhāsyāmi strirūpā cintitā yataḥ |
śṛṇu loke viśvakarman yatsukhaṃ taccidātmakam || 42 ||

p.587)

ataḥ sukhe paśunāñca prītiḥ sarvātmanā sthitā |
sukhāvahaṃ sundarañcaloke pratyakṣabhāvanāt || 43 ||

yos.idrūpaṃ sundarñca tasmālloke janaiḥ sadā |
prekṣyate yoṣitāṃ rūpaṃ sukhashādanabhāvataḥ || 44 |

anyaśca te'bhidhāsyāmi sā yā cidrūpiṇi parā |
svavaibhavātmakaṃ viśvaṃ svātantryeṇa prakāśitam || 45 ||

niyatyā svā'tmanaḥ śaktyā niyataṃ sarvameva hi | 
abhimāno vṛthā mohādahaṃ kartetisaṃsthitaḥ || 46 ||

yadi kartā svayaṃ kuto'nyat prasamīkṣyate |
tathā'pyasammataprāptirbhavedvā kathamīraya || 47 ||

tasmāt sṛṭyādikaṃ kiñcit sarva tasyā vijṛmbhitam |
tasmādyoṣinmayadhyānaṃ tayaiva pravibhāvitam || 48 ||

sarvaṃ viśvasya vaicitryaṃ tatsvātantryanibandhanam |
evaṃ  sthitā mahārājñī cintāmaṇigṛhe tadā || 49 ||

mahāpadbhavanā'ntaḥsthaṃ vīkṣya tadgṛharājakam |
matvā tadapi no samyaṅmahādevaḥ sadāśivḥ || 50 ||

śrīdevyājñāṃ samādāya nirmame proktvatpuram |
brahmāṇḍeṣu sthitā devā daityā martyādayo'pi ye || 51 ||

p.588)

upāsya tripureśānīṃ sālokyāṃ muktimāyayuḥ |
te tatra tattatsthāneṣu nivasanti pṛthak pṛthak || 52 ||

indrabrahmādayo'pyevaṃ novasanti ciraṃ pure |
tattadvaprāntarabhuvi svāmyaṃ prāpya sunirmalam || 53 ||

tattatkālapramāṇasya niyutaṃ propya vai tataḥ |
viśanti paramaṃ bhāvamavāṅmanasagocaram || 54 ||

tadantare tu samprāptā vidhīndrendumukhādayaḥ |
pūrvaṃ saṃsthitasāyujyaṃ labdhvā tiṣṭhanti sarvataḥ || 55 ||

sāmprataṃ yo vidhistatra vidyate tena saṃyutāḥ |
brahmāṇḍāntarasamprāptāḥ ṣaṣṭisāhasrasaṃkhyakāḥ || 56 ||

p.589)

ṣaṭśatañca triṣaṣṭiśca sāyauyaṃ brahmaṇā gatāḥ |
iti te sarvamākhyātaṃ mānaṃ śrīpurasaṃsthitam || 57 ||

atra merau proktarītyā puraṃ racaya sundaram |
yatra śrīpurasāmrājñī tripurāmbā bhaviṣyati || 58 ||

vayaṃ yatra sthitāṃ nityaṃ paśyāmo netrasundarīm |
iti dhātṛvacaḥ śrutvā viśvakarmā'tiviṣmitaḥ || 59 ||

nirmātuṃ śrīpuraṃ devyā meruśṛṅge mano dadhe |
vibhāvayan vidhiproktaṃ harṣanirbharitā'taraḥ || 60 ||

iti śrīmaditihāsottame tripurārahasye māhātmyakhaṇḍe śrīlalitāmāhātmye 
śrīpuravarṇanapūrvakaṃ sāyutyapadavīparyantaṃsiddhiprāptivarṇanaṃ 
nāmā'ṣṭapañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 4749 ||

p.590)

ekonaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ 
hayagrīvāgastyasamvāde nāradena bhaṇḍasamudbodhanavarṇanam 

evaṃ śrutvā hayagrīvavākyamatyantasundaram |
kumbhodbhavaḥ prāha bhūyastatkathāśravaṇotsukaḥ || 1 ||

bhagavannaśvavadana bhaṇḍadaityaḥ samāgataḥ |
tapovighnaṃ nirjarāṇāṃ cikīrṣan vahninā'vṛtān || 2 ||

devānniśāmya vimukhaḥ prayātaḥ śūnyapattanam |
tataḥ kimakarottatra tanme vada suvistaram || 3 ||

saṃśṛṇvan laliteśānyāḥ kathāṃ kathayatastava |
punaḥ śrotuṃ samīhā me vardhate'titarāṃ nanu || 4 ||

evaṃ pṛṣṭaḥ kṛmbhajena muniḥ prāha hayā'nanaḥ |
śṛṇu kumbhodbhava kathāṃ vicitrāṃ paramā'dbhutām || 5 ||

apayāte bhaṇḍadaitye deverṣiratha nāradaḥ |
śūnyakaṃ pratyājagāma śaradabhrmivā'mbarāt || 6 ||

tamāyantaṃ devamuniṃ bhaṇḍadaityo vihāyasi |
dadarśa vṛṣṭameghābhaṃ vādayan vallakīṃ śubhām || 7 ||

p.591)

samudgamya samānīya muniṃ daityaḥ prapūjayat |
sampūjya vidhinā prāha kṛtāñjaliridaṃ ca || 8 ||

devarṣe svāgataṃ nūnaṃ cirātte'haṃ  samīpsitaḥ |
kṛpayā paśya śiṣyaṃ te durlabhaṃ te kṛpekṣaṇam || 9 ||

brūhi dvaścinnirjarāṇāṃ prakṣīṇāṇāṃ sthitiṃ sthirām |
prāyo viprā bhīravo hi gururme bhayamohitaḥ || 10 ||

madbāhuvīryamavidaṃ devā'surabhayāvaham | 
devāḥ śaktimupāsīnāḥ prasannā yadi sā bhavet || 11 ||

parājayaste dhruvaṃ syāttasmādvighnaṃ samācara |
ityukto'haṃ daityagaṇaistatrā'yāto jighāṃsayā || 12 ||

vahnijvālā'valīḍhāṃstān dṛṣṭvā devān savāsavān |
hṛṣṭaḥ pratyāgataḥ sthānaṃ tatra pṛcchāmi prastutam || 13 ||

kathaṃ dāvāgnīnā grastā devā bhasmatvamāyayuḥ |
kaścittatra vimukto vā tanme śaṃsamunīśvara || 14 ||

pṛṣṭa evaṃ devamuniḥ prahasya prāha daityapam |
daityeśvarebhyo'sūyā me na kartavyā kadācana || 15 ||

nā'nṛtaṃ kutracidbrūmo vayaṃ satyaikasaṃśrayāḥ |
prabhāṣase kathaṃ mugdha iva tvaṃ paṇḍito'pi san || 16 ||

p.592)

viparīto nanu vidhiryasmātte buddhirīdṛśī |
śokasthāne harṣayuto yatastvaṃ vīkṣito mayā || 17 ||

susamṛddhāḥ śatravaste mahādevī samāśrayāt |
āvirbhūtā tvadvadhāya toṣitā śakramukhyakaiḥ || 18 ||

cidagnikuṇḍānniryātā lalitā parameśvarī |
sarvalokeśvarī divyarūpā'dabhutamahābalā || 19 ||

sarvā'yudhasamupetāśaktiśaṅghaiḥ parīvṛtā |
sameṣyati tvāṃ nihantuṃ kiṃ tuṣyasi hi mūḍhavat || 20 ||

śrutvā nāradasamproktaṃ prahasya prāha daityarāṭ |
nūnaṃ mayā puraivoktaṃ viprāḥ prakṛtibhīravaḥ || 21 ||

mune jānāsi no maṃ tvaṃ sākṣānmṛtyorbhayaṅkaram |
viṣṇuḥ sureṣvatibalī sa yuddheme parājitaḥ || 22 ||

pañcottaraśatāṇḍānāmadhipo'haṃ parākramī |
devāsurādisraṣṭā'haṃ lokaśastrā'strasantateḥ || 23 ||

mama vaśe sthitā brahmaviṣṇumukhyaparamparāḥ |
kathaṃ bhīto'si strīgetornisargādabalāḥ striyaḥ || 24 ||

tadantare bhaṇḍaḍaityapatnyaḥ sammohinīmukhāḥ |
devarṣimānayamāsuravarodhe sakhīgaṇaiḥ || 25 ||

p.593)

āgataṃ muniśārdūlaṃ sampūjyā'sanamukhyakaiḥ |
pādyā'rghyaiḥ subalidravyaiḥ praṇamya prāhurādarāt || 26 ||

devarṣe tvtprasādena dhanyāstvaddarśanena ca |
vayamasmānnirīkṣasva dṛṣṭyā karuṇayā bhṛśam || 27 ||

vayantu gurūpatnībhyaḥ śuśrūmo'tyantasādhvasam |
utpannā lalitā devī devaiḥ samabhipūjitā || 28 ||

bharturvadhāya no devasaukhyāya parameśvarī |
tadbodhyā'surapatiṃ yathā nāśaṃ na caiṣyati || 29 ||

śaraṇaṃ tvāṃ prapannā'smo nimagnā duḥkhasāgare |
uddharā'smān duḥkhasindhoḥ pāhi naḥ paricārikāḥ || 30 ||

ityuktvā caraṇau spṛṣṭvā rurudurdaityayoṣitaḥ |
vīkṣya tāsāṃ praruditaṃ karuṇā'krāntamānasaḥ || 31 ||

ānāyya daityarājaṃ taṃ tatra provāca nāradaḥ |
daityeśvara ! maduktaṃ tvaṃ śṛṇu vākyaṃ sudhāsamam ||32 ||

mā śocayā'valāścemā mā nāśāya ca bāndhavān |
hitavaktā hi lokdeṣu janaḥ paramadurlabhaḥ || 33 ||

avicāryā'gamā'pāyaṃ madhuraṃ yaḥ samakṣataḥ |
vadettamabhijānīyācchatruṃ mitrasvarupiṇam || 34 ||

p.594)

yo brūyādāgamā'pāyaṃ vimṛṣya parayā dhiyā |
kaṭukaṃ hyauṣadhasamaṃ jānīyānmitrameva tam || 35 ||

guruḥ kāvyo'tyantadhīradīrghā'marśī priye hitaḥ |
taduktamatyantapathyaṃ rogiṇo bheṣjaṃ yathā || 36 ||

guruktamaparityajya saubhāgyaṃ paramāpnuyāt |
tadahaṃ prabravīmi tvāṃ yattaddaityapate śṛṇu || 37 ||

manyase yatsvamātmānaṃ sarvebhyo balavattaram |
tatrā'pi śṛṇu vakṣyāmi kaiṭabho daityaśekharaḥ || 38 ||

yasyā'bhūnmedasaśceyaṃ medinī lokadhāriṇī | 
viṣṇunā nihato yuddhe'pratimo balaprauruṣe || 39 ||

striyaṃ tāmabalāṃ yattvaṃ manyase tat purā śrūtam |
śumbhāsuraniśumbhau ca mahiṣaśca baloddhataḥ |
to'nayaiva parayā mahāsurabalairvṛtaḥ || 40 ||

alaṃ te baladarpeṇa ripuṇā svā'tmaghātinā |
na kiṃ smarasi gauryā te yuddhe pūrvaparābhavam || 41 ||

gauryādibhirbiśiṣṭeyaṃ lalitā parameśvarī |
sevyate yā'nekakoṭigaurīpramukhaśaktibhiḥ || 42 ||

p.595)

sā'syāḥ śakteḥ pādanakhasamaṃ brahmāṇḍamaṇḍalam |
yasya prasādādeva tvaṃ manyase balināṃ varam || 43 ||

sa'pi tasyā mahādevaḥ pīṭhapādāṃśasambhavaḥ |
tadalaṃ svā'tmasarvārthanāśanā'bhiniveśataḥ || 44 ||

prapaśyemāḥ priyāḥ sādhvīḥ śocantīrdainyamāgatāḥ |
ahaṃ tvāṃ tatra neṣyāmi parāśakteḥ padāntikam || 45 ||

kṣāmayāmi ca tāṃ devīṃ tvadarthaṃ sarvayatnataḥ |
rājyaṃ prasādhi pātāle divaṃ śāsatu vāsavaḥ || 46 ||

rakṣa daityaparīvārān mā vinaṅkṣīḥ priyaiḥ saha |
na kariṣyasi matproktaṃ yadā yahi vinaṅkṣyasi || 47 ||

daityeśa kaścinmatproktaṃ śrutaṃ manasi rocate |
dhiyā sāttvikayā caitadvimṛśa svātmarakṣaṇam || 48 ||

śrutvetthaṃ nāradavaco bhaṇḍadaityaḥ prahasya ca |
hastenā'dāya devarṣiṃ prārohat saudhaśekharam || 49 ||

meruśṛṅgapratīkāśaṃ kāñcanaṃ saudhaśekhare |
vātāyane maṇimaye viveśa mṛduviṣṭare || 50 ||

p.596)

sanniveśyā'sane tatra devarṣi mṛdutūlaje |
devarṣamayi roṣaṃ tvaṃ na śiṣye kartumarhasi || 51 ||

jānāmi tvā. mahābhaktṃ parāśakteḥ padā'śrayam |
yadbravīṣyakhilaṃ tatte nā'nyathā viditaṃ mayā || 52 ||

jānāsi sarvaṃ me vṛttaṃ līlayaivaṃ bravīṣi mām || 53 ||

ahaṃ nanu ramādevyā dūto māṇikyaśekharaḥ |
kadācit sā toṣaṇāya tripurāyā mahattapaḥ || 54 ||

cakre'bdānāṃ triniyutaṃ jāhnavītaṭasaṃśrayā |
kadācit kinnarī kācittāruṇyā'mṛtapūritā || 55 ||

unmajjantī nimajjantī patitā jāhnavījale 
sroto'bhiruhyamānā sā trātāraṃ nā'dhigacchata || 56 ||

dṛṣṭvā karuṇayā'viṣṭa āplutaḥ svardhunījale |
tāṃ pṛṣṭhataḥ samāropya taṭamāruhya sattvaram || 57 ||

tāṃ samāveśayaṃ tatra vijvarā sā kṣaṇādbabhau |
tadaṅgasparśanāścāhaṃ lāvaṇyasya samīkṣaṇāt || 58 ||

kāmena mohito jātastāmavocamimāṃ giram |
kalyāṇi tvaṃ rakṣitā'si mayā mṛtyormukhādivā || 59 ||

p.597)

tvadaṅgasaṅgāt kāmārto jālo'haṃ rakṣa māmiha |
kṛte pratikṛtīṃ kuryācchaktaḥ sarvātmanā svayam || 60 ||

akurvan pratyupakṛtiṃ nihantyātmānamātmanā |
tanmāṃ kāmāgninā'viṣṭamārtaṃ te śaraṇā'gatam || 61 ||

rakṣā'dhunā svātmadānānno cet prāṇān jahāmyaham |
śrutvā madviparītoktiṃ sādhvī sā kinnarī vacaḥ || 62 ||

abravīdamṛtasyandi madhuraṃ guṇavataram |
mā buddhibhraṃśamāgaccha kinnarī patidevatā || 63 ||

tapasyabhirato bhartā ito me krośapañcake |
āste tasya priyā cā'smi tarucchāyeva saṅgatā || 64 ||

nā'tivarte patiṃ kvā'pi śṛṇvan yadapi kāraṇam |
ya āpadbhyaḥ samuddhartā yaśca bālyaprapoṣakaḥ || 65 ||

sa pitā dharmataḥ śāstre proktastasmāt pitā mama |
tvaṃ te sutā dharmato'haṃ naivaṃ māṃ vaktumarhasi || 66 ||

puruṣaḥ svātmanaḥ śatruṃ mano yacchedapasthalāt |
nāśayettaṃ manaḥ śīghraṃ mano yasya vaśe na hi || 67 ||

vimṛśa svacchayā buddhya dhīrayā lokaceṣṭitam |
strīsukhaṃ sarvato hyekaṃ na viśeṣaḥ paśuṣvapi || 68 ||

p.598)

tat svakāntāsveva ratiṃ manasā bhā nā'nyataḥ |
patibratānāṃ krodhāgniṃ prāpya mā bhasmatāṃ vrajāḥ || 69 ||

ityādihitavākyāni vadantī bahudhā'pyaham |
kāmamohaparītā'tmā parāmrṣṭumupadruta || 70 ||

sā krośantī ramādevyāścaraṇāntikamāgatā |
vadantī rakṣa rakṣeti tatastasyāstu sammukhe || 71 ||

parāmṛṣṭottarīye sā mayā kāmāndhacakṣuṣā |
athaṣṭvā'nayaṃ devī ramā kruddhā śaśāpa mām || 72 ||

naiṣā devatanurmūḍha ! durvinīta tavocitā |
kṛtyaṃ tavā'suraṃ hye tattatastvamasuro bhava || 73 ||

taṃ śāpaṃ ghorarūpantu śrutvā kāmasya vegataḥ |
pratibuddhaḥ śokasindhunimagno'bhavamañjasā || 74 ||

samāśvastā kinnarī sā ntvā svabhavanaṃ yayau |
punaḥ prāha ramādevī māṃ magnaṃ śokasāgare || 75 ||

dhiganārya matsamīpaṃ na vastuṃ tvamihār'si |
śariraṃ te ghorarūpaṃ paradārā'bhimarśanam || 76 ||

gacchatvadṛśyatāṃ sadyo bhava vāyyuśarīrakaḥ |
evaṃ tayā praśapto'haṃ bhīto devīśriyaṃ tadā || 77 ||

p.599)

prasādyaṃ bhubidhaiḥ prārthanaiḥ sannatairapi |
evaṃ cireṇa bhūyo'pi prasannā'bhavadambikā || 78 ||

mayā śāpavimokṣāya prārthitā sā'ha māṃ tadā |
māṇikyaśekhara ! kṛtaṃ tvyā'tyantavininditam || 79 ||

naitasyā'pacitiṃ cānyāṃ pa"yāmi svalpamapyuta |
nā'nyathā me bhavecchāpaḥ kṛtaṃ bhoktavyameva te || 80 ||

naṣṭadeho vāyubhūtaściraṃ sthitvā'suro bhava |
kintu tāṃ patitāṃ toye dayayoddhṛtavānasi || 81 ||

tena puṇyaprapākena vibhavaṃ sarvato'dhikam |
prāpyā'nekā'ṇḍanāthatvaṃ bhuktā bhogāna yathepsitān || 82 ||

mahādevyā hataḥ saṅkhye prāpya tallokasaṃsthitim |
prāpya tatra ciraṃ kālamasuraiḥ pratipūjitaḥ || 83 ||

ante tatpadamāsādya na bhūyaḥ sambhaviṣyasi |
śrutvaitadvacanaṃ bhūyo hyapṛcchaṃ lokamātaram || 84 ||

devi ! kā sā mahādevī nihataḥ syāṃ yayā mṛdhe |
sā kiṃ tvatto'dhikā devī nā'haṃ jāne tavā'dhikām || 85 ||

p.600)

brūhi sā katamā kā ca tasyāḥ kiṃ vā mahitvakam |
ityāpṛṣtā mayā devī prāha śrīlokamātṛkā || 86 ||

māṇikyaśekhara śṛṇu sā devī sarvataḥ parā |
mahācitisvarūpā sā yasyāḥ prakṛtidharmataḥ || 87 ||

brahmā viṣṇurharaścaiva pratyaṇḍaṃ guṇamūrtayaḥ |
īśavaro'pitirodhānakarastriguṇajeśvaraḥ || 88 ||

tatrā'pyanugrahakaraḥ sadāśiva itīritaḥ |
yasyā īkṣaṇaleśenā'nugrahādakhilaṃ sṛjet || 89 ||

anādikālato māyāpāśitānaṇusaṅghakān |
anugrahānmocayituṃ sṛjatyeṣa maheśvaraḥ || 90 ||

na muktiḥ pralayasthānāmaṇūnāṃ śūnyabhāvataḥ |
dehabhāvaṃ samāpannā sṛṣṭau jñātuṃ prabhāvitaḥ || 91 ||

so'pi tasyā aṃśabhūta iti sā sārarūpī | 
sarvā'tmarūpā paramā citiśaktirudīritā || 92 ||

saivā'styatra jagaścitrabhittidarpaṇasammitā |
sarvā'tmanā vṛṃhaṇena brahmaśabdena śabditā || 93 ||

na stri na ṣaṇḍho na pumāṃstripurā ciccharīriṇī |
avāṅmanasagamyā sā cetyanirmuktacinmayī || 94 ||

p.601)

sā tu saptadaśī nityā parameśvarasaṃśrayā |
dve kale tasya nāthasya kāryatā kartṛteti ca || 95 ||

kāryatā syāt ṣoḍaśadhā kartṛtā caikarūpī |
indriyāṇāntu daśakaṃ bhūtānāṃ pañcakaṃ tathā || 96 ||

antaḥkaraṇamityevaṃ kāryatā ṣoḍaśā'tmikā |
kartṛtā syāt saptadaśī kalā nityā maheśituḥ || 97 ||

sā ṣoḍaśā'śrayībhūtā saṃvita sarvasya kāraṇam |
tāṃ hitvā na śivaḥkaścit brahmā vā'pi sadāśivaḥ || 98 ||

īśvaraḥ śivaviṣṇū vā brahmā vā devatāgaṇaḥ |
nara nāryaḥ prāṇijātaṃ jaṅgamaṃ sthāvaraṃ tathā || 99 ||

prāṇavaścā'prāṇakaṃ vā na kiñcidavaśiṣyate |
bhūṣaṇeṣu svaraṇamiva jalavat sāgarādiṣu || 100 ||

śūnytvamiva cā'kāśe spaśaṃvat syandavatpare |
tejasyuṣṇaṃ yathā rūpaṃ jalespandoraso yathā || 101 ||

pṛthivyāṃ gandhakāṭhinye iva sā sarvataḥ sthitā |
sā svā'bhāsitalīlā'tmalokoddharaṇahetunā || 102 ||

paraśrīcakranagarasāmrājñītvamadhiṣṭhitā |
jātā vayaṃ tadaṅgebhyo vākśrīgaurīsamāhvyāḥ || 103 ||

p.602)

kālī krodhāt samutpannā mahākālena saṃyutā |
vacanāttu tathā jātā tārā bhairavasaṃyutā || 104 ||

parākramāt samudbhūtā durgā durgatināśinī |
krauryāt pratyaṅgirodbhūtā mahāśarabhasaṃyutā || 105 ||

śāryājjātā śūlinī ca mālinī vāksamudbhavā |
caṇḍikā caṇḍaśabdena dhūmrā krūrekṣaṇena ca || 106 ||

evaṃ sarvāstadaṅgotthā na hi kācittataḥ parā |
tasyā līlā'vatāreṇa lalitā'khyena saṅgare || 107 ||

hatastallokavasatiṃ ciraṃ prāpya vimokṣyase |
ityuktastu tayā paścāt prārthanaṃ kṛtavān punaḥ || 108 ||

evañcedamba bhavatu kīñcittvāṃ prārthaye punaḥ |
āsuratvaṃ bhavedyonisambandhena vinā mama || 109 ||

parāśaktermukhyabhaktaśarīrānme janirbhavet |
na jātu māmiyaṃ prajñā jahātu jagadambike ! ||110 ||

prārthitaivaṃ tathaivā'stu tavetyuktavatī ramā |
tadahaṃ bhaktamūrdhanyāt kāmā'ṅgājjanimāptavān || 111 ||

pañcottaraśatā'ṇḍānāmādhipatyaṃ ciraṃ kṛtam |
viṣayānuttamān bhuktvā nityañca cirakālataḥ || 112 ||

nirviṇṇo viṣayebhyo'haṃ kāṅkṣe tasyā vadhaṃ svakam |
vastuṃ tasyāḥ samīpe'haṃ talloke kṛtaniścayaḥ || 113 ||

p.603)

tasyāḥ padāmbūjaṃ dhyāyannajasraṃ parameśvarīm |
pratīkṣāmi kadā'gatya vidhiṣyati ca māmiti || 114 ||

putrapautrakalatrādyairnihatastu tayā raṇe |
talloke nivasāmyāśu iti me tvarte manaḥ || 115 ||

tadgatvā'śvāsya me bhāryāṃ bodhyā'śvavivekataḥ |
iti śrutvā bhaṇḍavaco nārado hṛṣṭamānasaḥ || 116 ||

saṃślādhya bhaṇḍadaityeśaṃ samāśvāsya ca tatpriyām |
jagāmā'bhimataṃ sthānaṃ nārado munisattamaḥ || 117 ||

iti śrīmaditihāsottame tripurārahasye māhātmyakhaṇḍe lalitāmāhātmye 
śrīnārada-bhaṇḍasamvāde bhaṇḍadvārā 
svapūrvajanmakṛtakarmmavipāakātparāyāḥ 
kṛpāvaśāddevīdhāmaprāptivarṇanaṃ nāmaikonaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 4875 ||

p.604)

ṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ 
nāradahāritāyanasamvāde bhaṇḍāsuravicāravarṇanam 

ityudīritamākarṇya nārado hāritāyanam |
prasannaḥ prāha saṃhṛṣtaḥ svā'nubhūtanirūpaṇāt || 1 ||

hāritāyana ! sādhuktaṃ tvayāitaccirakālajam |
mānasaṃ bāhyamapi ca mamā'sīdyat purātanam || 2 ||

tadyathātathanirdeśāt smṛtaṃ me proktavatkramāt |
cirakālenā'ntaritamapyadyeva tavoktitaḥ || 3 ||

bhātyantaḥkaraṇe sarvaṃ dhanyastvamasi bhūtale |
yatparākṛpayā sarvaṃ viditaṃ bhāhyamāntaram || 4 ||

tattvāṃ pṛcchāmi me brūhi saṃśayo'ntaścirāt sthitaḥ |
atītamāntaraṃ bhāhyaṃ kathate viditaṃ hyabhūt || 5 ||

adṛṣṭamaśutaṃ vā'pi kathaṃ jānāsi suvrata |
śrutvetthaṃ nāradavacaḥ prahasya hāritāyanaḥ || 6 ||

devarṣe śṛṇu vakṣyāmi bahvalpamidamīritam |
atropapattirapare khaṇde samyagbhaviṣyati || 7 ||

p.605)

yathā'nubhūtaṃ smarati jana evamahaṃ nanu |
vareṇa brahmaṇaḥ sarvabāhyaṃ vā'pyāntaraṃ tathā || 8 ||

tvatprasādena jitā sā mātreṇaivā'nubhūtavat |
saṃsmarāmyavidagdhaṃnu śrīdevyāḥ suprasādataḥ || 9 ||

tato dattaguruḥ prāha rāmāyaikāgracetase |
śṛṇu bhārgava ! tatpaścāddhāyā'syaḥ kumbhajanmane || 10 ||

prāha yuddhakathāṃ bhaṇḍā'suradevyormahādbhutām |
śṛṇukumbhodbhava mune homā'gnijanitāṃ śivām || 11 ||

lalitāṃ sarvalokaikasundarīṃ parameśvarīm |
dṛṣṭvā śakramukhā devāścintayāmāsurāntare || 12 ||

nanviyaṃ taruṇī sarvalokasaundaryamandirā |
latevā'lambarahitā bhartṛhīnā na śaobhate || 13 ||

devacintāṃ viditvaivaṃ brahmādyā guṇamūrtayaḥ |
dadhyuḥ kāmeśvaraṃ yāvattāvatso'pi vinirgataḥ || 14 ||

tanostasyā mahādevyāḥ samānā'kārabhūṣaṇaḥ |
dṛṣṭvā'nurūpa devyāste patirasyā varo mataḥ || 15 ||

bhūyādvivāhaḥ (stva?) anayorityāśaṃsanatatparāḥ |
tadvijñāya vidhimukhāḥ prārthya devīṃ parātparām || 16 ||

p.606)

mahotsavavidhānena vivāhaṃ samakalpayn |
atha devaiḥ prārthitā sā lalitā parameśvarī || 17 ||

badhāya bhaṇdaityasya parivārānakalpayat |
samastā'varaṇaughāṃśca smaraṇāt samavā'sṛjat || 18 ||

tadantare tu devarṣiḥ prāpto devyāḥ padā'ntikam |
praṇamya daṇḍavaddevīṃ stutvā vividhasaṃstavaiḥ || 19 ||

kṛtāñjalirbabhāṣe tāṃ vinīto natakandhraḥ |
devyahaṃ samanuprāpto bhaṇḍadaityasya sannidhe || 20 ||

āgacchati mahāsenāṃ vikarṣan balavattaraḥ |
tadahaṃ preṣitastena tvāmāhā'surabhūpatiḥ || 21 ||

abalā tvaṃ kasya balānmayā yoddhuṃ hi vāñchasi |
ajñātvā madbalaṃ mugdhā vināśaṃ mā vrajā'dhunā || 22 ||

bhava matpārśvagā śīghraṃ tyaja brahmamukhān surān |
bhūyaḥ parājitānmatto māyāmātrasamāśritān || 23 ||

mayā virudhya ko'pyatra yoṣidvā puruṣo'pi vā |
dveyāt sukhaṃ mṛtyumukhapraviṣṭamiva sarvathā || 24 ||

ityuktvā yuddhasannahaṃ paramaṃ samyagādiśat |
manye'haṃ tvaṃ jitaprāyā tena dhīreṇa sāmpratam || 25 ||
