p.607)

pṛcchāmi devi ! tvāṃ kiñcidrahasyājñāpayā'śu mām |
niśamya nāradoktiṃ tāṃ mantriṇīṃ samavekṣata || 26 ||

viditvā devyabhiprāyamiṅgitajñā'tha mantriṇī |
kareṇā'dāya devarṣi rahasi prāviśat parā || 27 ||

atha tatra samasīnā prāha taṃ mantranāyikā |
pṛccha nārada ! praṣṭavyaṃ yatte manasi vidyate || 28 ||

jānāmi tvāṃ parādevyāḥ pādabhaktiparāyaṇam |
nūnaṃ nā'viditaṃ te'sti loke śrīpādasaṃśrayāt || 29 ||

tathā'pi pṛccha te'bhīṣṭaṃ chedmi saṃśayamāhitam |
ājñapta evaṃ mantriṇyā praṇamya suratāpasaḥ || 30 ||

prāha baddhāñjalipuṭaḥ praśnaśabdān supeśalān |
devī! bhaṇḍāsuro dṛṣṭo mayā samyak parīkṣitaḥ || 31 ||

nūnaṃ sa sarvabhaktānāmasmākaṃ śekharāyitaḥ |
kathaṃ yuddhe parābhūyāt parāpādaikasaṃśrayaḥ || 32 ||

athā'pi tena samproktamahaṃ devyā hato yudhi |
ciraṃ nivasya śrīloke tato gacchāmi tatpadam || 33 ||

p.608)

lallokabādhakaḥ kasmāddhīnā'cāro'pi mucyate |
vayaṃkasmāllokagatiṃ vindāmastadubravīhi me || 34 ||

pṛṣtainaṃ śyāmalā rājñī nāradaṃ pratyuvāca ha |
śṛṇu me nārda ! vaco rahasyamapi sāmpratam || 35 ||

śrīpādasaṃśrayaḥ kvā'pi hīyate na kadācana |
tasyottamapadaprāptiḥ sarvathā syānna cā'nyathā || 36 ||

yathā bālasyā'mayino mātā pathyamapīcchitam |
na yacchati tathā pathyamauṣadhaṃ hyanapekṣitam || 37 ||

dadāti balatastvevaṃ tatprapoṣaṇatatparā |
taṃ bhaktaśekharaṃ hatvā duṣkṛtaṃ vitataṃ kṛtam || 38 ||

darśanā'lāpaśastrārdyairvinā'śya balavattaram |
svapadaṃ prāpayatyāśu tathā śṛṇu bravīmyaham || 39 ||

yastu yaṃ prārthayatyarthamekāntena svacetasā |
tasmai tattripurā śighraṃ prayachati na saṃśayaḥ || 40 ||

pratikūlamabhīṣṭasya yāvanno nāśamṛcchati |
tāvadeva tasya ciraṃ tataḥ sveṣṭamavāpnuyāt || 41 ||

śṛṇu tvaṃ nityamukto'si na te muktiḥ samīhitā |
ajñasya tatsamīhā syāt jñasya vai tatkuto bhavet || 42 ||

p.609)

baddho hi muktiṃ vāñcheta yathā bhūktiṃ bubhukṣitaḥ |
tṛptaḥ kathaṃ bhuktimicchettava'nyo bhojayet katham || 43 ||

aprāptasya prāptiriṣṭā kā yā prāptasya sambhavet |
ākāśasyeva pūrṇasya kiṃ prāptiḥ syāt kathaṃvada ? || 44 ||

tasmādajñasya prāptavyo muktiste jñasya sā katham |
śrūtvetthaṃ nāarado mantriṇīproktaṃ hṛṣṭamanasaḥ || 45 ||

prṇamya tāñca tripurāṃ jīvanmukto jagāma ha |
atha sā tripurā devī bhaṇḍā'suravadhaṃ prati || 46 ||

prārthitā vidhiviṣṇvādyairniryayau śaktisenayā |
cakrarājasamakārarathe parvanavā'tmake || 47 ||

ūrdhvordhvabhūmikā'karasadṛśe samalaṅkṛte |
ūrdhvabhūmau sthitā yuddhasambhāraughasusambhṛtā || 48 ||

abhedyakavaca (adyaiśca?) deśitā'ptibibhīṣaṇī |
śastrā'stranicayopetā roṣā'ruṇitalocanā || 49 ||

dhanurvedo mūrtidharaḥ sthitaḥ pārśve kṛtāñjaliḥ |
śastrāṇyastrāṇi ca tathā mūrtimanti sthitāni vai || 50 ||

p.610)

adho'dhḥ kramataḥ sarvāstathā'varaṇaśaktayaḥ |
deśitāścitrakavacaiḥ śastrā'straiḥsupariṣkṛtāḥ || 51 ||

gurbaughā api sannāddhāḥ saṃsthitā yuddhakāṅkṣiṇaḥ |
taddkṣiṇe tu mantreśī saptaparvavirājite || 52 ||

saṃsthitā saparīvārā sannaddhā yuddhakarmaṇi |
śukādiśyāmalāvargaiḥ sevyamānā viniryayau || 53 ||

divyapānā'modamadaghūrṇanetrā'mbujadvayī |
tathāvidhā'saṅkhyakoṭimātaṅgatanayā'vṛtā || 54 ||

dānairvādyairnartanaiśca sopahāsapaṭūktibhiḥ |
mantriṇī toṣayantyastā vilāsairhāsyamiśritaiḥ || 55 ||

sā'pi śrīlalitārājñīmantriṇo mantrakovidā |
tasyā mano'nukūleṣu mantreṣu niyataṃsthitā || 56 ||

viśeṣato gītavādyanarmakrīḍāsamutsukā |
atha tadvāmato daṇṇdasāmrājñī śūkarā'nanā || 57 ||

kiricakre pañcaparvayute sarvordhvataḥ sthitā |
parivṛtā hyanekābhirjambhinyādisvaśaktibhiḥ || 58 ||

krodhamūrtiḥ krūratarā kṣamāpanaparapriyā |
aparādhaparān sarvān daṇḍayantī sadā sthitā || 59 ||

p.611)

mahāmahiṣasiṃhābhyāṃ vāhanābhyāṃ svapārśvayoḥ |
sthitābhyāṃ sīramusalamukhaśastraiśca śobhinīm || 60 ||

baṭukairyoginībhiśca koṭibhiḥ sā parīvṭtā |
tathā śrīlalitādeśyā bālā tripurasundarī || 61 ||

kumārī laghucakrākhyasyandane samavasthitā |
nijāvṛttimahāśaktigaṇaiḥ parisamāvṛtā || 62 ||

tasyā dakṣiṇāpārśve tu prāptā sampadadhīśvarī |
raṇakolāhalā'khyāne tridhā bhinne'tibhīṣaṇe || 63 ||

vāraṇendre vindhyagirimahāśikharasannibhe |
nisargamatte saṃruḍhā mahāṅkuśavidhāriṇī || 64 ||

tāmanvasaṃkhyā mattebhāḥ koṭikoṭyatisaṅkhyakāḥ ||
vindhyodbhavā malayajāḥ prāgjyotiṣasamudbhavāḥ || 65 ||

suśikṣitā yuddhagatiparijñāḥ ṣaṣṭihāyanāḥ |
praceluḥ sampadīśitrīparīvārasuśaktibhiḥ || 66 ||

śastrā'strasamarajñābhiradhiruḍhāḥ suveginaḥ |
tasyā śritā vāmabhāgamaśvāruḍhā maheśvarī || 67 ||

aparājitasaṃjñānamaśvaratnaṃ mahonnatam |
gandharvajātijaṃ vāyujavanaṃ samadhiṣṭhitā || 68 ||

p.612)

uccai'hśravaparībhāvisarvalakṣaṇamaṇḍitaḥ |
"śuktyardhaśuktimaṇibhirdevasvastikapadmakaiḥ || 69 ||

gaṇḍikādiśubhaiścihnaiścihnito hemabhāsuraḥ |
sūkṣmā'vadhānato'pyasya na viduḥ khurasaṅgatim || 70 ||

abhijñḥ svāmicittasya parāgamavidhānavit |
tāmanvaśvā asaṃkhyeyāḥ sindhuṭaṅkaṇambhavāḥ || 71 ||

āradāḥ pārvatīyāśca vārvarāśca  sutejanāḥ |
śoṇāḥ śyāmāḥ sitāḥ pītāḥ kapilāḥ pāṭalāstathā || 72 ||

haritā dhūmravarṇāśca kalmāṣā nīlalohitāḥ |
vicitrāścārusarvāṅgā ghanakesaramaṇḍanāḥ || 73 ||

vicitraśikṣāgatayo nṛtyajñā layasaṅgatāḥ |
vicitraśastrā'stradharaśaktibhiḥ samādhiṣṭhitāḥ || 74 ||

puraḥ patantaḥ paritastaraṅgā iva vāridheḥ |
dārayanto'vanimiva khurakṣepairviniryayuḥ || 75 ||

khuravikṣipasambhūtaiścaṭaścaṭaravairyutaiḥ |
dveṣitasvanasaṅghātairgajacītkāramiśritaiḥ || 76 ||

p.613)

śaktīnāṃ siṃhanādaiśca vīrā'sphoṭamahāsvanaiḥ |
jayabheraīravairmṛṇḍḍmṛdaṅgajhallarīravaiḥ || 77 ||

gomukhā'nakaniḥ sāṇatālakāhalaniḥsvanaiḥ |
miśritaḥ śrīcakrarājarathanemigaṇodbhavaḥ || 78 ||

mahādhvaniḥ sarvadiśaṃ pūrayanniva sambabhau |
śrūtvā śrīlalitādevyā yaitrayātrāmahāsvanam || 79 ||

daityāḥ sādhvasamatyantaṃ mṛtyoriva samāyayuḥ |
bhaṇḍadaityaḥ svātmavadhaṃ cirābhilaṣitaṃ tayā || 80 ||

śrutvā taṃ ninadaṃ hyārādadhyavasyata sarvathā |
atha daityān samālokya trastān śuṣyanmukhāmabujān || 81 ||

kṛpayā parayā'viṣṭa idaṃ svāntaracintayat |
nūnaṃ hīme mama kṛte vinaśyanti sabāndhavāḥ || 82 ||

loke mṛtyornātiriktaṃ bhayamasti kadācana |
matkṛte nihatānetān śociṣyanti priyātmajāḥ || 83 ||

bhaginyo mātṛmukhyāśca hanta vaiśasamīdṛśam |
tadahaṃ purato gatvā yuddhvā śrīlalitāmbayā || 84 ||

tacchastrā'nalapūtātmā prapadye'bhimatāṃ gatim |
śocayāmi kutastvetā viyojya svasvabāndhavaiḥ || 85 ||

p.614)

tadalaṃ ghoranidhanarodanena kṛtena me |
śakto'rakṣan svīyajanaṃ śokasthānāt kathañcana || 86 ||

bandhughnaḥ sa tu vijñeyaḥ puruṣādasamo janaiḥ |
athaivaṃ mayi nirtya prāpte devyā nijā'tyayam || 87 ||

bandhūnāṃ śoka evā'smānmayā virahitā'tmanām |
kathaṃ mayi hate devatrāsanā mama bāndhavā || 88 ||

maharddhiśobhanā devaiḥ parībhūtā hṛtarddhayaḥ |
bhaviṣyanti śucā hīnā na caitadapi sammatam || 89 ||

prāpya sarvocchrayaṃ kaścidabhibhūtaḥ kathaṃ bhavet |
nūnāṃ mayi hate devyā bhrātṛputrādibāndhavaḥ || 90 ||

śūrāstu tatkṣṇe eva vīryakrodhasamucchritāḥ |
yudhā'gnau svātmaśalabhān bhasmīkuryurna saṃśayaḥ || 91 ||

atha ye kātarāḥ svalpavīryāste'vaniranghragāḥ |
devā iva dainyabhājo viyutāḥ strīsutādibhiḥ || 92 ||

śocayantastathā'nyonyaṃ bhaveyurvai mṛtā'dhikāḥ |
ārodanantu bandhūnā nāastīti na kathañcana || 93 ||

p.615)

tadvṛthā kiṃ cintanena manye nā'nyadito varam |
ādau devyā hateṣveṣu tato'hamabhavaṃhataḥ || 94 ||

ayaṃ śreyān hi me bhāti santyatra bahuśo guṇāḥ |
pāvanaṃdevatāśastraiḥ prāptirlokottamasya ca || 95 ||

parībhavā'dyasambhāvaḥ kīrtirloke mahattarā |
atrā'pi manye strībālamukhānāṃ n hi sambhavam || 96 ||

apratīrkāyametattu daivamatra parāyaṇam |
atha vaitanmayā pūrvaṃ śrutamabjāsanānmukhāt || 97 ||

sā parā tripurā devī bhaktavāñchāprapūraṇī |
iti tanme parīvāraṃ na śoṣayatu sā parā || 98 ||

na me caitenā'hameṣāṃ paramārthaṃsvabhāvataḥ |
jānānasyā'pi me devī mohamutpādayatyalam || 99 ||

tanme vicintanaṃ vyarthaṃ svapnasāre jagatsthitau |
tattaṃ sarveṇa bhāvena śaraṇaṃ prāptavānaham || 100 ||

tanmāṃ niyojayedyadvanniyukto'smi na cā'nyathā |
pāhi māṃ parameśāni śaraṇye ! śaraṇāgatam || 101 ||

mohajālaprabandhānmāmabhyuddhartumihār'hasi |
iti prārthya mahādevīṃ tripurāṃ bhaṇḍadānavaḥ || 102 ||

niśāmya dainyamāpannān bandhūn svaparitaḥ sthitān |
harṣayanniti hovāca parākramayutaṃ vacaḥ || 103 ||

p.616)

he dānavā ! mama vacaḥ śṛṇudhvaṃ śraddhayā param |
na me mṛyuḥ kuto'pyasti mṛtyormṛtyurahaṃ yatha || 104 ||

śastrāṇāñca tathā'strāṇāṃ mahāmāyāvināmapi |
sacarā'caralokānāṃ sraṣṭā'haṃ vaḥ samakṣataḥ || 105 ||

pañcottaraśatāṇḍānāmīśvaro'haṃ mahābalaḥ |
tatremāmabalāṃ jñātvā na śīghraṃ hantumutshe || 106 ||

yuddhanirbharatāmasyā dṛṣṭvā nirjitya līlayā |
svā'ntaḥ puraṃ samānīya mahā'nandamuṣaimyalam || 107 ||

iti śrīmaditihāsottame tripurārahasye māhātmyakhaṇḍe śrīlalitāmāhātmye 
śrīdevyā saha yuddhakaraṇāya sajjībhūtasya 
bhaṇṇdāsurasyohāmapohavarṇanaṃ nāma ṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 4983 ||

p.617)

ekaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ 
śrīlalitāyāḥ sainyavyavasāyajñānāya daityarājena svadūtapreṣaṇavarṇanam 

ityuktvā pārśvagaṃ sūtaṃ dīrghagrīvaṃ mahā'suraḥ |
babhāṣe krodhatāmrākṣo rathaḥ saṃyujyatāmiti || 1 ||

atha sūto nimeṣaṇa sannaddhaṃ syandanottamam |
nivedayaddaityanāthe praṇamyā'tivinītavat || 2 ||

āruroha rathaśreṣṭhaṃ bhaṇḍadaityḥ pratāpanaḥ |
gṛhītvā saśaraṃ cāpaṃ pracacālā'tiroṣitaḥ || 3 ||

dṛ.ṣtvā śrutvā ca daityeśaṃ vrajantaṃ yaddhakāraṇāt |
daityā viniryayuḥ śīghraṃ sannaddhāḥ krodhamūrchitāḥ || 4 ||

purataḥ pār"śvayoḥ pṛṣṭhe sarvataḥ parivārya tam |
niryayurdaityasubhaṭāḥ saṅghaśo dhṛtahetayaḥ || 5 ||

bhaṇḍadaityasutāḥ sarve bhaṇḍatulyaparākramāḥ |
kuṭilākṣo'grajasteṣāmameyabalavikramaḥ || 6 ||

syandanasthāḥ susannaddhāḥ samareṣvanivartinaḥ |
akṣauhiṇīśatayutā bhaṇḍadaityā'grato yayuḥ || 7 ||

ahampūrvikayā yuddhe ye saṃspardhanti nityaśaḥ |
pārśvayordaityparājasya viṣaṅgaśca viśukrakaḥ || 8 ||

celaturbhrātarau tasya viṣṇutulyaparakramau |
rathasthau tau mahācāpau krudhā'ruṇitalocanau || 9 ||

grasantāviva sainyāni pareṣām yayaturdrutam |
pratyekamakṣauhiṇīnāṃ śatena parivāritau || 10 ||

ugrakarmamukhāstasya mantriṇo bhīmavikramāḥ |
anujagmurdaityapariṃ śatā'kṣauhiṇisaṃyutāḥ || 11 ||

evaṃ pracalite daityanāthe samarakāraṇāt |
rarāja rājapathyā tu prāvṛṭkālanadī yathā || 12 ||

kūlaṅkaṣā daityajalā rathā'vartasamākulā |
turaṅgabhaṅgā dviradagrāhā kheṭakakacchapī || 13 ||

unmīṣatphenapiṇḍā'ḍhyā patākālaharīyatā |
khaḍgadīrghamahāsarpā tūṇimatsyavicitritā || 14 ||

dānavā'syā'mbhojatatistannetraśapharīgaṇā |
vicitracchatrahaṃsādipatatritatimālinī || 15 ||

ucchalatturagottuṅgaśiṃśumārakulā'vilā |
rathaghoṣamahāghoṣā vāhiṇī jalavāhinī || 16 ||

p.619)

nirgamattatpuradvārānmahato ratnagopurāt |
kulyāmukhājjalaṃ hyāśu kedāre prasṛtaṃ yathā || 17 ||

puradvārādvāhinī sā prasṛtā'bhudbahiḥ kṣaṇāt |
tadantre tu vijayaḥ prāpya daityeśvaraṃ drutam || 18 ||

mantrī praṇamya vinyasya mūrdhnyañcalipuṭaṃ sthitaḥ |
prāpyā'jñāmavadattaṃ suvacastatkālasammatam || 19 ||

daityeśvara mahārāja kīrtiste sarvatastatā |
pratāpatapanaścordhve brahmāṇḍāni tapatyasau || 20 ||

balavattara eṣo'tra harireko vibhāvitaḥ |
so'pi tvatsammukhaṃ prāpya yudde bhūyaḥ parājitaḥ || 21 ||

tattvāṃ yuddhe jayetkaściditi mogho hi niścayaḥ |
tathā'pi mantriṇā kāle cār'thaśāstrapramāṇataḥ || 22 ||

vimṛśya rājñe vaktvyaṃ hitabhāgamasammitam |
kāle yuktamanuktvā tu na sumantritvamarhati || 23 ||

catvāra eva samprotā upāyā daṇḍasāne |
tatraikiao'pi sve kāle sammato balavattaraḥ || 24 ||

anyakāle'nyamātiṣṭhanmūḍhadurmantrimantritaḥ |
naśyedasadṛśaṃ bhuktvā hyāmayīvā'gadaṃ nṛpaḥ || 25 ||

p.620)

tatrā'dhyasitaṃ prājñairdūtacāradṛśā'khilam |
dṛṣṭvā yuddhaṃ tena śreyaḥsamāpnuyāt || 26 ||

tatsammatau dūtacārau pareṣu drutam |
tāvat senā'pi sannddhā bhaviṣyatyaviśaṅkitā || 27 ||

rakṣaṇaṃ nagarasyā'pi vihitaṃ syādyathāvidhi |
vijñaptavyamidaṃ kāle mayā te svāminaḥ puraḥ || 28 ||

vijñāpitaṃ tadbhavatā kartavyaṃ kālasammitam |
niśamya mantrivākyaṃ tadyuktaṃ kuśalasammatam || 29 ||


samādhānāya bandhūnāmabhimanyata mantraṇam |
niveśya senāpatibhiḥ senāṃ tatra vibhāgaśaḥ || 30 ||

purarakṣāvidhānāya kuṭilākṣamavā'sṛjat |
amitraghnaṃ mantrivaryaṃ prāha bhaṇḍamahā'suraḥ || 31 ||

gacchā'mitraghana macchāstyā tāṃ brūhi lalitā'bhidhām |
nā'haṃ tvāṃ sundarīṃ devīṃ sarvathā saṃśrayocitām || 32 ||

vāñchāmi yoddhuṃ tatkasmānmāṃ yoddhuṃtvāmihāgatā |
yuddhaśraddhā taveyaṃ vai sammtaṃ mama sarvathā || 33 ||

na bibhemi mahāyuddhe tattvadyuddhe bhayaṃ kutaḥ |
kintvahaṃ tvāṃ priyākārāṃ dṛṣṭvā drutaṃ manobhavām || 34 ||

p.621)

tatsurāṇāṃ parityajya pakṣaṃ matsaṃśrayā bhava |
sāmnā yuddhena vā'pi tvāṃ karomyātmasamāśrayām || 35 ||

madvirodhena te loke hānireva na saṃśayaḥ |
vimṛśyaitaddrutataraṃ pakṣamekataraṃ bhaja || 36 ||

iti tāṃ brūhi sāmnā ca jñātvā tasyāḥ samīhitam |
balañcasarvaṃ tatkṛtyaṃ jñatvāa yāhi druta punaḥ || 37 ||

atha māyāvināṃ śreśṭhaṃ vidyunmālinamāha saḥ |
vidyunmālinmahāprājña māyāvikulanāyaka || 38 ||

parairavidito gatvā devyāḥ senāmaśeṣataḥ |
balañca vyavasāyañca bhedacchidrāṇi yuktitaḥ || 39 ||

vicārya śīghramāyāhi tvamatra kuśalohyasi |
ājñaptāvitarau daityau yayatuḥ śaktivāhinīm || 40 ||

anantāmabnudhiprakhyāmavigāhya kathañcana |
amitraghno mahāsenāṃ viveśā'bdhiṃ yathā plavaḥ || 41 ||

utkhātakaravālābhiḥ śaktibhiḥ parirakṣitām |
praviṣṭamātraṃ śaktyā sa gṛhīto balavattaraḥ || 42 ||

paricārikayā daṇḍanāthāyāḥ kāñcanā'bhayā |
capeṭayā nihatyā'śu gṛhītvā taṃ gale'suram || 43 ||

p.622)

tarjayāmāsa vacanaiḥ krodhayuktairbhayaṅkaraiḥ |
kastvaṃ senāmimāṃ devyā daṇḍarājñīsurakṣitām || 44 ||

avijñāpya praviṣṭo'si cā'smatpramukhavīkṣitām |
ājñāṃ vinā daṇḍarājñyāḥ praviṣṭo vāhinīmimām || 45 ||

jīvanno yāsyasi punaḥ sākṣānmṛtyurapi svayam |
tadantare tu samprāptāḥ sainyarakṣāniyojitāḥ || 46 ||

daṇḍinyājñāpratīkṣiṇyaḥ śaktaya śataśaḥ kṣaṇāt |
prabalābhiḥ samākṛṣṭo nihato mūrcchito'bhavat || 47 ||

śrutvā tadantare daṇḍanāthā daityasya saṅgatim |
samakṣaṃ hyānayāmāsa svaparīcāraśaktibhiḥ || 48 ||

pṛṣṭvā taṃ hyāgataṃ dūtaṃ jñātvā bhaṇḍā'surasya hi |
vijñāpya mantriṇī devyāḥ samīpamānayattataḥ || 49 ||

sā'pi vijñāpya taṃ prāptaṃ lalitāyāḥ samīpataḥ |
nītvā papraccha dūtaṃ taṃ kṛtyaṃ tatrā'gamasya hi || 50 ||

prāha praṇamyā'mitraghnaḥ prāpto bhaṇḍanideśataḥ |
dūto'haṃ tasya daityasya taduktaṃ saṃbravīmyaham || 51 ||

p.623)

āvayorna virodho'sti kuto yoddhuṃ samāgatā |
yuddhvāvā'pyayuddhvāvā vā devi bhūyāstvaṃ matsamāśrayā || 52 ||

tattvāmātmapriyāṃ hṛdyāṃ yodhayāmi kathaṃ vada ?
taddevān samparityajya bhava tvaṃ matsamāśrayā || 53 ||

vaktavyeti tvayā gatvā jñeyaṃ senābalā'balam |
ityahaṃ preṣitastena dūtabadhyā [dhaḥ] vininditā [naḥ] || 54 ||

śāstreṣu dūtasya vadhaṃ na vadanti vicakṣaṇāḥ |
iti matvā praviṣṭo'haṃ lakṣaitaḥ sarvaśaktibhiḥ || 55 ||

praviṣṭamātro'bhimato gṛhītastava śaktibhiḥ |
ānītaste samakṣañcetyetat sarvaṃ nirupitam || 56 ||

agre devyeva śaraṇamayogyome vadho bhavet |
niśamya dūtavacanaṃ lalitāyā mataṃ tataḥ || 57 ||

viditvā mantriṇī prāha smeramañjulabhāṣiṇī | 
mābhairdaitya vraja sukhaṃ hanyase na kadācana || 58 ||

brūyāstaṃ bhaṇḍadaityeśaṃ mayoktamavadhārya ca |
yadi te jīvane śraddhā drutamāgatya nirbhayaḥ || 59 ||

śaraṇīkuru deveśīṃ trilokīṃ samparityaja |
trilokeśo bhavedindra tvaṃ pātāle nivatsyasi || 60 ||

p.624)

kuru sakhyaṃ gotrabhidā saputrabalavāhanaḥ |
anyathā na vimokṣaste jīvan kvā'pi bhaviṣyati || 61 ||

nayainaṃ daṇḍasāmrājñi senā darśaya sarvataḥ |
darśayitvā tataḥ senabahirenaṃ visarjaya || 62 ||

ityājñaptā kolamukhīṃ pradarśya svāmanīkinīm |
vyasarjayattaṃ daiteyaṃ bahiḥ senāniveśanāt || 63 ||

atha muktaḥ punarjātamātmānaṃ samamaṃsata |
jagāma rājasavidhaṃ bhayavihvalitā'ntaraḥ || 64 ||

vidyunmālī niśāmyainamamitraghnavihiṃsitam |
bhīto rātrau mahāmāyaḥ kauśikiṃ tanumāśritaḥ || 65 ||

vicarannākāśamārge senāṃ sarvāṃ niśāmayat |
gaṇayitvā sarvasenāṃ brajantaṃ gaganā'dhvanaḥ || 66 ||

jagrāha kācicchaktistaṃ khecarī niśirakṣiṇī |
gṛhītamātro māyāvī proḍḍīya gaganordhvataḥ || 67 ||

pañāśadyojanādūrdhva yuyodha sa tayā bhṛśam |
ciraṃ yuddhvā śūlahatastayā muktaḥ kathañcana || 68 ||

jagāma daityanṛpatimīṣatprāṇā'vaśeṣitaḥ |
papāta puratastasya sūryasyodayanaṃ prati || 69 ||

p.625)

dṛṣṭvā'grapatitaṃ daityaṃ mriyamāṇaṃ bhṛśaṃ hatam |
bhaṇḍāsuro japanmantraṃ pasparśā'ṅgeṣu pāṇinā || 70 ||

mantrasparśanamātreṇa jīvitaśca gatavyathaḥ |
utthāya praṇanāmā'śu daityarājaṃ puraḥsthitam || 71 ||

athapṛṣṭaḥ prāha vidyunmālī vṛttaṃ svakaṃ kramāt |
mahārāja tvatsamakṣaṃ gato'mitraghnasaṃyutam || 72 ||

dṛṣṭavānasmi tāṃ senāṃ pralayāmbudhisannibhām |
athā'mitraghna evā'dau praviveśa mahācamūm || 73 ||

pa"yato me kṣaṇenaiva hṛhītaḥ śaktibhirbalāt |
madhuvanmakṣikābhiḥ sa samākrānto'tha śaktibhiḥ || 74 ||

kṣaṇena vigataprajñaḥ samabhūdbalavānapi |
aśaktaḥ spanditumapi kva yuddhaṃ kva palāyanam || 75 ||

kathañcidapi no jīvet sa itthaṃ mama niścayaḥ |
tāvadbhīto'pyahaṃ durāt palāyanaparo'bhavam || 76 ||

bhavadājñāgauraveṇa na nivṛtto'smi te bhayāt |
tato dinā'vasāne'haṃ māyāghūkatvamāsthitaḥ || 77 ||

uparyupari senāyāḥ sañcaraṃstāṃ niśāmayam |
sā senā mahatī dṛṣṭā bhīmā bhīṣaṇanādinī || 78 ||

saṃvartakālasaṃkṣubhamahormijaladhiryathā ||
darśanadeva senāyā yasyanti mṛtimāsurāḥ || 79 ||

p.626)

tatra sarvā mahābhīmāḥ śaktyo bhīmavikramāḥ |
ahampūrvikayā yaddhaṃ kartumādau samutsukāḥ || 80 ||

tatra bālā kumārī yā lalitāyāḥ priyā'tmajā |
kumāribhiḥ koṭiśatasaṃkhyābhiḥ parivāritā || 81 ||

śastrā'strakauśalā'bhijñā yuddhasannāhasambhramā |
bhaṇḍaputrān nihanmīti sā bravīti punaḥ punaḥ || 82 ||

kumāryo'pi catā brūyurbhaṇḍaputrasuvāhinīm |
nāśayāmaḥ kṣaṇenā'tigarjantyaḥ samarotsukāḥ || 83 ||

atha samptkarī cā'śvā'ruḍhākhyā śaktināyikā |
gajānaśvānasaṃkhyātān mahāśaktibhirāsthitān || 84 ||

karṣantyau te balodagre bālāpārśvadvyā'śrite |
atha bālā pṛṣṭhatastu mahārathanivāsinī || 85 ||

lalitā śrīmahādevī navā'varaṇaśaktibhiḥ |
aprameyabalakrauryaśaurthayuktābhirāvṛtā || 86 ||

tasyāḥ pārśvadvaye mantranāthā daṇḍadharā'sthitā |
pratyekaṃ rathamāruḍhā mahā'dbhutaparākramā || 87 ||

anekakoṭimātaṅgakanyābhiḥ svasamā'tmabhiḥ |
citravīryābhirabhitaḥ saṃvṛtāmantriṇī sthitā || 88 ||

vāarāhyapi mahābhīmavikarā'kṛtināmabhiḥ |
śaktibhirbaṭukādyaśca saṃyutā'tiruṣānvitā || 89 ||

p.627)

sarvatra śaktisenāsu na mayā kvā'pi lakṣitam |
yatra te vadhavṛttāntasaṅgatirna hi saṃstutā || 90 ||

tatraikā'pyavara śaktiḥ sarvamasmadbalaṃ kṣaṇāt |
nāśayediti me buddhirvyavasāyaṃ samāsthitā || 91 ||

śatakoṭiparārdhānāṃ kodyarbudaśatatmikā |
senaivaṃ śaktinicitā daityarāja ! vadāmi te || 92 ||

śaktiranyamatā teṣu bale vīrye ca vikrame |
sarvān daityagaṇānasmānatitiṣṭhati sarvathā || 93 ||

ityuktvā daityarājaṃ taṃ praṇipatya kṛtāñjaliḥ |
vidyunmālī samabhavattūṣṇī daityasabhāgataḥ || 94 ||

iti śrīmaditihāsottame śrītripurāhasye lalitāmāhātmye 
bhaṇḍadūtasyavidyunmālinaścāravākyaṃ nāmaikaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 5077 
||

p.628)

dviṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ
rākṣasarājāya rākṣasaguptacareṇāmitradhnena yathāyathaṃ 
devīsannāhavarṇanam  

atha kṣaṇādamitraghnaḥ prāpto daityasabhāntaram |
bhaṇḍāsurā'ṅghyrorapatad bhayavihvalitāntaram || 1 ||

āśavastaḥ prāha sacivaiḥ pṛṣṭo vṛttaṃ pravepitaḥ |
mahārāja tvadājñapto gataḥ śaktimahācamūm || 2 ||

praviṣṭaḥ śaktibhistābhirgṛhīto balavatkṣaṇāt |
na me vīryaṃ balaṃ vāpi babhau śaktyagrataḥ kvacit || 3 ||

raviprabhānimagnasya khadyotasyeva suprabhā |
kṣanena tāsāṃ grahaṇādvisaṃjño'bhavamañjasā || 4 ||

atha nīto daṇḍanāthāsavidhaṃ mṛtavat sthitaḥ |
sā kulādririvonnamrā kroḍāsyā kāñcanaprabhā || 5 ||

krodhāgnimudgirantīva sunetrairghargharāravā |
tādṛgvidhaṃ śaktigaṇaṃ tasyāḥ krodhapradīpitam || 6 ||

dṛ.ṣtvā'hamabhavaṃ bhūyaḥ śvasituñcā'pi no vibhuḥ |
tasyā niyogānnito'haṃ śaktibhirmantriṇīpuraḥ || 7 ||

p.629)

sā dṛṣṭā mantriṇī devi tāpicchadalasannibhā |
saundaryakulasāraṅgī pūrṇatāruṇyagarvitā || 8 ||

mandasmitamukhī pānamadamādyadvilāsinī |
gītahāsyaparā nityaṃ viṇāvādananiṣṭhitā || 9 ||

tasyāḥ prasannanetrābjā'mṛtavarṣā'bhijīvitaḥ |
anvavekṣamahaṃ sarvasaubhāgyaikasamāśrayām || 10 ||

tayā'nuyukto nikhilamavadaṃ tava bhāṣitam |
tayā nīto mahārājñyāḥ samakṣamahamāśvatha || 11 ||

sā dṛṣṭā lalitārājñī mahāvibhavasaṃśrayā |
sandeśaṃ vācayanmahyaṃ yattatte mantriṇīmukhāt || 12 ||

vakṣyāmi śṛṇu daityeśa sāvadhānena cetasā |
brūyāstaṃ bhaṇḍadaityeśaṃ jīvane yadi te matiḥ || 13 ||

trilokīṃ visṛjendrāya śaraṇīkuru mātaram |
nivasa tvaṃ parīvārayutaḥ pātālasadmani || 14 ||

indreṇa saṅgato bhūyāḥ putrā'mātyadisaṃyutaḥ |
akṛtvaivaṃ na te mokṣo lokā'ntaragatasya ca || 15 ||

indraśatrimahaṃ tvāṃ tu (?) sa neṣyāmyātmasaṃśrayam |
iti tvayā tatra gatvā vaktvyo bhaṇdadānavaḥ || 16 ||

tadantare śaktirekā śyāmā turagasaṃśrayā |
karālakaravālodyatkarā tatra samāyayau || 17 ||

p.630)

avarhyā svavāhanāt sā praṇamya lalitā'mbikām |
mantranātāñca vṛtāntaṃ babhāṣe sā'nuyojitā || 18 ||

preṣitā bhaṇḍadaityasya vāhinyāṃ nagare'pi ca |
madbhṛtyāḥ śaktyo gatvā dṛṣṭvā sarvaṃ samāgatāḥ || 19 ||

tābhirvibhāvitaṃ sarvaṃ sainyaṃ nagarameva ca |
iti vṛttaṃ tayā proktaṃ sarvamasmatsamāśrayam || 20 ||

daityānāṃ sainikānāñca sarvaśaḥ | 
na tayā'viditaṃ kiñcinā'sti bāhyā'ntareṣu ca || 22 ||

striyaḥ sarvāḥ suviditāstāsāñcāpi prabhāṣitam |
mantraste mantrito'pyatra nā'smābhirvidito'pi yaḥ || 23 ||

tayā prokto'thaṃ vacanaṃ vṛttañcāpi pṛthak pṛthak |
citraṃ manye cirādatra vasannaviditañca yat || 24 ||

tata sarvañca tayā proktaṃ yadyathā samavasthitam |
yattvayā'pyatra no jñātaṃ tattvayā kathitaṃ nanu || 25 ||

p.631)

manye'haṃ tadbalaṃ devyā ajeyaṃ daityapuṅgavaiḥ |
ekā'pi śaktiravarā sarvanno nāśayet kṣaṇāt || 26 ||

daityeśvarāhaṃ te sainye baliṣvanavaro nanu |
endrādayo'pi vijitā mayā bhūyaḥ samāgame || 27 ||

sao'haṃ praviṣṭamātro'pi śaktyā sāmanyayā nanu |
gṛhīto līlayaivā'śu hariṇā mṛgaśāvavat || 28 ||

yathā bālāḥ krīḍanakamācchidyā'nyakarasthitam |
gṛhaṇantyahaṃ tathā tābhirgṛhīto balavānapi || 29 ||

na me'bhūt sapanditumapi saktiḥ kva nu vimokṣaṇe |
aśakyaṃ sarvathā daityairjetuṃ nāstyatra saṃśayaḥ || 30 ||

ityuktvā virarāmā'tha daityo daityeśvarā'grataḥ |
śrutvetthaṃ daityavacanaṃ bhaṇḍaḥ svā'ntaracintayat || 31 ||

nūnaṃ sā tripureśānī kṛpayā mayi saṃśrite |
prasādamakarodadya cirakālā'bhikāṅkṣitam || 32 ||

yattayā lokamātroktaṃ tadadyā'vitathāyitam |
dhanyo'haṃ tripureśānīṃ dṛṣṭvā yuddhe'nubhāṣya ca || 33 ||

tacchastrāgnimahājvālānirdagdhakaluṣā'ṅgakaḥ |
vrajāmi lallokamamuṃ mahāsukhasamāśrayam || 34 ||

mahāpāpasamudbhūto deho'yaṃ pāpasaṃśrayaḥ |
daityasvabhāvavihitaścirabhārāyito nanu || 35 ||

p.632)

yathā'viṣṭigṛhīto hi bhāraṃ kṣiptvā palāyati |
tathā'haṃ duḥkhadaṃ dehamamuṃbhāraṃ visṛjya tu || 36 ||

palāyiṣye sukhasthānaṃ na me khedo'tra vidyate |
nunaṃ varṣasahasrāṇāṃ niyutairdaha eṣa me || 37 ||

samprāpto'tyantasukhitāmapi me na hi rocate |
ta eva bhogāstadrājyaṃ tāḥ striyastaśca saṅgatam || 38 ||

anvahaṃ sevamānasya sarvaṃ vairasyamāgatam | 
nirviṇṇo'haṃ dṛḍhataramasmāt paryuṣitātmanaḥ || 39 ||

vāñchāmi nāśaṃ dehasyā'mayaṃ dīrghā'mayī yathā |
parantu me virahitāḥ śociṣyanti priyā eme || 40 ||

tathā tairapyahaṃ hīno na vindāmi kṣaṇaṃ sukham |
ādau vā'nantaraṃ vā'pi duḥkhadaia mṛtirbhavet || 41 ||

tadetadviṣamaṃ bhāti kathaṃ nār'tirbhavenmama |
evaṃ me tripurā devī kṛpāṃ kuryādabhīpsitām || 42 ||

yugapadyadi no hanyāt kṣemaṃ me pratibhāti tat |
bhaktavāñchāsamadhikaphaladā sā nisargataḥ || 43 ||

kuto na pūrayediṣṭaṃ sā nijāṅghyrājrayasya me |
itthaṃ vicintya manasā namaskṛtya parāmbikām || 44 ||



p.633)

nāṭye naṭa iva svairaṃ krodhamahārayat param |
dhigastvadham daiteya ! katha mama samakṣataḥ || 45 ||

apriyāṃ parasaṃślaghāṃ kurvannapi na lajjase |
parasaṃślāghanaṃ yattalloke kāpuruṣabratam || 46 ||

parantu balinaṃ jñātvā na śūraḥ ślāghate kvacit |
balīvāpyabalo vā'pi śūrāstatra na bibhyati || 47 ||

ityuktvā khaḍagamudyamya kruddhaḥ kālamahā'hivat |
vamanniva netramukhāt krodhakākolapāvakam || 48 ||

niryayau nagaradvārānmahāvilamukhādiva |
paśyantu daityā ye bhītāḥ tasyāmeti parākramam || 49 ||

adya māṃ samare prāpya punaḥ sā na bhaviṣyati |
vadantameva daityeśaṃ sūtaḥ sarathamagrataḥ || 50 ||

abhyājagāmā'tha so'pi rathamāruhya saṃyayau |
taṃ menire janāḥ kālaṃ jagatsaṃharaṇodyatam || 51 ||

nā'sya krodhāgnimāsādya jagadbhūyo bhaviṣyati |
ityūcuḥ sahasā sarve bhītāśacā'surapuṅgavāḥ || 52 ||

niryayuḥ svasvasenābhiḥ senā'dhyakṣāśca mantriṇaḥ |
putrāḥ pautrāśca bhrātaraḥ sarvaḥ eva te || 53 ||

bhītā niḥśeṣataḥ senāṃ samādāya viniryayuḥ |
athā'jñpto viśukreṇa kuṭilākṣaśca bhūpatiḥ || 54 ||

p.634)

akṣauhiṇīpañcakena cakre nagararakṣaṇam |
tannivedya viśukrāya vikarṣanmahaīṃ camūm || 55 ||

yayau bhaṇḍāsurasyā'gre grasanniva kakuptaṭam |
evaṃ pracalite daityarāje nikhilasenayā || 56 ||

avādayaṃstu daiteyā raṇabherīḥ samantataḥ |
paṭahā'nakabherīṇāṃ vādyairddaityavigarjanaiḥ || 57 ||

āsphoṭairnemighoṣaiśca gajacītkāramiśritaiḥ |
vandimāgadhasannādairhayadneṣitavṛṃhitaiḥ || 58 ||

turaṅgakhuraviks.eponmiṣaścaṭacaṭāravaiḥ |
huṅkṛtā'hvānasaṃrāvaiḥ saṃhato hi mahāsvanaḥ || 59 ||

babhau jagatpaṭībhedapragalbha iva pūritaḥ |
senāsvanaṃ samākarṇya bhaṇḍadaityasamāgamam || 60 ||

viditvā cāramukhato niścitya mahadudyamam |
daṇḍinī tvaritā mantranāthāṃ prāpya vyajijñapat || 61 ||

saciveśyeṣa samprāpto bhaṇḍaḥ senāsamanvitaḥ |
mahāsannāhasambhāro yuddhaṃ mahadupasthitam || 62 ||

vijñāpya lalitādevīṃ sametā śaktibhirvṛtā |
saṃrakṣantī śaktigaṇairmahārājñyā anuvraja || 63 ||

ahaṃ yāsye tanniroddhuṃ purataḥ senayā'vṛtā |
eṣa śabdaḥ śrūyate vai gajavājisamākulaḥ || 64 ||

p.635)

manye bālā sampravṛttā yuddhe yuddhasamutsukā |
sampannāthā'śvanāthābhyāṃ yutā citraparākramā || 65 ||

evaṃ vadantyāṃ tasyāntu prāptā sandeśahāriṇī |
vardhayitvā daṇḍanāthāṃmantriṇīñcā'pyabhāṣata || 66 ||

devi pravṛttaṃ sumahadyuddhaṃ bālāmbayā saha |
daityarājasya sannāho mahān yuddhasamudyamaḥ || 67 ||

brātṛputrādisahitasarvasenāsamāvṛtaḥ |
samāgataḥ krurataro yudyatikrūravikramaḥ || 68 ||

iti vijñāpanāyā'haṃ sampatkaryā nirupitā |
kiṃ vadāmi punargatvā tāṃ devīṃ gajavāhinīm || 69 ||

śrutvā sandeśahāriṇyā vākyāṃ śrīdaṇḍanāyikā |
prāha tatkālasadṛśaṃ vākyaṃ vikramabṛṃhitam || 70 ||

gacchebhasenānetrīṃ tāṃ brūhi śrutvā madīritam |
apramattatayā nūnaṃyoddhavyaṃ daityapuṅgavaiḥ || 71 ||

bālā kumārikā nityaṃ yuddhagoṣṭhīsamutsukā |
sarvataḥ sā rakṣitavyā prāptā'smyahamapi drutam || 72 ||

yathā palāyya no gacchenmāyayā daityapuṅgavaḥ |
tathā saṃrudhya yoddhavyaṃ yāvanmama samāgamaḥ || 73 ||

tato'haṃ tasya na ciraṃ yaddhaśraddhāṃcirantanīm |
nāśayāmi kṣaṇenaiva tamaḥ sūryodaye yathā || 74 ||

p.636)

gaccha śīghraṃ samādāya sandeśaṃ gajavāhinīm |
ājñaptaiva nirgatā sā śaktiḥ sandeśahāriṇī || 75 ||

tataḥ kirirathāruḍhā daṇḍiṇī niryayau drutam |
asaṅkhyaśaktisenābhiryutā krodhasamākulā || 76 ||

nisargakrodhanā bhūyo yuddhopakramaroṣitā |
didhakṣantīva lokāṃśca sarvān roṣāgninā kṣaṇāt || 77 ||

rathanemimahāsvanayutaḥ senāmahāravaḥ |
pareṣāṃ hṛdayaṃ bhindan śrotramārgeṇa saṃviśan || 78 ||

pratisvānamahāghoṣaḥ proccaladbhīṣaṇā'kṛtiḥ |
evaṃ śrīdaṇḍasāmrājñī jaitrayātrāmupā'kramat || 79 ||

iti śrīmaditihāsottame tripurārahasye śrīlalitāmāhātmye bhagavatyāstanūjayā 
bālayā yuddhodyogavarṇanaṃ nāma dviṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 5156 ||

p.637)

triṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ 
bālāyāḥ samaraparākramavarṇanam  

bhaṇḍāsuramahāsenākolāhalamahādhvanim |
niśmya praikṣadatyantayuddhotsukatayā diśam || 1 ||

prāgudīcīṃ kumārī sā lalitāyā manaḥ priyā |
dadarśa daityasenāyāḥ sammardajanitaṃ rajaḥ || 2 ||

pratisañcarasambhūtasāṃvartakasamūhavat |
jñātvā prāptaṃ daityarājaṃ sarvasenāsamāvṛtam || 3 ||

samarotsukacittā sā bālā tripurasundarī |
laghucakrarathā'ruḍhā rathanetrīmacodayat || 4 ||

śrīmātuḥ pratirodhaṃ sā tarkayantyatha saṃyuge |
adṛṣṭvaiva mahādevīṃ mantriṇīmabhisaṃyayau || 5 ||

senānetrīñca senāñcā'pyākāṅkṣantyatisatvaram |
daityasenā'bhimukhato vāyuvegena saṃyayau || 6 ||

śaradīva mahāmegha ākāśe vāyuneritaḥ |
yathā puro nipatati tathā vegādratho yayau | 7 ||

p.638)

bālāyā yuddhayātrāṃ tāṃ viṣamāṃ vīkṣya satvaram |
aśvāruḍhā drutataraṃ tāmanu pratisaṃyayau || 8 ||

turaṅgaistuṅgabhaṅgābhaiḥ payodhiriva taścamūḥ |
upādravajjagajjvālāplāvanāyeva sarvataḥ || 9 ||

sampatkarī ca tatpaścādraṇakolāhalasthitā |
anekakoṭimātaṅgasenāsaṃvalitā drutam || 10 ||

vegādanusasāroścairbālārakṣaṇahetave |
noditā śīghrasaṃyāne rathavidyāviśāradā || 11 ||

bālāyāḥ sadṛśā'kārā vāhayat syandanaṃ javāt |
prāvṛṇnadīva jaladhiṃdaityasenāṃ vyavagāhata || 12 ||

sajyaṃ dhanurvikarṣantī varṣantī śaravarṣaṇam |
nadīvegavibhinne'bdhau nauriva syandanantu tat || 13 ||

viveśa daityarājasya śaraughadalitāṃ camūm |
kuṭilākṣo daityasenāmukhe kuñjarasaṃsthitaḥ || 14 ||

mahatyā senayā bālāṃ saṃrurodha mahābalī |
daityasenāpatiḥ so'pi dṛṣṭvā bālāṃ kumārikām || 15 ||

sṛjantīṃ śaravarṣāṇi mene'dbhutaparākramam |
ekākinīṃ rathāruḍhāṃ mṛdvaṅgīmaṣṭahāyanām || 16 ||

p.639)

aho citratamaṃ hyetat keyamekā kumārikā |
nirbhayā yodhayatyasmānindrādīnāñcatrāsanān || 17 ||

manye'hṃ lalitāmenāṃ nimittaiścārasaṃstutaiḥ |
lakṣaye sarvacihnāni kintveṣā taruṇī na ca || 18 ||

apyeṣā yasya tanayā bhavet pūrvaṃ hi yā śrutā |
kathaṃ tattanayā bālā senāvirahitā punaḥ || 19 ||

ekalā yuddhasannaddhā samāgacchenmṛdhe dvacit |
nūnameṣaiva sarvānno jeṣyatīva vibhāti me || 20 ||

lakṣayenayudhi sthātuṃ sammukhe'syā bhavet prabhuḥ |
viśukro vā viṣaṅgo vā rājā vā'ṣyahameva vā || 21 ||

kuto rājakumārādyāḥ pratyekaṃ militāśca vā |
asmatsenā yatra nirīkṣati tatastataḥ || 22 ||

śarajālena śithilāṃ karoti nimiṣā'rdhataḥ |
koṭiśo nihatā daityāstrāsitā'smanmahācamuḥ || 23 ||

ete vibhinnavāhvṅghihṛdayakroḍamastakāḥ |
patitāśca mṛtāścā'nye dhāvantyanye mahābhayāt || 24 ||

śoṇitapravahāṃtartumaśaktā bhayakampitāḥ |
patitāstatra śataśo nimagnā raktasindhuṣu || 25 ||

p.540) 

 prāptaiṣā'smaścamūṃbhittvā praviśet kṣaṇamātrataḥ |
roddhavyeyaṃ mayā yatnāddhiṅmāmevaṃvidhaṃsthitam || 26 ||

yosidbālā matsamakṣaṃ bhittvā senāṃ pravekṣyati |
naitadindreṇa marutā vāyunā'pi kṛtaṃ purā || 27 ||

viṣamaṃ me hyetadeṣā jeyeti sarvathā |
iti niścatya tāṃ roddhuṃ mahāgajasamāsthitaḥ || 28 ||

sasāra puratastasyāḥ śarajālānyavākirat |
yuyodhā'tibalo daityaḥ kuṣtilākṣastayā saha || 29 ||

bālayā yuddhakuśalo māyāśastrā'strakovidaḥ |
vavarṣa śaravarṣeṇa bālāṃ yuddhe'grataḥsthitām || 30 ||

udyantaṃ bhāskaramiva himasaṃhativarṣavat |
sā'pi bālā'ruṇā'bhāṅgī śaratīkṣṇatarāṃ'śubhiḥ || 31 ||

anayannāśamatyantaṃ taṃ varṣaṃ nimiṣār'dhataḥ |
ardayāmāsa daityasya senāṃ niśitasāyakaiḥ || 32 ||

atilāghavatastasyāḥ śarasandhānamokṣaṇam |
na veda kaścitkuśalo'pyatisūkṣamadṛśā'pi ca || 33 ||

apa"yan śarasaṅghātadhārāṃ cāpavinirgatām |
prāvṛṅgirimukhāttoyādhāramiva nirantarām || 34 ||

vidhūya tāṃ mahāsenāṃ muhūrtā'ṣṭamabhāgataḥ |
kuṭilākṣaṃ samāsādya jaghān niśitaiḥ śaraiḥ || 35 ||

p.641) 

yuyodha so'pi balavān sarvaprāṇena saṃyuge |
tasya śastrā'srajālāni pratiśastrā'stravarṣataḥ || 36 ||

moghaṃ kṛtvā śaraistaīkṣaṇaiḥ prāharaddaityanāyakam |
ekena tīkṣṇabhallena cāpaṃ tasyā'cchinajjavāt || 37 ||

tataścaikena vāhasya gajasya prāharanmukhe |
iṣuṇā tāḍito vaktre girirājasamo gajaḥ || 38 ||

cītkurvan vibhran bhūmau gatāsurapatat kṣaṇāt |
mariṣyantamibhaṃ jñātvā kuṭilākṣo gadādharaḥ || 39 ||

krodhenā'bhyadravaddhantuṃ bālāṃ rathasamāśrayām |
grasantamaruṇaṃ bālaṃ rāhuṃ drutagatiṃ yathā | 40 ||

apaśyan vibudhāḥ sidhā nabhomaṇḍalasaṃśrayāḥ |
āyāntamativegena gadāhastaṃ niśāmya sā || 41 ||

śareṇā'karṇapūrṇena vivyādha hṛdayā'ntare |
sa viddhastīkṣṇabāṇena bahuśoṇitamudgiran || 42 ||

papāta mūrcchito bhūmau vajrā'hatamahīghravat |
atha bālā prāha sakhīṃ rathanetrīṃ smitā'nanā || 43 ||

sakhi śīghraṃ naya rathaṃ bhaṇḍadaityasamīpataḥ |
āyātyeṣā pṛṣṭhato me daṇḍinī śūkaradhvajā || 44 ||

utkaṇṭhitā'smi daityena yoddhuṃ bhaṇdena saṅgare |
māmāsādya daṇḍanāthā pratirotsyati vai dhruvam || 45 ||

yathā nā'sādayenmāṃ sā tathā vāhaya satvaram |
ityuktā rathanetrī sā bālāṃ proce sakhī tadā || 46 ||

p.642)

daityasenāṃ madhyatastu dhvajo yo'yaṃ mahaonnataḥ |
dṛśyate kāñcanamayo rathastasyaiṣa eva hi || 47 ||

ito dūratare sa syānmahadbhirdaityapuṅgavaiḥ |
mahārathibhiratyugraḥ saṃvṛto nanu dṛśyate || 48 ||

vivarantu na paśyāmi rathaṃ yena nayāmi tat |
śastreṇa sṛja mārgaṃ tvaṃ vāhayāmi tato ratham || 49 ||

ityuktā laghuhastā sā bālā mārgamakalpayat |
śaravṛṣṭyā nimeṣeṇa daityasenāsu madhyataḥ || 50 ||

sāyadaughapravāheṇa bhagnaseturiva'babhau |
senāmukhaṃ mahāśūrairdaityairuddhamapi kṣaṇāt || 51 ||

kecidbhagnarathāścā'nye naṣṭā'"śvagajavāhanāḥ ||
ashyāṃ śaradhārāṃ tāṃ prāpya daityāḥ pradudruvuḥ || 52 ||

apare chinnabāhvakṣicaraṇodaramastakāḥ |
tathā'nye śastrasaṅghātapātena tilaśaḥ kṛtāḥ || 53 ||

bavuḥ śoṇitavāhinyo bhīruṇāṃ sāgaropamāḥ |
suphenilāstaraṅgaḍhyā uṣṇokakavahā eva || 54 ||

śarajālamahāmeghacchannasūryakarā diśaḥ |
andhībhūtā na viditā daityairvidrāvatatparaiḥ || 55 ||

menire te mahāmṛtyuṃ prāptaṃ bālāsvarūpataḥ |
patanti saṅghaśastatra daityānāṃ mastakānyalam || 56 ||

p.643)

tṛṇarājaphalānīva pakvāni prauḍhavāyunā |
hā tāta putra susakhe bhrātarmitra hato'smyaham || 57 ||

iti ghoṣo mahānāsīddaityānāṃ raṇasaṅkaṭe |
tadantare mukhe bhagne senāyāstasya vegataḥ || 58 ||

praveśayadrathavaraṃ rathanetrī nimeṣataḥ |
viśukradaityo'varajo bhaṇḍadaityasya saṃyuge || 59 ||

dṛṣṭvā bhttvā svasenāyā mukhaṃ bālāṃ samāgatām |
gacchantīṃ daitarājena yoddhumutsukitāṃ tadā || 60 ||

jñātvā vicārayannūnameṣā bālā'ṣṭahāyanā |
niḥsīmabalavīryā'ḍhyā yudhyatyativicitritam || 61 ||

naiṣā jeyā'suraḥkaiścidbalavadbhirapi kvacit |
tathā'pi samare śūrairyoddhavyaṃ sarvathā nanu || 62 ||

eṣaiva nāśayet sarvān daityānasmānna saṃśayaḥ | 
kiṃ kariṣyati sā devī lalitā samare punaḥ || 63 ||

sarvaprāṇena yoddhavyaṃ daivamatra parāyaṇam |
iti niścitya nāgendramairāvatakulodbhavam || 64 ||

śvetaṃ caturdantayutaṃ rajatā'drisamaprabham |
viśukra āruhya javāt śarān dhārādharo yathā ||| 65 |

varṣan vireje kailāsā'ruḍhanīlāmbudopamaḥ |
rurodha bālāṃ rathagāṃ praviśya daityavāhinīm || 66 ||

p.644)

samāyāntīṃ javenā'jau cinvatīṃ daityajīvitam |
saṃrudhya śaravarṣeṇa shiṃhanādamatha'karot || 67 ||

āhvānena daityasenāṃ vidrutāṃ sa nyavartata |
daityā naitatsamaṃ yuddhe kulajānāṃ palāyanam || 68 ||

bhītyā yudde vidrutantu dharmaścar'thaḥ prahāsyati |
āhārya vīryaṃ krodhañca nivartadhvaṃ samantataḥ || 69 ||

vinirjitāḥ striyā yuddhe palāyya sadanaṃ gatāḥ |
kiṃ vakṣyatha gṛhe stribhiḥ pṛṣṭā yudhānnivartane || 70 ||

mṛtireva tataḥ śreyaḥ strīkrṭādapamānataḥ |
viśukravacanaṃ śrutvā parāvṛttastatastu te || 71 ||

āgatya bālāṃ tripurāṃ paribavruḥ samantataḥ |
daityasenābhirākrāntāḥ sā bālā rathasaṃsthitā || 72 ||

pariveṣitabālārka iva tatra vyarājata |
atha tāṃ rathanetrīṃ sā sakhī prāha priyaṃvadā || 73 ||

kumāri ! manye daityānāṃ senābhāro'tiduḥsahaḥ |
na te pārṣṇigrahā kāciścakragoptryadhividyate || 74 ||

agre senābharo bhūyān manyase cedahaṃ drutam |
rathaṃ nivartayāmyeṣā senāyuktā punardrutam || 75 ||

āgatya caitān daityendrān yathechaṃ yodhayiṣyasi |
srutvā sakhīvacaḥ prāha prahasya mṛdubhāṣiṇī || 76 ||

bhītā'si kiṃ priye yuddhe naivaṃ śataguṇā api |
paryāptā me daityasenā yudhyantyā yudhi me dvacit || 77 ||

p.645)

līlayā'haṃ vilambena yudhyāmi raṇakautukī |
manyase cedimaṃ paśya kṣaṇaṃ nirdaityalokakam || 78 ||

ityuktā sā punaḥ prāha sakhī rathavahā tadā |
devi nāstyeva me bhītiḥ sainye'pītaḥ "śatottare || 79 ||

tathā.pk rathanāthāyā jñeyaṃ cittaṃ raṇodyame |
śaṅke śrīlitādevyā yadājñāmantarā tvayā || 80 ||

yuddhe vinirgatā cā'smi etāvat sādhvasaṃ mama |
brūhi śīghraṃ rathamahaṃ kvanayāmi raṇā'vanau || 81 ||

iti śrīmaditihāsottame śrītripurāhasye māhātmyakhaṇḍe lalitācaritre 
devīśaktigaṇadaityasainyayuddhodyame bālāparākramavarṇanaṃ nāma 
triṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 5237 ||

p.646)

sakhīvaco niśamyā'tha bālā saṃhṛṣṭamānasā |
prāha śighraṃ naya rathaṃ yatrā'sau gajasaṃśrayaḥ || 1 ||
stūyamāno bandigaṇairviśukrākhyo mahāsuraḥ |
ityājñptā kṣaṇādeva nināya syandanottamam || 2 ||

bālā'pi daityasenāṃ tāmaparāṃ sāgaraprabhām |
śarasantatidhārābhiścakre'tiśithilāṃ tadā || 3 ||

nāśayat kuliśāstreṇa bhasmaśeṣatvamānayat |
hatāstatra mahādaityāḥ kuliśā'streṇa sarvataḥ || 5 ||

bhasmaśeṣīkṛtāḥ kecit keciddhatavarā'gakāḥ |
ekabāhvakṣicaraṇapārśsvībhūtāstathā pare || 6 ||

mahākrandabhīmatvāṃ naṣtaprayāṃ camūṃ nijāam |
niśāmya prāhiṇonnāgamairāvatakulodbhavam || 7 ||

p.647)

yoddhuṃ viśukrastāṃ bālāṃ dṛṣṭvā'dbhutaparākramām |
vavarṣa śaradhārābhirmaṇiśṛṅgamivā'mbudaḥ || 8 ||

prativarṣaṃ vavarṣā'tha pratidaityaṃ śilīmukhaiḥ |
śaravarsaṃ dvayaṃ tattu tamobhūtaṃ raṇā'vanau || 9 ||

tadantare tu niśitaśareṇā'nataparvaṇā |
jaghānā'karṇapūrṇena haste tāṃ rathavāhinīm || 10 ||

sā gāḍhaviddhā bāṇena kiñcitkaśamalamāgamat |
tdadṛṣṭvā roṣatāmrākṣī garjantaṃ daityapuṅgavam || 11 ||

jaghāna tarasā bhāle tīkṣṇabhallacatuṣṭayaiḥ |
bhallārdito gāḍhamūrcchāṃ viśukraḥ prāpatadgataḥ || 12 ||

punaḥ kṣṇena cotthāya vavarṣa niśitānśarān |
atha bālā kṣaṇenaiva laghuhastakriyā'nvitā || 13 ||

dvābhyāṃ cāpañca chatrañca cicheda yugapanmṛdhe |
athaikenā'karṇaśareṇa nataparvaṇā || 14 ||

vivyādha vāhanagajamairāvatakulodbhavam |
indrāyudhaṃ girimiva dadāra sa śaro gajam || 15 ||

kumbhapradeśe jaghanābhyāṃ vinirgataḥ |
saṃchinnaḥ krakaceneva mahāsthāṇurbabhau gajaḥ || 16 ||

p.648)

tādṛgvidhaṃ gajapatiṃ vyasuṃ vīkṣya sa daityarāṭ |
khaḍgahastaḥ khamutpatya tāṃ prahartumupadrutaḥ || 17 ||

āyantamantakanibhaṃ karavālakarodyatam |
lāghavenaiva sā bālā dūrādekena patriṇā || 18 ||

karavālaṃ dvidhā cakre taṃ mūrghni tribhirāhanat |
viśukramūrghni tanmagnaṃ kiñcidbāṇatrayaṃ yadā || 19 ||

vyarājata tadā daityastriśṛṅgo nagarāḍiva |
gāḍhaviddho'pi dhairyeṇa pakṣirāḍiva vegavān || 20 ||

ādāya bālāṃ pāṇibhyāmuccacālanabho'ṅgaṇam |
niśāmya hviyamāṇāṃ tāṃ rathanetryabravīdidadm || 21 ||
alaṃ bāle līlayā te mahā'narthā'vasānayā |
jahi vīryeṇa daiteyaṃ yāvaddūraṃ na gacchati || 22 ||

śrutvā sakhivaco bālā matvā kālasamaṃ vacaḥ |
nirvartya muṣṭiṃ sudṛḍhāṃ pātayattasya mūrdhni || 23 ||

vajrakalpamuṣṭihato bhinnamūrdhā vamannasṛk |
gāḍhamūrchāsamāviṣṭo nipapāta mahītale || 24 ||

atha bālā rathāruḍhā sakhiṃ tāmabhicodayat |
priye śighraṃ naya rathameṣo'gre daityanāyakaḥ || 25 ||

śvetā'tapatrarājībhiścāmarairabhisevitaḥ |
yathā nīlagirirhasairjalanirjharasaṃvṛtaḥ || 26 ||

p.649)

āste yathā rājate'yaṃ naya tatra rathaṃ drutam |
eṣā pṛṣṭhato me daṇḍanāthā camūvṛtā || 27 ||

śrūyate siṃhavāhasya garjato bhairavo ravaḥ |
avaśyaṃ mama yuddhasya vihatiṃ sā kariṣyati || 28 ||

tāvaddrutaṃ yodhayāmi bhaṇḍadaityaṃ mahābalam |
utkaṇṭhitā'smi tadyuddhe sarvathā naya māṃ drutam || 29 ||

śrutvā tadgaditaṃ sa'pi sakhī rathamacodayata |
yāvadbhaṇḍasya puratastāvadrathavarā'śrayaḥ || 30 ||

jñātvā sarvaṃ viṣaṅgo'pi matvā'jeyāṃ surā'suraiḥ |
anayā yuddhyamānasya daityarājasya sarvathā || 31 ||

jīvato na vimokṣo'sti vijitastvanayā yadi |
tadā cirārjitā kīrtirvyarthaṃ hīyeta sarvathā || 32 ||

yadi sā lalitā rājñī vijeṣyati samaṃ hi tat |
tasmādatra yathāśaktyā yatitavyaṃ bhavenmayā || 33 ||

iti niścitya bālāyā nipapātā'grto balī |
āyāntaṃ sammukhaṃ daityaṃ dṛṣṭvā rathavarasthitam || 34 ||

p.650)

bhaṇḍayuddhamahāvighnaṃ matvā prāha sakhīṃ prati |
priye! bhaṇḍena yoddhavye mahān vighno hyupasthitaḥ || 35 ||

paśya me lāghavañcainaṃm vārayāmi sudūrataḥ |
ityuktvā niśitān bāṇāṃścaturo visasarja ha || 36 ||

taistasya bāhā nihatāḥ pronnamrā vātaraṃhasaḥ |
athaikena dhvajaṃ chittvā sūtamekena patriṇā || 37 ||

nijaghānā'tha daiteyo viṣaṅgo'tyantakoṣitaḥ |
moghaṃ sodyogamālakṣya turaṅgā'ntarasaṃśrayaḥ || 37 ||

ādāya paridhaṃ ghoramabhidudrāva vegataḥ |
turageṇa samāyāntaṃ vātanunnā'bhrakhaṇḍavat || 39 ||

vavarṣa sāyakaudhena prāvṛḍabda ivā'calam |
śaravarṣamagaṇayannāyāntamābhimukhyataḥ || 40 ||

dṛṣṭvā bālā drutataraṃ śareṇaikena karṇinā |
akarodbyasumaśva taṃ hatāśvo'th viṣaṅgakaḥ || 41 ||

udyamya parighaṃ ghoramupā'yāntaṃ subhīṣaṇam |
samudramiva garjantaṃ kālāntakayamopamam || 42 ||

supuyṅkhena śareṇā'śu jaghāna balabaddhṛdi |
sapuṅkhena śareṇā'śu jaghāna balāddhṛdi |
sa gāḍhaviddho bāṇena bhramannuṣṇamasṛgvaman || 43 ||

p.651)

mūrcchitaḥ prāpatadbhūmau kṛttapakṣamahīghravat |
tadadbhutaṃ karma dṛṣṭvā daityā devāśca vismitāḥ || 44 ||

aho daityā'dhipo hyeṣaḥ purā śakramukhān surān |
bahudhā hyajayadyuddhe viṣṇutulyaparākramaḥ || 45 ||

sa eṣa bālayā nūnaṃ līlayaiva nipātitaḥ |
sarvān vijeṣyatyeṣaiva lalitā kiṃ kariṣyati || 46 ||

prāhurevamatha punārathanetrī rathaṃ nayat |
bhaṇḍāsurasamakṣaṃ stvaṃ dṛṣtvā bālā mahāsuram || 47 ||

sādhu priyaṃ kṛtaṃ hydya cirā'bhilaṣitaṃ mama |
anena yuddhvā santuṣye cirābhilaṣitantvataḥ || 48 ||

ityuktvā śaravarṣeṇa vavarṣā'surabhūpatim |
atha bhaṇḍāsuro dṛṣṭvā bālāṃ sammukhasaṅgatām || 49 ||

jahṛṣe vāñcchitaṃ prāpya cirāyā'bhimataṃ tadā |
sa kāla eṣa samprāptaḥ śriyoktaściravāñchitaḥ || 50 ||

eṣā dṛṣṭā mahādevī bhaktavāñchitapūraṇī |
nūnameṣaḥ kṣaṇo me'dya ghoradehaviyojakaḥ || 51 ||

anayā nihatastvatra brajāmi samalokatām |
eṣā śrīlalitārājñyāḥ kumārdbhutavikramā || 52 ||

p.652)

tasyā bimbasamudbhūtapratibimbavadāsthitā |
māṃ nihantuṃ sā'pi devi ramāvacanagauravāt || 53 ||

āyāsyati na sandeho ramoktaṃ nā'nyathā bhavet |
bhavatvenāṃ pūjayāmi tadabimbādutthitā'tmikām || 54 ||

kālopapannavidhinā śarabhūtaprasādhanaiḥ |
itthaṃ vicintya saśaraṃ jagrāha sudṛḍhaṃ dhanuḥ || 55 ||

jalā'stramantritaśarua prākṣipattasyāḥ pādayoḥ |
athā'paraṃ pauṣpamantramantritaṃ mūrdhani kṣipat || 56 ||

śaru siṣicatuḥ pādau nirmalaiḥ śītalairjalaiḥ |
śaro'paro mūrghni tasyā mālāvarṣamavā'kirat || 57 ||

tadadbhutaṃ niśāmyā'tha papraccha rathanāyikā |
kumāryetanmahaścitraṃ prapaśyāmyasureśvare || 58 ||

tenaiva vihitaṃ kiṃ vā jātaṃ syāttava tejasā |
tvayi varṣaṃ kirantyāṃ vai śarāṇāmāhavā'vanau || 59 ||

etasya pūjanavidhiḥ śaraiḥ kimiva te bhavet |
ahaṃ saṃśayayuktā'smi tanme dūrikuru drutam || 60 ||

pṛṣṭaivaṃ prāha sā bālā śṛṇu preyasi madvacaḥ |
purā śrutaṃ yallalitā proktamatyantagūhitam || 61 ||

p.653)

eṣa lakṣmyā mahādūto nāmnā māṇikyaśekharaḥ |
śāpena daityatāṃ prāptastripurābhaktaśekharaḥ || 62 ||

eṣa yuddhe vinihataḥ śrīpuraṃ pratipatsyati |
māṃ matvā lalitāputrīṃ pūjayāmāsa sāyakaiḥ || 63 ||

tamīkṣa matpratikṛtimityuktvā śaramutsṛjat |
sa śaraḥ pañcaśākhātmā pasparśa tasya mūrdhani || 64 ||

karāmbujaṃ mastake sve nyastaṃ mene mahāsuraḥ |
prasādamakaroddevī ceti saṃhṛṣito'bhavat || 65 ||

taddṛṣṭvā vismitā'tyantaṃ bālāyā rathanāyikā |
athā'bhavanmahāyuddhaṃ kumārībhaṇḍadaityayoḥ || 66 ||

kṛtapratikṛtopetaṃ ghoraṃ bhīrubhayāvaham |
dvandvabhūtamanupamaṃ dvairathyaṃ śaravarṣaṇam || 67 ||

bhaṇḍāsuro'pi bhaktyaivā'dbhutaḥ svīyaparākramaiḥ |
devīṃ santoṣayāmīti yuyodha balavattaram || 68 ||

vikirantau śastragaṇānmarmapraharaṇodyamau |
jñātvā'nyonyaṃ śastrayogalāghavaṃ ślāghanāparau || 69 ||

p.654)

sādhu śūra raṇaślāghya sādhu devi mahābale |
ityevaṃ yudhyatostatra kumārībhaṇḍadaityayoḥ || 70 ||

prāvartanta mahāstrāṇi lokamohakarāṇi vai |
kuliśā'straṃ pārvatasya nāgā'strasya ca gāruḍam || 71 ||

āgneyasya ca pārjanyaṃ tasya mārutanāmakam |
tasya sārpaṃ tasya punarnākulaṃ tasya caiva hi || 72 ||

vaiḍālaṃ kukkuraṃ tadvadbakaṃ vaiyāghrameva ca |
mahāvyāghraṃ mṛgenadrañca tasya śārabhamastrakam || 73 ||

gaṇḍameruṇḍamityevaṃ tathā yāmyañca vāruṇam |
kauberaṃ naiṛtañcaindraṃ raudraṃ mohanameva ca || 74 ||

painākaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ brāhmamevamastraistu saṅkulam |
yuddhamāsīnmahāghoraṃ kumārībhaṇḍadaityayau || 75 ||

tatra daityāḥ śaktyaśca sahāyārthasamāgatāḥ |
prekṣāñcakrurvicitreṇa yuddhenā'tyantavismitāḥ || 76 ||

evaṃ pravṛtte samare bālāyā lāghavāttu saḥ |
hīyamānaḥ samabhavadbhaṇḍadaityo yadā tadā || 77 ||

viṣaṅgaśca viśukraśca kuṭilākṣaśca bhūpatiḥ |
saṃvṛttā daityasenābhiḥ parivārya kumārikām || 78 ||

yuyudhuḥ śastrasaṅghātairvamadbhirvahnimulvaṇam |
bālāpi tān līlayaiva yodhayāmāsa sarvataḥ || 79 ||

evaṃvidheddhe mahāyu pravṛtte bālayā saha |
samprāptā daṇḍasamrājñī jitvā daityamahācamūm || 80 ||

p.655)

bhittvā senāmahādriṃ taṃ daityānāmatīstṛtam |
musalātmamahāvajrā'yudhena krūravikramā || 81 ||

āsasāda mahāyuddhe bālāṃ yuddharasotsukām |
yudhyantīmekalāṃ daityaiḥ pa"yantī vismitā tadā || 82 ||

iti śrīmaditihāsottame tripurārahasye māhātmyakhaṇḍe śrīlalitācaritre 
bālāparākramavarṇanaṃ nāma catuḥṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 5319 ||

p.556)

pañcaṣaṣtitamo'dhyāyaḥ 
bālāyāḥ samaraparākramavarṇanam  

atha bhaṇḍasya sā senā kolavaktrāparākramāt |
viśīrṇā parvata iva devendrā'yudhagauravāt || 1 ||

senāyāṃ daityarājasya vārāhīmusalā'hateḥ |
viśīrṇāyāmapaśyat sā bālāṃ daityaiḥ susaṅgatām || 2 ||

ekalāṃ yodhayantīṃ tānmahādaityān samantataḥ |
samarotsukitāṃ bhūyo dṛṣṭvā viṣmayamāgamat || 3 ||

atha tāṃ prāha kolāsya samāsādya viśṛṇvatīm |
alaṃ bāle sāhasena yuddhena viṣameṇa te || 4 ||

naitadanyatra paśyāmi sāhasaṃ yattvamekalā|
daityānmahābalān bhīmān saṃyuge jitavatyasi || 5 ||

tvāṃ niśamyaikalāṃ daityairyudhyamānāṃ maheśvarīm |
śrīdevī vatsalā nūnaṃ bhavedvyākulitāntarā || 6 ||

imaṃ kṣaṇaṃ draṣṭumarhasi yāhi tat |
ityuktā'jñāpayacchaktimaśvasenā'dhināyikām || 7 ||

p.657)

aśvāruḍhe prayāhyenāṃ samādāyā'tisatvarā |
mahārājñyeai nivedyaināmāgaccha tvaritaṃ punaḥ || 8 ||

aśvāruḍhaivamājñaptā samarādanivartinīm |
bālāyā rathāaviśya nyavartayata taṃ ratham || 9 ||

athā''gatya mahārājñyai bālāṃ tāṃ vinivedayat |
mātareṣā yuddhabhuvo balātkārānnivartitā || 10 ||

atisāhasacāritrā'dbhutavīryaparākramā |
tvadājñāyā vayaṃ yāvadenāṃ yuddhānnivartitum || 11 ||

prayātāstāvadeṣā tu daityasenāmahārṇavam |
praviṣṭā tatra daityānāṃ koṭayo balāttarāḥ || 12 ||

nipātitā mahāyuddhe bhaṇḍadaityā varodbhavau |
nirjitau bhīmacāritrau tau viśukraviṣaṅgakau || 13 ||

tato bhaṇḍaṃ samādāya jitalokeśvarā'suram |
yuddhvā tenā'pi suciraṃ jitamāyaṃ cakāra vai || 14 ||

kathañcideṣā vārāhyā vikramyā'sāditā bhavet |
ityuktvā niryayau sā'pi yudhāya turaśā'śrayā || 15 ||

p.658)

atha bālāṃ mahārājñī svāṅkamānīya satvaram |
pariṣvajyā'tivātsalyānmūrdhnyu pājighradambikā || 16 ||

vatse ! naivaṃ punaḥ kāryaṃ kvacit sāhasamātrayā |
anuktvaiva gatā yuddhe naitadaupayikaṃ tava || 17 ||

atha prāha kumārī sā lalitāṃ mātaraṃ prati |
eṣā'ruṇitanetrāntā niḥśvasantī punaḥ punaḥ || 18 ||

mātaścirādahaṃ yuddhakrīḍākautukitā'ntarā |
śaṅkamānā tvayā tatra vighnaṃ naitamavedayam || 19 ||

yuddhakrīḍārasabharādavitṛptaiva vāritā |
tāvakyā daṇḍarājñā'haṃ vṛthaiva samarāṅgaṇāt || 20 ||

tvatto'tibhītayā'sādya mānitā raṇasambhrame |
anyathā sā'pi madyuddhaṃ vimadā prāpya vai bhavet || 21 ||

vadantīmiti tāṃ bālāṃ pariṣvajya maheśvarī |
maivaṃ vatse ! punabrūryā ityuktvā sāntvayat parā || 22 ||

atha daṇḍamahārājñī kiricakrasamāśrayā |
yuyodha bhaṇḍadaityena yutena daityasenayā || 23 ||

p.659)

śaktisenā daityasenā yudhyamānā parasparam |
krodhā'veśasamāyuktā praviṣṭā'bhūtparasparam || 24 ||

daityāḥ śaktigaṇān dhnanti musalaprāsatomaraiḥ |
nārācairbhindipālaiśa khaḍgaśūlaparaśvadhaiḥ || 25 ||

śaktyo'pi praharaṇairvividherdaityapuṅgavān |
jaghnuryuddhe mahābhīme marṣitā balavattarāḥ || 26 ||

tatra śaktigaṇāhatacchinnabāhūrunāsikāḥ |
keciddvidhā krṭāḥśastraiḥ kecittu tilaśaḥ krṭāḥ || 27 ||

kecinmahābhāraśastriarniṣpaṣṭā'śeṣadehakāḥ |
krandantaḥ putra mitreti hā bhrātastāta ityapi || 28 ||

evaṃ śktigaṇā'strāgnibhasmaśeṣatvamāgatāḥ |
daityāḥ śiṣṭāḥ suvitrastāḥ palāyanaparāstadā || 29 ||

abhavan sā yuddhabhūmiragamyā'bhavadañjasā |
śoṇitodā vavurnadyaḥ phenilā bhīṣaṇasvanāḥ || 30 ||

pretagomāyukaṅkā'diśavā'hāramahotsavāḥ |
evaṃ vinihataprāyāṃ daityasenāmupadrutām || 31 ||

viśukraḥ sa viṣaṅgo'tha nivāryā'bhyāyayau mṛdhe |
punastatrā'bhavadyuddhaṃ krūraṃ śaktigaṇaiḥsaha || 32 ||

p.660)

māyayā śaktisenāṃ tāṃ viśukro mohayat kṣaṇāt |
sasarja māyayā dhvāntaṃ tatra varṣa śilāmayam || 33 ||

vahnivāyusamopetaṃ tena śaktimahācamūḥ |
vitrāsitā mūrcchitā cā'pyabhavadbhīmasaṛavā || 34 ||

taṃ dṛṣṭvā'bhyāyayau sampatkarī gajasamāśrayā |
viśukreṇa mahāyuddhe saṅgatā'tidrutaṃ babhau || 35 ||

aśvāruḍhā'pi yuyudhe viṣaṅgeṇa mahābalā |
vārāhī bhaṇḍadaityena yuyodhā'tiruṣā'nvitā || 36 ||

raṇakolāhalā'khyānaṃ gajamāruhya veginam |
asaṅkhyagajasainyena yutā sampatkarī parā || 37 ||

viśukraṃ yodhayāmāsa mahāgajasamaśrayam |
vavarṣa śaravarṣeṇa viśukraṃ māyayā yutam || 38 ||

vimāyā'streṇa hatvā tanmāyāmatyantabhītidām |
prativarṣeṇa daityo'pi vavarṣa viśikhātmanā || 39 ||

evaṃ yuddhvā ciraṃ tasya dhanuściccheda madyataḥ |
so'pyanyadādade cāpaṃtañcā'pyeṣā samācchinat || 40 ||

evaṃ dhanuḥśataṃ chīnnaṃ gṛhītantu punaḥ punaḥ |
athā'tikrodhasaṃyukto viśukro'dbhutavikramaḥ || 41 ||

p.661)

bhallena prāharattasyāḥ sampatkaryā mahābhuje |
bhallā'hatabhujāttasyā nyapatattanmahaddhanuḥ || 42 ||

sampatkarī tataḥ kruddhā prāhiṇodvāhanaṃ gajam |
jagarja patitaṃ dṛṣṭvā hastāścāpaṃ mahattaram || 43 ||

prahitasso'pi karirāṅ daityebhamanusaṃyayau |
tayorabhūnmahadyuddhaṃ kariṇoragayoriva || 44 ||

karasaṃveṣṭanaiḥ kumbhasaṅghaṭṭaiḥ pārśvadharṣaṇaiḥ |
dantā'kramairmahāśabdairmuhūrtamatibhīṣaṇam || 45 ||

raṇaṃ kolāhalaścā'tha balavān daityavāraṇam |
ākramya bhūmau vegena nipātya radanadvayam || 46 ||

udare viniveśyordhvamutthāpya prākṣipadruṣā |
evaṃ nikṣiptamātrastu cītkurvan kuñjaradhipaḥ || 47 ||

apatattriḥparikramya vyasuḥ parvataśṛṇgavat |
patantaṃ bāhanebhantamālakṣya sa mahāsuraḥ || 48 ||

utpatyā'ṅkuśamādāya raṇakolāhalaṃ gajam |
prāharat kumbhayormadhye sarvaprāṇena roṣitaḥ || 49 ||

gato'ṅkuśena balavat sa gajo bhinnamastakaḥ |
apā'sarat pañcadhanurmātraṃ cītkārabhairavaḥ || 50 ||

viśukraḥ punarutpatya sṛṇihasto mahāgajam |
tameva vāhanaṃ tasyā sampatkaryā yuyodha ha || 51 ||

p.662)

tasyāḥ karagataṃ cāpamācchidya tvaritaṃ ruṣā |
babhañja tad dvidhā madhye samādāyaikakhaṇḍakam || 52 ||

prāharanmūrghni balavān sampatkaryāḥ suvegtaḥ |
evaṃ hatā sā balavadīṣatkaśmalamāgatā || 53 ||

punarmuṣṭi vinirvartya vajrasārāṃ nyapātayat |
mūrdhni tasya viśukrasya ruṣā sampatakarī balāt || 54 ||

muṣṭipātādbhannimūrdhā vahadrakto'timūrcchitaḥ |
kṛttapakṣaḥ śaila iva patāta vasudhātale || 55 ||

tāvdgajo'pi saṅkruddhaḥ padā'krāntuṃ samudyataḥ |
ālakṣya tadbhaṇḍasutaḥ yudhyamānaḥ samīpataḥ || 56 ||

matvā hataṃ pitṛvyaṃ vai māyayā tamapā'bahat |
evaṃ parājitaḥ sampannāthayā sa mahāsuraḥ || 57 ||

tathā'śvāruḍhayā yuddhyadviṣaṅgo vājivāhanaḥ |
vividhairastraśastraiśca yudhyantaṃ taṃ mahāsuram || 58 ||

aśvā'ruḍhā'śvasaṃsthānā prāharadgadayorasi |
tāḍito gadayā ceṣanmūrcchāṃ prāpya punarjavāt || 59 ||

padāsikāṃ kheṭakañca śatacandramanuttamam |
samādāyā'śvasenāsu vicacārā'ntakopamaḥ || 60 ||

p.663)

paṭṭāsiprāntasaṃchinnāsturagāḥ śaktyo'pi ca |
chinnamūrdhāṅgacaraṇāḥ prapetuḥ saṅghasaṅghaśaḥ || 61 ||

evaṃ śaktirvinighnantamaśvā'ruḍhā'tikopitā |
baddhvā pāśena gadayā sā'śvaṃ hantuṃ samudyatā || 62 ||

atha so'pi mahādaityo matvā svātmaparābhavam |
antardhānaṃ yayau sāśvomāyayā māyināṃ guruḥ || 63 ||

evaṃ palāyito yuddhe viṣaṅgo vājināthayā |
daṇḍanāthā ca bhaṇḍena yuyodha balasambhṛtā || 64 ||

vihatyā'strāṇi cā'straughaiḥ śastraiḥ śastrāṇi sarvaśaḥ |
parākrameṇā'kramata bhaṇḍāṃ kolamukhī yudhi || 65 ||

khaḍagayuddhe gadāyuddhe dhanuḥparaśuyuddhayoḥ |
muṣṭiyuddhe ca daityeśamatyaricyata daṇḍinī || 66 ||

evaṃ parājitaḥ śastrayuddheṣu vimṛśaṃstataḥ |
māyāṃ prāduścakāroścairmāyināṃ śekharo'suraḥ || 67 ||

andhakāraṃ mahāvāyuṃ vidyudvarṣaṃ mahāravam |
śilāvarṣaṃsarpavarṣañca pāvakam || 68 ||

sārameyān vṛkān vyāghrāṃstarakṣūn rākṣasānapi |
siṃhāṃśca śarabhānāṇḍabheruṇḍānasṛjadyudhi || 69 ||

evaṃ tena mahāmāyāṃ sṛjyamānāṃ punaḥ punaḥ |
pratikriyā'strasandhānairnāśayāmāsa sarvataḥ || 70 ||

p.664)

atha daṇḍamahārajñīśastratejobhirarditaḥ |
nabhasyantarhito bhūtvā yuyodha saratho'suraḥ || 71 ||

prāduścakārā'śmavarṣamatibhīmaṃ mahāravam |
śastrā'śanigaṇopetaṃ śaktisenābhayaṃkaram || 72 ||

tena varṣeṇa mahatā pīḍitā śaktivāhini |
bhinnasetudakamiva viluptā'bhūt samantataḥ || 73 ||

dṛṭ.vā tadakramaṃ daṇḍarājñī caṇḍaprakopanā |
utpapātā'tivegena sāyudhā nabhaso'ṅgaṇam || 74 ||

varṣaṃ patantaṃ tadāviśya śilāmayamatidrutam |
mṛgayāmāsa daityeśaṃ nabhasyandhatamovṛte || 75 ||

nijā'ṅgakāntyā kurvantī nabhodeśaṃ subhāsvaram |
apahatya mahāmāyāmāsasāda mahāsuram || 76 ||

rathasaṃsthaṃ śailanibhaṃ sṛjantaṃ śailavarṣaṇam |
gadayā'bhyāhananmūrghni vajrā'hata ivā'drirāṭa || 77 ||

papāta mūrcchito bhūmau strotaḥ kṛtataṭo yathā |
punaḥ sā gadayaivā'śu rathamaśvāṃśca sārathim || 78 ||

vināśayaddaṇḍanāthā krodhena mahatā'vṛtā |
tāvanmūrcchāvinirmukto gadāhasto hāsuraḥ || 79 ||

utpapāta nabho vegādyoddhuṃ kirinibhā'nanām |
athā'bhavanmahāyuddhaṃ vārāhīdaityarājayoḥ || 80 ||

p.665)

gadāprahāratumulaṃ vjrapātamahāravam |
muhūrtamātraṃ yuddhvaivaṃ balotasiktaṃ mahāsuram || 81 ||

matvā cakarṣa sīreṇa musalodyanmahābhujā |
tāvanmatvā nijātmānaṃ hataṃ musalaghātataḥ || 82 ||

cintayadbhaṇḍadaityeśaḥ purā lakṣmīprabhāṣitam |
nūnaṃ proktaṃ ramādevyā yat purā na tadanyathā || 83 ||

bahveta musalaghātenā'moghena syāṃ kathaṃ hataḥ |
tripurā sā mahādevī bhaktavāñchitapūraṇī || 84 ||

māṃ hanyāt samare sākṣādetanme'bhimataṃ cirāt |
daitye vicintayatyevamabhavannābhasaṃ vacaḥ || 85 ||

daṇḍanāthe ! naiṣa vadhyastava yuddhe kathañcana |
amogho musalaste'yaṃ sannivarya vai drutam || 86 ||

śrutvetthaṃ nābhasavacaścaṇḍikā daṇḍanāyikā |
mocayattaṃ sīragarbhānmatvā'vadhyaṃ sureśvaram || 87 ||

atha so'ntarhito daityonirjito daṇḍanāthayā |
daityasenāśaktigaṇairgāḍhaviddhā palāyitā || 88 ||

dṛṣṭvā bhagnāṃ daityasenāṃ jayabherīmavādayat |
śaktyo vividhāṃścāpi vādyān jayavidhau matān |
89 ||

atha drutaṃ preṣayitvā jayā'khyā nāthadaṇḍinī |
lalitāyai śaktisenāṃ samavāpyā'bhitaḥ sthitām || 90 ||

p.666)

kṣatā hatāḥ śaktyo yā amṛteśī ca tā drutam |
ajīvayadvirujayat tato hṛṣṭāñca tāṃ camūm || 91 ||

saṃnahya svarathā'ruḍhā sampaddevyā'nusaṅgatā |
puro'vrajadaśvanāthā senayā daṇḍanāyikā || 92 ||

draṣṭuṃ tāṃ lalitādevīṃ yayau drutataraṃ mudā |
gajavāditraśabdaughaiḥ khyāpayantī svakaṃ jayam || 93 ||

iti śrīmaditihāsottame tripurārahasye māhātmyakhaṇḍe lalitācaritre 
bhaṇḍāsuraparābhavo nāma pañcaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 5412 ||

p.667)

ṣaṭṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ
sampatkaryaśvāruḍhābhyāṃ durmadavadhavarṇanam 

atha gatvā daṇḍanāthā śaktibhirvividhairyutā |
āsādya mantriṇīṃ devīṃ praṇamya jayamādiśat || 1 ||

sā'pi prītā pariṣvaṅgaṃ tasyai mānena saṃdadau |
atha gatvā tayā sārdhaṃ mahārājñīṃ praṇamya ca || 2 ||

jayavṛttaṃ samācakhyau śakticakrapraharṣadam |
bhaṇḍāsuro'pi daityānāṃsenayā naṣṭaśeṣayā || 3 ||

yayau bahiḥkhinnataya hṛṣṭaścā'ntarabhīpsitāt |
śūnyakaṃ nagaraṃ prāpya jagāmā'ntaḥpuraṃ svakam || 4 ||

tataḥ priyāḥ samametya bhaṇḍaṃ sammohinīmukhāḥ |
brūyurnātha kimadya tvāṃ paśyāmaḥ kṛpaṇaṃ yathā || 5 ||

nāradena puroktaṃ yattadanyathayitaṃ katham |
prāha patnivacaḥ śrutvā jñātvā svā'bhimataṃ śubham || 6 ||

nāradoktyā'bhividitamābhiḥ sarvātmanetyatha |
abhavat prakaṭstāsu priyāḥ śṛṇuta me vacaḥ || 7 ||

p.668)

kathamiṣṭārthalābheṣu khinnaḥ syāmatimugdhavat |
viditaṃ te nāradokteḥ sarvaṃ me samabhīpsitam || 8 ||

acirādeva tallokaṃ prāpsyāmi parayā hataḥ |
kintu daityeṣu svarūpaṃ nigūhitumiti sthitiḥ || 9 ||

atha prāhuḥ punardārā nāthanrūmo'bhivāñchitam |
yadi te niścyo yuddhe hatā yāsyāmaḥ tatsthitam || 10 ||

iti cedvaitvadekāntā nayā'smān sahabhāvataḥ |
tatte mārgaḥ pravidito vadā'smākaṃ kathaṃ gatiḥ || 11 ||

bhaktā hātumayogyāste vayaṃ chāyeva saṅgatāḥ |
śrutvā proktaṃ priyābhistat prāha harṣasunirbharaḥ || 12 ||

śṛṇudhvaṃ madvacastathayaṃ mayetaścintitaṃ puraḥ |
na bhavedanyathā hyetat sarvathā kāranam bruve || 13 ||

ramādevyāḥ prasādena smarāmi prāka samudbhavam |
nūnaṃ sā me'brabīdevaṃ tripurā'smatsamāśrayā || 14 ||

saṃśritānāṃ subhaktānāṃ vāñchitaṃ sā hi sarvathā |
diśatyasādhyamapi ca tato naḥ syāt samīhitam || 15 ||

mayā'bhivāñchitaṃ yāmi bhavatībhiḥ saheti vai |
tallokaṃ tadbhavedevaṃ sarvathā 'pi na saṃśayaḥ || 16 ||

p.669)

atha'pi me samīhā'nyā cā'sti (?) putrādibhiḥ saha |
gantavyamiti tatrāpi bhavedeva tathā 'pyaham || 17 ||

cintayāmyakrameṇaiva bhavettasyāḥ kṛpāvaśāt |
nūnaṃ loke suhṛdbandhuvaidhuryaṃ jātu vai kṣaṇam || 18 ||

soḍhuṃ na yogyaṃ vai yatra truṭiḥ kalpagaṇāyate |
etāvat prārthyate nūnaṃ na bhavet sakṣaṇaḥ kvacit || 19 ||

tasmādyuṣṃābhirapyetat prārthanīyamaharniśam |
sā sādhayati bhaktānāmasābhyamapi vāñchitam || 20 ||

etadanyaurna vijñeyamityahaṃ śuchyavasthitaḥ |
śokaṃ jahatha rāj~yo'tra prāpto'bhudaya ucchritaḥ || 21 ||

ityuktvā'staṅgate sūrye viveśamahatīṃ sabhām |
athā'gatā viśukrādyā bhrātaro mantriṇastathā || 22 ||

putrā bhṛtyāḥ sabhāstārāḥ sabhāmadhye'tibhāsvare |
praṇamyā'surabhūpālaṃ viviśuḥ svasvaviṣtare || 23 ||

viśukraḥ prekṣya rājānaṃ śocantamiva saṃsthitam |
kṛtā'ñcjaliḥ praṇamyā.'ha vardhayitvā'dbhutaṃ vacaḥ || 24 ||

śrīdevyā bhaṇḍaityeśavāñchitārthasamīhayā |
nāśitā'khiladaityānāṃ buddhirnītisamānugā || 25 ||

p.670)

yo'tibuddhimatāṃ śreśṭho vicāre śukrasammitaḥ | 
viśukraḥ so'pi vimataṃ prāha svārthaviṣaryayam || 26 ||

agastyaitāvadeveha kṛtyaṃ kālasya bhāvitam |
bhāvyarthasyā'nurodhena bhuddhimunmeṣayatyalam || 27 ||

mahārāja lakṣyase ma śocanniva sabhāsthitaḥ |
jīvatsvasmāsu daityeṣu pa"yāmyetadasāmpratam || 28 ||

ityuktaḥpunaruce sa daityeśo daityamaṇḍale |
viśukra kiṃ vadāmyadya kālasyaivaṃ viparyaye || 29 ||

yo'haṃ pañcottaraśatabrahmāṇḍānāṃ prasāsakaḥ |
yasya yuddhe viṣṇumukhā yūyo jātāḥ parāṅmukhāḥ || 30 ||

yasya nāmnā'pi vṛtraghno nidrāmapyetinokvacit |
so'dyā'hamabalāsaṅghairvyarthayuddhe kadarthitaḥ || 31 ||

kiṃ tādṛśaṃ pṛcchasi māṃ śocasīti mṛtaprabham |
kāle mṛtiḥ ślāghyatamā nedṛśasya kadarthanā || 32 ||

viśukra iti tadvākyaṃ śrutvā prāha tato vacaḥ |
daityeśaivaṃ nar'hasi tvaṃ bhāṣituṃ karuṇaṃvacaḥ || 33 ||

abalā yoṣidityevaṃ kāruṇyaṃ nā'pnuyāttu kaḥ |
naisargikaste dharmo'yaṃ strīṣu śastraparāvṛtiḥ || 34 ||

p.671)

alaṃ tvayā mayā vā'pi rājaputrādibhistathā |
daityeṣvavanyatamo'pyekasvayā'jñapto muhūrtataḥ || 35 ||

hanyācchaktigaṇaṃ sarvamatra pādau spṛśāmi te |
tvaṃ tiṣṭhā'ntaḥpure krīḍhan suravāragaṇaiḥ saha || 36 ||

gato'haṃ tāṃ vijityaiva pādau paśyāmi te punaḥ |
ityukvā parśvataḥ praikṣat kuṭilākṣaṃ camūpatim || 37 ||

tāvat so'pi praṇamyainaṃ kṛtāñjalirabhāṣata |
yuvarāja kimevaṃ tvaṃ bravīṣi mayi jīvati || 38 ||

rājñā saha viṣaṅgeṇa rājaputrairūpāviśa |
senāsenādhipaiḥ sākaṃ gatvā'haṃ sādhayāmi tām || 39 ||

anyathā nā'gamiṣyāmi satyaṃ pratiśṛṇomi te |
ityuktvā praṇipatyaitānniryayau nagarādbahiḥ || 40 ||

anekā'kṣauhiṇīsenāyuktaḥ senā'dhiṇaiḥ saha |
athā'gatya durmadā'khyovāhinīṣo mahābalaḥ || 41 ||

daśā'kṣauhiṇikāyukta uṣṭrā'roho gadādharaḥ |
prāhā'haṅkāraśailasthaḥ praṇamyā'tmacamūpatim || 42 ||

senādhiśa! tvamatraiva tiṣṭha senāsamāvṛtaḥ |
avalāvijayenaiva prayāṇaṃ vaḥ susammatam || 43 ||

p.672)

āneṣyāmi muhūrtena baddhvā tāmabalādhipām |
muhūrtamātrādyadi tāṃ nā'neṣyāmi tatastvayā || 44 ||

yatitavyañca gantavyamityuktvā niryayau javāt |
vādayan jaitrayātrā'ṅgavāditrāṇi muhurmuhaḥ || 45 ||

yayau śakticamūvaktraṃ sūryasyodayanaṃ prati |
daityasenāsamārabhmakolāhalamaharavam || 46 ||

śrutvā sampatkarī devī cā'śvāruḍhā mahābalā |
gatvā śrīmātaraṃ natvā mantriṇī daṇḍinīmapi || 47 ||

vijñāpya daityasannāhamanujñāte'timodite |
yayatuḥ śaktisenābhirasaṅkhyābhiḥ samāyute || 48 ||

tatra sampatkarī devī samāruhya mahāgajam |
raṇakolāhalā'khyānaṃ prāleyā'dririvonnatam || 49 ||

suvaṇa bhā japā'raktavāsoratnavibhūṣaṇā |
tāruṇyāmbhodhilaharī lāvaṇyaughasamudrikā || 50 ||

vikarṣantī maṇimayaṃ cāpamindradhanuṣprabham |
daityāmbudheḥ śoṣaṇāya śarān bhānukaropamān || 51 ||

kirantī niryayau sūrya udayā'drigato yathā |
asaṅkhyā vāraṇā'ruḍhā śaktyaścā'pi tādṛśāḥ || 52 ||

p.673)

parivārya yayurbāṇān vikirantyaḥ samantataḥ |
pratyekaṃ tādṛśī śaktirgarjane tryaṅkuśagrahā || 53 ||

gajagrīvāsamāruḍhā nayatī śatrivāhinīm |
kasmiṃścidekā saṃruḍhā'nekāścā'nyeṣu saṃsthitāḥ || 54 ||

ityasaṅkhyebhasenābhiḥ sametā sampadīśvarī |
grasantīva daityasenāṃ vyāpya bhūmimuṣā'yayau || 55 ||

nisargabhinnagaṇḍāste gajāḥ parvatasannibhāḥ |
daityasenāṃ samāseduste yuddheṣvanivartinaḥ || 56 ||

atha viāditranisvānaḥ senayorubhayorabhūt |
pravṛttaḥ "śastrapāto'pi prāṇacoramahāravaḥ || 57 ||

daityā rathebhaturagakharoṣṭramṛgarohiṇaḥ |
gṛghrakākakaṅkaghūkabhāsakekyadhisaṃśrayāḥ || 58 ||

anye mārjāranakulasaraṭapretavāhanāḥ |
caṇḍaśūlakhaḍgacakrabhindipāladharāḥ pare || 59 ||

tomaraprāsaparighabhuśuṇḍīlaguḍā'yudhāḥ |
evaṃvidhairdaityagaṇaiḥ sametā śaktivāhinī || 60 ||

yuyudhe prauḍhasaṃrambhā krodhasaṃraktalocanā |
parasparaṃ kṣipantaste vākyaiḥ kaṭurasodayaiḥ || 61 ||

p.674)

hato'si tiṣṭha śauryeṇa pṛṣṭha svaṃ na pradarśaya |
ityādibahuvākyāni vadantyaḥ śaktyo raṇe || 62 ||

jaghnurdatyāṃste'pi tadvannijaghnuḥ śaktivāhinīm |
evaṃ pravṛtte samare prāṇadyute bhayaṅkare || 63 ||

chinnabāhnaṅghrimūrdhānaḥ pāṭitār'dhārḍhadehinaḥ |
evaṃvidhāḥ śaktyo'pi daityahetibhirāhatāḥ || 64 ||

pāṭitā'ṅgā api dṛḍhaṃ krodhāt sandaṣṭadacchadāḥ |
jaghnuḥ pratyarthināṃ vṛndaṃ śastrairdantaiśca muṣṭibhiḥ || 65 ||

naṣṭaśastrāḥ parakarācchastrāmācchidya vai balāt |
anye bhagnairathāṅaiśca hetibhiryuyudhurbhṛśam || 66 ||

evaṃ pravṛtte samare ghore saṃhṛtisannibhe | 
bavurnadyo lohitaughāḥ saphenilataraṅgikāḥ || 67 ||

śaktibhirgajarohābhirdaityasainyaṃ vināśitam |
palāyitaṃ gādhaviddhaṃ daśā'kṣauhiṇiśeṣitam || 68 ||

dṛṣṭvā durmada evaṃ svāṃ bhagnāṃ senāṃ samantataḥ |
uṣṭrā'ruḍho gadāhastaḥ kālā'ntakayamopamaḥ || 69 ||

daśatālasamunnamraṃ karabhaṃ piṅgalā'kṛtim |
prāhiṇicchaktisenāsu mahāvegaṃ mahābalam || 70 ||

p.675)

atha praviṣṭaḥ śaktīnāṃ senāsūṣtro'tivegavān |
sṛkkiṇīprāntavigalanmāṃsapiṇḍā'bhajihvakaḥ || 71 ||

samudramathanodañcanmahāśabdapratisvanaḥ |
phenapiṇḍānudgiraṃśca mūtrayan pṛṣṭhato bahu || 72 ||

urasā pādavikṣepaistuṇḍā'ghātaiḥ karivrajam |
nāśayan daśanaiścaiva vyacaracchaktisainyake || 73 ||

durmado'pi gadā'ghātairibhaṃcchaktīśca nāśayan |
śaktisenā'ntaka iva kadanaṃ prakarodruṣā || 74 ||

taddṛṣṭvā śaktisenāyā nāśanaṃ sampadīśvarī |
raṇakolāhalaṃ tasya nāśāya samacodayat || 75 ||

athebharāja āsādya karabhaṃ daityavāhanam |
ayudhyata viṣāṇā'grakumbhā'ghātairmuhurmuhaḥ || 76 ||

so'pi dantairmūrdhaghātaiḥ padakṣepairayudhyata |
evaṃ yuddhaṃ samabhavattumulaṃ karabhebhayoḥ || 77 ||

athebharājaḥ karabhaṃ prasahyāpatayadbhūbi |
nipātya pādenā'kramyā'karottaṃ karabhaṃ vyasum || 78 ||

mariṣyantaṃ viditvoṣṭraṃ gadāhastaḥ khamāplutaḥ |
saṃbhrāmya pātayanmūrghnaiṃgadāṃ vāraṇakumbhyoḥ || 79 ||

p.676)

gadayā'bhihato hastī bhīnnamūrdhā bhramannuraḥ |
jānubhyāṃ vikalo bhūmiṃ prāptaḥ śoṇitamudgniran || 80 ||

tāvat sampadadhīśyā'pi śareṇorasi tāḍitaḥ |
jahau prāṇān durmado'pi dvidhorasi vipāṭitaḥ || 81 ||

hataṃ senādhipaṃ dṛṣtvā vidrutā daityasainikāḥ | 
adṛṣṭvā pṛṣṭato yāntiḥ śaktīrjīvitahetave || 82 ||

iti śrīmaditihālottame tripurārahasye māhātmyakhaṇḍe śrīlalitāmāhātmye 
durmadavadho nāma ṣaṭṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 5494 ||

p.677)

saptaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ  
nakulīparākrame karaṅkādibadhavarṇanm 


durmadaṃ nihataṃ śrutvā kuraṇḍo durmadā'grajaḥ | 
kruddha ājñāṃ samādāya kuṭilākṣān mahābalaḥ || 1 ||

viṃśatyakṣauhiṇīyukto yoddhumabhyāyayau javāt |
vilokya punarāyāntīṃ daityasenāṃ nabhonibhām || 2 ||

aśvāruḍhā'śsenābhirapārābhirabhibrajat |
turaṅgā vividhāstuṅgāstaraṅgā iva rejire || 3 ||

pṛthagvarṇā saṅghaśaḥ sā senā'tyantaṃ vyarājata |
phenapiṇḍanibhānaśvānāruḍhāḥ kanakaprabhāḥ || 4 ||

nīlāṃ'śukāḥ khaḍgakheṭadharāstāruṇyagarvitāḥ |
aparāḥ kālajīmūtanibhavājisamāśrayāḥ || 5 ||

candrakāntisamā'bhāsaḥ śoṇā'bharaṇavāsasaḥ |
dhanurbāṇadharā nīlaratnakoṭīraśobhitāḥ || 6 ||

anyā gārutatmatanibhānaśvānāruhya veginaḥ |
kuruvindasamānāṅgyaḥ pāṭalāṃśukabhūṣaṇāḥ || 7 ||

p.678)

dvacit samānā'ṅgabhūṣāṃ'śukā'śvāḥ śaktyo yayuḥ | 
evaṃ vicitrā'bhrayutasandhyā'kāśanibhā camuḥ || 8 ||

ativelā'mbudhinibhā grasaddaityacamūṃ drutam |
bherīkāhalagośṛṅgapaṭahā'nakanisvanaḥ || 9 ||

turaṅgakhurakuddālatālanisvanamiśritaḥ |
huṅkārā'hvānavikṣepā'sphoṭasaṃrāvamāṃsalaḥ || 10 ||

dyāvāpṛthivyantarālamāpūryā'dhivyarocata |
atha pravṛttaḥ samaraḥ karālo'suvikarṣaṇaḥ || 11 ||

hetipātamahāśabdabadhīrīkṛtadiṅmukhaḥ |
turaṅgakhuravikṣepavidhūtarajasāṃ gaṇaḥ || 12 ||

sāṃvartakamahāmeghasaṅghavat khaṃ samākramat |
kṣaṇaṃ kuhuniśīthā'bhaṃ jātaṃ hīnā'valokanam || 13 ||

tato hetiprapatanocchalacchoṇitavṛṣṭibhiḥ |
rajo'ndhakāraḥ saṃśāntaḥ punarāsīnmahāraṇaḥ || 14 ||

parasparaṃ prāṇaharaḥ śaktisanyasya cā'suraiḥ |
balīyasībhirdaityānāṃ śaktibhisteja āhṛtam || 15 ||

p.679)

bhagnā'bhavaddaityasenā śaktibhirgāḍhamardditā |
chinnapādakaraśrotrakukṣipṛṣṭhā mahāsurāḥ || 16 ||

prāsya śastrāṇi parito vidhunvantaḥ karānmuhuḥ |
vadanto hā hatāsmeti tvaktvā yuddhaṃ vidudruvuḥ || 17 ||

niśāmya senāṃ svāṃ bhagnāṃ kuraṇḍaḥ krodhamūrcchitaḥ |
aśvāruḍho mahaścāpaṃ vikarṣan sāyakān kṣipan || 18 ||

śaktisenāṃ samāviśya nāśayattāḥ sahastraśaḥ |
śaravarṣaiḥ kuraṇḍā'bdanirmuktaiḥ śaktivāhinī || 19 ||

bhagnā seturivonnabrā mahājalasamāgamāt |
bhagnāyāṃ śaktisenāyāmaśvāruḍhā samāsadat || 20 ||

aparājitanāmānaṃ vājinaṃ samadhiṣṭhitām |
taptahemanibhāmacchadukūlā'bharaṇojjvalām || 21 ||

pāśā'ṅkuśadhanurbāṇadharāṃ dṛṣṭvā'bravīdvacaḥ |
duṣṭe'baliptā'si ciraṃ mocayāmyavalepakam || 22 ||

yanme tvayā hato bhrātā tatphalaṃ prāpsyasi drutam |
tvāṃ nihatya raṇe paścānmukta syāṃ bhrātṛdoṣataḥ || 23 ||

mayi pradarśayā'dau te vīryaṃ yaścirasambhṛtam |
evaṃ proktā roṣitā'śvāruḍhā tīkṣṇaiḥ śaraistribhiḥ || 24 ||

vivyādha tatra caikena vāhasya śira ācchinat |
dvitīyena ca koṭīraṃ pātayāmāsa bhūtale || 25 ||

tṛtīyena tasya vakṣo dārayāmāsa vegataḥ |
hatā'śvo bhraṣṭamukuṭo vihato balavaddhṛdi || 26 ||

mūrcchitaḥ kṛttamūlasya papāteva mahāsuraḥ |
kṣaṇena mūrcchānirmuktaḥ prāha tāṃ raṇamūrdhni || 27 ||

raṇa.ślādhyā hi sādhutvaṃ sammatā'si raṇe mama |
śrutvetyāha punardevi dhiktvaṃ jīvasi daityaka ! || 28 ||

yatnānmayā bhāsituṃ tvaṃ jīvaśeṣīkṛto hyasi |
paśyemaṃ taṃ jīvaharaṃ śaraṃ śatrumadā'paham || 29 ||

śrutvā devīvaco daityaḥ krodhāgnijvalilā'kṛtiḥ |
devyāścāpaṃ praciccheda śareṇā'nataparvaṇā || 30 ||

ciccheda sā'pi khaḍgena dhanurdaityakarasthitam |
atha daityo'pi khaḍgena tasyāḥ khaḍgaṃ dvidhā'karot || 31 ||

punarjaghāna turagaṃ khaḍgenaivā'tivegataḥ |
khaḍgaprahāranirbhinnamastako'śvo hyapa'kramat || 32 ||

tāvaddevyapi saṃkruddhā baddhvā pāśena vai dṛḍham |
ākṛṣya prāharanmūrghni sṛṇinā dīptatejasā || 33 ||

p.681)

tenā'ṅkuśena nirbhinnamūrdhā daityo mamār ha 
evaṃ kuraṇḍaṃ nihataṃ śrutvā senā'dhipo ruṣā || 34 ||

pārśvasthān prekṣya seneśān karaṅkapramukhāṃstadā |
kuṭilākṣaḥ prāha senādhīśo'ruṇitalocanaḥ || 35 ||

he he karaṅkapramukhāḥ pañca yuyaṃ svasenayā |
yutā gatvā drutaṃ hatvā baddhvā tāṃ vāraṇe drutam || 36 ||

samānayadhvaṃ vaḥ śaktirasti māyāmayī nanu |
sarpiṇyākhyā tayā sarve nanvajeyāḥ surā'suraiḥ || 37 ||

ityājñptāḥ karaṅkādyā natvā senādhipeśvaram |
viṃśatyakṣauhiṇīyutāḥ pratyekaṃ yoddhumāyayuḥ || 38 ||

karaṅko garddabharathe karabhe vajradantakaḥ |
vajralomā gṛghrarathe gaje kākamukhastathā || 39 ||

vajravaktraḥ kharabare saṃruḍhā bhīmadarśanāḥ |
yuddhasaṃrambhadaityānāṃ niśamyā'timahādhvanim || 40 ||

aśvāruḍhā ca sampatīdevī senāmayuyujat |
atha sammukhamāsādya sene te śaktidaityayoḥ || 41 ||

vicitravāditrarave hetisañcalanojjvale |
cakrāte śaravarṣāṇi durānmeghatatī iva || 42 ||

p.682)

atha senādvayaṃ śliṣṭaṃ vinighnaditaretaram |
krodhadaṣṭadacchadāḍhyaṃ bhrukuṭīkuṭilānanam || 43 ||

pāṭitaṃ sitakhaḍgena protaṃ bhalleṣu prāṃśuṣu |
chidritaṃ śarajālena parighotpātapeṣitam || 44 ||

śastrairuścāvacaireva parasparamanāśayat |
gajavājimahāsenā śaktīnāmatiroṣitā || 45 ||

muhūrtenaiva niḥśeṣāñcakre daityendravāhanīm |
dṛṭvā senāṃ hataprāyāṃ karaṅkādyā mahāsurāḥ || 46 ||

praviśya śaktisenāṃ tām nāśayāmāsurojasā |
kālyamānāṃ daityavaraiḥ senāṃ dṛṣṭvā parāhatām || 47 ||

aśvāruḍhā tathā sampatkarī daityānayudhyata |
tābhyāṃ bhagnā yudhi tadā karaṅkādyā mahāsurāḥ || 48 ||

nirmamustāṃ mahāmāyāṃ sarpiṇīṃ sarpabhūṣaṇām |
bhīmarūpāṃ caṇḍaravāṃ vividhaṃ sarpamaṇḍalam || 49 ||

sṛjamānāṃ śaktisenānāśāya samarā'vanau |
atha sā mukhenetrādisambhūtaiḥ  sarpamaṇḍalaiḥ || 50 ||

nāśayāmāsa śaktīnāṃ senāmadharmuhūrtataḥ |
tadvaiśasamatīvograṃ dṛṣṭvā kāścana śaktyaḥ || 51 ||

p.683)

palāyitāḥ samākhyātuṃ daṇḍarājñyai drutaṃ yayuḥ |
prāpya tāṃ kolavadanāṃ śrīmātṛsavidhe sthitām || 52 ||

praṇamya mantriṇīñcā'piprocurdatyāśca hā bhayam |
daṇḍanāthe vayaṃ yātāḥ sampaddevyā sahā'suraiḥ || 53 ||

tatra yuddhaṃ samabhavadghoraṃ prāṇaharaṃ param |
durmado nihato yuddhe sampadīśyā kuraṇḍakaḥ || 54 ||

nīto'śvāruḍhyā kīrtiśeṣaṃ senā'pi bhūyasī | 
nāśitā'smābhiratyugrā evaṃ daityā vinirjitā || 55 ||

karaṅkādyaiḥ pañcasenānāyakairhatapattibhiḥ | 
pravartitā mahāmāyā sarpiṇyākhyā hi sāmpratam || 56 ||

netrādyakṣaiḥ sṛjantī sā'saṅkhyān sarpagaṇānmuhuḥ |
taiḥ sarpairnihatā daṣṭā baddhā bhasmīkṛtā parā || 57 ||

śaktisenā mṛtaprāyā sañjātā nanu sarvaśaḥ |
devyau te śaravarṣeṇa sarpān ghanntyau muhurmuhaḥ || 58 ||

hantuṃ daityān suhīmena musalena viniryayau |
nivārya tāṃ mantranāthā jñātvā'moghāñca sarpiṇīm || 60 ||

p.684)

tadvṛttaṃ tarasā gatvā svayaṃ devyai vyajijñapat |
atha dhyātvā kṣaṇenā'mbā jṛmbhamāṇā mukhā'mbujam || 61 ||

vikāśayattadvaktrāttu nirjagāmā'tivegataḥ |
kanakābhā mahāśaktirdevītālusamudbhavā || 62 ||

suparṇasaṃsthā nakulī vāgdevī ratnabhūṣaṇā |
tāṃ praṇamya sthitāmagre prāha śrīlaliteśvarī || 63 ||

gaccha vatse sarpiṇīṃ tāṃ nāśayā'śu svatejasā |
ityuktā sā kṣaṇenaiva samprāptā'havamaṇḍalam || 64 ||

suparṇapakṣavātena sarve sarpāḥ palāyitāḥ |
sarpabandhavinirmuktāḥ śaktyaḥ protthitāstataḥ || 65 ||

bhūyaḥ sasarja sarpān sā nāśituṃ śaktimaṇḍalam |
taddṛṣṭvā sarpanivahavyāptaṃ śaktigaṇaṃ muhaḥ || 66 ||

sasarja nakulān svīyaromabhyo'tibalān kṣaṇāt |
nakulāste mahāvegāścitravarṇāḥ suroṣitāḥ || 67 ||

stabdharomabāladhayo vajradantā dṛḍhā'ṅgakāḥ |
te sṛṣṭā nakulāḥ śīghraṃ tān sarpān sarvataḥ sthitān || 68 ||

dadaṃśurvajraradanaiḥ khaṇḍañcakrurdvadhā tridhā |
nakulastādṛśaiḥ sarpāḥ khaṇḍitā bhakṣitā api || 69 ||

p.685)

niḥśeṣitāḥ śaktisenā nāśakāmā yathotthitāḥ |
atha te nakulāḥ sarpān vināśyā'pariśeṣataḥ || 70 ||

dadaṃśuḥ sarpabhūṣāḍhyāṃ sarṇīmapi sarvataḥ |
sarpiṇyāntu praṇaṣṭāyāṃ karaṅkādyā mahāsurāḥ || 71 ||

yuyudhurnakulā devyā śastrā'pyastrairanekaśaḥ |
tatasteṣāñca śastrāṇi vāhanānyapi sarvataḥ || 72 ||

praṇāśyayugapatteṣāṃ khaḍgena śira ācchinat |
khaḍgacchinnottamāṅgāste nipeturvigatā'savaḥ || 73 ||

tāvat senāpi saṃśiṣṭā bhītā dikṣupravidrutā |
jayaśabdairvardhyamanānnakulīnakulān hitām || 74 ||

sā'ṅge saṃhṛtya vārāhīṃ mantriṇīṃ laliteśvarīm |
praṇamya jayavṛttāntamācakhyau vidhṛtā'ñjaliḥ || 75 ||

iti śrīmaditihāsottame tripurārahasye māhātmyakhaṇḍe śrīlalitācaritre 
nakulīparākrame karaṅkādivadho nāma saptaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 5570 ||

p.686)

aṣṭaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ 
kuṭilākṣaviṣaṅgasamvāde devīparājayārthayuktivimarśavarṇanam 

atha daityairviditvā tān karaṅkapramukhān hatān |
kuṭilākṣo viniḥśvasya cintāmāpa mahattarām || 1 ||

nūnameṣā mahāśaktiravijeyeva bhāti me |
durmadaśca kuraṇḍaśca balinau nihatau raṇe || 2 ||

astu tanna vicintyaṃ syājjayasyā'niyatasthiteḥ |
ete karaṅkapramukhāḥ sarpiṇyāmoghayā yutāḥ || 3 ||

ajeyā devadaityānāṃ kathaṃ yuddhe nipātitāḥ |
yā śrūyate śaktigaṇanāyikā lalitā'bhidhā || 4 ||

sā sthitaiva parīvāraśaktibhirnihataṃ balam |
aho ! kiṃ na bhavelloke viparīte vidhau nanu || 5 ||

āścaryametannihatā karaṅkādyāśca yoṣitā |
balaṃ kṣīṇaṃ caturthāṃ'śaṃsainyeśā balino hatāḥ || 6 ||

na hatā śaktisenāyāṃ mukhyaikā'pi kathaṃ bhavet |
pratijñāya śaktijayaṃ rājñe'haṃ kathamadya vai || 7 ||

p.687)

aśakyamiti vakṣyāmi punaḥ kāpuruṣo yathā |
evaṃ cintāsamākrānte kuṭikākṣe camūpatau || 8 ||

ājagām viṣaṅgo'pi viśukreṇa prayojitaḥ |
dṛṣṭvā viṣaṅgaṃ seneśaḥ kuṭikākṣaḥ samutthitaḥ || 9 ||

āsānādyaiḥ pūjayitvā niṣasādā'sane svake |
atha dṛṣtvā viṣaṇṇā'syaṃ viṣaṅgaḥ sainyanāyakam || 10 ||

abravīttaṃ bhaṇḍadaityaprārthitaṃ yā samīhati |
tayā bhakteṣṭadāyinyā mohito daityapuṅgavaḥ || 11 ||

kuṭilākṣa ! viṣīdantamiva pa"yāmite mukham |
tadvadā'śu kiṃ nimittaṃ samaṃ sarva karomyaham || 12 ||

śrutvā proce so'pi sarvaṃ daityanāśaṃ hattaram |
aviśāśañca śaktīnāṃ mahābhayamupasthitam || 13 ||

śrutvā jahāsa daiteyaṃ ā moha iti saṃvadan |
mohito māyayā devyā babhdāṣe buddhimāniva || 14 ||

śṛṇu senāpate ! sarvaṃ iditvaivā'hamāgataḥ |
vinā yuktyā na jīyeta mahābalavatā paraḥ || 15 ||

jayo yuktau sarvathaiva sthitaḥ sā būdhisaṃśrayā |
mayā śaktijayo prāyo niścitaḥ sūkṣmabudhinā || 16 ||

p.688)

jahi śokā'ndhakāraṃ tvaṃ madyukteravisaṃśrayaḥ |
ahaṃ manye hatāḥ śaktigaṇā ityeva yuktitaḥ || 17 ||

śṛṇu te tat pravakṣyāmi yanmayā niścitaṃ jaye |
kūṭenaiva vijeyā sā tatkrmaṃ niścitaṃ bruve || 18 ||

adyaiva carame bhāge dinasya daityasenayā |
mahatyā daṃśitāḥ sarve viśukreṇā'pi saṃvṛtāḥ || 19 ||

yuddhāya parato yāntu rājaputraiśca saṃyutāḥ |
gantavyameva yaddhāya yathā śaktigaṇāḥ api || 20 ||

niḥśeṣeṇaiva yudhyanti lalitaikākikā bhavet |
tāvadrātrābandhakārairdetyairbahubhirāvṛtaḥ || 21 ||

nirgamya pārśvabhāgena pṛṣṭhadeśaṃ samāśrayan |
tasyā rathaṃ samāruhya tatratyā api kāścana || 22 ||

śaktīrhatvā grhīṣyāmi jīvagrāhaṃ balādaham |
lalitāmabalāṃ duṣṭāṃ śaktisaṅghaśiphātmikām || 23 ||

na jīvagrahaṇaṃ yāti ceddhnmi gadayā bhṛśam |
tasyāṃ hatāyāṃ baddhāyāmapi vā śaktivāhinīm || 24 ||

naṣṭaprāyāmavehi tvaṃ bhavadbhirnāśyate'pi ca |
yā tu sā lalitā rājñīsukumārakarāṅghrikā || 25 ||

p.689)

na samarthā mayā yoddhuṃ viditaṃ me cirādidam |
evaṃ mayā vyavasitaṃ buddhyā'tyantapragalmayā || 26 ||

sakhe kaścittava mataṃ doṣabhedavivarjitam |
"śrutvā viṣaṅgavacanaṃ kuṭilāks'śca bhūpatiḥ || 27 ||

tripurāmāyayā muḍho jitāṃ tāmabhimanyata |
prahṛṣṭa utthāya ca taṃ taṃ drutaṃ sa pariṣasvaje || 28 ||

sādhu daityakulaślādhya vedmi tvāṃ buddhimattamam |
kāle jayavahā buddhirdurlabhā sarvathā nanu || 29 ||

naivaṃvidhā hyauśanasī buddhirāṅgirasī tatha |
ciraṃ jīva rājakāryasādhakastvaṃ mato mama || 30 ||

tat prayāhyaciraṃ rājñe nivedyā'daya mantriṇaḥ |
rājaputrān viśukrañcā'yāhi yudhāya sarvathā || 31 ||

lambatyeṣa divānātho prayātuṃ salilā'rṇavam |
vayaṃ yadā śaktisenāṃ niyudhyāmastadā drutam || 32 ||

sādhayiṣyasi tatkāryaṃ buddhyā vīryeṇa saṃvṛtaḥ |
ityukte kuṭilākṣe'tha viṣaṅgo nagaraṃ yayau || 33 ||

tatra rājñe viśukrāya cā'pi saṃvedya sarvaśaḥ |
māyāvimūḍairdaityeśaḥ sammataḥ saṃyayau drutam || 34 ||

rājaputrairviśukreṇa mantribhiścā'bhisaṃvṛtaḥ |
kuṭilākṣeṇa saṅgamya sarvasenāsamāvṛtaḥ || 35 ||

p.690)

yoddhumabhyāyayau tatra yatra śaktimahācamuḥ |
viditvā daityarājasya sannāhamatisaṃvṛtam || 36 ||

sarvasainyadaiyavarasaṅgamaṃ cāravaktrataḥ |
mantriṇī lalitādevīṃ natvā vṛttaṃ vyajijñapat || 37 ||

ājñptā lalitādevyā mantriṇī prāha daṇḍinīm |
vatse bhaṇḍāsurasyā'yaṃ saṃrambho dṛśyate mahān || 38 ||

ekaṃ daityeśvaraṃ muktvā sarve yuddhāya saṅgatāḥ |
asmābhirapi gantavyaṃ niḥśeṣeṇa drutantu tat || 39 ||

kuru sainyasya sannāhaṃ śaktīnāṃ praviśeṣataḥ |
ityājñaptā kolamukhī sarvaśakticamūvṛtā || 40 ||

mantriṇyā ca samāyutā devīṃ natvā yayau mṛdhe |
nirgatā śaktisenā sā gajavājirathā'kulā || 41 ||

samudra iva lokānāṃ ṣlāvakaḥ pratisañcare |
yuddhavāditraninadairgajavājirathasvanaiḥ || 42 ||

miśritaḥ siṃhanādo vai śaktīnāmabhivardhata |
taṃ bhīmaṃ lokadalanaṃ śrutvā daityā mahāsvanam || 43 ||

vyamuñcan pratirāvaṃ te tato'tivipulaṃ tadā |
sa ghoṣaḥ senayormiśro vyarucadrodasīgataḥ || 44 ||

bhīruṇāṃ prāṇaharaṇaḥ palvalānāṃ praśaṣaṇaḥ |
athā'bhavanmahāyuddhaṃ senayoḥ śaktidaityoḥ || 45 ||

p.691)

jaghnudaityāḥ śaktigaṇaṃ vicitrarāyudhaiḥ pṛthak |
tathā daityān śaktyo'pi mamadrdurhetipātanaiḥ || 46 ||

śaraprahāraistilaśaḥ kecittatra kṛtā raṇe |
decit kṛpāṇinā madhye pāṭitāḥ śakalīkṛtāḥ || 47 ||

kecicchaktiṣu bhalleṣu protāḥ śūleṣu madhyataḥ |
keciścakreṇa saṃcchinnakandharodarabāhavaḥ || 48 ||

peṣitāḥ parighaiḥ kecicchataghnīmudgarādibhiḥ |
amoghapāśaiḥ paritaḥ pāśitāḥ kecidañjasā || 49 ||

kecit kuṭhāraparaśupramukhairmūrgni bheditāḥ |
vikṣatāḥ kecidurasi tomarā'ṅkuśasāraṇaiḥ || 50 ||

evaṃ yuddhe babhau tatra muhūrtaṃ samabhāvataḥ |
athaśaktigaṇairbhagnā daityasenā pravidrutā || 51 ||

krndamānā hatāḥ smo vai hā heti ca dṛḍhaṃ hatāḥ |
anudrutāḥ śaktigaṇaiḥ śaraṇaṃ prepsavo'bhitaḥ || 52 ||

asṛgvahā bavustatra kravyādaśubhadarśanāḥ |
taraṅgaiḥ kaṅkālagaṇān vāhayantyaḥ samedhitāḥ || 53 ||

kecittāsu prapatitā nītā dūraṃ nimeṣataḥ |
evaṃ daityapravidrāvaṃ dṛṣṭvā bhaṇḍā'nujastadā || 54 ||

viśukraḥ prāha pārśvasthān balāhakamukhā'surān |
provācā'hūya vijñāya tānameyaparākramān || 55 ||


p.692)

he balāhaka ! paśyemān daityān śaktigaṇairhatān |
anāthāniva yuddheṣu kālyamānān samantataḥ || 56 ||

gaccha tvaṃ bhrātṛbhiryukto nāśaya pratyarīnimān |
tapasā bhavatāṃ netre sūryaḥ svāṃ'śaṃ dadau kila || 57 ||

gatvā'valokanenemā bhavanto nirdahantu vai |
ityājñapto bhrātṛyuto yayau śaktīrvihiṃsitum || 58 ||

balāhakaḥ kālamukho vikarṇo vikaṭā'nanaḥ |
sūcīmukhaḥ karālākṣaḥ karaṭaśceti sapta te || 59 ||

bhrātaro'tikrūravalā yamīkṣantyatiroṣataḥ |
sa santapto'titarṣeṇa prayāti nidhanaṃ drutam || 60 ||

tairūpāsya divānāthaṃ prāptamevaṃvidhaṃ balam |
evaṃ vidhāste senāyāṃ praviśya yugapattadā || 61 ||

vinighnantaḥ śaktigaṇaṃ dadṛśuḥ krūracakṣuṣā |
atha tāḥ śaktyaḥ sarvā daityānāmavalokanāt || 62 ||

mahatyā tṛṣayā vyāptāḥ santāpenā'khilā'ṅgakaiḥ |
evamatyantasantāpatṛṣābhyāṃ samabhiplutam || 63 ||

avasannaṃ śaktigaṇaṃ śaṅghaśaḥ samarā'vanau |
mantriṇīṃ daṇḍinīṃ tyaktvā sarvaṃ śaktigaṇaṃ yadā || 64 ||

gajā'śvavāhanopetaṃ patitaṃ tāpahetutaḥ |
tannetravīryaṃ na tayoḥ pravṛttaṃ tanmahitvataḥ || 65 ||

p.693)

niśāmya taddṇṇdanāthā mantriṇyai saṃnyavedayat |
vicārya mantriṇī cā'pi viditvā ravyanugraham || 66 ||

śrīdevīcakrarakṣārtha yā nā'yātā mahāyudhi |
sasmāra manasā śaktiṃ tiraskaraṇikā'bhidhām || 67 ||

sā prāptā smṛtimātreṇa kālāñjananibhe haye |
samāruḍhā nīlavapurnīlāṃśukavibhūṣaṇā || 68 ||

nīilamālyadharā gāḍhaghvāntasantamasā vṛtā |
svasamākāraśaktīnāṃ koṭibhiḥ parivāritā || 69 ||

na tadicchāṃ vinā ko'pi pa"yettaṃ jagatītale |
āgatya mantriṇīṃ natvā prāha kiṃ saṃsmṛteti vai || 70 ||

pṛṣṭaimuktvā daityānāṃ balaṃ tannāśanāya tām |
visarjayāmāsa punaḥ śaktīnāṃ śāntihetave || 71 ||

amṛteśīṃ sudhāsindhumājñāpayadanudrutam |
ājñaptaivaṃ samāruhyā'dṛśyavāhanamuttamam || 72 ||

tiraskṛtiradṛśyāṅgī tādṛkśaktigaṇāvṛtā |
nāśayāmāsa daityānāṃ senāṃ śastranipātanaiḥ || 73 ||

tirskṛtiḥ sasarjā'stramandhākhyaṃ tena te'surāḥ |
andhībhūtāḥ kiñcidapi dadṛśurno kathañcana || 74 ||

tathāpi yudhyamānāṃstān balavīryeṇa sambhṛtān |
ciraṃ yuddhairvinirjitya tiraskaraṇikā tataḥ || 75 ||

p.694)

khaḍgena yugapatteṣāmāharanmastakaṃ ruṣā |
hate balāhakamukhe tiraskaraṇikāṃ surāḥ || 76 ||

avarṣan divyakusumairvādayantaśca dundubhīn |
jahṛṣurdevatāmukhyā lokakaṇṭakanāśanāt || 77 ||

amṛteśyamṛtā'sārairmṛtāḥ śaktīrajīvayat |
evaṃ tiraskṛtirjitvā daityāṃstapanalocanān || 78 ||

mantriṇīṃ daṇḍinīṃ natvā provāca vijayaṃ svakam |
āliṅgya te kṛtaṃ kṛtyamasādhyaṃ sarvathā tvayā || 79 ||

ityūcastuṣṭuvaturmantriṇīdaṇḍanāyike |
evaṃ balāhakamukhe hate sūryo'stamāyayau || 80 ||

iti śrīmaditihāsottame tripurārahasye māhātmyakhaṇḍe śrīlalitāmāhātmye 
tiraskaraṇikāyuddhe balāhakādivadho nāmā'ṣṭaṣaṣtitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 5650 ||

p.695)

ekonasaptatatimo'dhyāyaḥ 
viṣaṅgacchalayuddhavarṇanam 

evaṃ tapananetreṣu tiraskṛtyā'hateṣvapi |
tripurāmāyayā muḍhāḥ saṅkalpajayaharṣitāḥ || 1 ||

viśukrakuṭilākṣādyā viṣaṅgamasurairvṛtam |
pārṣṇigrahavidhānāya preṣayitvā vidhāntaḥ || 2 ||

yuyudhuḥ śaktisenābhirāhṛtā'tyantavikramāḥ |
viśukro mantriṇīṃ daṇḍarājñīṃ senāpatiḥ svayam || 3 ||

bālāṃ bhaṇḍasutāścā'pi mantriṇaśca tirskṛtim |
ulūkajitprabhṛtayo gajavājivahe tathā || 4 ||

daityasenāḥśaktīsenāṃ yodhayāmāsa saṅgatāḥ |
astaṃ yāte savitarī sarvatastamasā'vṛte || 5 ||

pravṛttaḥ samaro'tyantadāruṇaḥ śaktidaityayoḥ |
muhūrtamātraṃ (tat?) sandhyāprakāśairabhavadraṇaḥ || 6 ||

tato gāḍhe'ndhatamasi pravṛtte sarvato diśam |
abhavaddāruṇaṃ yuddaṃ śaktidānavasenayoḥ || 7 ||

p.696)

tārā jyotiḥ samūhena śabdena ca parasparam |
yuyudhuḥ śastrajālaiste śaktibhirdaityapuṅgavāḥ || 8 ||

athā'tisammarddavaśāt senayo raja uthitam |
ācchādayannabhobhāgaṃ tārakāgaṇasaṃvṛtam || 9 ||

andhakārasamāviṣṭā bhūyo roṣā'ndhatāṃ gatāḥ |
yuyudhuḥ śaktidaityāṇāṃ gaṇāścā'krmayogataḥ || 10 ||

kecittatrāa'hvayāñcakruranye tānanusaṃyayuḥ |
śastraṇyanyeṣu yuñjanti tānyanyān ghātayanti hi || 11 ||

śabdavedhamahāyuddhamabhavadbhṛśadāruṇam |
tatra daityairhatā daityāḥ śaktibhiḥ śaktyastathā || 12 ||

evamatyantabībhatsaṃ yudhamākrandabhīṣaṇam |
kecchistrairvinihatāḥ pare rathavigharṣitā || 13 ||

aśvairāsphālitāścā'nye gajā'krāmaiḥ supeṣitāḥ |
evaṃ mahattare tatra sammrdda sati vaiśase || 14 ||

na ke'pi saṃviduḥ svīyāṃ tathā senāṃ parāmapi |
antaṃ madhyamathā'diṃ vā senāyāḥ ke'pi no viduḥ || 15 ||

niśāmyaitanmantranāthā matvā taṃ viṣamaṃ raṇam |
jvālāmukhīṃ mahādevīṃ sasmāra tripurāṃśajām || 16 ||

p.697)

smṛtamātraiva sā devī prādurāsīdraṇā'jire |
jvalatparvatasaṅkāśā jvālāvyāptadigantarā || 17 ||

tasyā aṅgaprabhāpurairnāśitaṃ nikhilaṃ tamaḥ |
tadā daityāḥ śaktyo'pi yuyudhuḥ kramasaṅgatāḥ || 18 ||

evaṃ punaḥ kramādyuddhe pravṛtte niśi kumbhaja ! |
bālā bhaṇḍasutairyuddhaṃ vicitramatanodbhṛśam || 19 ||

caturbāhumukhā bhaṇḍasūnavo viṣṇuvikramāḥ |
triṃśatsaṃkhyā ekakālaṃ yuyudhurbālayā dṛḍham || 20 ||

rathā'ruḍhā vicitreṣūn kirantaḥ sarvato diśam |
bālāṃ samācchaduḥ śastrairdineśaṃ stuhinairiva || 21 ||

atha bālā'pi śastraughānnijaśastrasya tejasā |
nāśayāmāsa vegena tamaḥ sūryodayo yathā || 22 ||

tato'tidrutasandhānāścaturbhirniśitaiḥ śaraiḥ |
