pratyekaṃ bhaṇḍatanujarathāśvānakaronmṛtān || 23 ||

rathanetṝṃstathaikena dhanūṃṣyekena cā'cchinat |
tathaikena punasteṣāṃ hṛdi vivyādh līlayā || 24 ||

p.698)

hatā'śvānaṣṭaśastrāste viddhā bāṇena vakṣasi |
mṛtaprāyāḥ samabhāvan vyathayā gāḍhamūrcchitāḥ || 25 ||

ulūkajitprabhṛtayo bhaṇḍasya bhaginīsutāḥ |
te ṣaṣtisaṅkhyā yuddheṣu bhīmavīryāḥ suyodhinaḥ || 26 ||

tairyuyodha hayāruḍhā samddevī ca sāyakaiḥ |
vicitrayuddhaṃ tanvāte draṣṭṛvismāpanaṃ param || 27 ||

pṛthagyuddhaṃ samabhavattriṃśatsaṅkhyai rdvadhā sthitaiḥ |
yuddhdhvā ciraṃ hayāruḍhā baddhvā pāśena tān kramāt || 28 ||

ciccheda sitadhāreṇa śiraḥ khaḍgenaroṣitā |
sampannāthā'pi tairyuddhvā bhūyo bhallena tān kramāt || 29 ||

hṛdi viddhvā'ntakapuraṃ preṣayāmāsa sattvaram |
ugrakarmaprabhṛtayo yuyudhustatra saṃyuge || 30 ||

tiraskaraṇikādevyā śastrairastrairanekaśaḥ |
yuddhvaivaṃ mantribhiḥ sā'pi kruddhā khaḍgena pātayat || 31 ||

parasparaṃ vavṛṣatuḥ śaraiḥ svarṇavicitritaiḥ |
parasparaprayuktāni nānāśastrāṇi cichiduḥ || 33 ||

rathastho bhaṇḍaseneśaḥ kuṭilākṣaḥ prakopanaḥ |
prasahya kolavadanāṃ yuyodhā'tisuśikṣitaḥ || 34 ||

p.699)

yuddhvaivaṃ kolavadanā vājinastasya patribhiḥ |
niṣprāṇānakarodaṣṭau karṇā'kṛṣṭairghanuścyutaiḥ || 35 ||

ekena sarathiṃ hatvā nichakhānā'paraṃ hṛdi |
śareṇorasi saṃviddha īṣat niṣprajñatāṃ gataḥ || 36 ||

punarmūrcchāvinirmukto gadāṃ prādāya cotplutaḥ |
kiricakrarathā'baddhasiṃhamūrghni prapātayat || 37 ||

gadāṃ sarvā'yasīṃ vajrakalpāṃ sarvabalena saḥ |
tathā jagarja kariṇaṃ hatveva harilocanaḥ || 38 ||

siṃho'pi gadayā viddho mūrcchāṃ prāpya nimeṣataḥ |
punaḥ prajñāṃ prāpya ruṣā dṛṣṭvā daityaṃ puraḥ sthitam || 39 ||

talena vakṣasyahanaddhato haritalena saḥ |
pātitoraḥsthalo raktadhārāṃ muñcadanekadhā || 40 ||

punarāplutya vārāhīrathaṃ ditikulodbhavaḥ |
parivāraśaktigaṇaḥ kadanaṃ kalyayadruṣā || 41 ||

evaṃ taṃ rathamāruhya yudhyamānaṃ mahāsuram |
vārāhyā vṛttimukhyā yā jambhinī śaktiruttamā || 42 ||

sā'yudhyat kuṭilākṣeṇa ciraṃ paścādatīva tam |
yudhyamānaṃ śareṇā'sye nijaghāna mahābalā || 43 ||

p.700)

hato gāḍhaṃ śareṇā'sye kṣaṇaṃ stabdho'tiṣīḍayā |
punarutplutya gadayā tāṃ mūghni prāharadruṣā || 44 ||

atha sā gāḍhasaṃviddhā rathopasthe upā'viśat |
evaṃvidhāṃ śaktigaṇā dṛṣṭvā hā heti cukruśuḥ || 45 ||

tāvattasyā dhanurdaityo babhañjā'cchidya satvaram |
utthitā jambhinī bhūyo dṛṣṭvā bhagnaṃ nijaṃ dhanuḥ || 46 ||

ruṣā jvalantī mahatīṃ gadāṃ svīyāṃ parā'mṛśat |
athā'bhavadgadāyuddhaṃ bhīmaṃ tvāṣṭrendrasannibham || 47 ||

jambhinīdaityayordṛṣtvā viṣmitā devadānavāḥ |
matvā rathaṃ saṅkucitaṃ bhūmau tābhyām bhūt kṛtam || 48 ||

gadāsaṅghaṭṭanodañcatpāvakaughapravarṣaṇam |
vicitrā'kramaṇotplāvacitrasaṃsthānasundaram || 50 ||

tata ulplutya vegena hantuṃ tāmabhicakramat |
yāvattāvattayā murghni balavadgadayā hataḥ || 51 ||

prasphuṭaddṛgajuhāvaktrāducchaladraktadhārayā |
varṣastāṃ patito bhūmau śīrṇasarvā'ṅgabandhanaḥ || 52 ||

gadāmāliṅgya tarasā mumocā'sūn luṭhan bhūvi |
hataṃ vīkṣya camūnāthaṃ daityā bhītāḥ palāyitāḥ || 53 ||

p.701)

devatāḥ śaktyaścā'pi jahṛṣuḥ śatrunāśanāt |
evaṃ daityaṃ nihatyā'śu jambhini daṇḍanāyikām || 54 ||

praṇamya saṃsritā pārśvaṃ daṇḍinī ca praharṣitā |
dadau parīrambhaṃ premṇā tāṃ prasamīkṣata || 55 ||

viśukreṇa yuyodhoścairmantriṇī vīryavattarā |
viśukro'pi mahāśūro yuvarājo'tivīryavān || 56 ||

vicitramakaroddevyā raṇamāścaryakāraṇam |
parasparasya śastrāṇi pratiśastrairvināśayan || 57 ||

astrairastrāṇayapi tathā nāśayantau parasparam |
evaṃ ciraṃ niyudhyantamatyadbhūtaparākramam || 58 ||

dṛṣtvā mantramahārājñī darśayantī svacāturīm |
yugapaccharasaṅghena rathamaśvāṃśca sārathim || 59 ||

dhanurmukuṭakaṃ ketuṃ ciccheda nimiṣā'rdhataḥ |
evaṃ yuddhe parībhūto viśukro manyunā jvalan || 60 ||

jagṛhe kheṭanistriṃśau hantuṃ devīṃ mano dadhe |
athā'yāntaṃ khaṅgakaraṃ dūrādekena patriṇā || 61 ||

karavālaṃ dvidhā kṛtvā kheṭmanyena bhedayat |
chinnakheṭakanistriṃśamāyāntaṃ baddhamuṣṭikam || 62 ||

p.702)

jaghānorasi bhallena niśitena garīyasā |
bhallāhato viśukro'pi saphenaṃ rudhiraṃ vaman || 63 ||

ghūrṇito vibhraman krośadvaye vegena pātitaḥ |
avaśaḥ saṃvartakena vāyuneva mahāgiriḥ || 64 ||

gāḍhamūrcchāmupagato yathā lokāntarāt kṛtī |
evaṃ viśukre'bhihate daityasenā hyabhidrutā || 65 ||

nikṣipya hetīn santyajya vāhanāni diśo daśa |
viśukraṃ mūrcchitaṃ daityāḥ samādāya puraṃ yayuḥ || 66 ||

viśukrādiṣu yudhyatsu niḥśeṣaśaktisenayā |
viditvā lalitāṃ cakrarathasthāmekalāṃ tadā || 67 ||

katicitparivārā'tmaśaktisaṅghaikasaṃśrayām |
jetuṃ śakyeti saṃhṛṣṭo yayau tatrā'suro drutam || 68 ||

damanādyaiḥ pañcadaśā'kṣauhiṇīpaiḥ susaṃvṛtaḥ |
alpasenāparīvāraḥ śastrāstrabahusaṅkulaḥ || 69 ||

prayāṇe pādasaṅghātaśabadamālakṣya so'suraḥ |
gajāruḍhairyuto mattagajamāruhya saṃyayau || 70 ||

sarve nīlāṃ'śakadharā nīlābharaṇabhūṣitāḥ |
sametya saṃvidaṃ kṛtvā pṛthaṅnāgaiśca saṇghaśaḥ || 71 ||

p.703)

yayurdaityacamūpṛṣṭhe yojanadvayataḥ param |
tataḥ kecichaktisenāṃ vāmato'nye ca dakṣataḥ || 72 ||

parikramya yayurmadhye hitvā yojanayugmakam |
evaṃ pṛthak saṅghaśaste yayurdasyuryathā tataḥ || 73 ||

cakrarājaśatā'ṅgasya krośadūre hi pṛṣṭhataḥ |
militāste sarva eva tato nimiṣamātrataḥ || 74 ||


samāseduḥ pṛṣṭhabhāgaṃ viṣaṅgapramukhā'surāḥ |
vidyāmayī śrīlalitā jñātvā'pyasurasammatam || 75 ||

tatrā.ā.cakhyau līlayaiva sthitā jñāteva tatra sā |
śaktyo yuddhasandeśaṃ śrutvā dūre sunirbhayāḥ || 76 ||

suṣvaṣuḥ sarva aivatāḥ svasthāneṣu rathottame |
suptaśaktigaṇopetaṃ prajvaladdipasaṅghakam || 77 ||

dīpaprabhāmūrcchanena samedhitamaṇiprabham |
saṅghairmaṇigaṇairdyotamānaṃ samantatam || 78 ||

dṛṣṭvā sametya daiteyā dūrādekaprapātataḥ |
āroḍhuṃ ratharājantaṃ samīpamabhisaṃyayuḥ || 79 ||

utkhātakaravālasu sahastramitaśaktiṣu |
rathasya parito guptyai prākramat suviśeṣataḥ || 80 ||

p.704)

kāściddṛṣṭvādaityasaṅghaṃ jajāruḍhaḥ samantataḥ |
kṛtvā'hvānamahāśabdaṃ praviśurdaityavāhinīm || 81 ||

khaḍgena jaghnurdaityāṃstānākṣipantyo vacogaṇaiḥ |
tadā daityaiḥ śaktibhiśca yuddhamāsit subhairavam || 82 ||

iti śrimaditihāsottame tripurārahasye māhātmyakhaṇḍe śrīlalitācaritre 
śakticakroparidaityaprahāravarṇanaṃ nāmaikonasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 5732 ||

p.705)

saptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ
viṣaṅgaparājayavaraṇanaṃ

atha śaktisamāhvānaṃ śrutva'varaṇaśaktyaḥ |
vijñāya daityā'gamanamaṇimāpramukhāstadā || 1 ||

svasvavāhasamāruḍhāḥ śastrāstrairabhisaṃvṛtāḥ |
yuyudhurdaityasenābhiḥ sametya balavattaram || 2 ||

śu"śruvuḥ kramaśaḥ sarvāḥ śaktyorathasaṃsthitāḥ |
vyajijñapustacchrīdevyai nityāḥ kāmeśvarīmukhāḥ || 3 ||

kāmeśvaryājñayā jvālāmālinī yā caturdaśī |
nityā sā parvatā'kāravapuṣā jvālayā'vṛtā || 5 ||

jajvāla tajjvālayā hi parito yojanā'yatam |
prakāśitamabhūttatra yuyudhuḥ śaktyo'suraḥ || 6 ||

jaghnurvividhaśastraistān daityān yuddhasamāgatān |
evaṃ tānasurān yuddhvā ninyuryamapuraṃ drutam || 7 ||

p.706)

atha śiṣṭā daityagaṇāḥ palāyanaparā'bhavan |
te śaktibhiḥ parivṛtā nā'labhannirgamaṃ kvacit || 8 ||

hanyamānāḥ śaktigaṇairhā hetyuścairvicukruśuḥ |
taddṛṣṭvā daityanidhanaṃ damanādyā mahāsurāḥ || 9 ||

vikirantaḥ śaraiḥ śaktīrnyarundhan sarvatobhṛśam |
uścāvacaiḥ śastragaṇairjaghnuḥ śaktīḥ samantataḥ || 10 ||

evaṃ daityarhatā gāḍhaṃ śaktyo'pi nijā'yudhaiḥ |
vabṛṣargiriśṛṅgāṇi ghaṇā ghanāgaṇā iva || 11 ||

muhūrtamabhavadyuddhaṃ śaktīnāṃ damanādibhiḥ |
tāvatte daityaseneśā damanādyā mahābalāḥ || 12 ||

cakrurvyākulitaṃ śaktigaṇaṃ śastrā'stravarṣaṇaiḥ |
naṣṭavāhā naṣṭaśastrā naṣṭā'ṅgā naṣṭajīvitāḥ || 13 ||

na sehurdaityaśastrāṇāṃ prapātaṃ prāṇakarṣaṇam |
mūrcchitāḥ śaktayaḥ kāścita kāścit prāṇān jahuḥ parāḥ || 14 ||

saṃchinnāḥ khaṇḍaśaścā'nyā pāṭita'ṅgyo gatā'yuṣaḥ |
dṛṣṭaivaṃ nihataṃ śaktigaṇaṃ kāmeśvarīmukhāḥ || 15 ||

tithidevyo rathāruḍhāścukrudhurbalavattarāḥ |
damanādyaistithimitaiḥ saṅgatāḥ samasaṅkhyakāḥ ||| 16 ||

p.707)

yuyudhuḥ śastranicayairbalavīryasusambhṛtāḥ |
śastrāṇi pratiśastraistā astrāṇyastrairvināśya tān || 17 ||

jaghnurlāghavayogena daityān marmasu sāyakaiḥ |
evaṃ tābhirhanyamānā daityāḥ krodhaṃ paraṃ yayuḥ || 18 ||

cakruratyadbhūtaṃ yuddhaṃvīryasāhasasaṃyutam |
vaḍaveva dhanurvaktrādudgirantaḥ śarār'ciṣaḥ || 19 ||

lokān didhakṣanta iva vyarājanta raṇā'jie |
atha tāṃstithidevyastā yuddhvā'dbhutaparākramān || 20 ||

aśvān rathaṃ sārathiñca dhvajaṃ chittvā krameṇa tān |
bhaṅktvā cāpaṃ tīkṣaṇabhallaiḥ śiraścicchidurojasā || 21 ||

atha teṣu praṇaṣṭeṣu daityeṣu damanā'diṣu |
vidrutā daityasubhaṭāḥ śiṣṭā bhītyā diśo daśa || 22 ||

viṣaṅgo gajasaṃruḍho yuyodha tithiśaktibhiḥ |
bahubhirbalavāneko yuyodhā'tiruṣā'nvitaḥ || 23 ||

yudhyantamatidairyeṇ parivārya samantataḥ |
jaghnuḥ kāmeśvarimukhā nityāḥ sāyakavarṣanaiḥ || 24 ||

p.708)

atha kāmeśvarī prāha viṣaṅgaṃ yuddhasaṅgatam |
daityā'dhamā'laṃ yuddhe na prāṇāntakaraṇena te || 25 ||

śastraṃ tyaktvā pādacāro gaccha śīghraṃ yathāsukham |
viṣamaṃ bahubhiryuddhamekasya pratibhāti te || 26 ||

śrutvā kāmeśvarī vākyaṃ jahāsoccairmahāsuraḥ |
kiṃ katthasi vṛthā duṣṭe hitvā lajjāṃ sudūrataḥ || 27 ||

ahamekaḥ kṣaṇenaiva sarvāṃ śaktiṃ camūmimām |
hatvā vo lalitāṃ devī baddhvā jīvagraheṇa vai || 28 ||

neṣyāmyatra na sandehaḥ śastraṃ te prua ālabhe |
striṣu kāruṇikatvaṃ me sarvathā tena vai ciram || 29 ||

ityuktvā niśitairbhallaiḥ pratyekaṃ tā jaghāna ha |
tacchrautvā prāha nityā'dyā evaṃ cedastu vai raṇaḥ || 30 ||

ekaikayā mayā sākaṃ samabhāvena satvaram |
ityuktvā deaityarājaṃ taṃ prāha bhūyo maheśvarī || 31 ||

bhagamālinimukhyā he madvākyaṃ śṛṇutā'darāt |
ahamenaṃ yodhayāmi yūyamenaṃ n hi yūyamenaṃ hi rakṣatha || 32 ||

yathā palāyya no gacchet sāvadhānena sarvataḥ |
ityuktvā dṛḍhabāṇena viddhvā daityamuvāca ha || 33 ||

re mūḍha mayi tvaṃ dayāṃ tyaktvā hi sarvathā |
yudhyasva sarvavīryeṇa paśyāmi tava pauruṣam || 34 ||

p.709)

yadi tvaṃ puruṣastarhi apalāyya brajiṣyasi |
ahaṃ tvāṃ nava hiṃsyāmi kuryāṃ tvāṃ hi palāyatam || 35 ||

śrutvā puruṣabhāvena yudhyasva vigatatrapaḥ |
ityuktvā niśitān bhallān dehe tasya samākṣipat || 36 ||

yuyudhe so'pi kāmeśyā balamāhārya roṣataḥ |
atha karṇāntapūrṇena śareṇa tasya vāhanam || 37 ||

jaghāna kumbhayormadhye tena bāṇena sa dvipaḥ |
dvidhā vidaloito bhūmau papāta vyasuradrivat || 38 ||

hatavāho viṣaṅgao'pi gṛhītvā saśaraṃ bhanuḥ |
yoddhumabhyāyau krodhādbhrukuṭīkuṭilā'nanaḥ || 39 ||

atha tribhiḥ śarardevī dhanustūṇīrameva ca |
mukuṭañcā'pi ciccheda kāmeśī kṣaṇamātrataḥ || 40 ||

tataḥ karālaṃ vipulamādāya karavālakam |
upadhāvadyāvadasau tāvattīkṣyoṣuṇā punaḥ || 41 ||

tridhā chīttvā mahākhaḍgaṃ smitapūrvamuvāca tam |
daitya ! śūratamastvaṃ via kathamevaṃ niyudhyasi || 42 ||

śastraṃ na khalu kasmānme pātitaṃ kiñcidaṅgake |
kiṃ dayā te sumahatī jātaḥ kasmānnirāyudhaḥ || 43 ||

kathaṃ tāṃ lalitāṃ baddhvā'smān jitvā neṣ.yasīraya |
upalabdhaṃ śastraṃ vyarthaṃ vijayahetave || 44 ||

p.610)

palāyya vā gaccha mama purato yadi pauruṣam |
śrutvā kāmeśvarīvākyaṃ kaṭukaṃ sopahāsakam || 45 ||

muṣṭimudyamya tāṃ hantuṃ prayayau sammukhe tadā |
tadā tribhiḥ śariarbhūyo vivyādhā'tibalena tam || 46 ||

pādahṛnmuṣṭiṣu tathā'hatasthānatrayā'turaḥ |
padānna calituṃ draṣṭuṃ muṣṭiñca vinivartitum || 47 ||

asamartho lajjito'tha gatonatardhānamā"śu vai |
tāvat kāmeśvarī devī śarajālaiśca sarvataḥ || 48 ||

rurodha mārgaṃ daityasya taddabhutāmivā'bhavat |
ruddho'tha sarvato mārge māyāṃ daityo vitānayat || 49 ||

śastrā'śmarudhirā'hīnāṃ pāṃśvaṅgāramayībhṛtām |
vṛṣṭiñcakre mahabhūtān rākṣasapretasaṅghakān || 50 ||

prāduścakāra vividhāṃ māyāmatibhayāvahām |
sṛṣṭāṃ sṛṣṭāñca tāṃ māyāṃ daityasyāstrairapā'karot || 51 ||

athonmatto mahāmūrtiḥ karālaḥ kṛṣṇapiṅgalaḥ |
sahasravaktro niyutahastapādo'tivegavān || 52 ||

bhūtvā jagāma tāṃ hantuṃvyāttā'syaḥ pāvakaṃ vaman |
dṛṣṭvā bhītaṃ śaktigaṇaṃ taṃ yojanamitonnatam || 53 ||

dṛṣṭvā'surasya sā māyāṃ nirmāyā'stramavā'sṛjat |
tenā'streṇa kṣaṇādeva māyāḥ sarvā vyanīnaśat || 54 ||

p.711)

athā'pyantarhitaṃ daityaṃ kāmeśī mantrapāśataḥ |
baddhavā nipātayāmasa svapuraḥ kṣaṇamātrataḥ || 55 ||

sa daityaḥ pāśasambaddhaḥ patitaḥ pādasannidhau |
lajjito namravadano mṛtavanmīlitekṣaṇaḥ || 56 ||

badhamevaṃ viṣaṅgaṃ taṃ cakrarāja rathā'ntikam |
ānayadhvaṃ śaktigaṇā nityāsā (pyavadattadā) hi sā (?) || 57 ||

yayau nityā parivṛtā śrīdevīpādasannidhim |
gatvā rathaṃsamāruhya praṇemurlalitā'mbikām || 58 ||

ācakhyurvijāyañcā'pi tāvatā mantriṇīmukhāḥ |
samājagmurvijityā'rīnnatvā procurjayaṃ raṇe || 59 ||

śrutvā sā mantriṇī mukhyā daityānāṃ kaitavodyamam |
vismitāstatra surāścā'pi daityānāṃ śaṭhatāṃ prati || 60 ||

athā'nītaṃ śaktigaṇairbaddhaṃ daityā'nujaṃ tadā |
niśāmya śaktyaḥ procuratikrodhatayā'kulāḥ || 61 ||

badhyatāṃ hanyatāñcaiva chidyatāṃ bhidyatāmiti |
śrutvā daṇḍā'dhisammrājñī vadhaṃ tasya vyacintayat || 62 ||

p.712)

atha śrīmātṛsammatyā mantriṇī prabravīdvacaḥ |
vadho na yukto baddhasya mṛtatulyo hi sa smṛtaḥ || 63 ||

gachatveṣa samākhyātuṃ daityarājāya svāṃ daśām |
ityuktvā mocayāmāsa prāha taṃ mantriṇī tadā || 64 ||

re daitya bhūyo maivaṃ tvamakārṣiḥ śūragarhitam |
śrutvaivaṃ mantriṇīvākyaṃ lajjito daityapuṅgavaḥ || 65 ||

natā'nano yayau śīghramapaśyan pṛṣṭhadaḥ punaḥ |
atha tatra śaktigaṇaṃ sametaṃ lalitā'mbayā || 66 ||

parasparaṃ yuddhavṛttaṃ mahāyuddhamadya daityaiḥ subhīṣaṇam |
rātriralpāvaśeṣā'sti punaḥ sūryodayaṃ prati || 68 ||

bhavedyuddhamatīvograṃ daityā kapaṭyodhinaḥ |
tadasmābhiḥ punaryuddhe gantavyaṃ sarvasenayā || 69 ||

punaratra cakrarājarathastiṣṭheta caikalaḥ |
yadypi śrīmahādevyā mahimno nā'sti durghaṭam || 70 ||

tathāpyasāmpratañcaitadbhāti tatra kathaṃ bhavet |
vimṛśya mantraṃ buddhyaitadyuktaṃ jñātvā samīkuru || 71 ||

itiṃ vākyaṃ samākarṇya daṇḍinyā mantriṇī svayam |
amaṃsatā'tha tadyuktaṃvicāryaśuddhayā dhiyā || 72 ||

p.713)

lalitāṃ prāṇipatyā'ha mātarācakṣva me vacaḥ |
vadatyeṣā varāhā'syā sūrasyodayanaṃ prati || 73 ||

punaryuddhāya gantavyasmabhiḥ śaktisenayā |
akośe senayā hīne ratha eṣa tavā'śrayaḥ || 74 ||

na śobhate hyanaucityādatha śaktigaṇo'khilaḥ |
niyuddhaśrāntimabhyetya rātrau viśrāntimeṣyati || 75 ||

adya saṃvṛttayuddhena kaitavena viśaṅkitāḥ |
na svasthahṛdayā bhūyo viśrāmaṃ nopayanti vai || 76 ||

manyase yadi tatrā'lamanujānīhi māṃ punaḥ |
praviṭtiṃ bhāvayiṣyāmi śatrūṇāmapradharṣiṇīm || 77 ||

iti vijñāpitā devī pravṛttau tāṃ samādiśat |
atha sā tithirnityāsu yā tatrā'sti caturdaśī || 78 ||

ājñāpayattaṃ hi sālanirmāṇe śatrurodhane |
ājñāmātreṇa sā devī jvālāmālinikā'bhidhā || 79 ||

vahnijvālāmahāsālaṃ nirmame'surarodhanam |
valayīkṛtya śaktīnāṃ senāṃ sālaḥ samāsthitaḥ || 80 ||

anullaṅghayonnamramūrtirbahirjvālo'tibhīṣaṇaḥ |
antaḥ śītalayan śaktīrbahiryojanadūragam || 81 ||

dahan yojanavistāramadhyadvāro vyarājata |
athaivaṃ sālamālokya śaktyo gatasābhvasāḥ || 82 ||

suṣvapuḥ saukhyatastatra yuddhaśrāntā hi śaktyaḥ |
sahasrasaṅkhyā rakṣartha mantranāthā samādiśat || 83 ||

evaṃ jvālāsālamadhye sthitā śrīlalitāmbikā || 84 ||


iti śrīmaditihāsottame tripurārahasye māhātmayakhaṇḍe śrīlalitācaritre 
viṣaṅgaparājayavarṇanaṃ nāma saptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 5816 ||


p.715)

ekasaptatatimo'dhyāyaḥ 
śrīcakranāśāya viśukreṇa vighnayantraprayogavarṇanam

bandhamukto viṣaṅgo'tha yayau daityacamūṃ prati |
dukṣito'titarāṃ dīno hatotsaho'tiniḥsvasan || 1 ||

ekalo vijane kvā'pi niviṣṭo hīnavarcasaḥ |
tatra śuśrāva seneśaṃ kuṭilākṣañca mantriṇḥ || 2 ||

hatāṃstato'pi duḥkhābdau nirmagnaḥ samajāyata |
tāvadviśukro'bhiyayau rājaputrasamāvṛtaḥ || 3 ||

dadarṣā'tiviṣaṇṇā'syaṃ viṣaṅgamanujaṃ sthitam |
papraccha vatsa kiṃ te'dya paśyāmi malinaṃ mukham || 4 ||

pārṣṇiyuddhe kimāsītte vada me tadaśeṣataḥ |
śrutvaivaṃ bhrātṛvacanaṃ niḥśvasan prāha sa'nujaḥ || 5 ||

bhrātaḥ kiṃ te'bhivakṣyāmi nā'sīdevaṃ kadāpi me |
jānāmi daityanāśāya kāla eṣo'pradakṣiṇaḥ || 6 ||

aho'ticitraṃ daityānāṃ viṣṇuvīiryā'tiyodhinām |
parābhavo'balāsaṅghairmṛgeśasyaivajambukaiḥ || 7 ||

p.716)

evaṃ daśā'bhipannasya na yuktaṃ jīvitaṃ mama |
tat kiṃ karomi nāsīnme mṛtyuryuddheṣu sarvathā || 8 ||

aho dhanyā ye mṛtāste kuṭilākṣādayo'surāḥ |
na tai'svātmā parībhūto dṛṣto duḥkhāya kutracit || 9 ||

bhartṛpiṇḍaviniryogaṃ kṛtvottamatamāṃ gatim |
khyātiñca saṅgatāste'smin loke'muṣminnapi dhruvām || 10 ||

dhiṅ māmevaṃvidhaṃ daityaṃ na jīvantañca no mṛtam |
bhrātaḥ kiṃ bahunoktena sāraṃśṛṇu vadāmi te || 11 ||

daityānāmeṣa samprāptaḥ kālo'tyantamadakṣiṇaḥ |
tasmādahaṃ na jīviṣye strībhiryuddhe parājitaḥ || 12 ||

ito daityaiḥ sameto'haṃ prayātaḥ kṣaṇadāmukhe |
pārṣṇiyuddhe tāṃ vijetuṃ samayasyā'nurodhataḥ || 13 ||

tadrathasya pārṣṇibhāge vayaṃ sarve hi saṅgatāḥ |
yāvadrathaṃ samāruhya tāṃ grahitumabhīpsavaḥ || 14 ||

tāvat prāptāḥ śaktigaṇā ratharakṣāvidhau sthitāḥ |
taṃ rathaṃ paraivartantyo dhṛtodvātakṛpāṇikāḥ || 15 ||

asmābhistarjayantyastā yuyudhurbalavattarāḥ |
anāloke raskhalantyastamistrāyāntu sarvataḥ || 16 ||

p.717)

tāvattasyā ratha"śreṣṭhānnirgatāstithisaṅkhyakāḥ |
nirmamurjvalitaṃ śailamandhakāranivāraṇam || 17 ||

parivabruḥ sametyā'smān vrṣantyaḥ śarasantatīḥ |
kṣaṇena damānādyāṃstā hatvā māmabhisaṃyayuḥ || 18 ||

tāsu mukhyā śaktirekā kāmeśyadbhutavikramā |
dvandve māmabhisaṅgamya babandhā'rdhanimeṣataḥ || 19 ||

na tasyāṃ me vikramo'bhūdvīryaṃ māyābalaṃ tathā |
baddho nītaḥ pātito'tha lalitācaraṇāntike || 20 ||

tasyā yā saciveśāṇī tamāladalasacchaviḥ |
saundaryasārasutanurmadhurā'lāpareśalā || 21 ||

tayā'haṃ bandhanānmukto dayamānasvabhāvayā |
tato me jīvato nārtho hatakīrterhataujasaḥ || 22 ||

śrutvaivaṃ laṣitaṃ tasya viśukraḥ prāha sasmitam |
bhaṇḍā'bhimatasiddhayarthaṃhatadhīrlalitā'mbayā || 23 ||

vatsa tvaṃ na vijānāsi nītiṃ yudhajayā'vahām |
yuddhe mahāntaḥ śūrāśca kvacidalapairjitā nanu || 24 ||

bhavantyeva na taścitraṃ phalaṃ vā śūrasammatam |
gacchanhi pādavān bhūmau divā'pi skhalati kvacit || 25 ||

p.718)

na skhalanit kvacidapi sthāvarāḥ kiṃ tato bavet |
jayā'jayau yuddha vidhāvanirdeśyau kathañcana || 26 ||

parantu jaya evaiṣa yaḥ paryavasito bhavet |
nā'nyo jayo jayaḥ proktastvantarā saṃvibhāvitaḥ || 27 ||

aho mohasya māhātmyaṃ kiṃ vadāmyahamadya te | 
vicāro dhairyasahito mantrayukto jayāvahaḥ || 28 ||

viramaivaṃ viparyāsābhāvat sarvārthanāśanāt |
śṛṇu me mantritaṃ sadyastava duḥkhavināśanam || 29 ||

yenopāyena vibudhāḥ śakramukhyā vinirjitāḥ |
purā bhūyo vismṛtaṃ te tadahaṃ śṛṇu yadbrū ve || 30 ||

pratyaripratyūhakaramābhicārātmakantu tat |
yantraṃ śatrupratīghātaṃ viditaṃ bhārgavānmayā || 31 ||

tadadya racayāmyāśu vidhidṛśṭena vartmanā |
tatprayogādbhavedevaṃ nihataṃ śaktisainakim || 32 ||

likhitvā'haṃ śilāpade tubhyaṃ dāsyāmi samprati |
tvaṃ yuktyā śaktisenāsu prakṣipyā'gaccha satvaram || 33 ||

tatastāḥ śaktyo naṣṭavīryodyogāstato vayam |
tagvā daityacamūyuktā anāyāsena satvaram || 34 ||


p.719)

nāśayāvaḥ śaktigaṇaṃ nā'tra saṃśayamāpnuhi | 
uttiṣṭha jahi santāpaṃ śokajaṃ vīryanāśanam || 35 ||

śrutvetthaṃ bhrātṛkathitaṃ mahāmāyāvimohitaḥ |
jitamityeva niścitya harṣamāhārayat param || 36 ||

tuṣṭāva bhrātaraṃ bhrātā saṃślāghan buddhikauśalam |
athaitaddaityarājāya mantramāvedya sammataḥ || 37 ||

nirmame tadvignakaraṃ yantraṃ dārṣadapaṭṭake |
snātvā śucividhānena yantramālikhya taddrutam || 38 ||

sampūjya tatra devīṃ tāmāsurīṃ vighnakāriṇīm |
surāmāṃsopahārādyairnivedya vividhaistadā || 39 ||

svayaṃ digvasano bhutvā śmaśānabhasitā'śrayaḥ |
papau surāṃ karoṭīsthāṃ vāmamārgasamāśrayaḥ || 40 ||

jajāpa japyaṃ kaikasyā mālayā dakṣadiṅkumkhaḥ |
samantānnagnayuvatīgaṇena parivāritaḥ || 41 ||

evaṃ saṃsādhya tadyantraṃ viṣaṅgāya dadau tadā |
vatsaitacchaktisenāyā madhye'bhimukhabhāvataḥ || 42 ||

lalitāratharājasya nikṣipyā'yāhi satvavaram |
amoghaṃ vighnayantraṃ tadādāya tatra saṃyayau || 43 ||

p.720)

rātriśeṣe viṣaṅgo'tha jvālāsālaṃ dadarśa ha |
aghṛṣyaṃ taṃ samālakṣya dvāre śaktigaṇaṃ tathā || 44 ||

na tatsamīpaṃ gatavān bhītyā dūre samāsthitaḥ |
vicārya khamavaplutya sālaṃ tamativartitum || 45 ||

asamartho balenā'ntaḥ prakṣipya prayayau punaḥ |
matvā kṛtārthamātmānamācakhyāvagrajāya tat || 46 ||

vighnayantreṇa vihatāḥ śaktyaḥ syurato drutam |
yasmādamoghaṃ tadyantramāpūrya samadhiṣṭhitam || 47 ||

iti matvā yudhavidhāvidyogaṃ vidadhe'suraḥ |
viśukraḥ sarvadaityānāṃ bhrātṛsaṃyutaḥ || 48 ||

rājaputraiśca rājñā ca niryayau nagarādbahiḥ |
yoṣitasthavirabālāṃśca varjayitvā'khilā'surāḥ || 49 ||

rakṣaṇārtha śūnyakasya nikṣipyā'kṣauhiṇīmitām |
niryayuḥ sarvaḥ evaite daityā yuddhāya daṃśitāḥ || 50 ||

ājagmuragnisālasya samīpamativegataḥ |
dṛṣṭvā'praghṛṣyantaṃ sālaṃ bhettuṃ te mana ādadhuḥ || 51 ||

viśukrapramukhā daityā astrāṇi sasṛjustataḥ |
vāyavyaṃ pārvataṃ cāndraṃ śaiśiraṃ pārthivaṃ tathā || 52 ||

sāmudraṃ vāruṇaṃ vāraṃ pārjanyapramukhāni ca |
visṛṣṭaṃ sālamabhito mantrasandhānasandhitam || 53 ||

p.721) 

saṃvartā'gnistṛṇamiva sālo niḥśeṣatāṃ nayat |
matvā'bhedyantu taṃ sālaṃ viśukrapramukhā'surāḥ || 54 ||

utplutya yātumantaste mana uccaiḥ samādadhuḥ |
utplutya yāvadyo daityo gantumantaḥ samīpsati || 55 ||

tato'dhikataraṃ tatra prākāraḥ samavardhata |
taddapyaśakyaṃ matvā te praveṣtuṃ dvāramāyayuḥ || 56 ||

praviśantaṃ tato'ṣyagniḥ kṣaṇādbhasmīkarotyalam |
matvā'ntargamanaṃ tasya durghaṭaṃ dvārade"śagāḥ || 57 ||

śastrāṇi dvāramārgeṇa cā'strāṇyalamavā'sṛjuḥ |
viditvā daityasenāyāḥ sannāhaṃ dvārarodhanam || 58 ||

daṇḍarājñī śaktisenāsajjanārthaṃ samādiśat |
vighnayantreṇa vihatāḥ śaktyaḥ sarvataḥ sthitāḥ || 59 ||

ālasyaopahatāścā'nyā nidrāmohasamākulāḥ |
daṇḍarājñīsamādeśaṃna kiñcinmānayanti tāḥ || 60 ||

kinnaḥ kṛtyaṃ yuddhaviddhau daityānāṃ ghātanaṃ kutaḥ |
na noparāddhaṃ daiteyaiḥ kathaṃ tairvigraho mataḥ || 61 ||

kuto badhyā daityagaṇā devānāmiṣṭasiddhaye |
daityeṣṭasiddhaye kasmānna badhyā devatāgaṇāḥ || 62 ||



p.722)

prakṛtyā kopanā daṇḍarājñī sā mantriṇī tathā |
pānaprakarṣato mattā vicāraḥ syāttayoḥ kutaḥ || 63 ||

mahārājñapi kāmeśavilāsaparamā tadā |
yuktā'yuktavicāreṇa svato hīnā tu sarvadā || 64 ||

mantriṇyā mattayā proktaṃ manute sarvathā hitam |
bālāyāḥ ko vicāraḥ syāt krīḍāsaktā hi sā sadā || 65 ||

aho mohasya māhātmyaṃ kimarthamasurā hatāḥ |
vyarthahiṃsā'parādhena prāpsyāmo nāśamañjsā || 66 ||

ito nirgatya tīrtheṣu gatvā vihitamārgataḥ |
daityahiṃsanadoṣasya kurmo niṣkṛtimādarāt || 67 ||

kāścidāhurvṛthā yuddhādalaṃ no duḥkhavardhanāt |
gatvā snāneṣu tapasā sādhayāmaḥ samīhitam || 68 ||

anyā ūcuralaṃ yuddhairduḥkhadairdehanāśanaiḥ ||
anurūpaṃ patiṃ prāpya krīḍāmo'virataṃ sukham || 69 ||

vadantya ityādi bahupṛthaṅ matasamāśrayāḥ |
na yudhyāmo vayaṃ vyarthaṃ tavā'jñāyāṃ na ca sthitāḥ || 70 ||

krodhaṃ kariṣyasi yadi yudhyāmastarhi vai tvayā |
vṛthā'bhimānaṃ mākārṣīrmantriṇyā mattayā saha || 71 ||

p.723)

asmān yuddhe samāsādya vimadā śīghrameṣyasi |
tatrocuranyāḥ saṃkruddhāḥ śūkarāsye sadā hi naḥ || 72 ||

kutaḥ krudhyasi netrāṇi karālānyavamuñcasi |
vraja śīghramito no cedakṣīṇi vinipātaye || 73 ||

na nirlajjā śūkarāsyā tvatsamā bhavati dvacit |
anyā'bravīt khaḍgakarā niṣatyā'bhimukhe tataḥ || 74 ||

kiṃ balāsyati me vākyaṃ śṛṇu kolā'nane yadi |
darpo'sti cenmayā yuddhe vīryādbuddhiṃ samīkuru || 75 ||

jīvitaṃ hāsyasi mudhā yuddhvā śastrahatā mayā |
iti dṛṣṭvā śaktigaṇaṃ daṇḍinyatyantavismitā || 76 ||

kuta etadakāṇḍe vai śaktīnāṃ buddhināśanam |
iti matvā drutaṃ gatvā mantriṇyai tannivedayat || 77 ||

mantriṇyapi ca śaktīnāṃ buddhināśaṃ niśāmya tu |
vismitā śrīmahārājñīmāsādya praṇatabravīt || 78 ||

mātaratyadbhuto vighno nanvasmākamupasthitaḥ |
nūnaṃ bhavettu duṣṭānāṃ daityānāmeva ceṣṭitam || 79 ||

śaktīnāṃ buddhināśo'tra dṛśyate'tibhayaṅkaraḥ |
daityāḥ sālamukhaprānte sannaddhāḥ samupasthitāḥ || 80 ||

p.724)

daṇḍanīṃ māñca cakrasthaśaktīrhitvā hyaśeṣataḥ |
bhinnasvabhāvāḥ sambhūtāḥ śaktyo roṣabhāṣaṇāḥ || 81 ||

drutaṃ vidhehi yatkṛtyaṃ nār'haḥ kālo vilambane |
yadyapyakhiladaityāśca tava bhrubhaṅgamātrataḥ || 82 ||

na bhaveyustathāpyetannā'smākaṃ sammataṃ sammataṃ bhavet |
asmāsu dayayā mātardrutaṃ pratividhatsva tat || 83 ||

iti śrīmaditihāsottame tripurārahasye māhātmyakhaṇḍe śrīlalitācaritre 
śaktisenāsu vighnayantraprayogajanitavighnakaraṇavarṇanaṃ 
nāmaikasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 5898 ||

p.725)

dvisaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ 
sarvasenāsamāgamavarṇanam  

prārthitaivaṃ śyāmalayā śrīmātā parameśvarī |
līlāṃ vitanvatī śīghraṃ saṃsmitapremabhāvataḥ || 1 ||

koṭīndukiraṇā'bhāsān śṛṅgārā'mṛtavarṣaṇān |
kaṭākṣān kāmeśamukhasaundaryābdhāvamūrcchayat || 2 ||

atho devī smitā'lokakāmeśamukhasaṅgateḥ |
gaṇanāthaḥ samudabhūdibhāsyo'ruṇasamprabhaḥ || 3 ||

gadāṃ śūlaṃ śaṅkhamabjaṃ viṣāṇaṃ dakṣabāhubhiḥ |
mātuluṅgaṃ dhanuścakraṃ pāśaṃ dhānyasya mañjarīm || 4 ||

vahan vāmakaraiḥ śuṇḍāsamāttamaṇibhājanaḥ |
raktakauśeyavasano divyāmālyavibhūṣaṇaḥ || 5 ||

trinetraścandracūḍālo maṇikoṭīramaṇḍitaḥ |
śaktyā samāśliṣṭadeho galanmadajalā'ilaḥ || 6 ||

praṇamya lalitāṃ devīṃ baddhāñjalipuṭo'bravīt |
devī brūhi mayā yattatkṛtyamatra raṇodyame || 7 ||

p.726)

tate kṛpāvaśādeva sādhyāmyatiduḥśakam |
śrutvaivaṃ prāha gaṇapaṃ lalitāmbā maheśvarī || 8 ||

gaccha vatsa daityakṛtamabhicāraṃ vināśāya |
evaṃ tayā samājñapto gaṇeśo dhūnayan karam || 9 ||

vicinvannabnhitastatra kṣaṇādyantraṃ samāsadat |
āsādya vighnayantraṃ tacchaktisaṅghasya paśyataḥ || 10 ||

vajrasāramapi krodhādbibheda radaghātataḥ |
nimiṣāttaccūrṇayitvā tilaśastadanantaram || 11 ||

jvalatsaladvārabhāgānniryayau yoddhumutsukaḥ |
niviśya daityasenāṃ tāṃ yuyudhe daityapuṅgavaiḥ || 12 ||

pipeṣa gadayā kāścit kāściściccheda cakrataḥ |
śūlena dārayaścānyān śarairanyān vibheda ca || 13 ||

amārayadviṣāṇena daityānanyān sahasraśaḥ |
evaṃ daityapatīn bhūyo gaṇeśaḥ kṣayamānayat || 14 ||

karṇatālamahāvāyuvidhūtā daityavāhinī |
nāsāvāyumahāvegavikṛṣṭāḥ saṅgha"śo'surāḥ || 15 ||

avaśā vivaśuḥ śīghraṃ śuṇḍāsuṣiramārgataḥ |
śuṇḍā'ntarbhāgasaṅghaṭṭānmṛtāśchinnā'ṅgabandhanāḥ || 16 ||

p.727)

kecinmūrcchāmanuprāptā niśvāsādbhuvi pothitāḥ |
prāṇān jahustathā'nye cā'kṣatāḥ śrotravilāyanāt || 17 ||

vinirgatāḥ karṇatālā'sphālitā jīvitaṃ jahuḥ |
evaṃ vināśayan daityāṃstatsenāyāṃ gaṇeśvaraḥ || 18 ||

bhaṇḍaputrān samādāya gadāpāpairapātayat |
viṣaṅgaṃ hṛdi dantena cakarā'tyantavikṣatam || 19 ||

viṣaṅgo rājaputrāśca yadā mūrcchāmupā'yayuḥ |
tadā dṛṣṭvā daityagaṇā matvā pratyakṣamantakam || 20 ||

hā heti bhītāḥ samaraṃ tyaktvā dūraṃ palāyitāḥ |
dṛṣṭvaivaṃ vidrutaṃ sainyaṃ viśukro'tiruṣānvitaḥ || 21 ||

āyayau rathasaṃsthāno yudhyantaṃ vighnanāyakam |
athā'bhavanmahāyuddhaṃ viśukragajavaktrayoḥ || 22 ||

śastrā'stravarṣaṇaṃ ghoraṃ parasparajayaiṣaṇam |
athebhavaktraḥ saṅkruddho gadāpātena tadratham || 23 ||

sā'śvaṃ sasārathiṃ śīghraṃ cūrṇayāmāsa saṃyuge |
atha kruddho viśukro'pi gadāmudyamya vegataḥ || 24 ||

prāharat kumbhayostasya sarvaprāṇena manyunā |
gadāprahārasambhinnakumbhadeśādgaṇeśituḥ || 25 ||

bahu sustrāva rudhiramatha kruddho gaṇādhipaḥ |
jaghāna śūlena hṛdi viśukrasyā'tivegataḥ || 26 ||

p.728)

śūlanirbhīnnahṛdayo viśukro mūrcchito'patat |
evaṃ viśukraṃ nirjitya yayau bhaṇḍarathaṃ prati || 27 ||

dṛṣṭvā'yāntaṃ niyuddhāya gaṇeśaṃ svātmanā saha |
vicārayat svāntaraṅge tamajeyaṃ vibhāvayan || 28 ||

eṣa yoddhuṃ samāyāti sindhurāsyo mayā saha |
ajeyo'yaṃ mahāvīryaḥ sarvairdevāsurairapi || 29 ||

mama saṅklpa eṣo'sti tadā śrīlalitā'mbayā |
śarstrahata imaṃ dehaṃ tyaktvā tallokamāpnuyām || 30 ||

tatra vighnamimaṃ manye kathaṃ me syāt samīhitam |
nūnameṣa purā yuddhaṃ cakre yena mahaujasā || 31 ||

dvipā'sureṇa taṃ srakṣye tenaiṣa samiyādraṇe |
tato'haṃ tāṃ samāyāsye yoddhaṃ śrīlalitāmbikām || 32 ||

iti niścitya sasṛje gajadaityaṃ mahādbhutam |
parvatā'bhaṃ mahābhīmaṃ parighodyatsupuṣkaram || 33 ||

abhidravadgaṇapatiṃ sa daityo bhaṇḍadeśitaḥ |
āsādya gajadaityaṃ taṃ yuyodha gaṇanāyakaḥ || 34 ||

evaṃ yudhyati nāgāsye vighnayantrasya nāśanāt |
śaktyaḥ prakṛtiṃ prāpya niryuryuddhagetave || 35 ||

p.729)

ālakṣya yuddhasannahaṃ bhaṇḍadaityasya sarvathā |
aśeṣadaityasenānāṃ mantriṇī daṇḍinīyutā || 36 ||

mantrayitvā mahārājñī svayaṃ yuddhāya niryayau |
aśeṣaśaktisenābhirdaśitābhiḥ parivṛtā || 37 ||

ādau sampatkarī devī rṇakolāhalā'bhidhe |
samāruhyebhasenābhirnirjagāmā'tisambhramāt || 38 ||

athā'parājitā'śavasthā niryayau parameśvarī |
aśvāruḍhā'śvasenābhirāvṛtā yuddhasambhramāt || 39 ||

tanmadhye śrīmahārājñyā pratiruddhā raṇotsukā |
bālā praṇamya lalitāṃ prāha mañjulayā girā || 40 ||

mātarmāmanujānīhi yudhāya kṛtaniścayām |
sadā yuddhavidhānāya samutsukitamānasām || 41 ||

tvayā'haṃ pratiruddhā'smi prasakte'pi mahāraṇe |
pādau mūrdhnā spṛśāmyeṣā dehyajñāṃ samarāya me || 42 ||

cirādutkaṇṭhitā yuddhe nirgamaṃ no labhāmyaham |
ādye dine'pi me yuddhaṃ nā'sīnmānasapūraṇam || 43 ||

p.730)

madhye tayā kolamukhyā vihatā yudhi vai mudhā |
adya me pūrayasvā'śāṃ cirādyuddhāya sambhṛtām || 44 ||

iti bālāvacaḥ śrutvā lalitā śrīparāmbikā |
prahasantī mantranāthāmukhaṃ samavalokayat || 45 ||

devyāśayaṃ viditvā sā kumārīṃ prāha mantriṇī |
kumāri ! śṛṇu me vākyaṃ yadi tvaṃ yoddhumicchasi || 46 ||

tattvayā samanyenaiva yoddhvyaṃ yadi manyase |
ekena saha yudhyasva daṇḍinyā ca mayā saha || 47 ||

yuddhe nau na parityajya daityasenāsu caikalā |
praveṣṭavyaṃ kvacidvā'pi tavā'vāṃ pakṣasaṃśraye || 48 ||

bhavāvastadvadatraiva kena yuddhaṃhi vāñchasi |
śrutvaivaṃ mantriṇīvākyaṃ prāha bālā'mbikā punaḥ || 49 ||

yudhyāmyahaṃ bhaṇḍaputrairna teṣvanyā kathañcana |
sarvathā bāṇamekaṃ vā visṛjedahamekala || 50 ||

yuddhvā tastānnihanmyeva na tadvāhā rathā api |
āyudhādini nāśyāni anyābhirjātu kutracit || 51 ||

p.731)

śrutvā bālāvaco bhūyo mantriṇī tāmuvāca ha |
ekena yudhyasva raṇe devyādiṣṭe kathaṃ tvayā || 52 ||

triṃśadbhirviriyate yudhaṃ nedamaupayikaṃ tava |
bhaṇḍaputra mahāśurā bhaṇḍatulyaparākramāḥ || 53 ||

bhīmasaṃhananāḥ sarve kūṭayuddhaviśāradāḥ |
ekena teṣu yudhyasva yena yoddhuṃ samīpsitam || 54 ||

iti vākyaṃ śyāmalāyāḥ śutvā bālā'mbikā tataḥ |
praṇamya bhūyastairyuddhamayācata kṛtā'ñjaliḥ || 55 ||

yācatīṃ tāṃ samālokya prītā sā lalitāmbikā |
dadāvanujñāṃ samare āśliṣya prītipūrvakam || 56 ||

atha prāha punardevīṃ bālā śrītripurāṃ tadā |
mātaranyacchṛṇu vaco daṇḍinyesā mayā saha || 57 ||

na tiṣṭhatu yuddhabhuvi bhūya eṣā purā mama |
vyanāśayadyuddharasaṃ yadyāgacchenmayā saha || 58 ||

pṛṣṭhatastiṣṭhatu na tu vighnaṃ bhūyaḥ kariṣyati |
yadi yuddhe punarvighnaṃ kariṣyati tadā śṛṇu || 59 ||

mayā parāhatāṃ yuddhe drutamenāṃ prapaśyasi |
evaṃ vadantīṃ bālāṃ tāṃ matvā ruṣṭāñca otriṇī || 60 ||

p.732)

natvā tāṃ sāntvayāmāsa mṛduvākyaiḥ supeśalaiḥ |
devi śaṅkāṃtyaja mayi āgacchāmi tvayā saha || 61 ||

niśāmayāmi te yuddhaṃ karomi tava yatpriyam |
tavā'jñāmanurudhyaiva purataḥ pṛṣṭo'pi vā || 62 ||

sthāsyāmi na hi vighnaṃ me bhaviṣyati ca te yudhi |
iti śrutvā daṇḍinīṃ sā modādāśliṣya satvaram || 63 ||

rathamāruhya tarasā niryayau samarotsukā |
mantriṇī daṇḍanāthā ca svasvasyandanasaṃsthite || 64 ||

yayatuḥ pārśvayostasyāḥ śaktisenāsamāvṛte |
atha śrīcakrarājākhyarathamāruhya sattavaram || 65 ||

yudhāya niryayā devī vīrasannāhasannatā |
tatpṛṣṭhataḥ samāruhya timirastomasamprabham || 66 ||

truragaṃ mārutajavaṃ niryayau sā tiraskṛtiḥ |
vikarṣantī śaktisenāmameyāṃ sāgaropamām || 67 ||

atha senādvayamukhambhūdbhīmaṃ susaṅgatam |
yudhavādyacitraravo nemighoṣā'timāṃsalaḥ || 68 ||

vīrāsphoṭairhuṅkṛtaiśca gajamaṇḍalacītkṛtaiḥ |
hayahveṣitasaṃrāvaivṛrvadhe'titarāṃ tadā || 69 ||

p.733)

atha śastra prapāto'bhūścaṭaścaṭaravoddhataiḥ |
lohitānāṃ mahā'sāravarṣaṇo'tibhayaṅkaraḥ || 70 ||

hato'si haṃsyase śīṃ tiṣṭha mā vidrutiṃ tyaja |
drutaṃ vīryaṃ darśya te pa"ya me'dya parākramam || 71 ||

evaṃ vacāṃsi bahudhā śruyante tatra saṅgare |
śithilāṅgāḥ śaraiḥ kecit kecit paraśudāritāḥ || 72 ||

śūlaṣvanye'bhisamprotāḥ pare khaḍgairvipāṭitāḥ |
niṣpiṣṭāḥ parighairanye cakraiśchinnagalāḥ pare || 73 ||

tomarairbheditāḥ kecidvikṣatāḥ prasapaṭṭiśaiḥ |
bhindipālaiḥ śakalitā gadābhirbhinnamastakāḥ || 74 ||

daityā abhūvan samare śaktibhirgāḍhasaṃhatāḥ |
saritaḥ śoṇitavahā vavustatra sahasraśaḥ || 75 ||

evaṃ śaktibhiratyantamarditā daityavāhinī |
palāyitā krandamānā śaktiśastrā'tivedhitā || 76 ||

asahantī śaktisenā sammardabalavattaram |
jalāśayācchinnabandhāttoyānīva kṣayaṃ gatā || 77 ||

taddṛṣṭvā vihatāṃ senāṃ daityānāṃ bhaṇḍasūnavaḥ |
caturbāhumukhāstriṃśatsaṅkhyāścitraparākramāḥ || 78 ||

p.734)

bhaṇḍatulyabalāḥ śastrāstrā'bhijñā yuddhaduḥsahāḥ |
rathā'ruḍhā śaktisenāṃ jaghnuḥ sāyakavṛṣṭibhiḥ || 79 ||

taiḥ kalitamabhūcchaktisainyaṃ saratāpanaiḥ |
śastrā'stratejo daityānāmasahyaṃ prāpya sarvataḥ || 80 ||

iti śrīmaditihāsottame tripurārahasye māhātmyakhaṇḍe śrīlalitācaritre 
sarvasenāsamāgamavarṇanaṃ nāma dvisaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 5978 ||

p.735)

trisaptatimo'dhyāyaḥ  
mahāvīrabhaṇḍaputravadhavarṇanam 

evaṃ yuddhakathāṃ śrutvā prāha kumbhabhavo muniḥ |
hayā'nana ! mahāścaryamākhyānaṃ samyagīritam || 1 ||

gaṇanāthasya kathito gajā'surasamāgamaḥ |
yuddhaṃ tayoḥ kathamabhūttanmamā'cakṣva pṛcchataḥ || 2 ||

iti pṛṣṭhaḥ kumbhabhavamuninā turagā'nanaḥ |
gaṇanāthamahāyuddhāṃ pravaktumupacakrame || 33 ||

agastya śṛṇu vakṣyāmi kathāmatyadbhutāṃ tava |
gaṇanāthamahāvīryasambhṛtāṃ saṃyutāṃ rasaiḥ || 4 ||

bhaṇḍasṛṣṭena daityena yuyodha sa gaṇeśvaraḥ |
muhūrtamātramabhavattayoryuddhaṃ suduḥsaham || 5 ||

atha daityaḥ sasarjā'nyān daityānātmaparākramān |
tulyarūpāṃstulyajavāṃstulyasāhasavikramān || 6 ||

tānanekān yudhyamānān dṛṣṭvā kruddho gaṇeśvaraḥ |
sasarja svātmanastulyān gaṇeśānapi koṭiśaḥ || 7 ||

p.736)

tatraikaikena daityena pratyekaṃ gaṇanāyakāḥ |
yuyudhuścitraśastrastraistadadbhutamivā'bhavat || 8 ||

daityāḥ śaktigaṇāścāpi tayoryuddhaṃ mahādbhutam |
dadṛśuḥ prekṣakā bhūtvā yuddhasaṃrambhavismṛteḥ || 9 ||

atha te gaṇanāthāstu jaghnurvikramya hetibhiḥ |
kaścicchūlāhataḥ  kaściścakreṇa śakalīkṛtaḥ || 10 ||

kaścidgadābhagnatuṇḍaḥ kaściddntavidāritaḥ |
kaścinnighṛṣṭa urasā kaścitpādaprapeṣitaḥ || 11 ||

kaścit kumbhā'hatirbhinnakumbhaḥ prāṇān jahau yudhi |
evaṃ te nāśitā daityā daityasṛṣṭā gajānanaiḥ || 12 ||

athaikalaḥ samabhavat punargajamahāsuraḥ |
yuyodha balavāṃstatra gaṇeśenā'tisāhasī || 13 ||

mahāgaṇeśo'pi dṛṣṭva daityaṃ pūrvavadekalam |
sañjhāra gaṇapatīn svāṅge nimiṣamātrataḥ || 14 ||

athā'bhavanmahāyuddhaṃ tayorvikramatoryudhi |
anekaśastrā'stragaṇaiḥ saṅkulaṃ subhayaṅkaram || 15 ||

atha so'pi mahādaityo māyayā'bhūnmahonnataḥ |
sahastrā'syastaddviguṇabhujaścitrā'yudhairvṛtaḥ || 16 ||

p.737)

kṣaṇe kṣaṇe ca śstrāṇāṃ sahasraṃ sa sasarja ha |
sṛṣṭaṃ sṛṣṭaṃ śastragaṇaṃ nāśayad gaṇanāyakaḥ || 17 ||

dṛṣṭvā daityasya māyāṃ tāṃ tāṃ gaṇeśo'tiruṣā'nvitaḥ |
ciccheda yugapattasya dhṛtaṃ śastrasahasrakam || 18 ||

athā'bhavadgadāyuddhaṃ gaṇeśagajadaityayoḥ |
mahatyā gadayā nūnaṃ nighnatoritaretaram || 19 ||

tadā gaṇeśagadayā tāḍitā tasya sā gadā |
śakalībhūya patitā tato daityo nirāyudhaḥ || 20 ||

muṣṭimudyamyagaṇapaṃ hantumāyānmahāsuraḥ |
dṛṣṭvā'yāntaṃ daityavaraṃ gaṇeśaḥ prākṣipadgadām | 21 ||

sā gadā prāpya taddehaṃ naiṣadrujamudāvahat |
gadāpratihatā'ṅgena papāta bhuvi sā vṛthā || 22 ||

atha ckraṃ triśūlañca parighaṃ śaktimeva ca |
kṣiptaṃ kṣiptaṃ vṛthā bhūmāvapataddehasaṅgataḥ || 23 ||

upalānāṃ vṛṣṭiriva gaṇḍaśaila ivā'bhavat |
dṛṣṭvā daityaṃ sarvaśastrairajeyaṃ sa gaṇādhipaḥ || 24 ||

cintākulaḥ samabhavattāvanmuṣṭyā jaghān saḥ |
athā'bhavattayormuṣṭiyuddhamatyantadāruṇam || 25 ||

p.738)

muṣṭisaṃhananodañcadagnivarṣaṇasaṃyutam |
taṃ tato bāhubandhena baddhā daityaṃ nipātayat || 26 ||

nipātya pādenā'kramya hṛdi dantena dārayat |
evaṃ vidārito daityo jahau prāṇāṃstadā raṇe || 27 ||

hatvaivamibhadaityaṃ taṃ punarbhaṇḍāsuraṃ prati |
yudhāyā'bhyājagāmā'śu gaṇeśo'dbhutavikramaḥ || 28 ||

tadā'yāntaṃ gaṇapatiṃ dṛṣṭvā bhaṇḍamahāsuraḥ |
cintayalllitāṃ devīṃ bhītastasya parākramāt || 29 ||

mātastvayā'yaṃ samare hato deho vinaśyatu |
iti pūraya madvāñchāṃ prapannastvatpadā'mbujam || 30 ||

tadviditvā bhaktavāñchāpūraṇāya maheśvarī |
sasmāra sindhuramukhaṃ cakrarājarathasthitā || 31 ||

viditvā saṃsmṛtīṃ devyā ājagāma gajānanaḥ |
praṇamya prāha mātaḥ kiṃ smṛto yuddhavidhau sthitaḥ || 32 ||

prāha śrutvā vacastasya lalitā parameśvarī |
vatsā'laṃ yuddhavidhinā tiṣṭha matpārśvato'dhunā || 33 ||

etā yuddhotsukā devyo yudhyantvasurasenayā |
ityājñapto gaṇeśāno virarāma niyuddhataḥ || 34 ||

p.739)

atha bhaṇḍasutaiḥ śaktisenāṃ dṛṣṭvā'tivikṣatām |
tān sayakaiḥ kirantī sā bālā yuddhe samāsadat || 35 ||

asaṅkhyādaityasenābhiryatā daityeśasūnavaḥ |
atyadbhutakriyāṃ yuddhe bālāṃ vavrūraṇā'jire || 36 ||

koṭiśo daityasubhṭā vicitrā'yudhavarṣaṇaiḥ |
bālāṃ rathasthāṃ vavṛṣurgarjanto yudhdhadurmadāḥ || 37 ||

atha śaktigaṇaṃ prāha daṇḍinī paramṃ vacaḥ |
śṛṇudhvaṃ śaktyaḥ sarvā bālāmbājñāṃ bravīmi vaḥ || 38 ||

eṣā yairyudhyati yudhi na teṣu sahasā kvacit |
prayoktavyaṃ śastragaṇaṃ daṇḍyā sā me'nyathā bhavet || 39 ||

prekṣadhvamasyāḥ samaraṃ na yoddhavyaṃ kathañcana |
iti saṅghoṣya senāsu mantriṇyā sahitā svayam || 40 ||

pārśvaṃ samāśritavatī tasyāḥ samaravaibhave |
atha śrutvā daṇḍarājñyāḥ śāsanaṃ muditā hi sā || 41 ||

asaṅkhyairdaityasubhaṭairekalā yuyudhe'dbhutam |
siddharṣidevatāmukhyāḥ paśyantyā'kāśamāśritāḥ || 42 ||

yudhyantīṃ bahubhiścaikāṃ kumārīṃ vismitā'bhavan |
atha daityotsṛṣṭaśastravṛndairācchāditā'bhavat || 43 ||

p.740)

bālasūryo'bhrapa'lairiva sā sarvato diśam |
dṛṣṭvā channāṃ śastragaṇairvimatāḥ śaktyo'bhavat || 44 ||

tataḥ kṣaṇenaiva bālā pratiśastrā'bhivarṣaṇaiḥ |
vināśya tāṃ śastravṛṣiṃ bhūyaḥ sarvān jaghāna ha || 45 ||

atha teṣāmasaṅkhyānāṃ yugapallaghuvikramā |
āyudhāni vāhanāni nāśayiścitravikramā || 46 ||

bhūyo gṛhītamātrāṇi śastrāṇyapi samācchinat |
atha tīkṣaṇaiḥ śarairdaityānnāśayat sā'tivegataḥ || 47 ||

śarā'dānañca sandhānaṃ pūraṇañca visarjanam |
na lakṣyate'tinipuṇadṛśā kenā'pi sarvathā || 48 ||

kintu tasyā dhanurmadhyādbhūvilācchalabhā iva |
prasaranti śarāstīkṣṇā dikṣvantaravihīnataḥ || 49 ||

saṅghaśo daityamūrdhānaḥ patanti tṛṇarājataḥ |
phalānīva supakvān."caṭamāaravāḥ || 50 ||

kecicchinnakarā'ṅghyrūruvakṣaḥkukṣigalāḥ pare |
nirantaratayā viddhāḥ sahasrākṣa ivā'bhavan || 51 ||

anye ca khaṇḍitā daityāsthilaśaḥ kīrṇadehakāḥ |
kecidvakṣasi saṃviddhā hṛtaprāṇāḥ sthiraṃ sthitāḥ || 52 ||

p.741)

dhṛtaśastrāḥ pradṛśyante likhitāḥ subhaṭā iva |
keciccharā'hatervegānnīyante gatajīvanāḥ || 53 ||

yudhdhakātarabhāvena palāyanaparā iva |
evaṃ tayā muhūrtena koṭiśo daityapuṅgavāḥ || 54 ||

gatā'savo'bhavan peturvikṣatāste śatottarāḥ |
na tatra daityaḥ śūro'pi kaścit svātmā'vane vibhuḥ || 55 ||

kva yuddhaṃ kva ca vā śastrapātanaṃ kartumarhati |
na daityā yuddhamatyantamekākinya viduḥ kvacit || 56 ||

koṭiśaḥ śaktisenānāṃ yuddhaṃ jānīyurañjasā |
śaktisenā nabhaḥsaṃsthā devādyāśca samantataḥ || 57 ||

dṛṣṭvā bālāvikramaṃ tamāścaryaṃ paramaṅgatāḥ |

bhītāḥ śastrāṇī nikṣipya palāyanaparā'bhavan |
hā hatāḥsmo na ceyaṃ vai yoṣidbālā kumārikā || 59 ||

pratyakṣamantakaḥ prāpta evamasmān vihiṃsitum |
adya niḥśeṣitaṃ sainyaṃ daityānāṃ bhavati kṣaṇāt || 60 ||

śaradhārāśakalitāḥ petudaityāstu saṅghaśaḥ |
kecchiraiḥ susaṃviddhāḥ patitāḥ "śavarāśiṣu || 62 ||

p.742)

bhītā niviśya svātmānamarakṣanmṛtyubhītitaḥ |
evaṃ tadā daityasainyaṃ prāyo naṣṭaṃ palāyitam || 63 ||

śiṣṭañcā'pi niśāmyā'tha bhaṇḍaputrāḥ samāyayuḥ |
rathāḥ siṃvāhā mahācāpadharā bhṛśam || 64 ||

krudhā ekaprapātena bālāṃ vavruḥ samantataḥ |
mahāmeghā iva śyāmā garjantī mṛgarājavat || 65 ||

varṣantaḥ śaradhārābhirbālāṃ sūryamivā'mbare |
tairanekaiḥ kumārī sā viṣṇutulyaparākramaiḥ || 66 ||

saṅgatā yudhi dṛṣṭā'bhūllīlayā tān yuyodha ca |
mantriṇīdaṇḍinīmukhyā dṛṣṭvā bālāparākramam || 67 ||

viṣmitāḥ śrīparādevyai samācakhyurmahāraṇam | 
devi nūnaṃ sā kumārī na śeṣayitumarhati || 68 ||

bhaṇḍaṃ tadbhrātarau vā'.pi putrān senāgatānapi |
līlayaivā'dya niḥśeṣaṃ kariṣyati na saṃśayaḥ || 69 ||

evaṃ yuddhvā kṣaṇaṃ mandaṃ bhaṇḍaputraiḥ pratāpanaiḥ |

teṣāṃ sāyakavṛṣṭiṃ tāṃ vidhūya prativṛṣtibhiḥ | 
śarāsanaṃ kramātteṣāṃ ciccheda supatatribhiḥ || 71 ||

p.743)

atha te roṣitāḥ khaḍgagadāpramukhamāyudham |
parāmṛśurnihantuṃ tāṃ yāvattavadiyaṃ drutam || 72 ||

āttamāttaṃ praciccheda tadadbhutamivā'bhavat |
evaṃ nirāyudhā daityā jātāstasyāḥ parākramam || 73 ||

atyadbhutaṃ menire te cintāñcā'purmahattarām |
evaṃ bālamahākālagrastān yudhi niśāmya vai || 74 ||

viṣaṅgaśca viśukraśca mahāsinyaparīvṛtau |
rakṣituṃ tān rājaputrānabhyāyayaturāśu vai || 75 ||

mā vibhyā'gatāvāvāṃ vadantāviti sā śivā |
dadṛśe śakhavarṣāṇi kurvantau kālameghavat || 76 ||

dṛṣtvā bālā'tiharṣeṇa tāṃ vṛṣṭiṃ śastrasaṅkulām |
nivārayallīlayaiva tatastāṃ daityavāhinīm || 77 ||

k'ṇena vāyavyā'streṇa mahāvāyurghaṭaniva |
nāmaśeṣāṃ kṛtavatī tatastāvatiroṣitau || 78 ||

āyāntāvativegena gadākhaḍgakarāvubhau 
dṛṣṭvā śarairvāhanebhān yuddhe'nāśayadañjasā || 79 ||
