tānivṛttān samālokya virathānakarot kṣaṇāt || 81 ||

p.744)

pādacārānnihatyā'su jaṅghayorniśitaiḥ śaraiḥ |
prāhare bhaṇḍadāyādāḥ śūrāṇāṃ vaḥ kathaṃ tvidam || 82 ||

darśanaṃ yudhi pṛṣṭhasya yogyaṃ brūta palāyatām |
śutvā kaṭurasodarkaṃ bhāṣitaṃ te'timāninaḥ || 83 ||

brūyurmanyujvalannetrā nivṛttāḥ sammukhe raṇe |
dhiṅmugdhe bālikāṃ dṛṣṭvā dayamānasvabhāvataḥ || 84 ||

jīvasyasmān samāsādya mṛtyukalpān kathañcana |
tat paśya sadyastvāṃ kurmo vimadāṃ tat stharībhava || 85 ||

ityuktvāmuṣṭimudyamya dhāvamānān svasammukhe |
pratyekaṃ niśitairbhallaistacchirāṃsi cakartaha || 86 ||

pakvatālaphalānīva petustanmastakānyatha |
hāhākāraḥ samudabhūd daityānāṃ vāhinīmukhe || 87 ||

vavarṣa bālāṃ kusumaiḥ sugandhibhiḥ sureśvaraḥ |
jayaśabdo mahānāsīcchaktisenāsu sarvataḥ || 88 ||

iti śrīmaditihāsottame tripurārahasye māhātmayakhaṇḍe śrīlalitācaritre 
bhaṇḍaputravadhakaraṇavarṇanaṃ trisaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 6066 ||

p.745)

catuḥsaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ 
ubhayasenāsamāgamavarṇanam

evam hateṣu bhaṇḍasya putreṣu samarotsukām |
bālāṃ bhūyaḥ samālakṣya mantriṇīdaṇḍanāyike || 1 ||

ūcaturdevi śrīrājñīṃ dṛṣṭvā natvā nivedya ca |
anujñātā punaryuddhaṃ yāsyāmaḥ kartumañjasā || 2 ||

tāvaddaityāḥ samāyāntu samarāya sudaṃśitāḥ |
tvamasādhyaṃ kṛtavatī naitadasmacchakaṃ kvacit || 3 ||

ityuktvā tāṃ samādāya gatvā śrīmātṛsannidhim |
āruhya cakrarājākhyarathaṃ natvā parāmbikām || 4 ||

ūcaturvikramaṃ yuddhe bālāyā adbhutaṃ mahat |
vijayañca tataḥ śrutvā tuṣṭā śrīlalitāmbikā || 5 ||

āśliṣya bālāṃ suprītyā pārśve ca suniveśayat |
bhaṇḍaputrān hatān dṛṣṭvā hā hetyuccairvicukruśuḥ || 6 ||

palāyitāśca paritaḥ śuṣyadvaktrā hataprabhāḥ |
putranāśaṃ niśamyā'tha bhaṇḍadaityo'tiduḥkhitaḥ || 7 ||

p.746)

punarvimṛśya sadbuddhyā dhairyamālambya satvaram |
aho me vipulo mohaḥ smṛtibhraṃśaḥ samudgataḥ || 8 ||

kiṃ śocāmi vṛthaivā'haṃ devyā me'bhimataṃ kṛtam 
yathā prārthitaṃ me tattathaiva kṛtaṃ tathā || 9 ||

nūnaṃ madarthaṃ śoceyurete tasmānmamā'grataḥ |
yāntu pañcatvamatha vai tayā devyā raṇe hatāḥ || 10 ||

prāpnomi tallokagatiṃ parīvāreṇa saṃyutaḥ |
iti me prāgvyavasitaṃ prārthitā ca parāmbikā || 11 ||

atha sā bhaktkāmānāṃ dogdhro kāmaprapūraṇam |
cakre tatra mahāmoho vṛthā kiṃ māmupāgataḥ || 12 ||

tadalaṃ me vimohena devi ! tvāṃ śaraṇāgataḥ |
tanme'vaśeṣitaṃ kāmaṃ pūryā'śu maheśvari ! || 13 ||

saṃhṛtya śiṣṭāṃ senāṃ me bhrātṛbhyāṃ sahitāṃ tataḥ |
māṃ sayoṣidgaṇaṃ śīghraṃ yuddhe śastrāgnipāvitam || 14 ||

vidhāya svapadāmbhojasavidhaṃ netumarhasi |
kadā'haṃ candracūḍālaṃ netratrayasuśobhanam || 15 ||

krodhāruṇitanetrāntasabhrūkuṭiladarśanam |
paśyan śrīmātṛvadanaṃ dagdhastatsāyakā'gninā || 16 ||

p.747)

vrajāmyaśokamamṛtaṃ sthānaṃ yogisudurlabham |
prārthayanniti tadrūpaṃ koṭikandarpasundaram || 17 ||

dhyāyan śrīlalitādevyā nirvikalpo'bhavat kṣaṇam |
tāvattatrā'jagmatustau daityarājasya brātarau || 18 ||

taṃ dadarśaturatyantaniśceṣṭaṃ sthāṇuvatsthitam |
amaṃsatuḥ putraśokabharākrāntaṃ sumūrcchitam || 19 ||

samādhanavacastatra procatuḥ kālasammitam |
tayoḥ śamaparaiḥ śabdairdhyeyānnirgatamānasaḥ || 20 ||

unmīlya netre purato dadarśa bhrātarau sthitau |
svarūpagopanārthaṃ sa brātroḥ sammukhataḥ  sthitaḥ || 21 ||

putrān śuśoca nitarāṃ manasā tau hasaṃstadā |
hā putrā māṃ parityajya pitaraṃ duḥkhasāgare || 22 ||

majjntaṃ dīnamatyantaṃ kva yātāstyaktsauhṛdāḥ |
mātaro vo'tivātsalyakātarā īdṛśīṃ daśām || 23 ||

śrutvā kathaṃ bhaveyustā vidīrṇahṛdayā iva |
ityādi vilapantaṃ taṃ dṛṣṭvā tau bhrātarau tadā || 24 ||

śrīdevīmāyayā mugdhau daityeśaṃ procaturvacaḥ |
alaṃ daityeśa ! śokena dhairyavīryā'pahāriṇā || 25 ||

p.748)

śocantaṃ tvāṃ samālokyā'saṅkhyātabhuvaneśvaram |
jvaro nau dehaṃ karīṣamiva pāvakaḥ || 26 || 

muhūrtaṃ dairyamālambya rājaṃstvaṃ susthiro bhavaḥ |
gatvā'vāmekapātena tāṃ hatvā niśitaiḥ śariḥ || 27 ||

putraghnīṃ lalitā'khyātāṃ mārīṃ daityeṣvivā'gatām |
jīvantīṃ vā mṛtāṃ vā.pi vaddhvā neṣyāva āśu vai || 28 ||

ityuktvā saśaraṃ cāpamādāya syandanā'śrayau |
yuddhārthamabhyāyayaturativīryaparākramau || 29 ||

dviśatā'kṣauhiṇīsenāparīvārau sudaṃśitau |
pṛthak śaktimahāsenāpārśvabhāge prapetatuḥ || 30 ||

cakrarājarathavaradakṣapārśve'tivegataḥ |
śatā'kṣauhiṇīsenābhirviṣaṅgaḥ prāpatadbalī || 31 ||

viśukraścāpi vāmena tathā nyapatadañjasā |
tṛtīye yuddhadivase prātaḥ sūryodayaṃ prati || 32 ||

akasmācchaktisenāyāḥ pakṣayoḥ patitau yadā |
tadābhavanmahāśabdaḥ śaktisenāsu pārśvayoḥ || 33 ||

tadā śrutvā yuddhaśabdaṃ mantriṇīdaṇḍanāyike |
abhūtāṃ śaṅkite'tyantamūcatuḥ śrīparāmbikām || 34 ||

devi daityaśaktisenāsamāgamamahādhvaniḥ |
śruyate bhairavataro daityāḥ kapa'yodhinaḥ || 35 ||

p.749)

yudhyantyakāṇḍe sannaddhā akṛtvā saṃvidaṃ purā |
tadyāva āvāmanujānīhi śīghraṃ maheśvari || 36 ||

anujñāte tato natvā mantriṇīdaṇḍanāyike |
celatuḥ samarāyā'śu pṛthak śaktigaṇāvṛte || 37 ||

devyā dakṣiṇapār"śvasthā daṇḍinī caṇḍavikramā |
niryayau śrīcakrarājarathadakṣiṇadrārataḥ || 38 ||

kiricakraratahṃ pañcaparvaśo'bhisamūnnatam |
mahāsiṃhayugaṃ pārśve sairibhāggraṃ tathā puraḥ || 39 ||

yojitaṃ bhairavo yatra rathanetā svayaṃ shtitah |
tatparśve siṃhasaṃvāharathāruḍhe vicelatuḥ || 40 ||

svapneśvarī tathā kālasaṅkarṣā yudhasambhramā |
baṭukaiḥ kālikāvṛndairvarāhamukhaśaktibhiḥ || 41 ||

asaṅkhyābhiḥ parikrāntā niryayau kadanāya sā |
evaṃ cakrarathadvārāduttarasmāśca mantriṇī || 42 ||

vinirgatya geyacakraratharāje mahonnate |
saptaparvayute vedaharidaśvapracālite || 43 ||

hasantī śyāmalā tatra rathanetryabhavat parā |
śukādyā sārikādyā ca śyāmalā dakṣapārśvagā || 44 ||

saṅgītasāhityapūrve śyāmale vāmapārśvage |
laghu"yāmalikā pṛṭhe caivametā rathā'śrayāḥ || 45 ||

p.750)

parito geyacakrātmarathasyā'śu viniryayuḥ |
mantriṇyaṅgasasudbhūtāḥ śyāmalāḥ koṭikoṭiśaḥ || 46 ||

rathāśrayāḥ pracelurvai yuddhasambhārasambhṛtāḥ |
sarvāḥ ṣoḍaśavārṣikyastāruṇyaśrīniṣevitāḥ || 47 ||

raktakauśeyavasanā navaratnavibhūṣaṇā |
kaṭākṣamandahāsābhyāṃ mohayantyo'khilaṃ jagat || 48 ||

kāpiśāyanapānodyanmdadaghurṇitalocanāḥ |
kāvyā'lāparasā'bhijñā gītavādyavinoditāḥ || 49 ||

narmakrīḍāparāścā'tihāsyalīlāparāyaṇāḥ |
mantranāthā vāhinī sā suśubhe'titarāṃ tadā || 50 ||

tāpicchavanamāleva vikasaścārumañjarī |
athā'bhavacchaktisenādaityasenāsamāgamaḥ || 51 ||

udvelayoḥ sāgarayoriva saṅgamanaṃ bhuvi |
atha dhvanadyuddhavadyahuṅkārā'sphoṭani'svanaḥ || 52 ||

rathaghaoṣaturaṅgoścaheṣāgambhīramāṃsalaḥ |
senayorubhayostatra badhirīkṛtadiṅmukhaḥ || 53 ||

śaktisaṅghamahāmeghanirmukteṣupravarṣaṇaiḥ |
daityasenāmukhataṭaṃ vidīrṇaṃ śatadhā'bhavat || 54 ||

p.751)

daityasenābhinnamukhadvāreṇa viviśurdrutam |
daityasenāhṛdaṃ śaktisenāstrotaḥ samantataḥ || 55 ||

niviśya daityasenāṃ tāḥ śaktyo balavattarāḥ |
"śastrā'gnibhirdaityasenāvanaṃ cakrurhi bhasmasāt || 56 ||

keciccharaiḥ sunicitāḥ kecitparighaprothitāḥ |
anye kṛpāṇinā cchinnanikhilāṅgā gatā'savaḥ || 57 ||

pare cakravikṛttā'ṅgāḥ paraśucchinnakandharāḥ |
itare śūlasūtreṣu protā maṇigaṇā iva || 58 ||

apare śaktipramukhairbhinnamūrdhodarā'bhavan |
evaṃ vināśyamānāyāṃ senāyāṃ śaktisenayā || 59 ||

dṛṣṭvā viśukrohyatyantaṃ roṣāgnijvalitekṣaṇaḥ | 
rathenā'bhyaderavacchaktivāhinīṃ śastravarṣaṇaiḥ || 60 ||

jīmūta iva varṣāu dhārāḥ śaramayāstataḥ |
śaktisenā'pi yuyudhe viśukre ca balīyasī || 61 ||

atha putrā vi"ukrasya pitṛtulyaparākramāḥ |
jīmūtakāyo devāriḥ karambhaḥ śikhibhāṣaṇa || 62 ||

niviśya śaktisenā'ntarjaghnustā balavattarāḥ |
viśukrātmajasaṅkhinnāṃ niśāmya śaktivāhinīm || 63 ||

caturdikṣu kālyamānāṃ śukādiśyāmalāmukhāḥ |
viśukraputrānāsedurvarpantya iṣusantatim || 64 ||

p.752)

tatra jīmūtakāyena yuyodha śukaśyāmalā |
ciraṃ yuddhvā śaraistatra rathaṃ sūtaṃ śarāsanam || 65 ||

ciccheda nimiṣārdhena tataḥ prakupito'suraḥ |
jaghāna tīkṣṇadhāreṇa khaḍgena rathavāhanam || 66 ||

tasyā rathāśvo nihataḥ praskhaladbhinnamastakaḥ |
tāvacchukaśyāmalikā ardhacandreṇuṇā javāt || 67 ||

apātayacchiro dehāt pakvatālaphalaṃ yathā |
devāriṇā śārikākhyā saṅgatā yuddhakarmaṇi || 68 ||

cchinnadhvajarathāśvaṃ taṃ yudhyantaṃ gadayā dṛḍham |
svakīyagadayā'plutya jaghānā'suramastake || 69 ||

bhinnamūdhḥ nipatito devārirgatajīvanaḥ |
saṅgītaśyāmalā yuddhaṃ karambheṇa cakāra ha || 70 ||

yuddhvā ciraṃ tena saha khaḍgena śira ācchinat | 
sāhitya"yāmalā yudhdhvā balinaṃ śikhibhāṣaṇam || 71 ||

śastrā'straistraṃ vinirjitya prākṣipadvakṣasi drutam |
triśūlaṃ tena dalitahṛdayaḥ prāpa tadvyasuḥ || 72 ||

yāvatputrā niyudhyanti viśukrastāvadāśu vai | 
channarūpaḥ "yāmalāya rathapārṣṇiṃ samāsadat || 73 ||

tatra coravadāyāntaṃ gadāhastaṃ mahābalam |
dṛṣṭvā laghuśyāmalākhyā sannidhānaṃ samāgatam || 74 ||

p.753)

mantreśyā rathavaryantamārurukṣantamañjasā |
jaghāna mūrghni tarasā khaḍgenā'tibalīyasā || 75 ||

khaḍgaprapātatastasya mukuṭaṃ śakalīkṛtam |
viśukro bhraṣṭamukuṭaḥ krodhāruṇitalocanaḥ || 76 ||

gadāmādāya vegena hantuṃ tāmabhisaṃyayau |
tāvacchareṇa daityasya gadāṃ ciccheda saptadhā || 77 ||

nirāyudho viśukro'pi muṣṭinā rathavājinām |
tasyā jaghāna tāvat sā prāharanmūrghni sāyakaiḥ || 78 ||

sāyakairvidalanmūrdhā papāta bhuvi mūrchitaḥ |
īśatprajñastāvadenaṃ grahītauṃ laghuśyāmalā || 79 ||

rathādavaplutya śīghraṃ bhāhubhyāṃ taṃ parāmṛśat |
pratibuddhastāvadasau matvā svasya hi bandhanam || 80 ||

muṣṭinā'bhyahanaddevīṃ sā'pīṣatkaśmalaṃ yayau |
tāvaj jñātvā rathasthānāḥ śaktyo ripumantike || 81 ||

koṭiśastaṃ samutpeturvāhinī śaktyo'pi ca |
tāvanmatvā śaktigaṇaṃ dusahaṃ daityaśekharaḥ || 82 ||

utplutya svarathaṃ prāpto daityasenāmukhasthitam |
tatra dṛṣṭvā mrṭān putrān śokasaṃvignamānasaḥ || 83 ||

bilspys kiñcidatyantaroṣāruṇitalocanaḥ | 
prajvalan śaktisenāṃ tāṃ vidhamaccharavarṣaṇaiḥ || 84 ||

dṛśtvā viśukraṃ śaktīnāṃ senāprāṇā'pahāriṇam |
mantriṇī sāyakā'sārairdhanurjīmūtani'sṛtaiḥ || 85 ||

vavarṣa nīlaśailoścaśṛṅgaṃ parjanyavadbhṛśam | 
tīkṣṇasāyakadhārābhirviśukro'tyantavikṣataḥ || 86 ||

dehakṣatebhyo'titarāmasṛgdhārāṃ mumoca vai |
nīlādrirvṛṣṭidhārābhirgairikārasavatkṣṇam || 87 ||

mantriṇyā gāḍhasaṃviddho roṣāt prajvalītā'kṛtiḥ |
vicitraśastraśalabhairācchādayata mantriṇīm || 88 ||

vireje mantriṇīcchannā daityaśastramadhuvrataiḥ | 
tamālamañjarīvā'timakarandasubṛṃhitā || 89 ||

kṣaṇena daityaśastrāṇi nivārya śaravarṣaṇaiḥ |
ākārṇapūrṇabhallena dhanurjyāmācchinaddrutam|| 90 ||

yāvajjyāmaparāṃ cāpe niveśayitumīhate |
tāvattikṣṇapṛṣatkena muṣṭideśe jaghāna tam || 91 ||

gāḍhabiddhadaityamuṣṭerdaityasya dhanurāpatat |
patitaṃ dhanurādātuṃ yāvannamro mahāsuraḥ || 92 ||

tāvadanyenā'bhihanat patriṇā mūrghnī | 
sa tāvadiṣuṇā bhinnamūrdhā gāḍhasumūrcchayā || 93 ||

p.755)

apatat svarathopasthe vajrāhata ivā'drirāṭ |
atha kṣaṇena mantriṇyā nāśitaṃ daityavāhanam || 94 ||

sārathiśchtramukuṭe śastrasaṅghṃ śaraiḥ pṛthak |
tāvanmūrcchāvinirmukto dadarśā'havasādhanam || 95 ||

sākalyena vinaśṭaṃ tat kruddha utplutya tāṃ ruṣā |
śīrṇa rathāṅgamādāya nihantumupasaṃyayau || 96 ||

tāvaddakṣabhuje'tyantavegāccharamapātayat |
śareṇā'bhihatāddhastādrathaṅgaṃ prāpatat kṣitau || 97 ||

tato muṣṭiṃ samudyamya ratha āplutya satvaram |
mantriṇīhastagaṃ cāpraṃ bhaṅktuṃ yāvat samācchinat || 98 ||

tāvattikṣṇā'sina kāyācchirastasya nipatayat |
dṛṣṭvā viśukraṃ nihataṃ vidrutā daityavāhinī || 99 ||

pariśiṣṭā'tivitrastā śastrāṇyutsṛjya sarvataḥ |
jayaśabdaṃ śaktigaṇāścakrurdevamukhā api || 100 ||

iti śrīmaditihāsottame tripurārahasye māhātmyakhaṇḍe lalitāmāhātmye 
viśukravadhavarṇanaṃ nāma catuḥsaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 6166 ||

p.756)

pañcasaptatimo'dhyāyaḥ 
viṣaṅgavadhavarṇanm  

viṣaṅgenā'pi vārāhī saṅgatā samare yadā |
tadā daityaśakticamborabhavadbhairavo raṇaḥ || 1 ||

śaktīnāṃ khaḍgacakrā'siparighaprāsatomaraiḥ |
baṭukānāṃ triśulādyardetyā bhūyo'bhivikṣatāḥ || 2 ||

tilaśaḥ chinnavarṣmāṇi jahuḥ prāṇān raṇā'jire |
dṛṣtvā daityacamūṃ śaktigaṇaiḥ prāyo vināśitām || 3 ||

kruddhā'bhidudruvuḥ śakticamuḥ parvatasannibhāḥ |
viṣaṅgaputrāḥ proddaṇḍavikramā vīryavattarā || 4 ||

karālavaktro viśikho vibhāṇḍo vikaṭekṣaṇaḥ |
indraśatruḥ surā'rātirmahākukṣirmadoddhataḥ || 5 ||

ete'ṣtau bhīmabalino śaktīrjaghnuḥ samantataḥ |
tairhanyamānāṃ tāṃ śaktivāhinīmabhivīkṣya tu || 6 ||

abhyāyayustānniyoddhuṃ jambhinī stambhinī tathā |
andhinī mohinī caiva bhairavaḥ kālakarṣiṇī || 7 ||

p.757)

svapneśvarī ca baṭuka ityaṭau suvibhīṣaṇāḥ |
karālavaktriṇa yudhi jambhinī saṅgatā'bhavat || 8 ||

yuddhvā ciraṃ tathā sākaṃ śastrāstrairdaityapuṅgavaḥ |
gadayā'bhijadhānā'śu varāhaṃ śaktivāhanam || 9 ||

tāḍito jambhinīvāho śūkaro bhinnamastakaḥ |
īṣat papāta purato jānubhyāmavaniṅgataḥ || 10 ||

tāvatkruddhā jambhanī ca khaḍgena śira āharat |
viśikhaḥ stambhanīṃ prāpya yuyodhā'tibalī dṛḍham || 11 ||

stambhinī hariṇā'ruḍhā viśikhairviśikhaṃ tribhiḥ |
jaghāna tairmūghni lagnairviśikho'tivyarājata || 12 ||

triśṛṅga iva daityendraḥ susrāva bahuśoṇitam |
so'pi kruddho'tivegena tīkṣṇadhāreṇa karṇinā || 13 ||

dhanurjyāmācchinadbhūyo vikīrya niśitaiḥ śaraiḥ |
tāvatkruddhā stambhinī ca gārdhrpatreṇ tejinā || 14 ||

tadvāhano'ṭramanayacchittvā'ntakapuraṃ prati |
atha tasya kṣaṇenaiva niśitairvisikhaistribhiḥ || 15 ||

niṣaṅgaṃ mukuṭaṃ cāpaṃ ciccheda yugapadyudhi |
sa chinnadhanvā'tiruṣā triśūlamatibhāsvaram || 16 ||



p.758)

stambhinīṃ praticikṣepa tāvat sā laghuvikramā |
chittvā triśūlaṃ bhallena śiro daityasya pātayata || 17 ||

andhinyā yuyudhe tatra bibhāṇḍo balagarvitaḥ |
śastrairastraranekaiśca kṛtapratikṛtairaram || 18 ||

andhinī mahiṣā'ruḍhā vibhāṇḍaḥ kharavāhanaḥ |
cirāṃ yuddhvā tena tatra karṇāntā'kṛṣṭapatriṇā || 19 ||

jaghāna daityamurasi tathā viddho vibhāṇḍakaḥ |
papāta mūrcchito gāḍhaṃ chinnapakṣa ivā'drirāṭ || 20 ||

atha tadvāhanaḥ kharo parvatābho'tidhūsaraḥ |
dvāśabdaṃ bhīṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā samunnamya ca bāladhim || 21 ||

niviṣṭaḥ śaktisenāsu saṃstabdhāṅgaruho balī |
kāścitpratyakpādaghātairdaśanaiścā'vadhūnanaiḥ || 22 || 

anyāḥ svagativegena kharaḥ śaktīrnipātayat |
evaṃ śaktīrvinighnantaṃ kharaṃ dṛṣṭvā'ndhinī tadā || 23 ||

śareṇā'karṇapūrṇena jaghānā'tiruṣā'nvitā | 
sa vidvastīkṣṇabāṇena mūrcchitaḥ prapatadbhuvi || 24 ||

tāvanmūrcchāvinirmukto vibhāṇḍo'tyantaroṣitaḥ |
vegena gadayā tasyā vāhanaṃ tāḍayadbalī || 25 ||

andhinīvāhamahiṣastāḍito gadayā bhṛśam |
vidrutya manyujvalitastaṃ śṛṅgābhyāṃ samākṣipata ||

p.759)

vibhāṇḍo mahiṣā'kṣipta ākāśaṃ prāpya vegataḥ |
parato yojanādbhūmau papāta suvimūrcchitaḥ || 27 ||

tāvadabhyāyayau tasya vāho rāsabhaśekharaḥ |
muñcan suśīghraṃ kvāśabdaṃ saghargharamahāravam || 28 ||

āgatya mahiṣaṃ bhūyaḥ parāvṛtya suvegataḥ |
pāścātyapādaghātena santāḍya balavattaram || 29 ||

dadaṃśa taṃ punaḥ kaṇṭhe tāvat kruddhaḥ sa sairibhaḥ |
śṛṅgābhyāṃ dārayat kukṣiṃ bhūmāvāsphālya vegataḥ || 30 ||

nirgatā'ntraḥ kharo mṛtyuṃ prāpa niryātitekṣaṇaḥ |
vimūrcchastāvadabhyetya vibhāṇḍo'bhihanat padā || 31 ||

mahiṣaṃ tāvadiṣuṇā'ndhinī daityasya mastakam |
apāharadatikrodhāt kṛttamūrddhāmamāra saḥ || 32 ||

yuyodha mohinī daityaṃ śastrastrairvikaṭekṣaṇam |
mohinī garuḍāruḍhā gṛghrastho vikaṭekṣaṇaḥ || 33 ||

suparṇagṛghrayorbhīmamāsīidyuddhaṃ nabho'ṅgaṇe |
sapakṣayoḥ parvatayorivā'tyantavicitritam || 34 ||

tadā suparṇapakṣābhyāṃ prothito gṛghraśekharaḥ |
nakhaistuṇḍairdāritāṇgo mamāra patito'vanau || 35 ||

p.760)

vikaṭākṣo hate vāhe kruddho yuddhvā ciraṃ tayā |
mohinīcakrasamcchinnagalo mṛtyumupāgataḥ || 36 ||

indraśatruḥ sarpasaṃstho bhairaveṇā'bhisaṅgataḥ |
svavāhenā'tibhīmena yuyodhā'tyantasāhasī || 37 ||

bhairavaḥ kālameghābhaḥ svasamākārabhairavaiḥ |
koṭisaṅkhyāgaṇayutairaṣṭabhiḥ  sevito'niśam || 38 ||

sarve triśūladaṇḍāḍhyāstriṇetrāścandraśekharāḥ |
kālameghanibhāḥ sarpabhūṣaṇāḥ kṛttivāsasaḥ || 39 ||

indraśatroścamūṃ bhīmāṃ daśākṣauhiṇīsammitām |
niviśya te nimeṣeṇa niḥśeṣāñcakrurojasā || 40 ||

kecicchūleṣu samprotāḥ keciddaṇḍairvidāritāḥ |
kecinnakhaiḥ pāṭitāṇgāḥ kecinmuṣṭibhirāhatāḥ || 41 ||

dadhurdaityā'ntramālāṃ te bairavāṇāṃ gaṇāstadā |
evaṃ dṛṣṭvā pranaṣṭāṃ svāmindraśatruścamūṃ ruṣā || 42 ||

gadayā bhairavagaṇaṃ mardayāmāsa sarvataḥ |
evaṃ gaṇānardyamānān vīkṣya daityena saṃyuge || 43 ||

manthānādyaparāhvānā asitāṅgādibhairavāḥ |
indraśatruṃ parvatābhaṃ yuyudhuḥ sarpavāhanam || 44 ||

p.761)

tān yodhayāmāsa daityaḥ sarvāneko raṇā'jire |
vajrasaṃhanaṃ tasya śūladaṇḍaprapātanaiḥ || 45 ||

akṣataṃ sarvathā yadvadgaṇḍaśailaḥ phalairhataḥ |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā virujaṃ daityaṃ bhairavāṇāṃ bhayaṅkaram || 46 ||

yuyudhe tena samare bhairavāṇāmadhī"śvaraḥ |
yuddhvā ciramutpatantaṃ gadayā hantumambaram || 47 ||

āvidhyā hṛdi śūlena bhuvi taṃ vyasumākṣipat |
yuddhvā surā'rātirapi kālakarṣṇikā'hvyām || 48 ||

kākasaṣtho lulāyā'dhiruḍhayā suciraṃa balī |
tacchaktigaṇaniḥśeṣīkṛtadaityamahāgaṇaḥ || 49 ||

tadaṅkuśaprahāreṇa bhinnamūrdhā mamāra ha |
svapneśyā yuyudhe daityo mahākukṣirvṛkāśrayaḥ || 50 ||

rathādhiruḍhayā senā nāśitā śaktisaṅghakaiḥ |
ciraṃ yodhayatastasya mahākukṣerbalīyasaḥ || 51 ||

vidārya kukṣiṃ khaḍgena nāśayaddatyapuṅgavam |
madoddhato'pi baṭukabhairave' susaṅgataḥ || 52 ||

baṭukaiḥ svasamākārairdaśakoṭimitaistathā |
mahākapāladaṇdāḍhyaiḥ sarpabhūṣaṇasaṃyutaiḥ || 53 ||

pañcahāyanakairmattairdigvastrairaruṇekṣaṇaiḥ |
tamastomanibhaiścandracūḍairyuktena sarvataḥ || 54 ||

daśākṣauhiṇikāyukto yuyodhā'titarāṃ balī | 
baṭukānāṃ gaṇāḥ kruddhā niviśyā'suravāhinīm || 55 ||

daṇḍairnihitya tarasā nāśaṃ ninyuryathā'śu naḥ |
atha kruddhaṃ daityapatiṃ nipatantaṃ gadādharam || 56 ||

baṭukeśo jaghānoścairmūrghni daṇḍena līlayā |
daṇḍā'hatyā dalanmūrdhā vaman raktaṃ saphenilam || 57 ||

patitvā'śu viniśceṣṭo mamārā'suraśekharaḥ |
evaṃ viṣaṅgaḥ putreṣu naṣṭeṣvatyantaduḥkhitaḥ || 58 ||

krodhamāhārayattivraṃ viṃśatyakṣauhiṇīyutaḥ |
nipapātaikapātena pralayā'nilavajjavāt || 59 ||

kiricakropari tadā dhvanadvāditraniḥsvanaḥ |
śastradhārāṃ vimuñcan vai kurvan satimirā diśaḥ || 60 ||

jvaladvahnimahākūṭe śalabhānāṃ gaṇā iva |
atha jambhinimukhyābhirnāśitā kṣaṇamātrataḥ || 61 ||

viṣaṅgasainyaṃ śastrāgnijvālābhiḥ śalabhāniva |
kecittriśūlasphuṭitahṛdayā jīvitaṃ jahuḥ || 62 ||

pare daṇḍavikīrṇāṅgā anye parighaprothitāḥ |
apare sāyakaiśchannā tato'nye muṣṭidāritāḥ || 63 ||

p.763)

khḍgacchinnāṅghribāhūrukandharādyā apītare |
kecicchastraughasampātaśakalīkṛtadehakāḥ || 64 ||

evaṃ vinaṣṭe sainye sve viṣaṅgaḥ krodhamūrcchitaḥ |
nihatya gadyā śaktisenāṃ bhittvā hyaśeṣataḥ || 65 ||

daṇḍarājñīrathavahaṃ mahiṣañca mṛgādhipau |
gadayaiva hi santāḍay ratharājaṃ samāruhat || 66 ||

ārohantaṃ rathaṃ vegādviṣaṅgaṃ vīkṣya sarvataḥ |
śaktisenāsu samabhūddhāhā'kāramahādhvanīḥ || 67 ||

ratharvasthitāḥ śaktīrapi sammardya vegataḥ |
caturthaparvā'ntarālaṃ prāviśat satvaraṃ balī || 68 ||

daṇḍarājñapi taṃ dṛṣṭvā daityamāyantamantikam |
avaruhya yuyodhoścairmusalena svaparvataḥ || 69 ||

tatrā'bhavanmahāyuddhaṃ daṇḍinīdaityarājayoḥ |
śastrairvicitrairastraiśca sarvalokabhayaṅkaram || 70 ||

jñātvā'jeyāmastraśastrairdaṇḍinīṃ daityapuṅgavaḥ |
māyāṃ prāduścākāroccaiḥ sarvalokabhayāvaham || 71 ||

andhakāraṃ pāṃśuvarṣamaśmavarṣaṃ mahāravam |
agnivarṣaṃ raktā'medhyādivarṣaṇam || 72 ||

p.764)

muṇḍavṛṣṭiṃ mahābhīmāmatha daityamahācamūm |
sahasrapādabāhvāsyamātmānaṃ śatadhā'pi ca || 73 ||

tādṛṣyā māyayā tasya bhītāstāḥ sarvaśaktyaḥ |
bhītāḥ śaktiḥ samālokya daṇḍinī śaravṛṣṭibhiḥ || 74 ||

vyanāśayanmāyikāṃstānatha bhūyo'pi daityarāṭ |
māyāṃ sasarja mahatīṃ dadṛśuḥ sarvaśaktyaḥ || 75 ||

cakrajārarathaṃ tatra samāyāntaṃ drutaṃ tathā |
svayaṃ vāhānnihatyā'śu rathaṃ chittvā'pyanekadhā || 76 ||

javena devīṃ lalitāṃ keśapāśe parāmṛśat |
cukruśurmāyayā tasya hāhetyuścaistu śaktyaḥ || 77 ||

tāvacchrībhairavo devīṃ babhāṣe rathanāyakaḥ |
alaṃ daityeṣu te devi līlayā bhītidānayā || 78 ||

paśya śaktīrbhayā'krāntāḥ śocantīḥ sarvataḥ śive |
nirmāyā'streṇa daiteyaṃ vimāyaṃ nāśaya drutam || 79 ||

sandhyā samabhyeti purastasyāṃ māyā duratyayā |
rathaneturvacaḥ śrutvā matvā yuktañca daṇḍinī || 80 ||

nirmāyā'straṃ samutsṛjya māyāṃ tasya vyanāśayat |
vāyuneva ca nīhāro naṣṭā māyā ca sarvataḥ || 81 ||

tadā gadādharo bhūyo dadṛśe sammukhāgatam |
dṛṣṭvaiva taṃ daṇḍinī sākruddhā khaḍgena tacchiraḥ || 82 ||

p.765)

cakarta nimiṣārdhena vajreṇa tvāṣṭrakaṃ yathā |
evaṃ tasmin vinihate viṣaṅge bhīmavikrame || 83 ||

devadundubhayo nedurapatatpuṣpavarṣaṇam |
diśo vitimirā jātāḥ suprabhaśca divākaraḥ || 84 ||

iti śrīmaditihāsottame tripurārahasye māhātmyakhaṇḍe lalitāmāhātmye 
viṣaṅgavadhavarṇanaṃ nāma pañcasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 6250 ||

p.766)

ṣaṭsaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ 
bhaṇḍadrārā sveṣṭaprāptyā saṇtoṣabhāvavarṇanam 

evaṃ viṣaṅge nihate jayaśabdo mahānabhūt |
muditāḥ śaktyaḥ sarvāstuṣṭuvurdaṇḍanāyikām ||1||

bhrātarau saparīvārau śrutvā yudhi nipātitau |
śokakātaratāṃ prāpa moheneṣanmahāsuraḥ || 2 ||

vimṛśyā'tha śucaṃ hitvā svamātmānaṃ hasanniva |
aho māṃ kiṃ vadāmīha śocantaṃ muktabhāvataḥ || 3 ||

yadarthitaṃ jaganmātrā ciraṃ tanme prapūritam |
evaṃ sā pūrayeccheṣamāśu nāstyatra saṃśayaḥ || 4 ||

tadvrajāmi śeṣagaṇaiḥ śrimāturdṛṣṭigocaram |
vilambena mamā'trā'laṃ śreyo vighnairhi saṅkulam || 5 ||

aho loke sarvathaiva ekāntenā'bhiyācitam |
diśatyeva parāmbā sā bhāvarūpā hi kāmadhuk || 6 ||

evaṃ svāntaḥ samāsīnāṃ vāñchā'dhikaphalapradām |
paramānandajananīṃ hitvā daivahato janaḥ || 7 ||

p.767)

duḥkhasārā'lpaviṣayaprepsayā bhīmarūpayā |
nīyate vyarthamaniśaṃ mṛtyorbhakṣyopasekatām || 8 ||

dhanyo'haṃ kṛtakṛtyo'haṃ yacchrīdevīpadāmbujam |
samīkṣan kaśmalamamuṃ vimucya hatadehakam || 9 ||

viśokaṃ paramaṃ sthānaṃ samupaimyaviśaṅkitam |
iti sañcintya daityendraḥ svāntarasyā'bhiguptaye || 10 ||

bhṛtvā'tyantaṃ kruddha iva saṃyojyā'suravāhinīm |
ni'hśeṣām tarasā yuktastayā yuddhāya daṃśitaḥ || 11 ||

rathamāruhya sāhasrasiṃhavāhaṃ mahonnatam |
śvetamuktā'tapatreṇa vījyamānaśca cāmaraiḥ || 12 ||

yuktaḥ seneśvaraiḥ pañcāśatsaṅkhyākairbaloddhataiḥ |
nadadvāditravipulairāsphoṭā'ravabṛṃhitaiḥ || 13 ||

rathaneminadīghoṣairhneṣitaprāyamāṃsalaiḥ |
rathavāhanasihānāṃ gartjitaiḥ pūrayan jagat || 14 ||

niryayau jaitrayātrārthaṃ cālayanniva miedinīm |
athā'pa"yacchaktisenā bhaṇḍasenāmahārṇavam || 15 ||

apāramapradhṛṣyañca bhīmaṃ gambhīraniḥsvanam |
tatra mantramahārājñīṃ bhaṇḍaṃ yuddhāya saṅgatam || 16 ||

p.768)

ālakṣaya śrīmahārājñīṃ natvā vṛtaṃ vyajijñapat |
devi bhaṇḍaḥ svayaṃ prāpto yoddhumatyantasaṃyutaḥ || 17 ||

sarvasenāsamāyuktaḥ sarvasenādhipairapi |
jaitrayātrā bhavatyāśca yuktā'tra yadi manyase || 18 ||

vijñāpitaivaṃ mantriṇyā yuktaṃ taścāpyamaṃsata |
athā'bhavacchaktisenā sannaddhā nimiṣā'rddhataḥ || 19 ||

aśvāruḍhā'śvasenābhiḥ senāmukhamadhiṣṭhitāḥ |
gajā'nīkasvāminī sā tatpaścāt sampadīśvarī || 20 ||

tato bālā rathā'ruḍhā kumārīgaṇasaṃvṛtāḥ |
tadanu pracacālā'jā cakrarājamahārathaḥ || 21 ||

namaparvasamāyukto hyaṇimādyābhirāvṛtaḥ |
muktācchatramahonnamraḥ padmaketudhvajocchrayaḥ || 22 ||

navaratnasamākīrṇa indriyātmā'śvayojitaḥ |
vāhayantī taṃ rathendraṃ mahātripurasundarī || 23 ||

cakreśvarī vivekātmatodahastā vyarājataḥ |
cakrarājaśatā'ṅgasya vāmadakṣiṇabhāgayoḥ || 24 ||

kirigeyacakrarathau vyarājetāṃ mahonnatau |
daṇḍinīmantriṇībhyāṃ tau daṃśitābhyāmadhiṣṭhitau || 25 ||

p.769)

svasvasenāsamāyuktau yuddhasambhārasambhṛtau | 
tiraskṛtiścakrarājarathapṛṣṭhaṃ samāśritā || 26 ||

timiraughanibhā'tyuścaturaṅgamamadhiṣṭhitā |
utkhātavelladatyantakarālakaravālinī || 27 ||

svasamākāraśaktyaughamaṇḍalīkṛtamaṇḍalā |
evaṃ śrīlalitādevī senayā saha niryayau || 28 ||

yuddhāya kṛtasannāhā saṃstutā vibudheśvaraiḥ |
senayorubhayoraśvarathebhapadacāriṇām || 30 ||

pādavegābhisaṅghātaprāṃśupāṃśususaṃhatiḥ |
janayadgāḍhatimiraṃ dārśanaiśamiva mahī vā sainyamarditā || 32 ||

kleśāt prāptā'tikṛśatāṃ bhītyā nāthamupeyuṣī |
tathā rajomahāsaṃghavegodrdhvagatihetutaḥ || 33 ||

bhumyabhāvādadho vegāt senā yāntīva lakṣyate |
ityādi tarkamāpannā vaimānikagaṇāstadā || 34 ||

p.770)

athā'bhavanmahadyuddhaṃ senāyoḥ śaktidaityayauḥ |
nadatsu yuddhavādyeṣu yukteṣvāsphoṭagarjanaiḥ || 35 ||

śaraśaktyṛṣṭiśūlā'sipaṭṭāsiprāsatomaraiḥ |
gadācakrabhindipālaparighāṅkuśayaṣṭibhiḥ || 66 ||

nirantaramabhūdvyāptyā prayuktaiḥ sanyayugmakam |
hatāḥ smaḥ sarvathā'smākaṃ śokaḥ putrādināśataḥ || 37 ||

prāptaḥ kiṃ jīvitena syācchatrubhirnirjitā'tmanām |
vikramya yudhi jitvemāḥ śaktiḥ prāpsyām āśiṣam || 38 ||

anvathā nihatā yuddhe vasāmo nandane vane |
iti niścitya te daittyā yuyudhurbalavattaram || 39 ||

śaktyo'pyeṣa daityānāmavadhiḥ sarvathā tvimān |
hatvā jayaṃ paramakaṃ prāpsyāmo'dyaiva sarvathā || 40 ||

iti matvā'tivegena yuyudhurvīryavattarāḥ |
evaṃ niyudhyamāneṣu śaktibhirditijeṣvatha || 41 ||

parasparaṃ śastrahatāḥ peturvigatajīvanāḥ |
decijjālīkṛtā'ṅgāstu śarajālairgatā'savaḥ || 42 ||

yuddhavratasyeva siddhyā sahasrākṣātmatāṃ yayuḥ |
keṣāñcit khaḍgaghātena śiraścaṭiti cotpatat || 43 ||

p. 771)

rāhoḥ śira ivā'kāśe vyāttā'syaṃ grasituṃ raveḥ |
peṣitā mudgarāghātaiścakrarmadhye vidāritā ḥ || 44 ||

protā dīrgheṣu bhalleṣu saṅghāśastatra kecana |
śaraiḥ śirāṃsi kṛttāni gagane yānti vegataḥ || 45 ||

pravṛddhakākapakṣāṇi calatpakṣigaṇā iva |
vikīrṇāṅgaiścitā yuddhe bhūmirviruruce tadā || 46 ||

paṭe vilikhitānīva pratimāṅgānyupakrame |
asṛgvahā vavau tatra citrakārapramādataḥ || 47 ||

skhalito hyālaktaka iva kvacicchoṣaṃ samāgatā |
citrakāreṇa jhaṭiti pramṛṣṭa iva bhāṣate || 48 ||

śaraviddhā gataprāṇāstasthuḥ kecit kvacit kvacit |
samagralikhitānīva citrā.i dadṛśurnanu || 49 ||

evaṃ muhūrtamātreṇa bhaṇḍadaityamahācamūḥ |
nanāśā'sadya śaktīnāṃ senāgniṃ śalabho yathā || 50 ||

atha senā'dhipāstasya krodhāruṇitalocanāḥ |
śaktisenāṃ śastravarṣairarddayāmāsuruścakaiḥ || 51 ||

dṛṣṭvā senādhipaiḥ senāmarditāmativihvalām |
mantriṇī daṇḍinī cā'śvāruḍhā sampadadhīśvarī || 52 ||

p.772)

parivāraśaktiyuktā yuyudhurmanyudīpitāḥ |
tā yudhdhvā suciraṃ daityaiḥ sinādhīśairmahābalaiḥ || 53 ||

prasahya nāśayāmāsuḥ sarvān senāpatīn hi tāḥ |
vakrākṣaṃ vāmanaṃ tālaṃ dīrghanasaṃ (ghoṇaṃ?) subāhukam || 54 ||

vidyujjihvañca samare nāśayatturagāśrayā |
sampatkarī tālajaṅghaṃ kuṭilākṣaṃ raṇoddhatam || 55 ||

niśaṭhaṃ śambaraṃ krūraṃ cakāra vyasumāhave |
jambhanī krūravadanaṃ stambhinī sucikāmukham || 56 ||

andhinī kaṇṭhakakaraṃ mṛgavaktrañca mohinī |
kālakeyaṃ bhairavo'pi vyāghrāsyaṃ kālakarṣiṇī || 57 ||

svapneśī muṇḍamūrdhānaṃ vaṭuko bhaṇḍābhāṣaṇam |
umlukaṃ karabhā'syañca mahākukṣiṃ śivāravam || 58 ||

śoṇitā'kṣam tālaketuṃ tālagravaṃ mahāhanum |
gṛghravāhaṃ kākamukhaṃ daṇḍinī nāśayadyudhi || 59 ||

śukaśyāmā śārikā'khyā saṅgītā laghupūrvikā |
sāhityaśayāmalā ceti nikumbhaṃ kumbhamastakam || 60 ||

cakrākṣaṃ dīrghadaṃṣṭrañca nāśayāmāsurulbaṇakam |
kaṭaṅkaṭaṃ karañjā'kṣaṃ muṇḍaṃ caṇḍaṃ kṛkā'nanam || 61 ||

sālasīraṃ dviśṛṅgañca śunakaṃ sūrpkarṇakam |
vakrakukṣiṃ vāmamukhaṃ kālakaṃ koṭaraṃ viṭam || 62 ||

P.773)

mantriṇī nāśayāmāsa balādākramya dānavān |
evaṃ niḥśeṣataḥ senāsenapeṣu hateṣvatha || 63 ||

bhaṇḍo hṛṣṭamanāḥ prāha sārathiṃ ratha āsthitaḥ |
rathaṃ prāpaya me śīghraṃ sārathe śaktivāhinīm || 64 ||

paśyā'dya mama vīryaṃ tvaṃ vicitraṃ romaharṣaṇam |
śrutvā vākyaṃ daityapatervāhayan rathamāśu saḥ || 65 ||

sandihāno daityapatiṃ papraccha praṇatastadā |
daityeśvara ! tvayā'jñptaḥ kiñcitpraṣṭuṃ samīhitam || 66 ||

taddehi praṇatāya tvaṃ sandihāno'smi sarvathā |
iti pṛṣṭena cājñapto prāha daityena sārathiḥ || 67 ||

daityeśvaraitāṃ śaktīnāṃ senāṃ manye'parājayām |
viśukrādyā yayā yuddhe hatā līlāvilāsataḥ || 68 ||

atha tvāmabhipaśyāmi hatabhrātṛsutādikam |
hṛṣṭā'ntaram śokaleśā'spṛṣṭaṃ pravrajitaṃ yathā || 69 ||

bālayā saha te yuddhe pūrvaṃ samabhilakṣitā |
bhaktirdevyāmatitarāṃ tanme brūhi yathātatham || 70 ||

p.774)

evaṃ parābhavaṃ duḥkhagetāvatibhayaṅkare |
na śokaṃ nā'pi bhītiṃ vā lakṣaye tava leśataḥ || 71 ||

iti bhūyaḥ supṛcchantaṃ daityaṃ taṃ cirasevinam |
prāha bhaṇḍaḥ smayan kiñcitcchṛṇu daityetyupākraman || 72 ||

eṣā yā lalitā devī sā parātparamā śivā |
etayā nihato yuddhe prāpsyāmyatyuttamam phalam || 73 ||

tallokaṃ śokarahitamabhayaṃ sarvataḥ sukham |
emaṃ lokaṃ saśokaṃ vai sabhayaṃ duḥkhamātrakam || 74 ||

māyayā mohito janturmanyate'tyuttamaṃ mudhā |
tatrā'pyāsuraloko vai rājaso'tibhayaṅkaraḥ || 75 ||

imaṃ varaṃ yo manute samugdhapaśukaḥ smṛtaḥ |
ahaṃ śrīpadmasaṃsthāno dūto māṇikyaśekharaḥ || 76 ||

svakarmapākavaśata etāṃ prāpto'varāṃ daśām |
tathā'pi kṛpayā devyā māṃ smṛtirno jahāti vai || 77 ||

eṣā sarveśvarī devī brahmādijananī parā |
etayā nihatā yuddhe bhrātaro me sutā api || 78 ||

śastrāgnipāvitāstasyā lokaṃ prāptāḥ parātparam |
mayā saha tvamapyāśu tallokaṃ prāpya bhrātṛbhiḥ || 79 ||

p.775 )

putraiḥ sameṣyasi punastato yāsyamṛtāṅgatim |
iti śrutvā bhaṇḍavaco viṣmito'bhūt sasārathiḥ || 80 ||

saṃślādhya daityarājaṃ taṃ jahau śokaṃ svahṛdgatam |
dṛṣṭvā śrīlalitāmagre namaścakre subhaktitaḥ || 81 ||

iti śrītripurārahasye māhātmyakhaṇḍe lalitāmāhātmye 
lalitābhaṇḍasamāgame daityarājabhaṇḍadvārā 
śrīdevībhaktirahasyaprakaṭanaṃ nāmaṣaṭsaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 6331 ||

p.776)

saptasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ 
bhaṇḍāsuravadhavarṇanam 

atha yuddhe sampadīśīmaśvāruḍhāṅ~ca daṇḍinīm |
mantriṇīñca samāsādya yuddhakauśalamulvaṇam || 1 ||

pradarśya tāḥ susantoṣya kramāt provāca saṅgare |
devyo vo yuddhacāturyavikramaistoṣito'smyaham || 2 ||

yoddhumutkaṇṭhitaṃ cittaṃ svāminyā ca imaṃ kṣaṇam yūyaṃ samīkṣakāḥ 
sarvāstayā me yuddhakarmaṇi || 3 ||

mamaiṣa lajjito bāhuryuddhe ceṭīgaṇaiḥ saha |
tasyā padaṃ vinirjitya yuddhe vo'bhijayamyanu || 4 ||

tanme prayācato'stvevaṃ iti tasya vacastadā |
niśamya devyo matvā cā'jeyaṃ svīyaparākramaiḥ || 5 ||

ucitañca mahādevyā daityeśasya parājayam |
dadurmārgaṃ niyuddhāya mahārājñyā'surasya via || 6 ||

atha śrīcakrarājākhyaṃ rathamāsādya saṅgare |
samārabhadbhaṇḍadaityo yuddhamatyadbhūtaṃ tadā || 7 ||

p.777)

tatra śaktimahāsainyama bhūdyuddhasamīkṣakam 
vaimānikairdevagaṇaiḥ siddhairṝṣigaṇaistathā || 8 ||

prekṣaṇāyā'gataiḥ sarvaṃ vyāptamāsīnnabhastadā |
bhaṇḍa āsādya lalitāṃ puro dṛṣṭvā'tiharṣitaḥ || 9 ||

manasā'bhinamaścakre bhavatya tadanusatvaram |
vivyādha pañcabhirbāṇaistadadbhutamivā'bhavat || 10 ||

tatra dvayaṃ sukusumanicayaṃ pādayoḥ patat |
tṛtīyaṃ kaṇṭhadeśe'bhūnmālikā phullapāṅkajī || 11 ||

tataḥ paraṃ mūrdhnyapatatpuṣ.pavarṣaṇarūpataḥ |
antyaṃ ratnamayaṃ mūrghni bhūṣaṇaṃ mukuṭe sthitam || 12 ||

evaṃ tadvihitāṃ pūjāṃ dṛṣṭvā devyatiharṣitā |
devādayo'pi taddṛṣṭvā śaṅkitāstatra sarvathā || 13 ||

nāradādbhaṇḍadaityasya śrutvā bhaktā'gragaṇyatām |
deveśo'titarāṃ tatra viṣmayaṃ samapadyata || 14 ||

athā'bhavanmahadyuddhaṃ lalitābhaṇḍadaityayoḥ |
citraśastrāstranicayavyāptā'kāśamahodaram || 15 ||

bhaṇḍadaityo'pi vīryeṇa nijena parameśvarīm |
santoṣayitumudyukto yuyodha paramā'stravit || 16 ||

p.778)

praṇamya manasā devīṃ sammukhaṃ samupāgatam |
gūḍhā'padānavacanairadhikṣeptuṃ pracakrame || 17 ||

lalite śṛṇu me vākyaṃ tvaṃ naṭīva vibhāsi me |
mṛṣā'cāraratā nityaṃ straiṇaṃ puruṣamāsritā || 18 ||

strīrūpadhāriṇīva tvaṃ strīsvabhāvavivarjitā |
na striyaṃ manye na pumāṃsamapi kvacit || 19 ||

lokavidviṣṭacāritrā lajjāśaṅkādivarjitā |
na tvaṃ satkulasambhūtā kulahīneva bhāsi me || 20 ||

lokaninditamārgasthaiḥ puruṣairavivekibhiḥ |
api tvamabhimṛṣṭā'si sarvatheti vibhāsi me || 21 ||

aho me prākkṛtaṃ hye vaṃ yattvāmevaṃvidhāmapi |
anarhāṃ darśane nūnaṃ paśyāmi purataḥ sthitām || 22 ||

śṛṇu styena vakṣyāmi yadyathā'sti tathā'stu tat |
maddṛṣṭigocarībhūtā samprati tvaṃ kathañcana || 23 ||

mṛṣā'cārañca māyāñca tyaktvā mayi sthirībhava |
āsāditā mayā sadyaḥ pṛṣṭhaṃ na pradarśaya || 24 ||

nā'pi māṃ hi samakṣasthaṃ vañcayitvā kathañcana |
śakyaṃ hyadṛśyatāṃ yātuṃ tasmāttvaṃ susthirībhava || 25 ||

p.779)

viditvā madabhiprāyasekāntaṃ suvyavasthitam |
dṛṣṭvā madīyavīryañca saṃsādhaya nijaṃ vratam || 26 ||

śutvā nigūḍhavākyaṃ tat prasannā lalitā'mbikā |
śṛṇu daityeśvara vaco yaduktaṃ te tathaiva tat || 27 ||

sādhayāmi tavā'bhīṣṭaṃ tyājayāmi madādikam |
na hi me dṛṣṭiviṣayībhūya bhūyo bhavet kvacit || 28 ||

yuddhaśraddhādisaṃśeṣaḥ tat sthirībhava sarvathā |
ityuktvā śaravarṣeṇa vavarṣa daityabhūpatim || 29 ||

prativarṣaṃ vavarṣā'tha so'pi daityo'tivegataḥ |
śastraprayogavijñānasaṃślāhanapuraḥsaram || 30 ||

abhūttayormahadyuddhaṃ tumulam lomaharṣaṇam |
evaṃ niyudhya suciraṃ daityo'strāṇi sasarja ha || 31 ||

āgneyaṃ vāruṇaṃ saumyaṃ kauveraṃ pārvataṃ tathā |
pārjanyamaindraṃ vāyavyaṃbrāhmaṃ kaumārameva ca || 32 ||

astrāṇyevaṃvidhānyāśu lalitā parameśvarī |
pratyastrairnāśayāmāsa tuhinaṃ dinakṛdyathā || 33 ||

tathā nūtanasṛṣṭāni pratyastrairnāśayat kṣaṇāt |
sasarjā'tha bhaṇḍadaityo madhuṃ kaiṭabhameva ca || 34 ||

p.780)

somakañca hiraṇyākṣaṃ hiraṇyakaśipuṃ tathā |
balimarjunanāmānaṃ haihayaṃ rāvaṇaṃ tathā || 35 ||

kumbhakarṇaṃ muraṃ grāhaṃ kaṃsaṃ dvividameva ca |
evamādīn viṣṇuśatrūn sarvān lokapraṇāśanān || 36 ||

te sṛṣṭā bhaṇḍadaityena tatsvabhāvaparākramāḥ |
udyuktā lokanāśārthaṃ tamovṛttiparāyaṇāḥ || 37 ||

laliteśyā'pi nāśāya teṣāṃ karanakhāt pṛthak |
nārāyaṇā'vatārāṇāṃ sasarja daśak'm tadā || 38 ||

nāśitāste kṣaṇenaiva sṛṣṭaistairviṣṇumūrtibhiḥ | 
atha bhūyo'ndhakaṃ mṛtyūṃ kāmaṃ tripurameva ca || 39 ||

mahādevaripūnevaṃ nirmame'suraśekharaḥ |
tān dṛṣṭvā śrīparā devī phālanetrādvisarjayat || 40 ||

mahādevaṃ devadevaṃ tena te nāśamāyayuḥ |
atha daityamahāsenāmasaṅkhyātāṃ bhayāvahām || 41 ||

udbhāvayannimeṣeṇa śaktibhiryā purā hatā |
viṣaṅgaśca viśukraśca caturbāhumukhāḥ sutāḥ || 42 ||

karaṅkakuṭilākṣādyā naṣṭāṃ sṛṣṭimajo yathā |
atha te yuyudhuḥ śaktisenābhiratimaṣitāḥ || 43 ||

taddṛṣṭvā viṣmitāḥ sarve bhaṇḍasāmarthyamadbhutam |
bhītāścā'tha kiṃ bhavedai iti devāśca śaktyaḥ || 44 ||

p.781)

tadā'bhavanmahadyuddhaṃ bhūyaścopakramaṃ yathā |
hataṃ hataṃ tatra daityo bhūyo bhūyaḥ sasarja ha || 45 ||

taddṛṣtvā'tyantavitrastāḥ śaktyo devatā api |
tadā śrīlalitādevīṃ tuṣṭuvurvidhimukhyakāḥ || 46 ||

mantriṇī ca mahādevīmupabrajya praṇamya ca |
kṛtāñjalirmadhurayā prārthayāmāsa vai girā || 47 ||

devi daityasya vibhavaṃ manye'haṃ subhayāvaham |
paśya śaktigaṇaṃ sarvaṃ śuṣyadvaktraṃ samākulam || 48 ||

yudhyantīnāmaviśrāntyā cā'tyagāt praharadvayam |
hataṃ hataṃ śaktigaṇairbhūyaḥ sṛjati vaikṣaṇāt || 49 ||

caturbāhumukhāstasya sutā yudhi nipātitāḥ |
kumāryā saptakṛtvaste bhūyo yudhyanti samprati || 50 ||

viṣaṅgo daśadhā tadvadviśukro navadhā'pi ca |
hataḥ punaḥ sṛṣṭa eva bhaṇḍadaityena yudhyati || 51 ||

karaṅkakuṭilākṣādyā apyevaṃ bahudhā hatāḥ |
yudhyantyeva punaḥ sṛṣṭā netaḥ sādhyamidaṃ hi naḥ || 52 ||

bhītāḥ śrāntāḥ sarvataśca paśya śaktīrhataprabhāḥ |
rakṣa sarvaṃ lokamimaṃ nopekṣāṃ kartumarhasi || 53 ||

p.782)

iti samprārthitā devī smayitveṣduvāca tām |
mā bhaiḥ paśya kṣaṇeṇaiva nāśayāmyasureśvaram || 54 ||

ityuktvā kārmuke śīghraṃ śaramekaṃ suyojayat |
tatrā'vāhya pāśupatamastraṃ lokabhayaṅkaram || 55 ||

mumocā'surasenāyāmastraṃ tannimiṣārdhataḥ |
bhasmīcakāra daityānāṃ senāṃ sāgarasammitām || 56 ||

bhūyaḥ śaraṃ kārmuke sve niveśayadatiprabham |
tadā diśo'bhito jvālāmālālīḍhābhavan kṣaṇāt || 57 ||

cakampe bhūḥ bhitāḥ sāgarā bhīḥ samāviśat |
carācaraṃ jagajjālaṃ peturulkāḥ sahasraśaḥ || 58 ||

bhaṇḍāsuraṃ mṛgādyāścā'pasavyamabhisaṃyayuḥ |
tāvaddṛṣṭvā vinaṣṭāṃ svāṃ senāṃ sāgarasannibhām || 59 ||

dadhyau śrīlalitāpādapaṅkajaṃ niścalā'ntaraḥ |
dhyeyamātrātmatāṃ yāvat prāpto bhaṇḍamahāsuraḥ || 60 ||

tāvacchrīlalitādevī kāmeśā'streṇa yojitam |
śaraṃ mumoca bhaṇḍāya sa śaro'tibhayaṅkaraḥ || 61 ||

pralayānalavat jvālāṃ muñcan viṣvaṅmahāprabhaḥ |
carācaraṃ jagajjālaṃ dahanniva nimeṣataḥ || 62 ||

p.783)

muṣṇaṃścakṣūṃṣi sarveṣāṃ tṛṇaṃ dāvānalo yathā |
bhaṇḍāsurañca sarathaṃ nagaraṃ tasya śūnyakam || 63 ||

sastrīsthavirabālañca sasthānaprāṇimaṇḍalam |
bhasmaśeṣīkarottatra tadadbhutamivā'bhavat || 64 ||

brahmāviṣṇumukhāstatra bhaṇḍaṃ vā tasya vāhinīm |
nagaram vā prahatasya daityārbhakamapi kvacit || 65 ||

adṛṣṭvāa viṣmayaṃ prāptā jayaśabdamavā'sṛjuḥ |
abhijaghnuḥ śaktigaṇā viāditrāṇi sahasraśaḥ || 66 ||

vinedurdevavādyāni jagurgandharvanāyakāḥ |
lāsyaṃ cakrurdevavārā urvaśyādyāḥ suharṣitāḥ || 67 ||

stotrāṇi peṭhuṛrṣayo siddhā jepuḥ parāṃ śivām | 
dadhyurmunigaṇā rūpaṃ praṇemuritare janāḥ || 68 ||

vavṛṣuḥ puṣ.panikarān praphullānatisaurabhān |
balāhakāḥ saumyarūpā garjanto'tyantamantharam || 69 ||

sugandhatoyabindūṃśca yakṣakarddamamiśritān |
mārutāḥ saugandhyayuto vavurānandanaspṛśaḥ || 70 ||

divānāthaḥ suprabho'bhūnnīyataḥ sāgaro'pi ca |
sanmanāṃsīva toyāni samāsan saridādiṣu || 71 ||

p.784)

abhūjjagat punaḥ sṛṣṭaṃ pralayānta ivollasat |
nirākulamasaṅkīrṇaṃ tadā bhaṇḍā'sure hate || 72 ||

evaṃ daityaṃ hataṃ dṛṣṭvā bhaṇḍākhyaṃ lokakaṇṭakam |
devā maharṣayaḥ siddhāḥ sarve devīmupāyayuḥ || 73 ||

iti śrīmaditihāsottame tripurārahasye māhātmyakhaṇḍe śrīlalitācaritre 
bhaṇḍāsuravadhavarṇanaṃ nāma saptasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 6404 ||

p.785)

asṭasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ 
śrīlalitāstutipūrvakaṃ meruśṛṅge pratiśrīcakropari tadabhiṣecanavarṇanam 

upetya devapramukhāḥ sarve tāṃ lalitāṃ parām |
harṣanirbharitasvāntāstuṣṭuvurvividhaiḥ stavaḥ || 1 ||

śrī lalitā stutiḥ 

jaya jaya lalitā'mba ! tvatpadā'mbhojasevā phalamiha bhajatāṃ kiṃ kinna 
dadyādabhīṣṭam |
vibudhaviṭapimukhyān kāmadān svasvabhāvānapi vitarati bhūyaḥ 
saṃśritebhyo'tiśīghram || 2 ||
vayamiha vipadārttā bhaṇḍadaityapratāpā'nalajaṭilakarālajvālayā 
dagdhapakṣāḥ |
aparimitabhayābdhau yannimagnāḥ sametā jhaṭiti nanu tayaiva proddhatā 
vyomamārgāḥ || 3 ||

p.786)

praṇatajananitāntasvāntasantāpahantrī śritajanaduritā'liprauḍamāyāniyantrī |
sthiracaranikhilodyatprāṇināṃ jīvatantrī tava padanalinīyaṃ praidhate lokayantrī || 
4 ||

tava janani vilāsaḥ sarvalokā'vabhāsaḥ kuta iha tava bhūyāddaityayuddhe 
prayāsaḥ | 
bhavatu satatamasmanmānase nīrajaske viśadavikacavṛttau vāsite vāsanābhiḥ || 5 ||

(nanu viditamidaṃ yad dehabhājāṃ tvameva śaraṇamabhimatānāṃ śreyasāṃ 
śuddhahetuḥ ) 
cirajaḍamilitānāṃ yo vivekaikaheturbhavati caraṇahaṃsastāvako brahmavāhaḥ || 
6 ||

iti stutvā daṇḍavatte praṇatā vidhimukhyakāḥ |
bhūyo bhūyo'pi vividhaiḥ stavaistuṣṭuvurambikām || 7 ||

tāvadbrahmāṇḍakoṭibhyo brahmādyāḥ kāraṇeśvarāḥ |
ājagmurbhāratīmukhyaśaktiyuktā ghaṭodbhava ! || 8 ||

te pāṇisampuṭottaṃsāḥ praṇatā bhaktinirbharāḥ |
samīḍalalitāmīḍyāṃ prāhubarddhakarā'mbujāḥ || 9 ||

p.787)

deveśi ! bhaṇḍadaityastha vadhādadya surakṣitam |
brahmāṇḍānāṃ śataṃ pañca caiṣāṃ sa hyadhipo'bhavat || 10 ||

prokteṣvaṇḍeṣu śakrādyā baddhāḥ kecit palāyitāḥ |
lokā'dhipatyaṃ sarvatra daityaireva cakāra saḥ || 11 ||

pravartitā daityavedā daityayajñāśca sarvataḥ |
na kvaciddaivayajño vā brahmayajño'pi śaṅkari ! || 12 ||

pravṛtto'bhūdbrāhmaṇādua bhaṇḍe rājyaṃ praśāsati |
tadadya nihate daitye tvayā yudhi maheśvari || 13 ||

palāyitā daityagaṇā lokapālāśca saṃsthitāḥ |
svasvā'dhikārakṛtyeṣu jātaṃ sarvaṃ nirākulam || 14 ||

evaṃ vadatsu deveṣu tatrā'gatya ca viśvakṛt |
devīṃ vidhimukhāṃścā'pi praṇamya racitā'ñjaliḥ || 15 ||

pitāmahā'hamājñaptastvayā yanmerumūrdhani |
devyāḥ śrīpuranirmāṇe tattathā racitaṃ mayā || 16 ||

draṣṭumarhasi tacchāghram devya devaiśca saṃyutaḥ |
evaṃ tvaṣṭrā prārthito'jo devyai vṛttaṃ vyajijñapat || 17 ||

prārthitā devadeveśairlalitā parameśvarī |
śaktisenā devagaṇairyutā tatra yayau drutam || 18 ||

dadṛśustatpuraṃ cārutaraṃ tvaṣṭṛvinirmitam |
mahāśrīpurarājasya pratibimbavivārpitam || 19 ||

p.788)

vismitāḥ sarva evaite devāḥ śaktigaṇāstathā | 
atha tatra nivasane devāḥ śrīlalitāmbikām || 20 ||

prārthayāmāsuratyantaṃ praṇatāḥ sarvabhāvataḥ |
tatsammatiṃ samādāya buddhīśapramukhāstadā || 21 ||

rājyā'bhiṣekasāmagrīṃ kalpayāmāsuruttamām |
prakalpya sarbasambhārān śubhalagnamuhūrtake || 22 ||

brahmā munigaṇopeta ābhiṣecanikaṃ vidhim |
cakārā'gamadṛṣṭena mārgeṇottamakalpataḥ || 23 ||

vṛndārakā devagaṇā māruto vasavo'ganaya |
gandhrvā devadūtāśca dikpālā yakṣaguhyakāḥ || 24 ||

yātudhānāḥ kiṃpuruṣāḥ kinnarā uragā api |
vidyāghrāḥ siddhamunaya ṛṣayaḥ pitaro'surāḥ || 25 ||

brahmarudraharīṇāñca koṭayo'ṇḍāntarasthitāḥ |
sadāśiveśvarau cā'pi śrīkaṇṭho'nanta eva ca || 26 ||

saṅgatāstatra lalitāmahārājyā'bhiṣecane |
paricerustatra devīṃ lalitā'mbāṃ samantataḥ || 27 ||

brahmāṇīkamalāgaurīkoṭayo bhaktibhāvanāḥ |
upacāraiḥ |
pṛthagabhūtaiḥ pūjayāmāsurādarāt || 28 ||

evaṃ kṛta svastyayanāṃ maṅgalācārasaṃśrayām |
nadatsu śubhavādyeṣu mūrcchayatsu sumūrcchānām || 29 ||

avarohā'rohagatilayaśrutisamīraṇaiḥ |
gandharveṣu pranṛtyatsu devaveśyāsu sarvataḥ || 30 ||

p.789)

japatsu munivṛndeṣu ṛṣiṣu prapaṭhatsu ca |
stuvatsu vividheṣūccaiḥ prekṣatsu prāṇiṣūdyamaiḥ || 31 ||

vidhirārohayaddevīṃ mahāsiṃhāsane śubhe |
atha mantraprapūtā'dbhirnavaratnavicitritaiḥ || 32 ||

kalaśaiḥ sambhṛtaiścā'pi nadīsāgarasambhavaiḥ |
abhyaṣiñcallokadhātā vaśiṣṭhapramudhairvṛtaḥ || 33 ||

abhiṣicya mahādevīmanardhyā'bharaṇojjvalām |
vicitradivyavasanāṃ śrīcakrā'dhivirājitām || 34 ||

pañcabrahmākāramañcaśobhinīṃ lalitāmbikām |
pūjayāmāsuramarā vividhaiḥ susamarhaṇaiḥ || 35 ||

vidhiviṣṇumaheśādyāḥ pṛthagbhaktisamāhitāḥ |
stutvā vicitrastavanaiḥ praṇemurbhuvi daṇḍavat || 36 ||

evaṃ śrīlalitā devī tatra śrīpuraśekhare |
siṃhāsane'bhiṣiktā sā mahāśrīpuravattadā || 37 ||

mantriṇīpramukhānāṃ svasthānāni pradiśat pṛthak |
evaṃ ghaṭodbhava mune ! meruśṛṅge mahonnate || 38 ||

samāste śrīpure brahmamukhaśāsanatatparā |
devaśilpisvacāturyasarvasāravinirmitam || 39 ||

p.790)

nagaraṃ śrīmahādevyā bhūṣaṇaṃ chatrapāduke |
prārthitā vidhimukhyaiḥ sā svīkṛtya karuṇāvaśāt || 40 ||

cidānandaghanā'pītthamāste līlāvapurdharā |
iti śrutvā hayagrīvāt kathāṃ paramapāvanīm || 41 ||

agastyaḥ santuṣṭamanāḥ papracchā'tiviśaṅkitaḥ |
hayagrīva dayāsindho ! merāvasmin tvayoditam || 42 ||

nagaraṃ śrīmahādevyāstanme citraṃ vibhāti vai |
na me hyaviditaṃ sthānaṃ brahmāṇḍāntarbhavet kvacit || 43 ||

tatrā'pi merāvajñātaṃ mayā na syāt kvacit sthalam |
merurbhuvanacitrasya bhittivatparikalpitaḥ || 44 ||

santyūdrdhvaṃ saptabhuvanānyadhaḥ saptatalāni vai |
merumūrghni brahmaviṣṇuśivadhāmāni santi vai || 45 ||

tatra me nā'styavidito deśo'pi dvayaṅgulātmakaḥ |
tat kathaṃ varṇitaṃ tatra śrīpuraṃ me samīraya || 46 ||

iti pṛṣṭaḥ kumbhajena hyāsyaḥ prāha harṣitaḥ |
śṛṇu kumbhajaḥ ! vakṣyāmi rahasyaṃ hyetaduttamam || 47 ||

na hi tadbhavanaṃ sarvairdraṣṭuṃ śakyaṃ kathañcan |
ye tu śrītripurāṃ śaktiṃ vidhinā samupāsate || 48 ||

teṣā prapyaṃ dṛśyamapi nā'nyeṣāṃ tu kadācana |
tatte māhātmyasaṃśrutyā bhaktirhṛdi samudgatā || 49 ||

p.791)

prāptāyāṃ tu parābhaktau nā'prāpyaṃ vidyate |
prāpte ratnākare yadvadbhavenno ratnavāñcitā || 50 ||

tadiyaṃ te satī bhāryā lopāmudrā parā priyā |
tasyā dīkṣāṃ samādāya copāsya vidhivatparām || 51 ||

siddhastad drakṣasi puraṃ nānyathā tu kadācana |
etatte kumbhaja ! proktaṃ lalitāyā mahādbhutam || 52 ||

vibhavaṃ śṛṇvatāṃ puṃsāṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam |
kalau malīmasahṛdāṃ janānāṃ durdhiyāmidam || 53 ||

māhātmyaṃ tripureśānyāstyaktvā nā'nyatparāyaṇam |
vaidikaiḥ karmabhirbhūyo nirāśīrbhiḥ kṛtairvavet || 54 ||

upāsaneṣu śraddhā'nyadevatāviṣayā tataḥ |
viṣṇau śive śaktiṣu ca sarvānte paripākataḥ || 55 ||

tripurāmūrtisevāyāṃ śraddhā bhavati śāśvatī |
etadārādhanenaiva viditvā svā'tmarūpīm || 56 ||

cidānandā'dvayamayīṃ sarvalokamaheśvarīm |
abhivyaktaparaiśvaryo na punarbhavamṛcchati || 57 ||

evaṃ svātmapadaprāptau sopānaṃ prathamantvidam |
śravaṇaṃ śrīparāśaktimāhātmyavibhavonnateḥ || 58 ||

tasmādādau sarvathaiva śrotavyaṃ śreya icchatā |
etat pāpapraśamanaṃ sarvābhīṣṭaphalāvaham || 59 ||

p.792)

bhaktimutpādya tarasā mocayet paramādbhayāt |
ityuktvā kumbhajamunipūjito'śvānano bhṛśam || 60 || 

āpṛcchay niragāt svīyāmabhīṣṭāṃ diśamīśvaraḥ |
iti te kathitā rāma lalitāyāḥ kathā śubhā || 61 ||

iti śrimaditihāsottame tripurārahasye māhātmyakhaṇḍe 
dattātreyaparaśurāmasaṃvāde śrīlalitāmāhātmye śrīpure 
śrīlalitābhiṣekavarṇanaṃ nāmāṣṭasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 6465 ||

p.793)

ekonāśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ 
śrīdattabhārgavasambāde āgamasvarūpavarṇanam 


evaṃ śrīlalitādevyā māhātmyaṃ sarvato'dhikam |
jamadagnisutaḥ śrutvā harṣā'mṛtāniṣecitaḥ || 1 ||

bhaktivāridhinirmagno romāñcodayapīvaraḥ |
nirantarasravaddharṣanetravāripariplutaḥ || 2 ||

visaṃjña iva sambhūtaḥ kṣaṇamāsavamattavat |
babhāṣe gadgadaśrutyā bhūyo viracitā'ñjaliḥ || 3 ||

bhagavannahamatyantaṃ pāvito bhavatā'dhunā |
parākathātīrthavāridhārānivahasecanaiḥ || 4 ||

uddhṛto dayayā'pārasaṃsārajaladhernanu |
namaste śrīguro nātha duḥkhadhvāntadinādhipa ! || 5 ||

namasye taṃ muniṃ śāṃtaṃ saṃvartamakutobhayam |
yo me mārga niradiśadandhasya bhramato yathā || 6 ||

adya yāvadahaṃ loke vyarthaṃ kālamupāvaham |
śūnyakūpe bheka iva na svārthamavidaṃ kvacit || 7 ||

p.794)

dhanyo'haṃ kṛtakṛtyo'haṃ bhavaścaraṇasaṃśrayāt |
mokṣalakṣmīmabhiprepsurabhavaṃ sarvathā nanu || 8 ||

bhagavanme saṃśayo'tra mahān hṛdi samāhitaḥ |
tat pṛcchāmi bhavantaṃ yadvaktuṃj tanme samarhasi || 9 ||

jānāmyanugraho'tyantaṃ mayi te karuṇānidheḥ |
nā'vaktvyaṃ mayi bhavecchiṣye kāruṇikasya te || 10 ||

kathaṃ kumbhodbhavamuniḥ sarvaśāstraviśāradaḥ |
nopāsīttaṃ parāśaktiṃ sarvalokamaheśvarīm || 11 ||

yenā'dṛśyamabhūnmerau śrīdevyā nagarottamam |
kuto va vedanirate na hi syāttasya darśanam || 12 ||

bhūyaḥ kathaṃ tena dṛṣṭaṃ tatpuraṃ tadbravīhi me |
ityāpṛṣṭo jāmadagnyaṃ prāha dattagururmuniḥ || 13 ||

śṛṇu rāma pravakṣyāmi rahasyaṃ hye taduttamam 
na hyatra kevalaṃ śāstraṃ prayojakamupeyate || 14 ||

janānāṃ prākasamabhyastavāsanāvāsitātmanām |
vāsanānāṃ śataṃ citte sañcitaṃ prāṇināṃ pṛthak || 15 ||

prāpyodbodhaṃ prasarati satsaṅgastasya bodhakaḥ |
yāvadyatsaṅgamāpnoti tāvat tat syānna cā'nyathā || 16 ||

p.795)

hayagrīvaṃ samāgamya tripurāvaibhavaśruteḥ |
sañjātatripurābhaktiḥ patnyā dīkṣāṃ samādade || 17 ||

yāvaddīkṣāṃ na vindeta tripuropāsako'pi san |
na tāvanmokṣasaudhasya sopānā'rohaṇakṣamaḥ || 18 ||

vinopanayanaṃ yadvaddvijānāṃ sarvakarmasu |
na yogyatā tathā'trāpi vinā dīkṣāṃ bhṛgūdbaha || 19 ||

aprāpya sadgurordīkṣāmajñātvā gurūpaddhatim |
svabuddhyā tu kṛtaṃ karma vidhinā ca samanvitam || 20 ||

tathā'pi sādhakaṃ śīghraṃ nāśayatyeva sarvathā |
sevitāraṃ yathā hanti cā'pakvantu rasāyanam || 21 ||

mantraṃ mantravidhānañca svabuddhyaiva guruṃ vinā |
yaḥ samāsādayet sa hi devatādrohamāpnuyāt || 22 ||

neha lokaḥ paro vā.pi vidyate devatādruhām |
ajño drohakṛtaṃ doṣaṃ nāśayenmānavaḥ katham || 23 ||

lokadṛṣṭanyāya eṣa ājñābhaṅgakaraṃ naram |
yojayantyeva bhūpālā mahādaṇḍaiḥ pṛthagvidhaiḥ || 24 ||

nopekṣante sarvathaiva naramājñā'pakāriṇam |
vedavādaireva na hi bhavedatrā'dhikāritā || 25 ||

p.796)

upāsanā'dhikārārthaṃ dvijānāṃ vaidikakramaḥ |
puruṣārtho vedavidhau sarvathā na hi vidyate || 26 ||

yadi vedavidāṃ puṃsāṃ mṛṣāphalā'nusandhinām |
aṇumātrā'tmabhūtānāṃ puruṣārthā'bhimāninām || 27 ||

puruṣārthaḥ paraḥ syācyet sthāṇūnāṃ kuto bhavet |
vaidikā hyacalammanyāḥ phalaṃ dhūmasamudbhavam || 28 ||

kva dṛṣṭaṃ calabhūtena hyacalaṃ phalasammatam |
miteṣu duḥkhabhūteṣu phaleṣu sukhabuddhayaḥ || 29 ||

phalasvarūpā'nabhijñā hyaphale phalaśaṃsinaḥ |
pravartito guṇamayo vedo dhātrā jagatkṛtiḥ || 30 ||

jagadyātrāpravṛddhyarthaṃ surāṇāmabhitṛptaye |
martukāmasya viṣavat kāmināṃ kāmanāmayaḥ || 31 ||

paraśca bhāgaḥ saṅgūḍho vede yastaṃ vidurna te |
dhātraiva gopanāttasya guptabhāveṣu mohitāḥ || 32 ||

nayanti tamanyathaiva vyāso nārāyaṇaḥ svayam |
jīveṣu dayayā samyak cittā'rohopayogyayā || 33 ||

tamupabṛṃhayad bhūyo paramārthaikasādhanam |
ato'tra tyaktakāmānāmadhikāro vidhīyate || 34 ||

p.797)

sannyāsino'pi cicchakterūpāsanamabhīpsitam |
ajñānāmnohato vā'pi vidyopāstiṃ tyajettu yaḥ || 35 ||

sa viśedandhatāmisraṃ na tasyā'sti punargatiḥ |
sannyāsinā tu tyaktavyaṃ sarvaṃ bandhātmanā sthitam || 36 ||

idaṃ mokṣātmakaṃ rāma śrīvidyopāsanantu yat |
na (hi) tyaktuṃ samucitaṃ brāhmaṇyamiva vai yateḥ || 37 ||

mahāparādhastyāgo vai syādupāsterbhayāvahaḥ |
tāmisramandhatāmisraṃ kumbhīpākamavīcikam || 38 ||

prapadyante hi te bhūyo hyaparādhaparā janāḥ |
daṇḍarājñyā samājñaptāḥ śaktyo'tivibhīṣaṇāḥ || 39 ||

saṅkarṣiṇī karṣiṇī ca kālasaṅkarṣiṇī tathā |
upāstimārgasarujānaparādhaparān janān || 40 ||

yojayantyuktakuḥkheṣu sthāneṣu paramātṛkāḥ |
upāsanaparo martyaḥ kaivalyaṃ padamaśnute || 41 ||

karmiṇastāntrikasyā'pi yā bhavedgatiruttamā |
vaidikopāsakasyaiṣā sarvathā na hi vidyate || 42 ||

yato hi vaidikaṃ karma sarvathā hi bahirmukham |
anīśvaraṃ pauruṣaṃ syādataḥ paśuphalocitam || 43 ||

p.798)

tāntrikantvaiśvaraṃ karma jñānopāsanamiśritam |
mocayatyāśu saṃsiddhyā śraddhābhaktisubṛṃhitam || 44 ||

yathoktakarmaṇā cittaśuddhimāsādya vidyayā |
prāpyate'traiva śivatā prabuddhaiḥ pauruṣeṇa hi || 45 ||

abudhā api mucyante karma kṛtvā yathākramāt |
nā'dhikāro vinā dīkṣāṃ karmaṇi jñānasādhane || 46 ||

dīkṣāvantastu dehānte prāpya lokaṃ parātparam |
sadāśivena te samyak prabuddhāḥ śivarupiṇaḥ || 47 ||

karmiṇo'pyaprabuddhā ye te bhavanti bhṛgūdvaha ! |
tasmānna vedaśāstrādyaiḥ śuṣkaiḥ sadgatirāpyate || 48 ||

vinā śrītripurāsevāṃ pravṛttirvā kathaṃ satī |
na sādhanaṃ phalaṃ vā'pi pravṛttirvā.pi bhārgava ! || 49 ||

vinā śrītripureśānyāḥ kṛpayā sambhaviṣyati |
ārādhya śivaviṣṇavādīnapi yat prāpyate phalam || 59 ||

taścā'pi tasyāḥ kṛpayā bhavatīti viniścayaḥ |
pūjanaṃ sarvadevānāṃ parāśakteḥ prapūjanam || 51 ||

śaktiṃ vinā na pūjāyā grahaṇaṃ sambhavet kvacit |
tasmāt pūjā tu śakteḥ syāt sarvatra vihitā janaiḥ || 52 ||

p.799)

phalapradānaśaktintu vicāryā'dau vicakṣaṇāḥ |
pūjayanti pṛthak devaṃ phalaṃ vindanti tattathā || 53 ||

loke'pyaśaktaḥ kutrā'pi na pūjyaḥ syāttu bhārgava |
āśrayatvācchivamṛte śaktirnaiva tu vidyate || 54 ||

iti cennijasattātmaśaktihīnaḥ śivastathā |
prakāśaśaktihīno vai raviḥ kutra kadā bhāvet || 55 ||

yathā tathā citiṃ śaktimṛte syādvi śivaḥ katham |
citiśaktyā parityaktaṃ tṛṇaṃ vā'pi kathaṃ bhavet || 56 ||

satyāṃ citi hyasmi sarvamanyathā na hi kiñcana |
yadasti taścitiriti jānīhi bhṛgunandana || 57 ||

etacchāktaṃ hi vijñānaṃ matto'nyanna hi vidyate |
evaṃ buddhyā tu yat kiñcit tṛṇañca tripurātmakam || 58 ||

saṃjñāyāmeva loke'smin vivadanti marnīṣiṇaḥ |
tasmāttyajā'tra sandehametatsarvatra vai samam || 59 ||

vāṅniruktyai pravṛttā vai viśeṣā'lambanaṃ gatā |
nirviśeṣantu tadrūpamakhaṇḍaikacidātmakam || 60 ||

viśeṣajñao bhavedyāvattavanna syāddhi tatparaḥ |
vedā viśeṣabahulā gūhayantyaviśeṣakam || 61 ||

p.800)

ato'gastyastatpuraṃ tu nā'paśyadvaidiko'pi san |
vedārthaḥparamo yastu tripuraiva hi sā bhavet || 62 ||

atastraipurasaṃsiddhau sādhanaṃ veda ucyate |
vedārthasyaiva vyasanaṃ purāṇaṃ vyāsa ūcivān || 63 ||

vedo hyāgamabhāgaḥ syāt śabdarāśistathā'gamaḥ |
tasyā mūrttirataḥ sarvaṃ pravṛttaṃ saṃśrayāt || 64 ||

traivarṇikā'dhikāreṇa vedarūpaḥ pravartate |
dayayā parameśānaḥ sarvānuddhartumicchayā || 65 ||

veda āgamasaṃjñānaṃ vibhāvayadanuttamam |
āgamaḥ parameśasya vimarśa iti niścayaḥ || 66 ||

krameṇa brahmamukhyānāṃ mukhādudbhavayattu tam |
apāro hyāgamā'mbhodhiḥ sarvalokeṣu saṅgataḥ || 67 ||

kālena mandadhiṣaṇān kṛpaṇānabhilakṣya tu |
ṛṣibhistamāgamābdhiṃ mathitvā prājyayā dhiyā || 68 || 

sārasaṃgraharūpātmatantrā'mṛtamanuttamam |
deśabhedavibhedena pṛthageva vibhāvitam || 69 ||

tatra dvijānāṃ vedoktakarmabhiḥ saṃskṛtātmanām |
śrautakarmamukhenaiva tāntrike hyadhikāritā || 70 ||

p.801)

śūdrādīnāntu kaivalyādbhavettantrādhikāritā |
veda eva hi tantraṃ syāttantraṃ vedaḥ prakīrtitam || 71 ||

nā'nayorvidyate bhedo leśāṃśenā'pi kuṭracit |
tathā hi vedabhāgeṣu tantrabhāgaḥ pradṛśyate || 72 ||

yatra mantrayantrapūjāvidhānaṃ susphuṭaṃ sthitam |
evaṃvidho vedabhāgo yatastanmūrghni saṃsthitaḥ || 73 ||

tantrasaṅketasaṃyuktastatastantraṃ samuttamam |
tantreṣvapi vedabhāgā mantrabrāhmaṇabheditāḥ || 74 ||

dṛśyante karmavidhiṣu tasmāttantraṃ samuttamam |
evaṃ purāṇādiṣu ca tantrabhāgaḥ pradṛśyate || 75 ||

tathā tantreṣu sargādilakṣarṇo'śaḥ pradṛśyate |
sa purāṇaprabhāgaḥ syādevaṃ jānīhi bhārgava ! || 76 ||

tasmācchrītripurākhyāyāḥ sahajā'marśasambhavaḥ |
āgamābdhistato vedāṃstantrāṇi ca vibhāvaya || 77 ||

niyatiryā hi tacchaktistayā sarva vyavasthitam |
ato'gastyastantradīkṣārahito vaidiko'pi san || 78 ||

nā'paśyattatpuraṃ tatra merau niyatiyantritaḥ |
atha śrutvā mahādevyā māhātmyaṃ sarbato'dhikam || 79 ||

p.802)

bhaktiyuktaḥ prāpya dīkṣāṃ tāntrikīṃ kramasaṃyutām |
patnyā upāsya tripurāṃ tatra śrīnāthamaṇḍalo || 80 ||

sthānaṃ prāpya priyāyuktaḥ paramānandanirbharaḥ |
evaṃ tena samākrāntaṃ dṛṣṭañca sthānamuttamam || 81 ||

iti śrīmaditihāsottame tripurārahasye māhātmyakhaṇḍe āgamasvarūpanirṇayo 
nāmaikonā'śītitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 6546 ||

p.803)

aśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ 

upāsakamukhyadharmavarṇanam 

evaṃ śrutvā kathāṃ rāmo dattātreyanirupitām |
bhūyaḥ prāha prasannātmā praṇamya racitā'ñjaliḥ || 1 ||

bhagavannātha yatproktaṃ śrutaṃ tadamṛtopamam |
abhavaṃ muktasandehaḥ kiñcit pṛcchāmi vai punaḥ || 2 ||

evaṃ dīkṣāṃ samāsādya tripurā'rādhanodyataḥ |
kurvannityakriyāṃ kena viśeṣeṇa hi karmaṇā || 3 ||

śīghraṃ mahāphalaṃ preyādrājā cā'kiñcano'pi ca |
tanme samācakṣva guro kṛpayā mayi sevake || 4 ||

iti dattaguruḥ pṛṣṭaḥ prāha tadbhaktiharṣitaḥ |
śṛṇu rāma mahāguhyaṃ tripurāprītikārakam || 5 ||

śivo yathā'bhiṣekena praṇāmairdinakṛdyathā |
naivedaistu gaṇeśāno'laṅkārāddharireva ca || 6 ||

p.804)

pūjanena viśeṣaistu vidhinā prīyate tathā |
parāśaktirmaheśānī tatra sarvottamaṃ śṛṇu || 7 ||

prāsādamunnataṃ citraṃ nirmāya nijaśaktitaḥ |
tatra śrīcakrarājaṃ vā mūrtiṃ vā'pi sulakṣaṇām || 8 ||

sasthāpya vidhinā tatra svayaṃ vā'nyena vā'nvaham |
pūjayet pañcakālaṃ vā catustrirdvyekakālakam || 9 ||

uṣaḥ prātarmadhyadine pradoṣe cā'rdhayāmake |
nityaṃ vā dvitryekakālaṃ pañcakālañca parvasu || 10 ||

utsavo'pi prakartvyo mahāparvasu sarvathā |
rathena vāhanairyānairutsavapratimāṃ bahiḥ || 11 ||

parikrāmyā'gamoktena nṛtyamaṅgalagāyanaiḥ |
evaṃ yaḥ kurute loke sa vasecchrīpurā'ntare || 12 ||

nīlavaprā'ntarāleṣu sarvabhogopasambhṛtaḥ |
asamartho'nyavihite sthāpayeścakranāyakam || 13 ||

cakrasthāpanatulyaṃ no vidyate tripurāpriyam |
na tasya punarāvṛttiḥ sāyujyaṃ vindate kramāt || 14 ||

śrīckrasthāpanādeva phalānantyaṃ samīritam |
yatra syāt sthāvaraṃ yantraṃ nivasaṃstatsamīpataḥ || 15 ||

upāsako hemamaye vapre nivasati dhuvam |
upāsakastu jānīyāttatkṣetraṃ sarvato'dhikam || 16 ||

p.805)

tatrāpyaśakto vibhavānurodhācchubhaparvasu |
pūjayet sthāvaraṃ ckraṃ mūrtiṃ vā bhaktipūrvakam || 17 ||

carayantrādipūjāyāḥ sthirayantrādipūjanam |
phalaṃ samāvahellakṣaguṇaṃ rāma ! na saṃśayaḥ || 18 ||

yantre sāvṛtimambāntu vidhānena prapūjayet |
tatttsthāne bhāvayan vai mahāphalasamāptaye || 19 ||

yathā sūryasya kiraṇāḥ sarvataḥ samavasthitāḥ |
sūryā'tmabhūtāstasyāṃ'sāḥ sarvalokaprasādakāḥ || 20 ||

śītā'ndhakārarogādināśakāḥ prāṇinastathā |
tripurāyāḥ parāśakteḥ parivārākhyadevatāḥ || 21 ||

lokavāñchārthadānāya niyuktāstu tayaivaḥ tāḥ |
tasyāṃ'śabhūtāḥ sarvā vai tasmāt pūjyā yathāvidhī || 22 ||

kiraṇānāṃ piṇḍamayo yathā sūryo nabhaḥsthitaḥ |
tathaiva'vṛtiśaktīnāmaikyātmā tripurā matā || 23 ||

binducakre samāsīnā devadevamaheśvarī |
aśaktaḥ pūjayedbhatayā yathāmti yathākramam | 24 ||

parivārapiṇḍamayīṃ sāmānyairdravyavaibhavaiḥ |
yathākathañcidvā pūjyā sthāvare parameśvarī || 25 ||

p.806)

yastu prāpya sthāvarantu cakrādyaṃ na prapūjayet |
tamātmaghnaṃ vijānīyāt sarvalokavininditam || 26 ||

aśakto gandhakusumaphalā'kṣatajalaistathā |
dakṣiṇātāmbūladīpapraṇāmaparivartanaiḥ ? || 27 ||

sarvairekadvyādibhirvā śaktyā tāṃ tatra pūjayet |
sahasrādyairnāmabhistu yaścakrādau prapūjayet || 28 ||

sakṛt sampūjya ca naro nārī vā sarvapātakaiḥ |
mucyate nā'sti sandeha ityāha bhagavān śivaḥ || 29 ||

atha lakṣaprapūjādividhānaṃ śṛṇu vacmi te |
viśeṣaparvasu tathā śukravāre'pi vā'rabhet || 30 ||

saṅkalpya pūjya gaṇapaṃ svasti viprairhi vacayet |
tataḥ sampūjya vidhinā cā'vṛtyantaṃ maheśvarīm || 31 ||

pūjayet sāvṛttiṃ devīmupacāraistu pañcabhiḥ |
tatra puṣpopacārasya sthāne pūjāṃ samācaret || 32 ||

sahasrādyairnāmabhistu tato dhūpādipūjanam |
samāpayedyathāvatta pratyahañcaivamarcayet || 33 ||

samasa.khyāvidhānena pratyahaṃ pūjayet kramāt |
ekajātīyakaiḥ puṣpaiḥ yatraiva pūjayet parām || 34 ||

svayaṃ vā putrapatnyādyairbrāhmaṇadvārato'pi vā |
ante tu sarvatobhadre navayonisamāyute || 35 ||

kalaśaṃ supratiṣṭhāpya sauvarṇādisamudbhavam |
alaṅkṛtaṃ sūtravastrairmadhye taṇdulapuñjake || 36 ||

alaṅkṛtaṃ dhūpitañca nidhāya manumuścaran |
tamaṣṭagandhatoyena pūrayet pañcaratnakam || 37 ||

nikṣipya tasmin tadvat kramādācchādya pañcapallavaiḥ (?)
sataṇḍulaṃ phalaṃ pūrṇa pātrañcā'pi mukhe nyaset || 38 ||

tatra praitkṛtīṃ devyāḥ sarvā'vayavaśobhitām |
vinyasya tasyāmāvāhya pūjanantu samācaret || 39 ||

tatrā'dau sarvatobhadradevatāḥ kramato yajet |
daśadikṣu ca dikpālān śṛṅkhalāsu caturṣu ca || 40 ||

dharmādīnmadhyabhavane śvete'dharmādikān yajet |
raktār'dhabhavane pūrvāt prādakṣiṇyena pūjayet || 41 ||

asitāṅgādimithunaṃ mekhalāsu guṇatrayam |
evaṃ sampūjya kalaśe pīṭhapūjanapūrvakam || 42 ||

vidhīnā'vāhya tripurāṃ pūjayedupacārakaiḥ |
tattatpuṣ.pādikaṃ svarṇabhavaṃ vā rajatodbhavam || 43 ||

māṣādvā karṣato vā'pi kuryādanyūnamuttamam |
tadardha pādamapi vā kṛtvā tannavasaṅkhyakam || 44 ||

pūjayedupacāreṣu nāmabhirvaśinīmukhaiḥ |
madhye ca tripurāṃ rātrau pūjayeścakranāyakam || 45 ||

kalaśasya paścimataḥ sarvatobhadramaṇḍale |
svayaṃ vā pūjayedācāryeṇa vā kramavedinā || 46 ||

pūjayitvā yathāśāstraṃ kumārīṃ baṭukaṃ tathā |
guruṃ suvāsinīñcāpi brāhmaṇādīnapi kramāt || 47 ||

udvāsarahitāṃ pūjāṃ samāpyā'khilasaṃvṛtaḥ |
kathābhirgāyanairnṛtyaiḥ kuryājjāgaraṇaṃ niśi || 48 ||

paredyuḥ kṛtakṛtyo'tha pūjayeścakranāyakam |
pūjāṅgahomataḥ paścādagniṃ saṃsādhya śāstrataḥ || 49 ||

yathāvattatra juhuyāttattatpuṣpaiḥ sahasrakam | 
pūjāṃ samāpya coddhāsya kalaśaṃ vastrasaṃyutam || 50 ||

dakṣiṇāpratimāyuktaṃ suvāsinyai nivedayet |
brāhmaṇānāṃ ṣoḍaṣakaṃ suvāsinyaṣṭakaṃ tathā || 51 ||

baṭukāśca kumārīṃśca vittaśāṭhyādvivarjitaḥ |
bhojayedbhakṣyabhojyādyairdakṣiṇādyaiśca toṣayet || 52 ||

evaṃ pūjanamātreṇa sarvapāpairvimucyate |
prasannā tripureśānī vāñchitārthapradā bhavet || 53 ||

p.809)

sarvasaubhāgyasaṃyukto vaṃśaputrairyutastathā |
pitṝproddharate sarvanante mokṣaṃ samaśnute || 54 ||

rājyaprāptistu kamalaiḥ karavīrairmahacchriyam |
japāpuṣ.paiḥ santatiṃ vai jātīpuṣ.pairgṛhādikam || 55 ||

yonipuṣpairvaṃśavṛddhiṃ vakulaiḥ saumanasyatām |
kiṃśukairoganihatiṃ kuṭajaiḥ śatrunāśanam || 56 ||

evamanyaiḥ sugandhāḍhyaiḥ puṣpaiḥ patraiśca bhārgava |
pūjayitvā vidhānena mahāphalamavāpnuyāt || 57 ||

phalairdhānyairarcayeśca proktamārgānusārataḥ |
lakṣavartipradīpairvā pūjayettripurā'mbikām || 58 ||

samarthastatra varttibhyāṃ dīpāneva prakalpayet |
daśakena śatenā'pi sahastreṇā'pi vā tathā || 59 ||

pañcāśaddaśasāhasraiḥ sahasraśatameva vā |
ekaikañca ghṛtā'pūrṇamarpayedekanāmabhiḥ || 60 ||

sahasranāmabhirdevyāḥ śatanāmabhirevabā |
daśā'vartanakairvāpi caikāvartanakena vā || 61 ||

tathā pañcaśatā'vṛttyā dīpaṃ devyai samarpayet |
ante svarṇena raupyeṇa navakaṃ varttiyugmakam || 62 ||

p.810)

kṛtvā tāmramaye dīpe ghṛtavartisamūjjvale |
nivedayettu kalaśe pāyasena hunettathā || 63 ||

śrīsūktenā'bhiṣekaṃ vai kuryācchrīcakranāyake |
āvṛttīnāṃ lakṣakena sahasreṇa śatena vā || 64 ||

svayaṃ vā brāhmaṇairvāpi kṣirairikṣurasairghṛtaiḥ |
madhubhirvā phalarasairdadhibhirvā sugandhibhiḥ || 65 ||

toyaistīrthodbhavairvāpi rāma pūrvoktavartmanā |
ante daśāṃ'śato banhau pāyasena hūnet kramāt || 66 ||

pratyuṛcaṃ śrīsūktakasya pūrvattu samāpayet |
rudrābhiṣekato vā'pi mahāphalamudīritam || 67 ||

evaṃ bhārgava ! samproktaṃ dhanināṃ śubhasādhanam |
adhanaistu śarīreṇa kartavyaṃ śubhasādhanam || 68 ||

samarthastīrthayātrāntu kṛtvā śreyaḥ samāvahet |
atha vā devatāsthāne paricaryāṃ samācare tu || 69 ||

upāstitatparān vā'pi sevet vigataspṛhaḥ |
atimūḍhasya caitāvadeva kṛtyamudīritam || 70 ||

medhāvī prajapātpāṭhāt kathādīnāṃ pravācanāt |
devatāśāstrapaṭhanāt pāṭhanācchreya āpnuyāt || 71 ||

yogyeṣu devatopāstiśāstraṃ yaḥ samyagīrayet |
sa dehānte vaidrume tu prākāre nivaseściram || 72 ||

p.811)

citrāṇi yo likheddevyāḥ prāsādeṣu subhaktitaḥ |
nivasedvāsantavapre ciramānanditā'ntaraḥ || 73 ||

sammārjya śītatoyaiśca secayet yaśca mandiram |
abhirṣañcettathā śītatoyairapi suvāsitaiḥ || 74 ||

nivaseścāndravaprāntaḥ sa santāpavivarjitaḥ |
yastu rātrau dīpikābhiḥ prakāśayati mandiram || 75 ||

nivaset sūryavaprāntaḥ prāpya bhogān yathepsitān |
evamatra yathā yo yaḥ sevāṃ kuryāttu mandire || 76 ||

sa dehānte śrīpure tu sthānaṃ prāpnoti tādṛśam |
upāsakebhyo dānāni kṛtvā'pi sthānamāpnuyāt || 77 ||

yaḥ pustakaṃ devatāyā dadyādyogyāya bhaktitaḥ |
sa siddhavapravastiṃ prāpnuyāt susukhāvahām || 78

yastu pūjopakaraṇaṃ pradadyāt sādhakottame |
sa vaseddikpatīnāṃ hi vapre saukhyamavāpnuyāt || 79 ||

evaṃ yadyat pradadyādvai bhaktyopāsanasattame |
tattattulyanivasatiṃ prāpnuyānnā'tra saṃśayaḥ || 80 ||

atha te saṃpravakṣyāmi dānānāmuttamaṃ param |
śrīckrājadānantu mahāphalavidhāyakam || 81 ||

p.812)

suarvaṇi samabhyarcya cakrarājaṃ yathāvidhi |
pūjānte tripurāpūjāparaṃ vipraṃ sulakṣaṇam || 82 ||

sapatnīkaṃ samabhyarcya viśeṣadravyasādhanaiḥ |
vastrairābharaṇaiḥ gandhapuṣpādibhiḥ kramāt || 83 ||

sapīṭhaṃ sopakaraṇaṃ sādhanadravyasaṃyutam |
sāmānyā'rghodakaṃ haste viprasya samavāsṛjet || 84 ||

tato viprastu tāṃ pūjāmudvāsapramukhāñcaret |
bhojayedāvṛtimitān brāhmaṇān suvidhānataḥh || 85 ||

suvāsinīrbhojayettu nityāṣoḍaśanāmabhiḥ |
yathāśaktyā vā'pi viprān bhojayettu prayatnataḥ || 86 ||

atha vā bhojayennityānāmabhistu suvāsinīḥ |
ṣoḍaśa brahmaṇāṃścāpi dvādaśā'rādhya bhojayet || 87 || 

evaṃ dadyādyastu dānaṃ cakrarājasya bhaktitaḥ | 
tasya prītā mahādevī svasāyujyaṃ prayacchati || 88 ||

sarvatīrtheṣu sa snātaḥ tena sarvaṃ vrataṃ kṛtam |
sarvaṃ dānaṃ tena dattaṃ tena yajñāstathā kṛtāḥ || 89 ||

tapastena sutaptaṃ vai paṭhitā nikhilāgamāḥ |
tripurā'pi ca tatkarmasakṛśaṃ nā'sti vai phalam || 90 ||

iti matvā svasāyujyaṃ tasmai śīghra. prayacchati |
yataḥ sarvajagadrūpapratimaṃ rāma cakrakam || 91 ||

p.813)

p'tastena jagaddattaṃ na dattaṃ kiṃ kathaṃ bhavet |
yadi kāmanayā kuryādyaṃ yaṃ kāmamabhīpsati || 92 ||

taṃ taṃ prāpnoti sahasā mokṣañcā'nte samāpnuyāt |
evantu sthāvaraṃ yantramagre hārasamanvitam || 93 ||

mahādānayutañcā'pi dadyādviprāya yaḥ pumān |
tasya puṇyamananto'pi na śaktaḥ kathane kvacit || 94 ||

mūrti vāpi nārmadaṃ vā śivanāmamathā'pi vā |
śāligrāmaṃ kuṇḍalinīmeva dadyāttu bhaktitaḥ || 95 ||

śrīpure tu ciraṃ sthitvā mucyeddehā'vasānake |
mandiraṃ cakrarājāderjīrṇaṃ kuryānnavantu yaḥ || 96 ||

tatpuṇyaṃ syācchataguṇa navamandiranirmiteḥ |
pūjāṃ vā'pi cirocchinnāṃ pravartayati yaḥ punaḥ || 97 ||

dhanabhūmipradānena tasyā'pi śatadhā phalam |
sarvaguhyatamaṃ bhūyaḥ śṛṇu vakṣyāmi bhārgava ! || 98 ||

yastu bhakteṣu yogyeṣu sampradāyaṃ samādiśet |
dhanamānādyapekṣaḥ san kramadīkṣādirītitaḥ || 99 ||

tasyā'nantaphalaṃ rāma ! kastadvarṇayutuṃ kṣamaḥ |
yathecchaṃ nivasennāthamaṇḍale'nte vimucyate || 100 ||

p.814)

yastu yogyaṃ sampradāyaṃ prārthayantamupāsane |
kāmānmohāt pramādādvā vairāgyādvā niṣedhati || 101 ||

yaśca dravyādilobhenā'narhe śāstraṃ prayojayet |
tayorna vidyate lokaḥ prāpsyato hyadhamāṅgatim || 102 ||

tasmādyaḥ sampradāyaṃ bhaktānāṃ vaina nirūpayet |
vidyālobhayuto maurkhyāt so'pi yātyadhamāṃ gatim || 103 ||

tasmāt svaśiṣyamanyaṃ vā yuktaṃ bhaktisamanvitam |
sampradāyaṃ vijijñāsuṃ na niśidhyet kathañcana || 104 ||

etanmahāphalaṃ rāma naitattulyantu kiñcana |
anyaddānamanātmākhyamapi bandhakaram bhavet || 105 ||

etadātmapradānaṃ vai yadvidyādvidyādāne viśeṣataḥ |
vidyālikhitadānena cā'pi tatphalam syādanantakam || 106 ||

yastvaśakto buddhimāndyādvidyādāne viśeṣataḥ |
vidyālikhitadānena cā'pi tatphalamāpnuyāt || 107 ||

edamatyantasaṃguptaṃ prītyā tava nirupitam |
evaṃ mahāphalaprāptistripuropāsanāvidhau || 108 || 

eṣa bhārgava te proktastripurāyā rahasyake |
māhātmyavaibhavo yasya śravaṇādbhaktimān bhavet || 109 ||

p.815)

devarṣe ! saṃśrutaṃ kaścit sāvadhānena cetasā |
eṣa māhātmyakhaṇḍo vai tripurāyā rahasyake || 110 ||

yaḥ śṛṇoti sa sarvebhyaḥ pāpebhyaḥ pravimucyate |
paramaṃ sādhanaṃ hyetadātmabandhavimuktaye || 111 ||

yato bhaktirhi mokṣasya janayitri nigadyate |
sā māhātmayaśrutimṛte yato na bhavati kvacit || 112 ||

tasmāt parākhyasaudhasya sopānaṃ prathamaṃ nanu |
māhatmyaśravaṇaṃ loke mokṣadvāramidaṃ smṛtam || 113 ||

mahimānamasaṃśrutya kathaṃ bhaktirbhavediha |
bhakyā vinā paraṃ śreyaḥ kathaṃ syāddevatā parā || 114 || 

mokṣasya sādhanaṃ sarvaṃ vinā bhakyā na kiñcana |
yathā taruṇyāḥ saundarya vinasaṃsarvathā tathā || 115 ||

bhuvaṃ vinā yathā bījaṃ nāṅkuraṃ pratirohayet |
tathā bhaktiṃ vinā jñānaṃ phalaṃ na janayet kvacit || 116 ||

abhaktsya na karmāsti noṣāstirno vivekitā |
tasmādbhaktivihīnasya na kvacit syād dhṛtiḥ kvacit || 117 ||

māhātmyasaṃśrutistasyā mūlaṃ yasmāśca nārada ! |
tasmādetaṃ saṃśṛṇuyāt khaṇḍaṃ māhātmyasaṃjñitam || 118 ||

p.816)

śṛṇvatāṃ paṭhatāñcā'pi mahāpāpapraṇāśanam |
paṭhitavyaṃ nityametadadhyāyaṃ ślokameva vā || 119 ||

prasādaṃ tripurāśaktervāñchatā sarvathā śubham |
etadāyuṣyajananaṃ saumaṅgalyavivardhanam || 120 ||

adhno dhanvān bhūyādaputrā putriṇī bhavet |
yadyatsamīhitaṃ tattatprāpyate'sya hi saṃśruteḥ || 121 || 

vidyāpradaṃ vilikhitaṃ pūjitaṃ prepsitapradam |
vicāritaṃ jñānabhaktivairāgyādipradaṃ nṛnām || 122 ||

sarvā'gamābdhimathanādyadbhaktayamṛtamāpyatu |
amṛtāḥ śivamukhyāḥ syuḥ sā mātā tripuraiva hṛīm || 123 ||

iti śrīmaditihāsottame tripurārahasye māhātmyakhaṇḍe 
upāsakamukhyadharmavarṇanaṃ nāmā'śītitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 6669 ||

tripurāmbārpitamastu |
oṃm tat sat ||

samāptamidaṃ tripurārahasye māhātmyakhaṇḍam |


śrīvidyāyai sādarānurodhaḥ 
jāgradbodhasudhāmayūkhanicayairāplābya sarvādiśo yasyāḥ kā'pi 
kalākalaṅkarahitā ṣaṭcakramākrāmati |
dainyadhvāntavidāraṇaikacaturā vācaṃparāṃ tanvatī sā nityā bhuvaneśvarī 
viharatāṃ haṃsīva manmānase ||


tripurā-rahasyakam
devānāṃ tritayaṃ hutabhujāṃ śaktitrayaṃtrisvarā strailikyaṃ tripadī 
tripuṣkaramatho tribrahma varṇāsrayaḥ |
yatkiñciñjagatitridhāniyamitaṃ vastutrivargādikaṃ tatsarvaṃ tripureti nāma 
bhagavatyanveti te tattvataḥ ||
(laghvācāryakṛtalaghustavaḥ )


sindūrāruṇavigrahāṃtrinayanāṃ 
māṇikyamaulisphurattārānāyakaśekharāṃsmitamukhīmāpīnavakṣoruhām |
pāṇibhyāmalipūrṇaratnacaṣakaṃ raktotpalaṃvibhratīṃ 
saumyāṃratnaghaṭastharaktacaraṇāṃ dhyāyetparāmambikām ||


śrīvidyābhāvanam 
dhyāyetpadmāsanasthāṃvikasitavadanāṃ padmapatrāyatākṣīṃ 
hemāmāṃpītavastrāṃ karakalitalasaddhemapadmāṃvarāṅgīm |
sarvālaṅkārayuktāṃ satatamamayadāṃktigamyāṃbhavānīṃ 
śrīvidyāṃśāntamūrttisakalasuranutāṃ sarvasampatpradātrīm ||


salomāsthisvairaṃ palalamapi mārñjāramasite paraṃ coṣṭraṃ meṣaṃ 
naramahiṣayośchāgamapi vā |
baliṃ te pūjāyāmapi vitaratāṃ martyavasatāṃ satāṃ siddhiḥ sarvā 
pratipadamapūrvā prabhavati |
(karpurastavaślokaḥ)



tripurāsamarcanam 
ānandajanma bhavanaṃ bhavanaṃ śrutīnāṃ caitanyamātratanumamba ! 
tavā'śrayāmi |
brahmeśaviṣṇubhirūpāsitapādapadmāṃ saubhāgyajanmavasatiṃ tripure ! 
yathāvat |


hṛīṃkāramāhātmyam 

hṛoṃ hṛomiti /pratidinaṃ japatāṃ tavākhyāṃ kiṃ nāma durlabhamiha  
tripurābhidhāne |
mālākirīṭamadavāraṇamānanīyāṃstānsevate madhumatā svayameva lakṣmīḥ 
||
